kalvi uḷaviyal kōṭpāṭukaḷ








āciriyar
pērāciriyar ṭākṭar na. cuppu reṭṭiyār









tamiḻmaṇ patippakam
ceṉṉi-600 017.

patippuri
kalvi uḷaviyal kōṭpāṭukaḷ  eṉum innūl tamiḻaṟiñar na. cuppureṭṭiyār avarkaḷāl eḻutappaṭṭu māṇivācakar patippakattāl 1989 il mutal patippāka veḷiyiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. māṇivācakar patippaka niṟuvaṉar ayyā ca. meyyappaṉ avarkaḷiṉ aṟimukavuriyum, tiru. kē.ār. māṇikkam avarkaḷiṉ aṇinturiyum, āciriyariṉ nūlmukamum  innūlip paṭikkat toṭaṅkum aṉivarum mutalil paṭikka vēṇṭiya ceytikaḷākum.
kalvi uḷaviyaliṉ iyalpum aḷavum eṉum talippi mutal iyalākak koṇṭu ācāṉ eṉum talippi iṟuti iyalākak koṇṭu 13 iyalkaḷil kalvi uḷaviyal toṭarpāṉa pala ceytikaḷi innūlil pativu ceytuḷḷār.
ovvoru pakkattiṉ iṟuti varikaḷukkup piṟaku kuṟikkappeṟṟuḷḷa aṭikkuṟippukkaḷ paṭippavar aṉivarukkum mikunta payaṉit taravallavi. kalviyāḷarkaḷ, māṇavarkaḷ vāṅkip payaṉkoḷḷa vēṇṭukiṟōm.


citi āciriyark kallūri
uḷaviyal pērāciriyar kurucāmi reṭṭiyār
em.ē., (Oxon) I.E.S. (muttar) avarkaṭku*


aṉpup paṭiyal

talampukaḻ citip payiṟcikkal lūri
cālpuṟap purintapē rācāṉ
nalampoli uḷattiṉ iyalpiṉi virikkum
ñāṉacū riyaṉeṉat tikaḻntōṉ,
pulampeṟa eṉakku nuṇṇuṇar vaḷitta
puṇṇiyaṉ; taṇṇiyaṉ; eṅkaḷ
kulampuri tavattāl varukuru cāmik
koṇṭaluk kuriyatin nūlē.

 



tamiḻ vaḷarccikku

	piṟanāṭṭu nallaṟiñar cāttiraṅkaḷ
		tamiḻmoḻiyil peyarttal vēṇṭum;
	iṟavāta pukaḻuṭiya putunūlkaḷ
		tamiḻmoḻiyil iyaṟṟal vēṇṭum;1 
-pāratiyār



	ulakiyaliṉ aṭaṅkaḷukkum tuṟitōṟum nūlkaḷ
		oruvartayi illāmal ūraṟiyum tamiḻil
	calacaleṉa evviṭattum pāycciviṭa vēṇṭum;2
-pāratitācaṉ




vaḷarntu varum kalviyiyal
ca. meyyappaṉ em. ē.
aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakam
maṇivācakar patippakam ciṟanta nūlkaḷit toṭarntu veḷiyiṭṭu varukiṟatu. ilakkiyat tiṟaṉāyvu, nāṭṭup puṟaviyal, moḻi varalāṟu, ciṟṟilakkiya āyvu eṉat tiṭṭamiṭṭu ovvoru tuṟipaṟṟiyum ciṟappāṉa āyvu nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭṭu varukiṟatu.
nūliṉ talippu, poruḷ, kaṭṭamippu, tīrvukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟāl ciṟantu viḷaṅkum veḷiyīṭukaḷ aṉittiyum tamiḻ vaḻaṅkum intiyap palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷ aṉittum pāṭa nūṟkaḷāka ēṟṟuk koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. taramāṉa mutaltaramāṉa kallūrip pāṭanūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭum periya niṟuvaṉamākap patippakam vaḷarntu ciṟantuḷḷatu; pērum pukaḻum peṟṟut tikaḻkiṟatu. cila nūlkaḷ avaṟṟiṉ tarattālum taṉmiyālum poruḷamippālum palamuṟip pāṭa nūlkaḷāka ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ataṉāl avi pala patippukaḷip peṟukiṉṟaṉa. nalla kalvikkuc ciṟanta pāṭa nūlkaḷ uṟu tuṇiyākiṉṟaṉa. ataṟku maṇivācakar patippakattiṉ paṅkaḷippu peritu, mikap peritu. cīriya nūlkaḷiyē veḷikkoṇarum koḷkiyiṉāl maṇivācakar patippaka muttiri taramāṉa pāṭanūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭum uyartaramāṉa niṟuvaṉam eṉa aṟiñarkaḷāl ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu.
toṭakka nāḷiliruntē kalviyiyalpaṟṟiya pala āyvu nūlkaḷip patippakam veḷiyiṭṭu varukiṟatu.
		tamiḻar kaṇṭa kalvi
		kalvi neṟi
		aṟivāṟṟal
		tamiḻ payiṟṟum muṟi
		tamiḻ kaṟpittal
eṉak kalviyiyalpaṟṟi naṉṉūlkaḷip patippittuk kalvi ulakiṟkup periya paṇiyiṉic ceytu varukiṟatu. ippoḻutu inta variciyil pērāciriyar na. cuppureṭṭiyār avarkaḷiṉ kalvi uḷaviyal kōṭpāṭukaḷ eṉum āyvu nūlip pēra vāvuṭaṉ veḷik koṇarkiṟatu. vaḷarntu varum uḷaviyaliyum nāḷum ciṟantu varum kalviyiyaliyum naṉkuṇarnta pērāciriyar cuppureṭṭiyār tiṭpa nuṭpattuṭaṉ innūli ākkiyuḷḷār.
kalviyiyal māṉiṭaviyaliṉ ellā iyalukkum aṭippaṭi iyalākum. kaṟkat toṭaṅkum kuḻantip paruva mutal cāntuṇiyum kaṟṟal eṉum neṟippaṭi maṉitariṭam kaṟkum iyalpum kaṟka viḻiyum uḷḷattiyalpum kalvimuṟikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭi iyalpukaḷum kalviyiyaliṉ nuṇkūṟukaḷum ceṟivuṭaṉ mikat teḷivāka innūlil viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mēlip pulaṅkaḷil metta vaḷarntuḷḷa uḷaviyal kōṭpāṭu ovvoṉṟum eḷiya tamiḻil ellōrukkum viḷaṅkum vaṇṇam eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
nūlaṟivu, nuṇṇaṟivu, paṭṭaṟivu, pakuttaṟivu, potu aṟivu, kalvi kēḷvinalaṉkaḷ, māṉiṭa āṟṟalil makattāṉa kalvic cātaṉikaḷ attuṇiyum viḷakkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
innūlāciriyar pērāciriyar cuppu reṭṭiyār paṉṉūlāciriyar; paltuṟi aṟiñar; periya nūlkaḷi eḻutuvati viruppamiku paṇiyāka eṭuttuk koṇṭu eḻutum uḻip pukkañcāpperuvīrar; ceyaltiṟaṉ mikka cemmal. vēṅkaṭavaṉ palkalikkaḻakattil tamiḻttuṟiyit tōṟṟuvittu vaḷartta perumakaṉār. eṭutta kāriyattic cemmiyāka muṭikkum iyalpiṉar. aimpatu āṇṭukaḷākak kalviyiyalil īṭupaṭṭu uḻittu, paltuṟi nūlkaḷit tamiḻukku aḷittut tamiḻil kalviyiyal nūl ciṟakkat taṉip perum talimakaṉākat tikaḻkiṟār. ciṟappumiku tamiḻk kalikkaḷañciyattiṉ talimip patippāciriyarākavum tikaḻntavar. avarē oru kalik kaḷañciyam.


aṇinturi

tiru. kē. ār. māṇikkam, em. ē., el.ṭi.,
mutalvar (ōyvu)
aracu kalvi iyal kallūri, citāppēṭṭi
ceṉṉi-600 015
kalvi uḷaviyal kōṭpāṭukaḷ eṉṟa nūl eṉ ariya naṇpar pērāciriyar ṭākṭar. na. cuppureṭṭiyār avarkaḷāl tamiḻaṉṉiyiṉ tiruvaṭiyil cūṭṭappeṟṟa aḻakiya aṇi kalaṉkaḷuḷ taliciṟantatu. iṟavāta pukaḻuṭiya putu nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟal vēṇṭum eṉṟa tēciyakkavi pāratiyāriṉ aṟikūvalukku viṭiyaḷikkum pāṅkil amintuḷḷatu. tamiḻilum uḷaviyalilum peṟṟuḷḷa oruṅkiṇinta pulamiyum, paḷḷiyilumāciriyap payiṟcik kallūriyilum kaṟpitta anupavamum innūli naṉmuṟiyil yāttaṟkup perun tuṇi purintuḷḷaṉa. ittaṉikkum mēlākap pērāciriyar reṭṭiyāriṉ āṟṟoḻukkāṉa eḷiya tamiḻnaṭiyum, āṅkāṅkē poruttamākak kāṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷa anupava eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷum innūliṉ taratti mēlum uyarttukiṉṟaṉa.
uḷaviyal aṇmikkālattatu. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭilum ataṟku muṉpum meypporuḷiyaliṉ orupakutiyākak karutap peṟṟa uḷaviyal tuṟi atē nūṟṟāṇṭiltāṉ taṉiyākap pirintatu. aṟiviyal eṉṟa ciṟappum peṟṟatu. āṉāl, kalvi uḷaviyal irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiltāṉ aṟiviyal niliyi aṭintatu. iṉṟu itu potuuḷaviyalukkē cila uṇmikaḷi eṭuttiyampum niliyum peṟṟuḷḷatu. makkaḷ kaṟkakkūṭum; kaṟkattāṉ ceykiṉṟaṉar. eṉiṉum oḻuṅkākavum payaṉuṭaṉum kaṟka uḷaviyal aṟivu vēṇṭum (pak. 6) eṉṟa pēruṇmiyiṉi innūleṅkum parakkak kāṇalām.
ilakkiyattiṟkum uḷaviyalukkum neruṅkiya toṭarpu uṇṭu. peruṅkāppiyattiluḷḷa kati māntarkaḷ vāḻk kiyiluḷḷa pōrāṭṭaṅkaṭkuk kuṟiyīṭukaḷāka amin tiruppatu pōl tōṉṟum. uṇmiyil, appōrāṭṭaṅkaḷ yāvum taṉimaṉitaṉiṉ uḷḷattilēyē toṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu cintittu nōkkuvārkkup pulaṉākāmaṟ pōkātu. ivikaḷē taṟkāla uḷaviyaliṉ poruḷākavum amikiṉṟaṉa. vilaṅkiyal ārāycciyil kaṇṭa uṇmikaḷum uḷaviyalil ēṟṟa peṟṟi eṭuttukkoḷḷap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
āṅkilattiluḷḷa ciṟanta uḷaviyal nūlkaḷil āṅkāṅkuc ciṟanta āṅkila ilakkiya mēṟkōḷkaḷ kāṭṭap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. ivi paṭippōrukku oruvitat taṉi iṉpatti nalkukiṉṟatu. intac cāṉṟōrkaḷiṉ aṭiccuvaṭukaḷip piṉ paṟṟiyē pērāciriyar reṭṭiyārum kamparāmāyaṇama, tirumantiram, tiruvaruṭpā, tirukkuṟaḷ, pāratiyār kavitikaḷ, pōṉṟa tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntu poruttamāṉa mēṟkōḷkaḷik kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. ivi tamiḻil uḷaviyalik kaṟpārukku uḷaviyal uṇmikaḷit teḷivāka maṉattil amittuk koḷḷat tuṇipuriyum eṉpataṟku aiyamilli.
innūlil kalvi eṉpatu iyaṟpiyal cūḻnilikkum camūkac cūḻnilikkum poruttappāṭu aṭivatē eṉpatiyum, (pak.27) māṇākkarkaḷiṉ palvēṟu āḷumik kōlaṅkaḷi ōraḷavu kaṭṭuppāṭuḷḷa vakuppaṟiyilum, ōraḷavukaṭṭup pāṭaṟṟa niliyiluḷḷa vakuppaṟikku veḷiyilum pulaṉā vatit teḷivākkuvatum (pak. 81), kuḻantiyiṉ vaḷarcciyum tulakkamum iyal 3-il teḷivākak kāṭṭap peṟṟiruppatum, kuḻantik kalviyil kuṭivaḻiyum cūḻniliyum paṅku peṟuvataṉ viḷakkamum (iyal-4), māṇākkarkaḷiṉ kalviyil ūkkunili perumpaṅku peṟṟiruppataṉ viḷakkamum (pak. 169-172), kaṟṟalpaṟṟiya vitikaḷiṉ viḷakkamum (pak 205-216), kaṟṟalil aṭaṅkiya mūlakkūṟukaḷākiya akkaṟi, kavaṉam, pulaṉkāṭci, kaṟpaṉi, cintaṉi, niṉivu, pōṉṟavaṟṟiṉ virivāṉa viḷakkamum (iyal-7) teḷivāka viḷakkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivi kalvi kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷum māṇākkarkaḷiṉ peṟṟōrkaḷum, kalviyil ārvaṅkāṭṭum potu makkaḷum karuttil irutta vēṇṭiyavi. aṟitiṟaṉum taṉiyāḷ vēṟṟumikaḷum paṟṟiya viḷakkamum (iyal-8), māṇākkarkaḷiṉ iyalpu piṟaḻnta naṭattipaṟṟiya aṟivum (iyal-10) ellōrum aṟintu māṇākkarkaḷip parivuṭaṉ kavaṉittu avarkaḷit tiruttu vataṟkuttuṇi puriyum. oḻukka vaḷarccipaṟṟiya viḷakkam 
(iyal-11) āciriyarkaḷum peṟṟōrkaḷum karuttil irutta vēṇṭiyatu iṉṟiyamiyātatu. ācāṉ (iyal-13) eṉṟa talippiluḷḷa ceytikaḷ āciriyarkaḷ taṟcōtaṉi ceytu koḷvataṟkut tuṇi ceyyum.
innūlil kiyāḷappeṟṟuḷḷa kalic coṟkaḷ pakkaṅkaḷil aṭikkuṟippāka amintuḷḷaṉa. paṭippōrukku ivi peruntuṇiyāka amiyum. iccoṟkaḷ akara varicip paṭuttip piṉṉiṇippu-2 āka iṇikkappeṟṟirup patu nūl eḻutuvōrukkup payaṉpaṭum. iṅṅaṉam oru muṟiyāṉa amippil taramāṉa viḻumiya nūlip paṭitta pērāciriyar reṭṭiyāriṉ peruntoṇṭu pārāṭṭap peṟa vēṇṭiyatākum. āciriyarkaḷum kalviyiyal paḷḷi, kallūri māṇavarkaḷum kalviyil nāṭṭamuḷḷa perumakkaḷum innūlit takka muṟiyil payaṉpaṭuttuvār kaḷāyiṉ atuvē avarkaḷ nūlāciriyarukkuc ceyyum peru naṉṟiyāka amiyum. ittakiya pala ariya payaṉuṭiya nūlkaḷi mēlum mēlum paṭittu inta āciriyar moḻikkum tāymoḻi mūlam kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaṭkum perumakkaḷukkum toṇṭāṟṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu vāḻttukiṉṟēṉ.
A.H-220
aṇṇānakar,							kē. ā r. māṇikkam
ceṉṉi-600040
15-12-1985

nūl mukam
	eṉṉuṭi uyirē! eṉṉuḷat taṟivē!
		eṉṉuṭi aṉpeṉum neṟiyāy!
	kaṉṉalmuk kaṉitēṉ kaṇṭamir teṉṉak
		kalanteṉi mēviṭak karuṇi
	maṉṉiya uṟavē! uṉṉināṉ piṟiyā
		vaṇṇameṉ maṉameṉum karuvi
	taṉṉatu vaḻiaṟ ṟeṉṉuḻik kiṭappat
		taṉṉaruḷ varamatu vēṇṭum.1
-tāyumāṉaaṭikaḷ
uṭal nalam pēṇuvataṟku uṭaliyal aṟivu iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭappaṭuvatupōla uḷavaḷattip pēṇi vaḷarppataṟku uḷaviyal aṟivum mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatu eṉpatu kalvi muṟivallār kaṇṭamuṭivu. pāṭatti miyamākak koṇṭu payiṟṟum muṟi (Subject- centered) māṟik kuḻantiyi miyamākak koṇṭa muṟi ((Child- centered) vaḻakkil vantuviṭṭatu. intap putiya muṟikku mutaṉ mutalil vittiṭṭavar rūsō (1712-1778) eṉṟa pēraṟiñar. avar eḻutiya omili eṉṟa nūltāṉ putiya muṟikaḷiṉ vittu eṉalām. ivar karuttuppaṭi, iyaṟki aṉṉiyiṭamiruntu varum eviyum ciṟantu viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa: āṉāl aviyaṉittum maṉitaṉ kippaṭṭatum cīraḻikiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu ivar koḷki; itittāṉ iṉṟu ellā nilik kalvi payiṟṟutalilum nām kāṇum uṇmi; kalvi pukaṭṭutalil kuḻantitāṉ mukkiya kūṟu, maṉitaṉ vaḷartta paṇpāṭalla eṉpatu ivar karuttu. itaṉāltāṉ rūsōvik kalvi muṟiyiṉ kāparṉika eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. pūmiyi naṭuvāka vittutāṉ ēṉiya kōḷkaḷ iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟa paḻiya koḷkiyik kāparṉika maṟuttuk katiravaṉi naṭuvākak koṇṭutāṉ avi iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟa uṇmiyi ulakiṟku uṇarttiyatupōlavē, atukāṟum kalvi ēṟpāṭṭiṉi (ūracaciuraṭaraa) naṭuvāka vittuk kaṟpikkap peṟṟa muṟiyit tavaṟu eṉak kaṇṭittu kuḻantiyi naṭuvāka vittuk kaṟpikkap peṟavēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟiyavar rūsō. kuḻantiyi naṉṟāka ārāyak kuḻantiyi aṟivatu eḷitalla. kuḻantiyik kavaṉi; iyaṟkiyōṭu icintunaṭa eṉpavi rūsō kalvit tuṟiyil paṇiyāṟṟupavarkaṭkuk kāṭṭiya māperum uṇmikaḷ. ivar koḷki uḷaviyal uṇmikaḷi oṭṭiyum uḷḷatu.
ikkoḷkikaḷip payiṟci peṟṟa āciriyarkaḷ aṉi varum aṟivārkaḷ. aṟintiruntum kīḻvakuppukaḷil kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷ, kuṟippākak kuḻantip paḷḷikaḷil kaṟkum kuḻantikaḷi avarkaḷ koṭumip paṭuttum muṟikaḷ colli muṭiyā. atuvum āṅkilattik kaṟpikkum vāyilākak (Medium) koṇṭu kaṟpikkum paḷḷikaḷ kuḻanti kaḷik kiḷippiḷḷikaṭkuk kaṟpippatupōl kaṟpittu varuvatik kāṇum peṟṟōrkaḷiṉ kaṇṇil nīr vaṭiyāmal irukka muṭiyātu. illattil kaṟpikkum tāymārkaḷum kuḻantikaḷi nāṭāppativu kaḷākkukiṉṟaṉar; olittaṭṭukaḷākkukiṉṟaṉar. ippaṭik kiḷippiḷḷikaḷ pōl oppuvittaṟku āciriyarkaḷum atika matippeṇkaḷ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. inta matippeṇ māyattilum vakuppil peṟum nili (RANK) māyattilum peṟṟōrkaḷ mayaṅki mati iḻakkum nilikkuḷḷākiṉṟārkaḷ. pirāṇikaḷik koṭumip paṭuttuvatit tavirppataṟku cīvakāruṇyacaṅkam (SPCA) eṉṟa ōr amipput tōṉṟiyuḷḷatip pōl kuḻantiḷik koṭumip paṭuttuvatit tavirkka ōr amippu vēṇṭumpōl tōṉṟukiṉṟatu.
kalvi uḷaviyal kōṭpāṭukaḷ eṉṟa inta nūl kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaṭkum, kuḻantik kalviyil akkaṟi koḷḷum peṟṟōrkaṭkum, potumakkaṭkum oru vaḻikāṭṭi yāka amiyaṭṭum eṉṟē itaṉi eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭēṉ. inta nūlil kalvi uḷaviyaliṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmi, maṉitaṉum cūḻniliyum, kuḻantiyiṉ palvēṟu vaḷarccip paruvaṅkaḷil ataṉ maṉavaḷarcci, kuḻantik kalviyil ūkku nilikaḷ, kalviyil uḷḷak kiḷarcciyiṉ paṅku, kuḻanti kaṟṟalil aṭaṅkiya mūlak kūṟukaḷ, aṟitiṟaṉpaṟṟiya karuttukaḷ, ivaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭiyil taṉiyāḷ vēṟṟumikaḷ, kuḻantikaḷiṉ uṭalnalam paṟṟiya palvēṟu ceytikaḷ, pālkalviyip paṟṟiya karuttu, kuḻantiyiṉ iyalpu piṟaḻnta naṭattikaḷ, avaṟṟic camāḷikkum muṟikaḷ, kuḻantiyiṉ oḻukka vaḷarcci, kuḻantik kalviyil tērvu muṟikaḷ-ākiya palvēṟu karuttukaḷ viḷakkam aṭikiṉṟaṉa. ciṟuvar kalviyil akkaṟi koḷḷum peṟ ṟōrkaḷ, āciriyarkaḷ, potumakkaḷ ivarkaṭku iṉṟiyamiyāta ceytikaḷ palavum innūlil aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa.
taramāṉa nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭṭu veḷḷiviḻāk kāṇum maṇivācakar patippakattiṟkum, eṉṉiṭam aṉpu kāṭṭum kuṟippāka es. meyyappaṉ avarkaṭkum, itaṟku mutaṟkāraṇamāka irunta maṇivācakar nūlaka mēlāḷar 
tiru. irā. kurumūrtti avarkaṭkum, innūli aḻakuṟa acciṭṭu utaviya kaṟpakam accaka mēlāḷar, ceyvaṉa tiruntac ceyyum tiru. ka. nārāyaṇaṉ avarkaṭkum eṉ maṉam uvanta naṉṟiyip pulappaṭuttik koḷḷukiṉṟēṉ.
innūlukku aṇinturi vaḻaṅkiya tiru.kē.ār. māṇikkam, ceṉṉi-citiyiluḷḷa āciriyariya kallūriyil (ippōtu aracu kalvi kallūri eṉappeyar māṟṟam peṟṟatu) el, ṭi vakuppil payilumpōtu (1940-41) oru cāli māṇākkar; nalloḻukkam mikka eṉkeḻutaki naṇpar; māṇākkar uṇavu viṭutiyil taṅkiyiruntapōtu nerukkamākap paḻakiyavar. kallūri nāḷil turuturu veṉṟu aṅkum iṅkum ōṭip potuppaṇiyil ārvaṅkāṭṭi vakupputtōḻarkaḷiṉ aṉpiyum pērāciriyarkaḷiṉ matippiyum peṟṟup pukaḻuṭaṉ tikaḻntavar. iraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷ āciriyap paṇi āṟṟi, nāṉku āṇṭukaḷ pōrttuṟiyil paṅkēṟṟup paṇipurintavar. ataṉpiṉṉar mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷ māvaṭṭak kalvi atikāri yākavum, mūṉṟari āṇṭukaḷ aracuttērvukaḷiṉ ceyala rākavum, āṟu āṇṭukaḷ kalvi iyakkunariṉ taṉi aluvalarākavum, nāṉku āṇṭukaḷ kalvit tuṇiiyakkunarākavum, aintari āṇṭukaḷ maṇṭalap paḷḷi āyvāḷarākavum, tām payiṉṟa citi āciriyar kallūriyil āṟu āṇṭukaḷ mutalvarākavum paṇiyāṟṟip perumpukaḻuṭaṉ ōyvu peṟṟavar. paṇiyāṟṟiya kālattil. eṉ kaṭaṉ paṇi ceytu kiṭappatē2 eṉṟa kuṟikkōḷuṭaṉ paṇiyāṟṟiyatāl cakappērāciriyarkaḷ, māṇākkarkaḷ, potumakkaḷ mēliṭattār ivarkaḷiṉ aṉpukkum pārāṭṭutalukkum ilakkākip perum pukaḻ peṟṟavar. iṅṅaṉam paltuṟi anupavam peṟṟa aṉpariṉ aṇinturiyi innūl peṟṟatu innūliṉ perumiyip paṉmaṭaṅku uyarttukiṉṟatu; nūlāciriyarākiya aṭiyēṉukkum perumakiḻcciyi nalkukiṉṟatu. iṅkukkūṟiya perumikaḷ ellām peṟṟa naṇpar innūlukku aṇinturi vaḻaṅkiyamikku eṉ aṉpu kalanta naṉṟi eṉṟum uriyatu.
pērāciriyar kurucāmireṭṭiyār citi āciriyar kallūriyil eṉ uḷaviyal pērāciriyar. ākḵpōrṭu palkalikkaḻakattil em. ē. paṭṭam peṟṟu intiyak kalvip paṇiyāḷarākap (ai.nu.ḷu) paṇiyāṟṟiyavar. ciṟanta muṟiyil-āṉāl caṟṟu vinōtamāṉa muṟiyil-kaṟpippavar.
	kalipayil teḷivu kaṭṭuri vaṉmi
	nilamali niṟikōl malarnikar māṭciyum
	ulakiya laṟivō ṭuyarkuṇam iṉiyavum3
peṟṟut tikaḻntavarātalāl, ivar vakuppil nikaḻttum pērurikaḷi uṉṉippākak kavaṉippavarkaḷ perumpayaṉ aṭivar. aḷakkalākā aḷavum poruḷum tuḷakkalākā niliyum tōṟṟamum uṭiyavar. aiyan tīrap poruḷi yuṇarttum tiṟam paṭittavar. ivar,
urikkap paṭumporuḷ uḷḷat tamittu
viriyāṉ vekuḷāṉ virumpi mukamalarntu
koḷvōṉ koḷvaki aṟintuavaṉ uḷaṅkoḷak4
koṭuttalāl ivaruṭiya pērurikaḷ māṇākkariṭam akatteḻucciyi uṇṭupaṇṇi uḷaviyalil taṉi nāṭṭam ēṟpaṭac ceyyum; āḻntu kaṟkavum tūṇṭum. ivaṟṟāl perumpayaṉ peṟṟavaṉ aṭiyēṉ. ivaruṭiya pērurikaḷ iṉṉum eṉceviyil olippaṉa pōṉṟa pacumiyāṉa uṇarvu uṇṭākiṉṟatu. ittakiya perum pērāciriyarukku innūli aṉpup paṭiyalākki makiḻkiṉṟēṉ.
eṉṉuṭiya neñcil niliyāka iṭam peṟṟavaṉ vēṅkaṭam mēviyaviḷakku eppaṭi iṭam peṟṟāṉ teriyumā?
	paṉikkaṭa lilpaḷḷi kōḷip
		paḻakaviṭṭu ōṭivantu, eṉ
	maṉakkaṭa lilvāḻa valla
		māya maṇāḷanampi.5
iṉṉum avaṉ,
	aravattu amaḷiyi ṉōṭum
		aravintap pāṟkaṭa lōṭum
	aravintap pāviyum tāṉum
		akampaṭi vantu pukuntu
	paravit tiripala mōtap
		paḷḷi koḷkiṉṟa pirāṉ.6
1960-mutal ivaṉ kāṭṭum oḷiyil vāḻum aṭiyēṉukku eṇṇaṟṟa tollikaḷiyum tuṉpaṅkaḷiyum tantālum, nalla uṭal nalattiyum maṉavaḷattiyum aruḷi varupavaṉ. avaṉaruḷāltāṉ iṉṟuvari nūlulakil āḻaṅkāl paṭṭup palatuṟi nūlkaḷit toṭarntu eḻuti varukiṉṟēṉ; eṉ kalvit toṇṭum toṭarntu naṭipeṟṟu varukiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam eṉṉip palvakit toṇṭukaḷil āḷākki vittu eṉakkup palavita nalaṉkaḷi aruḷivarum apperumāṉi maṉam moḻi meykaḷāl vāḻtti vaṇaṅki avaṉ kaḻaliṇikaḷiyē caraṇamākak koḷḷukiṉṟēṉ.
aṉparpaṇi ceyyeṉi āḷākki
viṭṭuviṭṭāliṉpanili tāṉē vantu eytum parāparamē.7
     -tāyumāṉa aṭikaḷ.
vēṅkaṭam
AD-13,aṇṇānakar							na. cuppureṭṭiyār
ceṉṉi-600 040
20-12-1985 



iyal-1
kalviuḷaviyaliṉ iyalpum aḷavum
kalviuḷaviyal kōṭpāṭukaḷ eṉṉun toṭaril kalvi yāvatu etaṉik kuṟikkiṉṟatu? uḷaviyal etaṉi uṇart tukiṉṟatu? kōṭpāṭukaḷ yāvi? eṉpavaṟṟi ārāytal vēṇṭum.
mutalil, kalviyippaṟṟic ciṟitu ārāyvōm. oru kālattil, kaṟpittal maṭṭumē kalvi eṉṟum, paṭippu, eḻuttu, kaṇitam ākiya mūṉṟiyum kuṭimiyippaṟṟiya cila uṇmi kaḷiyum payiṟṟuvittalē kalvi eṉṟum karutiṉar. itu muṟṟilum cari eṉṟu colla muṭiyātu. aṭippaṭip pāṭaṅkaḷil payiṟci peṟutal maṭṭum kalviyaṉṟu; aḵtu ataṉilum cālapperitu. aṉṟiyum, nīṇṭakālamāka, kalviyil māṇākkaṉ tāṉ kaṇṭa aṟiviyum maṉappāṉmikaḷiyum eppaṭiyāvatu uṭkoḷva tākavum, kalvi avaṉukkuḷ ūṟṟap peṟu vatākavum karutappeṟṟatu. māṇākkaṉiṉ uḷḷam oru veṟṟiṭamākavum, ati āciriyar aṟivuttuṇukkukaḷāl nirappuvatākavum koḷḷappeṟṟatu. kalvi eṉpatu nām oruvaṉukku ūṟṟak kūṭiya poruḷaṉṟu; ūṭṭakkūṭiya poruḷumaṉṟu. aḵti avaṉē aṭital vēṇṭum.
iṉṟiya kalvi ārāyccikaḷum, makkaḷippaṟṟip piṟa tuṟikaḷāl aṟintaṉavum kalviyāṉatu makkaḷiṉ naṭattiyi māṟṟum oru ceymuṟi eṉṟu kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. kalviyil mūṉṟu aṭippaṭik kūṟukaḷ uḷa. avi: taṉiyāḷ1, avaṉ vāḻum cūḻnili2, taṉiyāḷukkum cūḻnilikkum iṭiyil nikaḻkiṉṟa iṭiviṉi3. maṉitaṉiṉ piṟappumutal iṟappuvari yil avaṉukkum avaṉ vāḻum cūḻnilikkum iṭiyaṟāta iṭinikaḻcciyum iṭiviṉiyum naṭipeṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa. taṉiyāḷ cūḻniliyāl pātikkappeṟukiṉṟāṉ; cūḻnili avaṉi māṟṟukiṉṟatu. avaṉ iṭiyaṟātu. iyaṟṟum iṭi viṉikaḷāl cūḻniliyum māṟṟap peṟukiṉṟatu. inta iṭi viṉikaḷāltāṉ kalvi ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu.
kuḻanti aḻukiṉṟatu. tāy ataṉi eṭuttup pālūṭṭu kiṉṟāḷ. aḻuki niṉṟu pōkiṟatu. piṟitoru camayam aḻu kiṉṟatu; ippoḻutu paciyāl alla; ciṟunīr viṭṭatāl āṭi naṉiyavē kuḷirāl aḻukiṉṟatu. tāy naṉinta āṭikaḷi nīkki vēṟu āṭikaḷi uṭuttukiṉṟāḷ; paṭuk kiyiyum māṟṟukiṉṟāḷ. aḻuki niṟkiṟatu. aḻutāl pala nalaṉkaḷip peṟalām eṉṟa anupavattik kuḻanti peṟu kiṉṟatu. ṭamvilli4 eṉṟa aṟiñariṉ karuttuppaṭi, kalvi eṉpatu ataṉ paranta poruḷil, taṉiyāḷ toṭṭililiruntu cuṭukāṭu pōkum variyil peṟum ellāc celvākku
kaḷiyum aṭakkik koṇṭuḷḷatu eṉpatākum.
kuḻantikaḷ paḷḷikkuc cellumuṉṉarē vīṭṭilum teruvilum viḷiyāṭṭiṉ mūlamum aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkiyiṉ mūlamum ettaṉiyō ceytikaḷik kaṟkiṉṟaṉar eṉpati nām aṟivōm. ākavē, kaṟpōriṉ ceyaliyō, uṇarcciyiyō, eṇṇattiyō māṟṟuvatē kalviyeṉap paṭukiṉṟatu. namatu vāḻkkiyi uṟṟu nōkkuṅkāl nām kaṇantōṟum namatu putikac cūḻniliyōṭum camūkac cūḻniliyōṭum ūṭāṭukiṉṟōm. kalviyiṉ nōkkam yāteṉil, nalvāḻkki naṭatta nammi namatu cūḻnilikkup poruttamuṟac ceyvatil tuṇi ceyvatākum. innōkkattip peṟa atu nam ceyal
kaḷi māṟṟa muyalukiṉṟatu. ākaliṉ, kalviyiṉ ilakkaṇa māvatu oruvari vāḻkkikkup poruttamuṟutaṟ poruṭṭu māṟṟam ceyvatākum.
iṉi, uḷaviyalippaṟṟic ciṟitu pārppōm. uḷaviyal eṉpatu uyiriyiṉ5 maṉacceyalkaḷi variyaṟuttuk kūṟum ōr aṟiviyal tuṟi. uyiruḷḷa oru maṉitaṉ māṟi māṟi varum ulakiluḷḷa poruḷkaḷukkum nikaḻccikaḷukkum ēṉiyōrukkum iṭiviṉiyāṟṟuvatip paṭippatē uḷaviyalākum. atu nam anupavaṅkaḷiyum ceyalkaḷiyum piṟavaṟṟiyum muṟip paṭuttik kōvi ceytu veḷiyiṭukiṉṟatu. uḷaviyalum ōr aṟiviyalē
mutalil aṟiviyal6 iṉṉatu eṉpati variyaṟi ceytu koḷvōm. aṟiviyal eṉpatu ōr oḻuṅkāṉa muṟiyil, oruṅkaminta- naṉku cōtikkappeṟṟa- ceytikaḷiṉ tokuppu. aḵtu oru kuṟippiṭṭa tuṟiyiyō allatu oru cilavakit takavalkaḷiyō nikaḻccikaḷiyōpaṟṟi virittu urikkum. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka- iyaṟpiyal7 āṟṟalip paṟṟi, aḵtāvatu veppam, oḷi, oli, miṉṉāṟṟal pōṉṟa vaṟṟiṉ cila meym mikaḷippaṟṟi eṭutturikkiṉṟatu; vētiyiyal8 caṭap poruḷippaṟṟi, aḵtāvatu caṭapporuḷkaḷ evvāṟu oruṅ kiṇintaṉa, avi evvāṟu pirikiṉṟaṉa eṉpatippaṟṟip pēcukiṉṟatu; uyiriyiyal9 uyiruḷḷa poruḷkaḷip paṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟatu; aḵtāvatu tāvaraṅkaḷ, pirāṇikaḷ evvāṟu vaḷarntu tulakkamuṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatik kūṟukiṉṟatu; uḷaviyal10 eṉpatu uyirikaḷiṉ maṉac ceyalkaḷi variyaṟuttuk kūṟuvatu; aḵtāvatu naṭattiyi eṭutturippatu. iṉṉum viḷakkamākak kūṟiṉāl makkaḷiṉ eṇṇaṅkaḷ, uṇarccikaḷ, ceyalkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟi viḷaṅka urippatu uḷaviyalākum. ākaliṉ, ovvōr aṟiviyal tuṟiyum ōṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭarpu koṇṭa meymmikaḷiṉ tokutiyi viḷakkukiṉṟatu.
ovvoru tuṟiyic cārnta poruḷ allatu meym mikaḷiṉ tokuppu oru kaṭṭuppāṭṭiṉuḷ aṭaṅkiya uṟṟu nōkkalāl ciṟappāṉa muṟiyil payiṟci peṟṟavarkaḷāl tiraṭṭappeṟṟatu; avarkaḷtām aṟiviyal aṟiñarkaḷ. eṉavē, ipporuḷ kaṟpaṉiyālum cintaṉiyālum maṭṭilum tiraṭṭappeṟṟatu aṉṟu eṉpatākiṉṟatu. ōvvoru tuṟiyilu muḷḷa ceytikaḷ variyaṟukkappeṟṟa ceṟmuṟikaḷiṉāl11 tiraṭṭappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. ic ceymuṟikaḷit tokuttu aṟiviyalmuṟi12 eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvar. aṟiviyal muṟiyil pala paṭikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. kaṭṭuppāṭṭukkuṭpaṭṭa uṟṟunōkkalāl takavalkaḷit tiraṭṭi avaṟṟi oru piracciṉipōl kūṟutal mutaṟpaṭiyākum. ivaṟṟik koṇṭu karutukōḷkaḷ13 amittuk koḷvatu iraṇṭāvatu paṭiyākum. ik karutukōḷkaḷ cariyāṉavitāmā eṉpati aṟivataṟku mēlum pala puḷḷi vivaraṅkaḷi (eṭukōḷkaḷi14)t tiraṭṭi avaṟṟiṟku viḷakkam tarutal mūṉṟāvatu paṭiyākum. piṟaku ivaṟṟiliruntu potu vitikaḷi uṇṭākkutal nāṉkāvatu paṭiyākum. ippotu vitikaḷi putiya ceyalkaḷil allatu nikaḻccikaḷil poruttip pārttal aintāvatu paṭiyākum. ākavē, aṟiviyal aṟivu niccayamāṉatu; nampat takuntatu; piṟarāl caripārkkak kūṭiyatu. pala karuvi karaṇaṅkaḷiyum aḷavi muṟikaḷiyum mēṟkoṇṭu tiraṭṭap peṟṟa ivvaṟivu mikattiṭṭamāṉatu.
uḷaviyal uyiriyiṉ maṉacceyalkaḷi viritturikkum tuṟi eṉṟu mēlē kūṟiṉōm. cūḻnilikkēṟṟavāṟu taṉip paṭṭa uyiriyiṉ ceyalkaḷippaṟṟiya takavalkaḷē it tuṟi viritturikkum poruḷ. ēṉiya aṟiviyaltuṟikaḷip pōlavē ittuṟiyilum takavalkaḷ kaṭṭuppāṭṭukkuṭpaṭṭa uṟṟunōkkalāl tiraṭṭappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. it takavalkaḷik koṇṭu avaṟṟiṉuḷ amintu kiṭakkun toṭarpukaḷik kāṇa muyalukiṉṟatu. it toṭarpukaḷē uḷaviyal vitikaḷ allatu kōṭpāṭukaḷ. ivi paruntu nōkkāka maṉitac ceyalkaḷiyum avaṟṟiṉ iyalpiyum eṭutturikkiṉṟaṉa. ivvitikaḷ maṉita naṭattiyip purintu koḷḷat tuṇi purivatuṭaṉ ataṉi muṟkūṟavum (ñacanaṉiuva)kaṭṭuppaṭuttavum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
uḷam mikavum cikkalāṉatu. ataṉōṭu ūṭāṭum poruḷ kaḷaṭaṅkiya cūḻniliyum makkaḷaṭaṅkiya camūkac cūḻniliyum māṟik koṇṭirukkum iyalpuṭiyavi. aṉṟiyum, caṭap poruḷkaḷ pōlaṉṟi uḷam cuyēcciyāka iyaṅkavallatu. inniliyil uḷattippaṟṟi aṟiyum takavalkaḷ muṟṟilum nampakkūṭiya muṟiyil illi. ākavē, uḷaviyal muṟṟiṉa aṟiviyal ākātu; ataṉi iḷam paruvattiluḷḷa aṟiviyal eṉṟē kūṟavēṇṭum. maṉitaṉuṭiya ceyalit tiṭṭamāka muṉṉarē aṟintu muṟṟilum ataṉmītu āṭcikoḷḷamuṭiyum eṉṟu kūṟuvatu naṭimuṟiyil iyalātatoṉṟu. naṭattik koḷ kiyiṉar aḵtu iyalumeṉṟu kūṟiṉum ciṟanta uḷaviyalaṟiñarkaḷ ataṉi oppuk koḷḷār. eṉiṉum, uḷaviyal ōraḷavu piṟari aṟintu avarmēl ātikkam peṟamuṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvati aṉivarum oppukkoḷvar. iḵtu iyalāviṭil camūkavāḻvu eṅṅaṉam naṭipeṟum?
uḷaviyalippaṟṟi iṉṉoru ceytiyum īṇṭuk kavaṉikkat takkatu. iyaṟpiyal, vētiyiyal, vilaṅkiyal15 pōṉṟavi iruppu nili16 nūlkaḷākum. aṟanūl,17 aḷavi nūl18, eḻiliyal19 pōṉṟavi uyarnili nūlkaḷākum.20 muṉṉavi uḷḷavaṟṟi uḷḷapaṭiyē urikkum, piṉṉavi poruḷkaḷ allatu nikaḻccikaḷ eṅṅaṉam irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kūṟāmal, eṅṅaṉam irukkavēṇṭum eṉṟu iyampum. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, aḷavi nūl nām eṅṅaṉam cintik kiṉṟōmeṉṟu kūṟāmal, uṇmiyi aṇuka vēṇṭumāyiṉ eṅṅaṉam cintikkavēṇṭum eṉṟu viḷakkukiṉṟatu. aṟanūlum nām eṅṅaṉam oḻukukiṉṟōm eṉṟu kūṟāmal eṅṅaṉam oḻuka vēṇṭum eṉṟu navilkiṉṟatu. uḷaviyal iruppunili nūl vakiyic cērntatu. ākavē, aḵtu uḷattiṉ cintaṉikaḷ, ceyalkaḷ ākiya aṉittiyum uḷḷatu uḷḷavāṟē kūṟukiṉṟatu. avaṟṟic cariyeṉṟō, tavaṟeṉṟō, nallateṉṟō, tīyateṉṟō muṭivukaṭṭu vatilli. atu iyalpāṉa21 ceyalkaḷ, piṟaḻvāṉa22 ceyalkaḷ, pittar ceyalkaḷ mutaliya aṉittiyumē viḷakkukiṉṟatu, atu matippuppaṟṟiya viṉākkaḷil taliyiṭuvatilli. ākavē, aṟanūlum aḷavinūlum uḷattiṉ ceyalkaḷi aṇukum muṟikaḷiliruntu avaṟṟi uḷaviyal aṇukum muṟiyi vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭuvatu iṉṟiyamiyātatu.
uḷaviyalukkum kalvi uḷaviyalukkum
uḷḷa toṭarpu
uḷaviyal eṉpatu uyirikaḷiṉ naṭattiyiyum, avi evvāṟu palvēṟu vitamāka māṟum iyalpuḷḷa tūṇṭal kaḷukkēṟṟavāṟu etirviṉikaḷ purikiṉṟaṉa eṉpatiyum viritturikkum nūlākum. atu makkaḷ naṭattiyiyēyaṉṟi, pirāṇikaḷiṉ naṭattiyiyum eṭuttuk kūṟum. ākavē, uḷaviyalvallār elikaḷ, pūṉikaḷ, vālillāk kuraṅkukaḷ23 pōṉṟavi evvāṟu cikkaṟikaḷil24 ōṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu paṟṟiyum, atu pōṉṟa ceyalkaḷ purivatu paṟṟiyum cōtaṉikaḷ naṭattuvatil atikak kālattic celaviṭukiṉṟaṉar; aṅṅaṉamē makkaḷ ellāvita nilimikaḷukkēṟṟavāṟu eṅṅaṉam etirviṉi purikiṉṟaṉar eṉpatiyum ārāykiṉṟaṉar; ittakiya vāyp pukaḷi āyvakattil ēṟpaṭutti, avaṟṟil taṉiyāḷ puriyum tulaṅkalkaḷik karuttuṭaṉ pativu ceykiṉṟaṉar. itaṉ viḷivāka avarkaḷ vaḷarnta maṉitaṉiṉ ceyalkaḷi (e-ṭu-pulaṉkāṭci, kaṟpaṉi, cintaṉi, kavaṉam, uḷḷak kiḷarcci, paṟṟu, muyaṟci pōṉṟavaṟṟi) viḷakkuvar. ākaliṉ, potu uḷaviyal vaḷarntavarkaḷiṉ ceyalkaḷi viḷakkum nūlākum.
kalvi uḷaviyal kōṭpāṭu naṭimuṟiyil kāṇum vitikaḷi eṭutturippatu payaṉmuṟi uḷaviyal.25 potu uḷaviyalil kaṇṭa uṇmikaḷ kalvit tuṟiyil kiyāḷap peṟṟaṉa. itaṉāl kaṟpavarkaḷiṉ iyalpum ceyalkaḷum viḷakkam peṟṟaṉa. itaṉāl atu kaṟṟalukkum payiṟṟalukkum uriya kōṭpāṭukaḷi viḷakkum nūlāyiṟṟu. atu cila kālamākac ceḻittōṅkit taṉittuṟiyāyc ciṟappuṟṟu varukiṉṟatu. iṉṟu atu potu uḷaviyalukkē cila uṇmikaḷi eṭuttiyampum niliyiyum peṟṟuḷḷatu. kuḻantikaḷ, iḷiñarkaḷ, vaḷarntavarkaḷ pōṉṟa yāvarum kaṟkum nikaḻccikaḷil tōṉṟum ceyalkaḷi eṭutturikkiṉṟatu; kaṟṟaliṉ iyalpu, vakikaḷ, muṟikaḷ, cātakamāṉa ētukkaḷ, kaṟṟaliṉ pōkku mutaliya kūṟukaḷiyum viḷakkukiṉṟatu. eṉavē, kalvi uḷaviyal kalvikkalikkum potu uḷaviyalukkum iṭippaṭṭa tuṟi eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṉum tavaṟaṉṟu. makkaḷ kaṟkakkūṭum; kaṟkattāṉ ceykiṉṟaṉar. eṉiṉum, oḻuṅkākavum payaṉuṭaṉum kaṟka uḷaviyal aṟivu vēṇṭum.
kalvi uḷaviyal iṉṟu kuḻanti uḷaviyal, kumarapparuva uḷaviyal, maṉanalattuṟi, camūka uḷaviyal ākiya tuṟi kaḷiṉiṉṟum pala takavalkaḷip peṟukiṉṟatu. aṉṟiyum, atu camūka maṉitaviyal,26 maruttuvattuṟi, uḷanōy maruttuvattuṟi,27 uyiriyal, camūkaviyal28 pōṉṟa tuṟikaḷiṉiṉṟum taṉakkup poruttamāṉa takavalkaḷip peṟukiṉṟatu.
potu uḷaviyalōṭu vittu oppiṭṭāl, kalvi uḷaviyal aṇmiyil tōṉṟiyatu eṉpatu teḷivākum. makkaḷ ātiyiliruntē oruvari yoruvar kavaṉikkum tiṟaṉuṭiyarāyiruntaṉarātaliṉ, uḷaviyalum mikat toṉmi vāyntatu. āṉāl ki. pi. 1879 il vilhelm vuṇṭ29 līpsik palkalikkaḻakattil uḷaviyal ārāycci niliyattit toṭaṅkiya nāḷtoṭṭu ik kalikku aṟiviyal ciṟappuk kiṭittatu. āṉāl kalvi uḷaviyal irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattiliruntutāṉ aṟiviyal niliyi aṭintatu.
uḷaviyaliṉ nōkkaṅkaḷ
makkaḷ, vilaṅkukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ ceyalkaḷippaṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷi oḻuṅkākavum tiṭṭamākavum muṟṟilum vari yaṟuttu viḷakka muyalvatu uḷaviyal. ataṉ nōkkaṅkaḷ yāvi?
nammuṭiya naṭattiyi aṟintu ataṉmēl āṭci koḷvatu oṉṟu; piṟaruṭiya naṭattiyi aṟintu ataṉ mēl āṭci koḷvatu maṟṟoṉṟu; nammuṭiya naṭatti yiyum piṟaruṭiya naṭattiyiyum avaṟṟiṉ poruṭṭē aṟital iṉṉoṉṟu; makkaḷiṉ iyalpi aṟintataṉ mūlam camūkap piracciṉikaḷit tīrppatu piṟitoṉṟu; uḷavi yalaṟiñaṉukku maṉitaṉ evvāṟu tōṉṟukiṉṟāṉ eṉpati aṟivatu vēṟoṉṟu; payiṟṟalil āciriyarukku mikavum payaṉpaṭṭu ataṟkut tuṇiyāka niṟpatu nām aṟiya vēṇṭiyatoṉṟu. ivaṟṟic caṟṟu virivāka ārāyvōm.
taṉṉippaṟṟiya meyyaṟivu uṇṭātal: taṉṉippaṟṟiya meyyaṟivu mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatu; ovvoruvarukku mukki yamāka vēṇṭap peṟuvatu. uṉṉiyē aṟintukoḷ eṉpatu āṉṟōriṉ aruḷmoḻi. namatu vāḻkkit toḻilum niliyum eṅṅaṉamiruppiṉum, vāḻavēṇṭiyavarkaḷ nām eṉpatu orutali. mikac cikkalāṉa taṉi vīṟṟiṉik30 koṇṭa nām eṇṇaṅkaḷilum, uṇarccikaḷilum, naṭattiyilum vēṟupaṭu kiṉṟōm. uḷaviyal eṉpatō potuvāṉa muṟi kaḷippaṟṟik kūṟum oru tuṟi. ataṉāl nam contap piracciṉikaḷukku uḷaviyalik koṇṭu tīrvum viḷakkamum kāṇpateṉpatu iyalātatoṉṟu. eṉiṉum eṇṇam, uṇarcci, naṭatti pōṉṟavaṟṟippaṟṟip paṭippataṉāl aṟintu koḷḷuṅ karuttukaḷi namatu contap piracciṉiyilum payaṉ paṭuttalām. namatu uṭal, uḷḷakkiḷarcci, camūkam, cintaṉi, mutaliyavaṟṟil eḻum tēvikaḷi yoṭṭi nammōṭum ulakattuṭaṉum nām oṭṭa oḻukuvataṟkuriya vaḻivakikaḷi uḷaviyal mūlam aṟintu koḷḷalām.
maṟṟavarkaḷic cariyāka uṇarntu koḷḷa utavutal: ivvulakil nām piṟaruṭaṉ icintu vāḻavēṇṭumāyiṉ, avarkaḷuṭiya eṇṇaṅkaḷ, uṇarccikaḷ, ceyalkaḷ mutali yavaṟṟiṟkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷit terintu koḷḷavēṇṭum. māṇākkarkaḷil cilar atikam uḻikkāmalēyē kuṟinta muyaṟciyāl uyarnta matippeṇkaḷ peṟuvataṟkum, vēṟu cilar evvaḷavu varunti uḻittālum tērcciyaṭiyāmal iruppataṟkum kāraṇam eṉṉa? ivvaḷavu vēṟṟumi ēṉ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu? ulakiluḷḷa palvēṟu makkaḷiṭiyē kāṇappeṟum palvēṟupaṭṭa anupavaṅkaḷ, karuttukaḷ, uṇarccikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkuk kāraṇam yātu? avarkaḷatu piṟappilēyē ivvēṟṟumikaḷ amintiruntaṉavā? allatu avarkaḷ vāḻum cūḻnili kāraṇamāka avi ēṟpaṭṭaṉavā? camūkattiluḷḷa maṉitarkaḷiṉ naṭatti palvēṟu vitamākak kāṇappeṟuvataṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṉa? eṉpaṉapōṉṟa viṉāk kaḷukku viṭi kāṇa uḷaviyal tuṇi ceykiṉṟatu. ic ciṟunūlil aṉittiyum viḷakka iyalātu. uḷaviyal aṟivu ēṟpaṭṭa piṟaku makkaḷiṉ cinti, uṇarcci, col, ceyal mutaliyavaṟṟippaṟṟi namakku uṇṭākum karuttiṟkum ataṟku muṉṉirunta karuttiṟkum atika vēṟṟumi uṇṭu. avaṟṟik kaṇṭu nām avvaḷavu atikamāka mayaṅkuvatilli eṉpatu veḷippaṭi.
aṟiviṉ poruṭṭē aṉittiyuma aṟital: maṉitaṉ aṟivuḷḷavaṉ. payaṉukkākac cilar ceytikaḷi aṟiyiṉum aṟivukkākavum ceytikaḷi aṟiyum avāvuḷḷavarkaḷum irukkattāṉ ceykiṉṟaṉar. aṟivut tiṉaviṉip pōkkik koḷḷa avvāṟu ceykiṉṟaṉar. eppoḻutāvatu ivi payaṉpaṭum eṉṟa nampikkiyāl tammuṭiya naṭattiyiyum piṟaruṭiya naṭattiyiyum kūrntu ārāykiṉṟaṉar. taṉi yāṭkaḷ evvāṟu cūḻnilikkēṟpat tulakka muṟukiṉṟaṉar eṉpatit teḷivāka aṟitalvēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiviyal uḷaviyalaṟiñaṉ viḻikiṉṟāṉ. evvāṟu nam koḷvāykaḷum31 mūḷikaḷum nām kāṇum ulakattiyum kāṇā ulakattiyum aṟintukoḷkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, evvāṟu nam anupavaṅ kaḷi nammiṭam irutti vittu aṟivārāycciyilum kaṟpaṉiyilum payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟōm eṉṟum terintukoḷḷa virumpu kiṉṟāṉ. ivvulakil cilar poruḷ tiraṭṭuvatilum cilar pukaḻi nāṭi alivatilum uḻalkiṉṟaṉar; cilar pakalveyilil kāyntu uḻittu amitiyākak kālaṅkaḻikkiṉṟaṉar; cilar tam koḷkikkākavum tam talivaṉukkākavum makiḻvuṭaṉ uyiriyum koṭukka āyattamāka irukkiṉṟaṉar; cilar etilum otuṅki niṟkiṉṟaṉar; cilar piṭivātamāka uḷḷaṉar. cilar kōḻikaḷāka vuḷḷaṉar. ikkuṇaṅkaḷil cilavō palavō ovvoruvariṭamum amin tuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟkellām kāraṇam eṉṉa eṉpati uḷaviyal aṟiñaṉ oru payaṉiyum karutātu avaṟṟiṟkeṉavē ārāykiṉṟāṉ. uṇmiyāṉa, niccayamāṉa, aṟiviṉi aṭiyum vaḻi mika nīḷamāṉatu; atu kaṭiṉamāṉatuvumākum.
camūkap piracciṉikaḷit tīrppatilum viḷakkuvatilum tuṇiyāka iruttal: camūkap piracciṉikaḷi aṟiviyal muṟiyil ārāyntu tīrvu kāṇpataṟku uḷaviyal uṇmikaḷ payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ittuṟi iṉṟu camūka uḷaviyal eṉṟa oru taṉit tuṟiyākavē vaṭivu peṟṟuḷḷatu. maṉitaṉ vaḻivaḻi yākap paṭittukkoṇṭa niliyaṅkaḷi māṟṟamuṭiyum eṉpatu uḷaviyal vallāriṉ nampikki. camūkattil eḻum pala cikkalāṉa pirac ciṉikaḷiyum uḷaviyal muṟippaṭi tīrkkalām; avi eḻāmalum tavirkkalām. aṇmiyil, uḷaviyalaṟiviṉ payaṉālum ārāyc ciyālum, poruḷiyal, araciyal, varalāṟu, camūkaviyal, maṉitaviyal pōṉṟa pala camūkap piracciṉikaḷukku viḷakkamum teḷivum ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. maṉitaṉuṭiya camūka naṭattiyiyum uḷaviyal viḷakkukiṉṟatu.
camūka naṭatti eṉpatu kaṟṟukkoṇṭa naṭattiyākum. ovvoru kuḻantiyum camūkattiṉiṭiyē piṟantu, camūkat tiṉiṭiyē vāḻvatāl ataṉuṭiya naṭatti camūkattiṉiṭam uṇṭākum palavitamāṉa māṟutalkaḷāl amikiṉṟatu. kuṭivaḻi32 muṟiyil kiṭikkum āṟṟalkaḷum camūka vāḻviṉ payaṉāl palavitamāṉa māṟutalkaḷi aṭikiṉṟaṉa.
maṉitaṉ piṟantatum oru kuḻuviṉ uṟuppiṉaṉ ākiṉṟāṉ. avaṉukku atika nerukkamāṉa kuḻuvāka iruppatu kuṭumpam ākum. avaṉ piṟanta kuṭumpamum ataṉuṭaṉ toṭarpuṟṟa piṟa kuṭumpaṅkaḷum cērntu avaṉuṭiya cūḻniliyāka amikiṉṟaṉa. ataṉ piṟaku kuṭumpam cātiyākavum, cāti camutāyamākavum accūḻnili virivaṭikiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu camūkaṅkaḷ oṉṟu cērntu oru mātiriyāṉa paṇpuṭiyaṉavāka amivatu āṟṟal mikka uḷaviyal pōkkukaḷiṉ viḷivēyākum. āṉāl cila camayam intap pōkkukaḷiṉ āṟṟal kuṉṟi, cērntuḷḷa amippu citaṟippōkavum kūṭum. aṅṅaṉam citaṟip pōva taṟkum uḷaviyal kūṟukaḷē kāraṇam eṉpati niṉivil iruttutal vēṇṭum. maṉitaṉuṭiya naṭatti avaṉ vāḻum kuṭumpam, cāti, camutāyam ākiyavaṟṟāl variyaṟukkap peṟukiṉṟatu. ivvariyaṟi oḻuṅku ceyyāta variyaṟi, oḻuṅku ceyta variyaṟi eṉa iru vakippaṭum. oḻuṅku ceyyāta variyaṟi maṉitaṉuṭiya valimik kuṟivi aṭip paṭiyākak koṇṭatu. ētēṉum oru vatanti kiḷampiyatum makkaḷ kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamākak kūṭit tavaṟāṉa ceyalkaḷil iṟaṅkuvatu itaṟku ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭu. makkaḷi oṉṟāka iṇikkum āṟṟalkaḷum, avaṟṟip payaṉpaṭuttak kūṭiya talivaṉum, makkaḷiṭiyē kuṟikkōḷ oṟṟumiyum iruk kumāyiṉ, appoḻutu oḻuṅkāṉa variyaṟi uṇṭākum. ivi yāvaṟṟiyum anutāpam, karuttēṟṟam33, pārttuc ceytal, kūṭi vāḻ iyalpūkkam mutaliya uḷaviyal kūṟukaḷē cāttiyamākkukiṉṟaṉa. perumpālum intak kūṟukaḷ uḷḷattiṉ naṉavili34 pākattil āḻntu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. ciṟuvayatil peṟṟa camūka uḷḷap pōkkukaḷ naṉavili uḷappakutiyil taṅkiyiruntu piṟaku periyavaṉākum poḻutu puṟampē tōṉṟuvatuṇṭu. oruvaṉ periyavaṉāṉa piṟakum uḷḷakkiḷarcci yip poṟut tavari kuḻantiyākavē iruppāṉ. avaṉuṭiya uḷaviyal vaḷarcci taṭippaṭṭup pōyirukkum. ataṉāl avaṉ kuḻanti pōlavē tavaṟāka naṭantu koḷvāṉ. iccamūka naṭattiyi naṉku aṟintu koḷḷa vēṇṭumāyiṉ, uḷaviyal ārāyc ciyil mēṟkoḷḷap peṟum aṟiviyal muṟiyik kiyāṇṭu terintu koḷḷalām.
 uḷaviyalaṟiñaṉ maṉitaṉi eṅṅaṉam kāṇkiṉṟāṉ: uḷaviya laṟiñaṉ maṉitaṉi ōr uyiriyākavē karutukiṉṟāṉ. poruḷkaḷaṭaṅkiya cūḻniliyum piṟa makkaḷ aṭaṅkiya cūḻ niliyum uṇṭākkum tūṇṭal kaḷukku maṉitaṉ eṅṅaṉam tulaṅkukiṉṟāṉ eṉpatik kāṇkiṉṟāṉ. eṅṅaṉam maṉitaṉ cūḻnilikkēṟpat taṉṉip poruttiyamittuk koḷḷukiṉṟāṉ eṉpatē avaṉ mēṟkoḷḷum ārāycci. maṉitaṉuṭiya uṭalukkup puṟampē, āṉāl avaṉuṭiya pulaṉ ellik kuḷḷaṭaṅkiya, ellāp poruḷkaḷum vicikaḷum aṭaṅkiya ōr amippē cūḻnili eṉpatu.
ovvoruvaruṭiya vāḻkkiyiyum ūṉṟi nōkkiṉāl, avaravar tamakku amintuḷḷa cūḻniliyōṭu ōyāmal ūṭāṭik koṭukkal-vāṅkal ceytu koṇṭiruppatu pulaṉākum. ik koṭukkal vāṅkalē poruttappāṭu eṉpatu; aḵtāvatu iyaṉṟavari naṉṉilikku māṟutal. cila camayam taṉiyāḷ cūḻniliyi yoṭṭit taṉṉip poruttik koḷkiṟāṉ. (e-ṭu.) ōriṭattiliruntu piṟitōriṭattiṟkuc cellutal; oru toṟṟunōykku oru taṭiyi (aiaaranvila) ēṟpaṭuttik koḷḷutal. cila camayam avaṉ taṉakkēṟṟāṟ pōl cūḻniliyi māṟṟiyamikkiṉṟāṉ. (e-ṭu.) oru poruḷit taṉakku appāl taḷḷutal allatu apporuḷit taṉṉi nōkki iḻuttal; tāṉ vāḻum aṟiyi nuṇṇiya nōyp puḻukkaḷ aḻiyumāṟu (ḷuvanacaṭiṇina) ceytu koḷḷutal. taṉṉi māṟṟik koḷḷuvatilum cūḻniliyi māṟṟiyamittuk koḷvatilum avaṉ taṉakkum cūḻnilikkum iṭiyiluḷḷa toṭarpi māṟṟukiṉṟaṉa.
cila camayam taṉiyāḷ cūḻnilikkēṟpat taṉṉip poruttik koḷḷukiṉṟāṉ eṉpōm; cila camayam cūḻ niliyit taṉakkēṟṟavāṟu poruttukiṉṟāṉ eṉṟu kūṟuvōm. oṉṟil avaṉ viṭṭuk koṭukkiṉṟāṉ; maṟṟoṉṟil āṭci celuttukiṉṟāṉ. tāṉ ōr ūrtiyil celluṅkāl vaḻiyil oru pāṟāṅkal iruntāl, ataṉi vilakic ceṉṟu taṉṉip poruttik koḷḷukiṉṟāṉ allatu ak kalli akaṟṟi viṭṭu ūrtiyic celuttukiṉṟāṉ. iraṇṭu naṇparkaḷ tām ceyya vēṇṭiyatip paṟṟi māṟupaṭṭa karuttuṭiyavar kaḷāka iruntāl, viṭṭuk koṭukkum iyalpuḷḷavar maṟṟavariṉ viruppattiṟ kēṟṟavāṟu tammuṭiya tiṭṭaṅkaḷi māṟṟik koḷḷa muyalukiṉṟār; ākkiramikkum iyalpuḷḷavar ivarit tam tiṭṭatti mēṟkoḷḷumpaṭi ceytu viṭukiṉṟār. iruvarum poruttappāṭu aṭiyum ceyalil iṟaṅki yuḷḷaṉar.
ivvāṟu taṉiyāḷum cūḻniliyum iṭiyaṟātu iṭi viṉi iyaṟṟum ceyali aṭiyiṟkāṇum vāypāṭukaḷāl uṇarttalām.
(i)	ma  u  ma
(ii)	u  ma  u
ivaṟṟil ma eṉpatu maṉitaṉiyum u eṉpatu ulakattiyum kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. mutalāvatu: maṉitaṉ ulakattit tākku kiṉṟāṉ; ulakam tirumpa maṉitaṉit tākkukiṉṟatu eṉpati uṇarttukiṉṟatu. iraṇṭāvatu: ulakam maṉitaṉit tākku kiṉṟatu; maṉitaṉ tirumpa ulakattit tākkukiṉṟāṉ eṉpati viḷakkukiṉṟatu. kalvi uḷaviyal kuḻantiyiṉ paṭṭaṟivu, ceyalkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟik koṇṭu avaṉic cūḻnilik kēṟṟavāṟu poruttamuṟac ceyvatil tuṇi niṟkiṉṟatu.
uḷaviyalāl āciriyarukkup payaṉ: uḷaviyalik kaṟṟal āciriyarukku eppaṭi iṉṟiyamiyātatākiṉṟatu? itic ciṟitu ārāyvōm. oru kālattil āciriyar kaṟpikkum pāṭap poruḷimaṭṭilum naṉku aṟintāl pōtum eṉṟa karuttu nilaviyatu. āṉāl, ciṟitu kālamāka atu maṭṭilum pōtāteṉṟu karutap peṟukiṉṟatu. payilvikkum māṇākkaṉiyum aṟiya vēṇṭiyatu mika iṉṟiyamiyātatu eṉṟu pala kalvi vallunar kaḷ kūṟi varukiṉṟaṉar. āciriyar parantāmaṉukkup pintamiḻip payilvittār-intac coṟṟoṭaril payilvittal eṉṟa viṉi parantāmaṉiyum taḻuvi niṟkiṉṟatu; pintamiḻiyum taḻuvi niṟkiṉṟatu kāṇka. āciriyar pintamiḻiyum aṟintiruttal vēṇṭum; parantāmaṉiyum aṟintavarāy iruttalvēṇṭum. parantāmaṉi aṟitaltāṉ uḷaviyal; inta uḷaviyal, āciri yarukku mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatu. paṇṭiya āciriyar pin tamiḻukku aḻuttam tantaṉar; iṉṟiya āciriyar parantāmaṉukku aḻuttam tarukiṉṟaṉar ākavē, viṭsarlāntu nāṭṭu uḷaviyalaṟiñar peṭalāsi35 eṉpār kalvikkaliyi uḷaviyal mayamākku eṉṟu kūṟip pōntār. eṉavē, uḷa viyal, āciriyarukku mikavum eṉṟum iṉṟiyamiyātatu eṉpatu peṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
inta uḷaviyalaṟivu āciriyarukku ettuṇi aḷavu payaṉpaṭukiṉṟatu? mūṉṟu tuṟikaḷil ivvaṟivu āciriyarukkup payaṉpaṭalām.
mutalāvatu: tām kaṟpikkum māṇākkarkaḷi naṉku aṟintu koḷḷutal, paḷḷi māṇākkarkaḷukkeṉṟē ēṟpaṭṭuḷ ḷatu; māṇākkarkaḷiṉ nalaṉukkeṉṟē āciriyar niyamikkap peṟṟuḷḷār. māṇākkarkaḷi iru muṟikaḷāl aṟintu koḷḷalām. oṉṟu, avarkaḷit taṉiyāḷkaḷāka aṟital; iraṇṭu, kuḻuvāka aṟital. enta iraṇṭu māṇākkarkaḷum orē mātiriyāka iruppatilli. ovvoruvarum iṇiyaṟṟa taṉiyāḷ36; itutāṉ avaruṭiya taṉi vīṟu.37 āyiṉum, enta iraṇṭu māṇākkarkaḷum kurutiyālum iṉattālum toṭar pillātavarāyi ruppiṉum, kiṭṭattaṭṭa orē mātiriyākavum uḷḷaṉar. avarkaḷukku orē vakiyāṉa pulaṉuṟuppukaḷum piṟa uṟuppukaḷum uḷḷaṉa; avarkaḷ oru cila uḷaviyal vitikaḷiṉ paṭi vaḷarcciyum tulakkamum aṭikiṉṟaṉar. āciriyar taṉippaṭṭa māṇākkaṉi aṟital vēṇṭum. kuḻuvāka avarkaḷukkup payiṟṟum vāyppu iruppiṉum, avaruṭiya kavaṉam muḻuvatum ovvoruvarum iṇiyaṟṟa taṉiyāṭkaḷ eṉpatil irukka vēṇṭum. avarkaḷip poṟittaṉmiyuḷḷa kuḻukkaḷ eṉṟu karutātu uyiruḷḷa oru kuḻuviṉ uṟuppiṉarkaḷ eṉak karututal vēṇṭum. ovvoruvarum orē vakiyil oruvarāka iruppiṉum, av vakuppiluḷḷa ēṉiyōriṭamiruntu muṟṟilum vēṟupaṭṭavar. āciriyar it taṉiyāḷ vēṟupāṭukaḷit terintu avvakuppiṉit taṉit taṉiyākak kiyāḷuṅ kāl avvēṟu pāṭukaḷukkut takka matippuk koṭuttal vēṇṭum. āṭukaḷam, uṇavu viṭuti, vakuppaṟi ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil taṉi yāḷākak kavaṉikkalām; cuṟṟulāc cellumpōtu itaṟku nalla vāyppu kiṭikkum. kuḻantiyiṉ iyalpu yātu? avaṉuṭiya iyaṟkip paṇpukaḷ, āṟṟalkaḷ yāvi? kuḻantiyiṉ vaḷarccik kōlaṅkaḷ yāvi? āḷumi38 eṅṅaṉam vaḷarkiṉṟatu? camūka uṇarcci eppoḻutu tōṉṟukiṉṟatu? ati eṅṅaṉam vaḷarkkalām? eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa viṉākkaṭku viṭi kāṇpatāl māṇāk karkaḷip paṟṟi naṉku aṟintu koḷḷalām.
iraṇṭāvatu: māṇākkarkaḷip paṟṟiya aṟivu āciriyarukku avarkaḷmītu taṉippaṭṭa celvākkut tarutal. avarkaḷi niṉivil vittiruntu peyar koṇṭu aḻittu, taṉṉampikkiyuṭaṉ avarkaḷi āciriyar aṇukalām. uṟ cākamum nērt toṭarpum koṇṭāl, avarkaḷi naṉ ṉiliyil uruvākkalām. aṟivē āṟṟal”. māṇākkariṉ maṉaṅkaḷippaṟṟiya aṟivu kalvip payaṉkaḷi eytum muṟiyil koṇṭu celuttat takka āṟṟalit tarukiṉṟatu. mēlum, uḷam evvāṟu kaṟkiṉṟatu eṉpati varuṇikkiṉṟatu; āciriyar, kaṟṟalil avvuḷḷaṅkaḷukkut tuṇiyāka irukkalām. paḻak kaṅkaḷum39 paṟṟukaḷum40 evvāṟu uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa eṉpati uḷaviyal uṇarttukiṉṟatu. naṟpaḻakkaṅkaḷum virumpattakka paṟṟukaḷum uṇṭāka āciriyar tuṇi ceyyalām. uṟṟu nōkkal tiṟaṉkaḷum, paṭippuk kaṟpaṉiyum eṅṅaṉam vaḷar kiṉṟaṉa eṉpati viḷakkukiṉṟatu; ittiṟaṉ vaḷarcciyil āciriyar tuṇi ceyvatu avaruṭiya poṟuppākiṉṟatu. eṉavē, uḷaviyal aṟivāl māṇākkar kaḷiṭam āciriyariṉ celvākku atikamākiṉṟatu; payiṟṟum poruḷiyum payiṟṟum muṟikaḷiyum oru kaṭṭuppāṭṭiṟkuḷ koṇṭu celutta muṭi kiṉṟatu. kaṟpikkum kaliyil uḷaviyal aṭippaṭi aṟiviyalākac ceyaṟpaṭukiṉṟatu.
mūṉṟāvatu : āciriyar tammic cariyāka uṇarvataṟkum tam paṇiyiṉ palaṉic cariyāka matippiṭuvataṟkum tuṇiyāka iruttal. māṉiṭat tiṟaṉ41 kaḷiṉ vīcciṉi42 aṟiyum aṟiviṉiyum tammuṭiya tiṟaṉit tammuṭaṉ paṇiyāṟṟum tōḻa āciriyar kaḷiṉ tiṟaṉuṭaṉ oppiṭṭup pārppataṟkuriya āṟṟaliyum43 uḷaviyal tarukiṉṟatu. ataṉāl taṉmuṭiya matippic44 cariyāṉa muṟiyil aḷaviṭṭut tammuṭaṉ paṇiyāṟṟu vōriṭam amitiyuṭaṉ vāḻat tuṇi ceykiṉṟatu. tammuṭiya āṟṟalkaḷ yāvi eṉpati aṟintu koḷḷāmalēyē pala āciriyarkaḷ toḻilil nuḻikiṉṟaṉar. tammuṭiya vēli yippaṟṟip perum pārāṭṭi etirpārttu atu kiṭik kāviṭil avarkaḷ cōrvu aṭikiṉṟaṉar. oruvar amitiyāṉa maṉanili eyta vēṇṭumāṉāl avar tammuṭiya naṟkūṟu kaḷiyum, valuvaṟṟa kūṟukaḷiyum aṟintu ataṟkēṟṟavāṟu ceyalāṟṟutalēyākum. ittakiya aṟivu naṟkūṟukaḷik perumpayaṉ aṭivatil payaṉpaṭuttalām. kuṟipāṭukaḷ nēriṭuvatik kuṟittuk koḷḷalām. uṉṉi aṟintu koḷ eṉṟu mēlē kuṟippiṭṭa aruḷmoḻiyiṉi āciriyarum tammi aṟintu koḷvatil mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiyatu iṉṟi yamiyātatu. uḷavil ataṟkut tuṇi niṟkiṉṟatu.
uḷaviyal ārāycci muṟikaḷ
uḷaviyal cila ārāycci muṟikaḷ mēṟkoḷḷap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟic ciṟitu aṟintu koḷvōm.
uṟṟunōkkal muṟi: ellā nūlkaḷum kiyāḷum muṟikaḷil mutalil tōṉṟuvatu uṟṟunōkkal muṟiyākum. itil iraṇṭu vakiyuṇṭu. oṉṟu, akakkāṭci45; maṟṟoṉṟu, puṟakkāṭci. uḷaviyalār paṇṭiya nāḷ toṭṭuk kiyāḷuvatu akak kāṭciyākum. paṇṭi nāḷil aḵtoṉṟē muṟiyāy viḷaṅkiyatu. akakkāṭci eṉpatu oruvar tammuṭiya maṉattiṉ ceyaliyō anupavattiyō uḷ nōkkit terintu koḷḷutalākum. uḷḷattiṉ ceyalkaḷ taṉittaṉiyē ov voruvarukkum uriyavi. ātaliṉ, piṟarāl avaṟṟi eḷitil aṟiya iyalātu. avaravarkaḷē tamatu maṉaniliyi veḷiyiṭ ṭāl maṭṭumē nām aṟital kūṭum. avvāṟu eṭutturikka vēṇṭumāyiṉ avvuḷac ceyal nikaḻntavuṭaṉēyē, avarkaḷ avaṟṟi uḷnōkkik kāṇṭal vēṇṭum. uḷnōkki ārāyvatu eṉpatu caṟṟuk kaṭiṉamē. mēlum, uḷḷattiṉ nilikaḷum ceyalkaḷum māṟikkoṇṭē pōkum iyal puṭiyavi; avaṟṟic cariyākak kavaṉippatu eḷitaṉṟu. tavira vilaṅkukaḷ, kuḻavikaḷ, pittarkaḷ, kāṭṭumirāṇṭikaḷ pōl muṉṉēṟṟa maṭiyātavarkaḷ āki yōriṉ uḷḷattippaṟṟiya ceytikaḷi akakkāṭciyāl aṟiya muṭiyātu; varuṇikkavum iyalātu. iṉṉum ōr iṭarppāṭum uṇṭu. ciṉam, iṉpam pōṉṟa uḷḷattiṉ ceyalkaḷi viruppu veṟuppiṉṟiyum tiṭṭamākavum uṟṟu nōkkuvateṉpatu arumiyiṉum arumi. vekuṇṭu viḻumpoḻutu vekuḷi varum vaḻiyi uṟṟu nōkkiṉāl vekuḷi maṟintaṉṟō ōṭum! itu kāṇum kaṇkaḷiyē kāṇa muyalvatu pōlākumaṉṟō? immuṟiyil uḷḷattiṉ niliyi ārāya muyalutal iruḷi ārāya miṉ viḷakki ēṟṟutal pōlākum eṉkiṟār oru periyār. āṉāl, immuṟi eḷitaṉṟāyiṉum, acāttiyam aṉṟu. uḷḷattiṉ ceyal naṭantu koṇṭirukkumpōtē, aḵtāvatu ataṉ iyalpu māṟuvataṟku muṉ, ati niṉivu kūrntu ataṉ iyalpi aṟiyalām. ākavē, akakkāṭci yaṉittum piṉ ṉōkkam46 ākum. uṇavi uṇṭu uvappativiṭa ataṉik kaṇṭu kaḷippataṟku ītu oppākum. eṉiṉum, payiṟciyālum paḻakkattālum im muṟiyiṉ tiṟaṉi mikutippaṭuttalām. pala uḷḷa nilikḷip paṟṟi vēṟu vakiyil aṟiya iyalāmiyiṉ im muṟiyiyum nām mēṟkoḷḷukiṉṟōm.
cila uḷaviyalār iyaṟpiyal pōṉṟa piṟa nūlkaḷiluḷ ḷatip pōl puṟak kāṭciyiyum kiyāḷukiṉṟaṉar. ik kāraṇattāl uḷaviyal aṇmik kālattil virivaṭintuḷḷatu. uḷḷam uṭaluṭaṉ neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyatāyiruppatāl uḷḷac ceyalkaḷ tōṉṟumpōtu uṭal māṟupāṭukaḷum tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. oruvar ciṉaṅkoḷḷumpoḻutu avar kaṇkaḷ civakkiṉṟaṉa: paṟkaḷ naṟa naṟaveṉkiṉṟaṉa: ki mūṭṭukaḷ nempukiṉṟaṉa: cuvācittalum kurutiyōṭṭamum pātikkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. im māṟupāṭukaḷāl avar ciṉaṅ koṇṭār eṉṟu eḷitil ūkikkalām. aṅṅaṉamē, oruvar avaruṭiya poṭṭunarampukaḷ acivatik kaṇṭu avar āḻntu cintik kiṉṟār eṉṟu anumāṉikkalām. ip puṟakkāṭci muṟiyāl pala uṇmikaḷ kaṇṭaṟiyappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. im muṟiyilum iṭarppāṭukaḷ irukkattāṉ ceykiṉṟaṉa. nammuṭiya viruppu veṟuppukaḷ itilum kalakkiṉṟaṉa. cilar ciṉaṅ koṇṭirun tālum puṉṉaki kāṭṭuvar; piṟar tuṉpuṟuvatil uvaki koḷḷiṉum, vicaṉa maṭintavar pōl naṭippar. ākavē, puṟakkāṭci muṟiyum kuṟiyuṭiyatē.
cātāraṇa uṟṟu nōkkal vēṟu, aṟiviyal uṟṟu nōkkal vēṟu. oru tāy taṉ kuḻanti viḷiyāṭuvatiyum, aḻuvatiyum, piṟa kuḻantikaḷuṭaṉ kalām viḷippatiyum, uṟavāṭuvatiyum kāṇkiṉṟāḷ. itu kaṭṭuppāṭṭiṟ kuṭ paṭāta uṟṟunōkkal. āṉāl, vakuppil māṇākkarkaḷ maṉappāṭam ceyyum muṟikaḷ paṟṟic cila kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷi ēṟpaṭutti, avarkaḷiṉ tērcci vēṟṟumikaḷik kavaṉittal kaṭṭuppāṭṭiṟ kuṭpaṭṭa uṟṟu nōkkal.
paḷḷi māṇākkarkaḷik kuṟippiṭṭa kālaṅkaḷil kavaṉittu, avarkaḷ ceyalkaḷi oḻuṅkāka eḻutivittu avarkaḷuṭiya puṉivuttiṟaṉ, uḷḷuṇarvu47, talimip paṇpu, poṟuppu ivi pōṉṟa kuṇaṅkaḷi aṟutiyiṭalām. pala kuṭimip paṇpukaḷiyum immuṟiyil kavaṉittu aṟiyalām.
pativēṭukaḷ: cila pativēṭukaḷi48liruntum uḷaviyal ārāycci muṟikaḷ mēṟkoḷḷap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ mūṉṟiṉi maṭṭilum īṇṭu viḷakkuvōm.
 (I) nikaḻcci muṟi49: oru kuṟippiṭṭa viṣayatti meyp pippataṟkākac cila tiṭṭamāṉa variyaṟutta nikaḻccikaḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭu vatip poṟuttu immuṟi amikiṉṟatu. vilaṅkukaḷiṭattum ciṟuvar kaḷiṭattum immuṟi mēṟkoḷḷap peṟukiṉṟatu. kuṟippukaḷiyum kati kaḷiyum koṇṭu viṣayaṅkaḷiyum viḷakkamuyalukiṉṟatu immuṟi. itu muṟṟilum nampattakkataṉṟu. peṟṟōrkaḷ tam celvak kuḻantikaḷip paṟṟiyum celvap pirāṇikaḷip paṟṟiyum uḷḷatu uḷḷapaṭi kūṟāmal mikipaṭak kūṟuvatu iyalpu. kākkikkum taṉ kuñcu poṉ kuñcu aṉṟō? katikaḷum kuṟippukaḷum uṇmiyip periteṉa niṉikkāmal cuviyi yum kaṟpaṉic ciṟappiyum periteṉak karutupavi. nāyippaṟṟiya uḷaviyaṟ kati yoṉṟil oruvar tam celva nāyiṉ muṉ cenniṟap pommiyiyum nīlaniṟap pommiyiyum kāṭṭumpōtellām, atu cenniṟap pom miyē virumpiyatik koṇṭu ataṟku niṟampaṟṟiya aṟivu uṇṭu eṉṟār. āṉāl, aṇmiyil mēṟkoḷḷappeṟṟa āyvukaḷmūlam nāykaḷ niṟakkuruṭuṭiyavi eṉṟa muṭivu kiṭittuḷḷatu. nāy cenniṟap pommiyit tērnteṭup pataṟkuk kāraṇam ataṉ maṇam, kaṉam, tiṇmi pōṉṟa vēṟṟumikaḷāka iruntirukkalām. allatu taṟceyalākavum avvāṟu nēriṭalām. ākavē, immuṟi ciṟantatākātu. tiṭṭamākak kaṇṭavaṟṟiyum, nipantaṉikaḷukkuṭpaṭṭu aḷak kakkūṭiya vivaraṅkaḷiyum maṭṭumē ēṟka vēṇṭum.
(II) taṉiyāḷ ārāycci 50: itu potuvāka oru taṉiyāḷip paṟṟi muṟṟilum vivaramāka aṟivatākum. taṉittaṉik kuḻantikaḷiṉ ceyalkaḷi nīṇṭakālam kuṟittu vittu, ak kuṟippukaḷi liruntu potuvitikaḷiyaṟiya muyaluvatu. immuṟi mēṉāṭukaḷil kuḻantikaḷiṉ uḷḷattiyalpiṉiyum uṭalniliyiṉiyum ārāynta aṟiñarkaḷ kuḻantikaḷip paṟṟip pala kuṟippukaḷi eḻuti vittuḷḷaṉar. ciṟuvarkaḷ, mētikaḷ, aṟivilikaḷ pōṉṟavarkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟip paṟṟiyum iṅṅaṉam kuṟippurikaḷ eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ tokutiyē taṉiyāḷ varalāṟākum. ituvum payaṉ tarattakka muṟiyē.
cila camayam, navīṉa aṟiviyal muṟippaṭi oru māṇākkaṉip paṟṟi āḻntu terintukoḷḷa vēṇṭiyatu iṉṟiyamiyāta tākiṉṟatu. māṇākkaṉuṭiya piracciṉikaḷ mika nuṇukkamum, cikkalum uṭiyavi eṉat tōṉṟiṉāl āciriyarkaḷ avaṟṟi oru vallunariṭam kūṟalām. paṭittal, eḻuttukkūṭṭal, kaṇitam mutaliya pāṭaṅkaḷil nīṭitta peruntiṟaṉiṉmi, kavarcciyiṉmi, maṉaveḻuccik kōḷāṟu, viparītac camūka maṉappāṉmi mutaliya aṟikuṟikaḷāl ittakiya piracciṉikaḷ uḷaveṉ pati aṟiyalām. vintiyāṉa niṉivut tiṟaṉ, aḷavu mikka puṉivut tiṟaṉ, mitamiñciya taṉik kavarccikaḷ, ciṟapput tiṟaṉkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟippaṟṟiya ālōcaṉi kūṟavum vallunarik kalantu koḷḷalām.
(iii) vāḻkki varalāṟṟu muṟi:51 kuḻanti piṟantatu mutal ataṉ varalāṟṟi eḻutip payaṉpaṭuttuvatuṇṭu. mēṉāṭṭār taṅkaḷ vīṭṭuk kuḻantikaḷiṉ varalāṟṟip paṟṟip piṟantatumutal eḻuti veḷiyiṭ ṭuḷḷaṉar. eṉ celvamē,’ eṉ kaṇmaṇiyē eṉṟu koṇṭāṭum peṟṟōriṉ kaṇṇukku uṇmi uḷḷavāṟu pulaṉātal arumiyiṉum arumi. aṉṟiyum, kuḻantikaḷiṉ naṭattikaḷil pala taṅkaḷukku viḷaṅkāmiyāl avaṟṟippaṟṟi oṉṟum eḻutāmalē avarkaḷ viṭṭu viṭuvatu muṇṭu. periyōrkaḷiṉ varalāṟum iṅṅaṉam eḻutap peṟukiṉṟatu. inta varalāṟukaḷiṉiṉṟu makkaḷiṉ uṇmit taṉmikaḷiyum avar kaḷuṭiya eṇṇaṅkaḷiyum aṟiyalām.
itaṟkuc ciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭu iv varalāṟukaḷik koṇṭu eḻutap peṟṟa uḷaviyal ārāycci. mēṉāṭukaḷil nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa mētikaḷiṉ vāḻkkik katikaḷik koṇṭu-mukkiyamāka avarkaḷ ciṟuvayatil iyaṟṟiya kavitikaḷ, paṭikkat toṭaṅkiṉa vayatu mutaliyavaṟṟic-cātāraṇak kuḻantikaḷiṉ ceyalkaḷuṭaṉ oppiṭṭu ārāyntaṉar. ittakiya cikkalāṉatoru muṟiyiṉi mēṟkoṇṭu avarkaḷ ulakōr mētiyar eṉṟu pukaḻnta cumār 30 perumakkaḷiṉ aṟi tiṟaṉi matippiṭṭuḷḷaṉar.
iv vārāycciyiṉ mūlam avarkaḷ kāṭṭiya uṇmi oṉṟu uṇṭu. ulakiṉar mēti eṉṟu pukaḻum makaṉ ciṟu vayatilēyē taṉ mēmpāṭṭik kāṭṭukiṉṟāṉ eṉpatu. viḷiyum payir muḷiyilē teriyum eṉpatu namnāṭṭup paḻamoḻiyaṉṟō? ip paḻamoḻiyilum mēṟkuṟippiṭṭa ārāycciyiṉ cāyal pulaṉākiṉṟatu.
maruttuva niliya muṟi:52 immuṟi uḷaviyal vaḷarc ciyil kaṇṭaṟiyappeṟṟa putiya kavarccikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. immuṟi maruttuvattuṭaṉ neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyatu. itil uḷaviyaliṉ koḷkikaḷ, muṟi vakikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟit taṉimaṉitaṉiṉ nalavaḷarccikkākap payaṉpaṭuttum cāttiramum ciṟappu muṟiyum aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa.
nōyāḷi ovvoruvarippaṟṟiyum pala vivaraṅkaḷ tayārippati nām aṟivōm. atupōlavē kuḻanti kaḷuṭaṉum, pala muṟi uriyāṭic ceytikaḷi varuvikkalām. ētāvatu kuṟipāṭukaḷ kāṇappeṟiṉ, avaṟṟi iṉṉavi eṉak kaṇṭaṟiyavum (ṉuyipanṭiḷana) avaṟṟiṟkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷiyum parikāraṅkaḷiyum tēṭavum im muṟi payaṉ paṭukiṉṟatu. maṉitaṉuṭiya nōykaḷik kuṇamākka vēṇṭiya ellāc cātaṉaṅkaḷum uḷaviyalāriṭam iṉmiyāl avar aṭikkaṭi maruttuvar utaviyi nāṭavēṇṭiyuḷatu. kaṟṟalilum, piṟaruṭaṉ paḻakutalilum kuḻantikaḷiṭam kuṟikaḷ kāṇappeṟalām; allatu kāraṇamaṟṟa payaṉ ēṟpaṭalām. ivaṟṟi yellām ārāyntu viḷakka im muṟi payaṉpaṭukiṉṟatu.
mēṉāṭukaḷil potup paḷḷikaḷ, ciṟār nītimaṉṟaṅkaḷ, kolikkuṟṟa nīti maṉṟaṅkaḷ, ciṟic cālikaḷ, maruttuva niliyaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟuṭaṉ uḷaviyal maruttuva niliyaṅkaḷ iṇaṉakkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. taṉiyār naṭattum ivvita nili yaṅkaḷum āṅkuḷa. uḷaviyalār kavaṉikka vēṇṭiya ellā vayatu niliyiluḷḷavarkaḷum ivaṇ koṇarappeṟu kiṉṟaṉar. iṅkup palvēṟu cōtaṉikaḷukku avarkaḷi uṭpaṭuttit tēviyāṉa vivaraṅkaḷit tiraṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. iv vivaraṅkaḷuṭaṉ taṉiyāḷ varalāṟum53 cērkiṉṟatu.
āyvumuṟi54: itaṉic cōtaṉi muṟi eṉṟum, ceykāṭci muṟi eṉṟum vaḻaṅkuvatuṇṭu. immuṟiyil nām ārāya vēṇṭiya kūṟukaḷi naṉku kaṭṭuppaṭuttik koḷḷa vēṇṭum. aṟiviyalār immuṟiyic ciṟappākak kiyāḷu kiṉṟaṉar. iraṇṭu eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ taruvōm. oṉṟu, cila ceṭikoṭikaḷ, mika veppamāṉa nilattiltāṉ viḷikiṉṟaṉa. kuḷirnāṭṭil vāḻvōr icceṭikoṭikaḷippaṟṟi ārāya vēṇṭumāyiṉ, tirikaṭalōṭiyum tēṭiyumē ārāyavēṇṭum. ak kuḷirnāṭṭiṉar avvāṟu uḻalātu veppa aṟi oṉṟiṉic ceyaṟki muṟiyil amittu atil icceṭi koṭikaḷip payir ceyvittu ārāykiṉṟaṉar. iraṇṭu, muṟkālattil miṉṉāṟṟalippaṟṟi aṟiya iṭi, miṉṉal ivi uṇṭākiṟa variyil kāttirukka vēṇṭiyiruntatu. ippoḻutu āyvakattil miṉṉāṟṟali varuvittu āykiṉṟaṉar. vēṇṭiyavaṟṟi vēṇṭiya iṭattil vēṇṭiya poḻutil vēṇṭiya aḷavil āyattam ceyvatē āyvumuṟiyiṉ ciṟappākum. itil nām mukkiyamākak kavaṉikka vēṇṭiya toṉṟuṇṭu. atu yāteṉil oru viḷivi iyaṟṟakkūṭiya pala kūṟukaḷuḷ nām ārāyum kūṟṟiṉit tavira ēṉiyavaṟṟi māṟātu vittirukka vēṇṭum. illiyēl anniliyil ik kūṟṟiṉatu taṉi vaṉmiyi matippiṭal iyalātu.
iṉṟu ittakiya āyvu muṟi uḷaviyalilum payaṉ paṭukiṉṟatu. it tuṟiyil ataṉik kiyāḷuvatu kaṭiṉam eṉpati aṉivarum oppuk koḷvar. aṟiviyal tuṟikaḷil tiṭṭamākap payaṉpaṭuttappeṟum aḷavukkum immuṟiyi uḷaviyalil kiyāḷa muṭiyātatutāṉ. kāraṇam, mikac cikkalāṉa toru maṉita niliyil ceyaṟpaṭum ellāk kūṟukaḷiyum kaṇṭaṟintu, ikkūṟukaḷ ellāvaṟṟilum camamāṉa iraṇṭu tokutikaḷik kaṇṭupiṭippatu aritu eṉpaṉa oṉṟu. maṟ ṟoṉṟu, cōtaṉi muṟiyil cōtikkappeṟum iyaṟki vaḷarcci taṭippaṭalām; allatu apāyantiṟkuṭpaṭalām. eṉiṉum, aṇmik kālattil im muṟiyiṉ kāraṇamāka uḷaviyal ciṟanta vaḷarcciyi aṭintuḷḷatu. kalvi uḷaviyal kōṭpāṭṭil pala āyvukaḷiyum pala nuṇ karuvikaḷiyum koṇṭu ariya periya uṇmikaḷik kaṇṭuḷḷaṉar. palvēṟu paṭippumuṟi, maṉappāṭam ceyvatiluḷḷa kūṟukaḷ, kavaṉattiṉ taṉmikaḷ pōṉṟavikaḷiluḷḷa pala piracciṉikaḷukku immuṟiyil tīrvu kaṇṭuḷḷaṉar.
puḷḷiyiyal55: puḷḷiyiyal muṟi iṉṟiya aṟiviyal tuṟikaḷil ciṟantatōr iṭam peṟukiṉṟatu. kalvi uḷaviyalilum itu ciṟanta muṟiyil payaṉpaṭṭuḷḷatu. puḷḷiyiyal muṟi eṉpatu kaṇitattuṟiyic cārnta oru muṟi. puḷḷi vivaraṅkaḷic cēkarittu oḻuṅkupaṭutti, vakuttu, aḷavu muṟiyil avaṟṟiṉ poruṇmi kāṇum oru kaṇita muṟi itu. im muṟiyāl ārāycci nilikaḷiṉ kuṟipāṭṭip perumpālum niṟi vākkik koḷḷalām; paricōtaṉi vivaraṅkaḷiṉ pōk kiyum nām koṇṭa muṭivukaḷiṉ uṇmiyiyum kaṇita muṟiyākak kāṇalām. aṉṟiyum, cila ārāyccikaḷil cātāraṇamāka māṟāmal vikkamuṭiyāta cila nipantaṉikaḷip puḷḷikkaṇita muṟiyil māṟātaṉavāka iyaṟṟik koḷḷa muṭiyum. iraṇṭu paṇpukaḷiṉ iṇippoṟṟumiyi56 -toṭarpi-varuṇippatē immuṟiyiṉ uyirnāṭi.
cila eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ itaṉit teḷivākkum. uṭal vaḷarccikkum, aṟitiṟaṉukkum, cūḻnilic ciṟappukkum atil vaḷarum māṇākkarkaḷ naṭattikkum, paḷḷit tērccikkum vāḻkki veṟṟikkum, peṟṟōr aṟivukkum, avarkaḷiṉ makkaḷ aṟivukkum, peṟṟōr uṭaṟpaṇpukaḷukkum avarkaḷiṉ kuḻantikaḷiṉ uṭaṟpaṇpukaḷukkum ivipōṉṟa pala vivaraṅkaḷi eṇṇikki muṟiyik koṇṭu kāṇkiṉ ṟōm. oṉṟiṉik koṇṭu itit teḷivupaṭuttuvōm. paḷḷiyil kaṟpikkum tiṟaṉukkum vayatu mutirccikkum uḷḷa toṭarpoṟṟumiyik kaṇṭaṟivatāka vittuk koḷ vōm. cariyākac ceyyavēṇṭumāyiṉ, vayatit tavira maṟṟellāk kūṟukaḷilum caricamamāṉa iruvaratu kaṟpikkum tiṟaṉi nām oppiṭavēṇṭum. aḵtāvatu, avarkaḷatu payiṟci, nuṇṇaṟivu, pāṭattiṭṭattiṉ kaṭiṉam, avarkaḷukkuḷḷa kaṟkum viruppam ākiyavi yāvum oṉṟāka iruttal vēṇṭum. ivi yāvaṟṟiyum cariyākak kaṭṭuppaṭuttiṉāl, kaṟpikkum tiṟaṉil nām kāṇum vēṟṟumikaḷ yāvum vayatu vēṟu pāṭṭiṉ payaṉeṉṟu koḷvatu cariyākum. āṉāl, uṇmiyil ivvāṟu muṟṟilum camamākap poruntiya iru tokutikaḷik kāṇal kutirikkompē. āyiṉum, puḷḷiyiyalik kiyāḷu vatāl ārāycciyāḷar vayatit tavira, maṟṟa vēṟṟumikaḷāl cōtaṉi vivarattil uṇṭākum māṟutalik kaṇittuc camaṉpaṭutta ētuvākiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam muṟṟum camamākāta tokutikaḷik kavaṉittuk kaṟpikkum tiṟaṉ vayatāl evvāṟu pātikkap peṟukiṉṟatu eṉpatippaṟṟi nampakkūṭiya muṭivukaḷi āyvāḷarkaḷ aṭikiṉṟaṉar.
ēṉiya aṟivuttuṟikaḷōṭu toṭarpu
uḷaviyalukkum ēṉiya aṟivuttuṟikaḷukkumuḷḷa toṭarpu aṟitaṟpālatu. ataṉi īṇṭuk kāṇpōm.
uḷaviyalum meypporuḷiyalum: meypporuḷiyal57 eṉpatu mikka toṉmiyatu; paṇṭiya kirēkka, intiyak kālaṅkaḷilēyē tōṉṟiyatu. meypporuḷiyal ulakap poruḷkaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyāṉa uṇmiyi aṟiya virumpu kiṉṟatu; ulakamaṉittiyum paṟṟiyatu. āṉāl, aṟiviyalkaḷ ulakiṉ ovvoru pakutiyiyum tuṟiyiyum paṟṟiyē ārāy kiṉṟaṉa. mutalil uḷaviyal meypporuḷiyaliṉ oru pakuti yākavē iruntu vantatu; palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷilum uḷaviyal meyporuḷiyaliṉ oru pakutiyākavē iruntu vaḷarcciyaṭintatu.
meypporuḷiyal uṇmiyāṉa aṟivip peṟa muyalukiṉṟatu; āṉāl aṟiviṉ taṉmi, aṟiviṉ vaḷarcci pōṉṟa piraccaṉikaḷ uḷaviyalic cārntavi. itip poṟutta maṭṭilum ēṉiya aṟiviyalkaḷiviṭa uḷaviyalukku ōr ēṟṟamuṇṭu eṉpatu niṉivukūrtaṟpālatu. aṉṟiyum meyp poruḷiyaliṉ pakutikaḷākiya aṟanūl58, aḷavinūl59 pōṉṟiviyum uḷaviyaluṭaṉ neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyavi. meypporuḷiyalum uḷaviyalum neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭi yaviyāyiṉum, avaṟṟuḷ cila vēṟṟumikaḷum uḷḷaṉa. meypporuḷiyal, nām kāṇum ulakiṉit tōṟṟam eṉṟu kūṟum; uḷaviyal, ulaki uṇmiyeṉa oppukkoṇṭu taṉ ārāycciyit toṭaṅkum. muṉṉatu cōtaṉikaḷiviṭa akakkāṭciyiyē mukkiyamāṉatākak karutukiṉṟatu; piṉṉatu piṟa aṟiviyalkaḷippōl cōtaṉikaḷ, puḷḷivivaraṅkaḷ pōṉṟa muṟikaḷi mēṟkoḷḷukiṉṟatu.
ivviṭattil kalvi meypporuḷiyalukkum60 kalvi uḷaviyalukkum uḷḷa toṭarpiyum kavaṉittal cālap payaṉtarum. kalvi meypporuḷiyal vāḻkkiyiṉ payaṉ, ulakattiṉ karuttu, kalvi kaṟṟaliṉ kāraṇam pōṉṟa pirac caṉikaḷi āykiṉṟatu. kalviyi aḷikkum muṟi, uyarnta kuṟikkōḷkaḷi vaḷarkkum vaḻivakikaḷ, cuviyaṟivi ūṭṭum muṟi, tavaṟāṉa eṇṇaṅkaḷi akaṟṟum muṟi, cikkaṉamākak kaṟkummuṟi, naṭattiyiṉ vaḷarcci muṟi, paṭittalit61 toṭaṅkum kālam, eḻutuvatit toṭaṅkum paruvam, naṟpaḻakkaṅkaḷi ēṟpaṭuttavum tīya paḻakkaṅkaḷi oḻikkavum āṉa muṟikaḷ, tiṟaṉkaḷi vaḷarkkum muṟikaḷ, paṟṟukaḷ uṇṭākum vitam pōṉṟa pala piracciṉikaḷik kalvi uḷaviyal ārāykiṉṟatu. mēlum, payiṟṟal kaṟṟal muṟikaḷ, uttikaḷ, vaḻivakikaḷ, tiṭṭaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟik kalvi uḷaviyaliṉ tuṇiyāltāṉ nām peṟukiṉṟōm. kuḻantikaḷ cūḻnilikkup poruttamuṟu vatiyum āciriyarkaḷ paḷḷic cūḻnilikkup poruttamuṟuvatiyum kalvi uḷaviyal eḷitākkip payaṉuṭiyatākavum ākkukiṉṟatu.
uḷaviyalum uṭaliyalum: uḷaviyal muḻumaṉitaṉiṉ ceyalkaḷik kūṟukiṉṟatu. uṭaliyal62 uṭalil pala uṟuppukaḷiṉ amippiyum avaṟṟiṉ ceyalkaḷiyum eṭuttiyampukiṉṟatu. eṉiṉum, uṭalum uḷḷamum neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyavi; ivviraṇṭiyumpaṟṟik kūṟum toḻilkaḷi muṟṟilum pirikka iyalātu. potuvākak kūṟumiṭattu, uṭal uṟuppukaḷiṉ vēliyākiya cerimāṉam, kurutiyōṭṭam pōṉṟavaṟṟi uṭaliyal viḷakkukiṉṟatu. uḷḷattiṉ ceyalkaḷākiya cintaṉi, niṉivu, uṇarcci, muyaṟci pōṉṟavaṟṟi uḷaviyal urikkiṉṟatu. eṉiṉum, ovvōr iyakkattiṟkum uṭaṟkūṟum uṇṭu; uḷak kūṟum uṇṭu. eṭuttukkāṭṭākap pēcci nōkkuvōm. pēccu nāviṉuṭiyavum mūḷiyiṉuṭiyavumāṉa iyakkaṅkaḷip poṟuttatu. āṉāl, pēcak kaṟṟal uḷḷattiṉ vaḷarcciyuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiyatu. cerimāṉam uṭaluṟuppukaḷiṉ ceyalāyiṉum, atu ciṉattāl keṭukiṉṟatu. atupōlavē, maṉamakiḻcci cerimāṉatti virivākku kiṉṟatu. poṟikaḷiṉ amippum iyakkamum uṭaliyalilum, avi vaḷarum vaḻikaḷ uḷaviyalilum viḷakkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
uḷaviyalum uyiriyalum: uyiriyal63 uyirikaḷippaṟṟiya nūlākum. uḷaviyal uḷḷattippaṟṟiyatu. uyiriyal naṉṟāka vaḷarnta ōr aṟiviyal tuṟi. atu uṭaliyal, pirāṇiyiyal, kālvaḻi iyal64 pōṉṟa tuṟikaḷik koṇṭatu. 1859-ām āṇṭil ṭārviṉ iṉaṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam eṉṟa nūli veḷiyiṭṭār. itaṉmūlam kāriya cittiviti, āyattaviti, tērtal naṉmikaḷ, iyal pūkkaṅkaḷ mutaliya uḷaviyaṟ karuttukaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. uyiriyal muṟikaḷ mūlam āyvakac cōtaṉikaḷum pirāṇi uḷaviyaṟ kalviyum vaḷarntaṉa. pirāṇi uḷaviyaṟ kalviyiṉ ōṅkiya vaḷarcci, uḷaviyalukkum kalviyiyalukkum peritum payaṉpaṭṭuḷatu.
uḷaviyalum camūkaviyalum: uḷaviyal taṉiyāḷiṉ ceyalkaḷi viḷakkuvatu; camūkaviyal65 kuḻukkaḷiṉ ceyalkaḷi eṭutturippatu. camūkaviyal camūkaṅkaḷ, niliyaṅkaḷ, caṭaṅkukaḷ, paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ, paṇpāṭu, nākarikam pōṉṟavaṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟatu. uḷaviyal camūkaṅkaḷiṉ uṟuppiṉarkaḷiṉ ceyalkaḷi ārāykiṉṟatu. curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, camūkaviyal kuḻuviṉariṉ uḷḷam iyaṅkuvatiyum, uḷaviyal taṉiyāḷiṉ uḷḷam ceyaṟ paṭuvatiyum ārāykiṉṟaṉa. ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭāl itaṉi viḷakkuvōm caṭṭacapit tērtalil oru kūṭṭattār oruvarit tērnteṭukkum tattuvattic camūkaviyal terivikkiṉṟatu. āṉāl, itiyaṟiya vākkāḷarkaḷ ovvoruvarip paṟṟiyum aṟiya vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. iti uḷaviyal kūṟukiṉṟatu.
camūkaviyal putitāyiṉum, ataṉkaṇ atika ārāycci naṭi peṟṟu varukiṉṟatu. itaṉ mūlamākap pala paḷḷip piracciṉikaḷ tīrkkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, nalla muṟikaḷiyum cātaṉaṅkaḷiyum payaṉpaṭuttiya piṉṉarum cila kuḻantikaḷ naṉmuṟiyil paṭikkak kaṟpatilli; itaṟkuk kāraṇam tiṟamik kuṟivu maṭṭumaṉṟu; perumpālum avarkaḷ paḷḷi vāḻkkikkiyiya naṭantukoḷḷātatālum ikkuṟikaḷ nēriṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṅṅaṉamē paḷḷikku veḷiyē māṇākkaṉukkuc camūkattil oru matippu illātatālum avaṉ paṭikkat tavaṟukiṉṟāṉ.
kalvikkum camūkattiṟkum neruṅkiya toṭarpu uṇṭu. eṉavē, uḷaviyal camūkavāḻviṟkup payaṉpaṭutalil kalvikkum oru peruntoṇṭu iyaṟṟiyatupōlākiṉṟatu. iṉṟu āciriyap payiṟcik kallūrikaḷil uḷaviyalum camūkaviyalum iṇittuk kaṟpikkappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.



iyal-2
maṉitaṉum cūḻniliyum
poruttappāṭu

enta uyiriyum cūḻnilikku ēṟṟavāṟu taṉṉi māṟṟik koḷḷum. ituvē vāḻkkiyiṉ ilakkaṇam eṉṟukūṭak kūṟalām. maṉitaṉum oruvaki uyiriyē. ākavē, avaṉum taṉ poruttappāṭu1 aṭikiṉṟāṉ. poruttappāṭu eṉpatu eṉṉa? maṉitaṉ taṉakkum taṉ cūḻnilikkum iyinta toṭarpi aṭiyum poruṭṭut taṉ naṭattiyi2 iṭiviṭātu māṟṟum muṟiyē poruttappāṭu eṉpatu. poruttappāṭu amin tāltāṉ ellā uyirkaḷum vāḻa vaḻi amiyum; aṉṟēl cākavēṇṭiyatutāṉ. oru ceṭiyō tavaḷiyō tāṉ irukkum cūḻnilikkēṟṟavāṟu taṉṉi amittuk koṇṭāl tāṉ uyirvāḻalām; iṉṟēl marittuviṭum.
āṉāl uyar iṉa uyirākiya maṉitaṉiṉ nōkkam vēṟu. maṉitaṉ cūḻniliyuṭaṉ poruttappāṭu aṭivatu mūṉṟu vitaṅkaḷiṉāl. oṉṟu, taṉ naṭattiyiyum maṉappāṉmi kaḷiyum cūḻnilikkēṟṟavāṟu māṟṟik koḷvatu; maṟṟoṉṟu, taṉ naṭattik kēṟṟavāṟu cūḻniliyi māṟṟi amittuk koḷvatu; piṟitoṉṟu, iraṇṭuṅkalanta kalaviyiṉāl amittuk koḷvatu. kuḻantip paruvattilum kumarap paruvat tilum ipporuttappāṭu tīviramāka naṭipeṟukiṉṟatu. maṉitaṉiṉ nōkkam vāḻkki maṭṭumaṉṟu; uṇmi, nērmi, aḻaku pōṉṟavi niṟinta nalvāḻkkiyākum atu. maṉitaṉ iyaṟpiyaṟ cūḻnilikkum3 camūkac cūḻnilikkum4 iṇaṅka māṟupāṭukaḷi aṭikiṉṟāṉ; ōraḷavu oruvāṟu cūḻnili kaḷiyum māṟṟi amikkiṉṟāṉ. iṅṅaṉam koṭuttu vāṅkum anupavattiṉāl aṟivu uṇṭākiṉṟatu; tiṟami ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu; uḷḷakkiḷarcci māṟṟaṅkaḷum naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ oṭṭumotta viḷivukaḷē anupavam eṉpatum kaṟṟal eṉpatum. virinta pārviyil, kalvi eṉpatu intap poruttappāṭēyākum.
kalvi eṉpatu poruttappāṭē: piṟanta kuḻantiyum paḷḷiyil cērum māṇākkaṉum iyaṟpiyaṟ cūḻnilikkum camūkac cūḻnilikkum poruttappāṭu aṭivatil piṟar utaviyi nāṭukiṉṟaṉar. peṟṟōrum āciriyarum takkavāṟu ivarkaṭku utavavēṇṭum. ellāvitap poruttappāṭu kaḷuḷḷum taṉiyāḷiṉ tēvikaḷukkum, attēvikaḷi niṟivēṟṟac cūḻnili aḷikkum vāyppukaḷukkum taṉiyāḷiṉ tiṟamikkum iṭiyēyuḷḷatōr cikkalāṉa campantam aṭaṅkiyuḷḷatu. cūḻniliyik kaṭṭuppaṭuttuvataṉāl nām virumpum tulaṅkalkaḷ ēṟpaṭāviṭiṉum, kuṟippiṭattakka viḷivukaḷ oḻuṅkāṉa muṟiyil ōraḷavu ēṟpaṭattāṉ ceykiṉṟaṉa. inta uṇmiyiṉ aṭippaṭiyiltāṉ kalvi eṉpatu amikiṉṟatu. āḻntu cintippiṉ, ipporuttap pāṭē kalvi eṉṟākiṟatu.
eṉavē, kalvi ēṟpāṭṭiṉ5 mutalviti yātu? nikaḻccikaḷ, ceyalkaḷ, toṭarpukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟit tiṭṭamiṭuṅkāl avaṟṟiṟ kotta poruḷkaḷit tērnteṭuttal vēṇṭum. ivvāṟu oḻuṅkāka aminta anupavaṅkaḷiṉāl kaṟpōriṭam ēṟpaṭum tulaṅkalkaḷ kuṟippiṭattakka ceytiyit tiraṭṭit tarukiṉṟaṉa; avarkaḷiṭam payaṉpaṭattakka paḻakkaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. contamuṟiyil paṇpāṭum camūka vāḻvum amivataṟ kēṟṟa maṉaveḻucciyiṉ palaṉkaḷum tiraḷkiṉṟaṉa. camūkamum oḻuṅkāṉa kalviyiṉāl kaṟpōriṭam cariyāṉa poruttappāṭu amivataṟkēṟṟa nilimikaḷit tērnteṭukka virumpukiṉṟatu. itaṉāl nalla, paranta, vitavitamāṉa anupavaṅkaḷi uṟutiyākap peṟavum eṇṇukiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉamē paḷḷiyāṭciyum, payiṟṟum muṟikaḷum vacatiyāṉa cūḻnili, parivuṭiyavaḻi, tūṇṭavalla toṭarpukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭiyil kaṟṟal anupavaṅkaḷi amittut tara vaḻikōlavēṇṭum; kālakkuṟi vilum muyaṟcik kaṉattilum takka poruttappāṭukaḷ amital vēṇṭum.

poruttamuṟutalil ceyaṟpaṭum uṟuppukaḷ
oru kuḻantiyiṉ ceyaliyaṟiya vēṇṭumāyiṉ ataṉ amippi naṉku uṇaravēṇṭum. kuḻantiyiṉ uṭalil taṉippaṭṭa palakōṭi uyiraṇukkaḷ uḷa. uyiraṇu6 eṉpatu uṭaliluḷḷa mikacciṟiya uyiruḷḷa pakutiyākum. uṭaliṉ ovvoru pakutiyilum palvēṟuvitamāṉa uyiraṇukkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. orē vitamāṉa paṇpukaḷik koṇṭu orē mātiriyākac ceyaṟpaṭum uyiraṇukkaḷ cērntu ōr uyiraṇut tokuppu ākiṉṟatu. ittokuppiṉi iḻiyam7 allatu ticu eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvar. pala iḻiyaṅkaḷ oruṅku kūṭik kuṟippiṭṭa oru toḻili iyaṟṟukiṉṟaṉa. iḻiyat tokutiyi uṟuppu8 eṉpar. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka-uṭaliluḷḷa itayam ōr uṟuppu. kallīral maṟṟōr uṟuppu. paṇpukaḷāl oṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭarpuḷḷa uṟuppukaḷiṉ tokuti maṇṭalam9 eṉappaṭum. (e-ṭu) mūccuṟuppu maṇṭalam, narampu maṇṭalam, taci maṇṭalam. im maṇṭalaṅkaḷ uṭaliṉ mukkiya ceyal oṉṟiṟku aṭip paṭiyāka irukkak kūṭiyavi. maṉitaṉ immātiriyāṉa toṭarpuṭiya pala maṇṭalaṅkaḷiyuṭiyavaṉ.
māṉiṭa uṟuppukaḷiṉ pākupāṭu: māṉiṭa uṟuppukaḷi iraṇṭu perum pirivukaḷākap pirikkalām. oṉṟu, nili niṟuttum uṟuppukaḷ10. ivi uṭaliṉuḷ ceyaṟpaṭṭut taṉi yāḷiṉ uṭalnalam, vaḷarcci ākiyavaṟṟi niliniṟut tukiṉṟaṉa. cuvācittal, kaḻivup poruḷkaḷi akaṟṟutal, cerimāṉam ātal ivi pōṉṟa ceyalkaḷi iyaṟṟum uṟuppukaḷ ippakutiyil aṭaṅkum. iraṇṭu, poruttamuṟum uṟuppukaḷ11. taṉiyāḷ taṉ tēvikkākac cūḻniliyiṉ taṭaṅkalkaḷi mēṟkoḷḷumāṟu ivi ceyalpurikiṉṟaṉa. poṟikaḷ12, naramputtokuti, tacikaḷ ākiyavi ippakuti yiṉuḷ aṭaṅkum. ippirivum pirivukaḷiluḷḷa vēṟṟumi kaḷum muṟṟilum poruttamāṉaviyeṉṟu kūṟa iyalātu. ivi iṇintum toḻil purikiṉṟaṉa. (e-ṭu) kurutiyōṭṭam niliniṟuttat toḻililum, porutta muṟum toḻililum paṅku peṟukiṉṟatu; nili niṟuttam uṟuppu kaḷiṉ nilimiyiṉāl ōr āḷiṉ poruttamuṟum toḻilkaḷ pātikkap peṟu kiṉṟaṉa. īṇṭu poruttamuṟum uṟuppukaḷi maṭṭilum ārāyvōm.
maṉita uṭalil nikaḻum ceyalkaḷum maṉitaṉ taṉmaṉat tāl mēṟkoḷḷum ceyalkaḷum tūṇṭal13 tulaṅkal14 eṉṟa tattuvatti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭavi. icceyalkaḷ yāvum taṉiyāḷukkum avaṉuṭiya cūḻnilikkum iṭiyē nikaḻpavi. ivi yāvum taṉiyāḷ mītu cūḻnili15 koḷḷum ātikkattiyō, aṉṟi, cūḻniliyit taṉiyāḷ payaṉ paṭuttuvatiyō, aṉṟi, cūḻnilikkēṟṟavāṟu taṉṉip poruttamuṟac ceytaliyō kāṭṭum. ivvāṟu cūḻnili yiyum taṉiyāḷiyum iṇittu vittup poruttamuṟac ceyvatil paṅkupeṟutalē poruttamuṟum uṟuppukaḷiṉ ceyalākum.
poruttamuṟutalil paṅku peṟum uṟuppukaḷi mūvaki yākap pakuttuppēcuvar uḷaviyalār. avi:
(a) pukuvāykaḷ16: ivi tūṇṭalkaḷi ēṟkum uṟuppu kaḷākum. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka-maṇiyoli cevikaḷ mūlam pukukiṉṟatu. miṉṉoli kaṇkaḷmūlam pukukiṉṟaṉa. cūṭu, kuḷir pōṉṟavi tōliṉmūlam pukukiṉṟaṉa. iṅṅaṉam aimpoṟi kaḷiluḷḷa17 taṉippaṭṭa uyiraṇukkaḷ mūlam cūḻniliyip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ pulappaṭuvatāl avaṟṟip pulaṉkaḷ eṉkiṉṟōm. pukuvāykaḷit tākkum vicikaḷ uḷaviyalil tūṇṭalkaḷ18 eṉa vaḻaṅkappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. tūṇṭalkaḷuk kēṟpat taṉiyāḷiṭam naṭipeṟum etirviṉiyit19 tulaṅkal eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvar uḷaviyalār.
(ā) iyaṅkuvāykaḷ20: ivi iyaṅkum karuvikaḷ. pukuvāy vaḻiyākat tākkali aṟiyumpōtu ataṟkēṟpa etirūṉṟi niṟkiṉṟōm. oḷi, oli, cūṭu, cuvi, maṇam, amukkam, valipōṉṟa tākkalkaḷukku ēṟpat tulaṅkum uṟuppukaḷtām iyaṅkuvāykaḷ. iyaṟkip parappāṉatu nammip pala vaki yālum tūṇṭukiṉṟatu. ataṟkuttaka nāmum tulaṅkukiṉṟōm. iyaṅkuvāykaḷ yāvum curappikaḷum tacikaḷumākum. cila eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ ivaṟṟit teḷivākkum. olikēṭṭut tiṭukkiṭṭeḻukiṉṟōm; pēroḷi kaṇṭu kūcik kaṇṇi mūṭukiṉṟōm. oli kēṭṭalum, pēroḷi kaṇṇil paṭuvatum tūṇṭalkaḷ (ūṉṟalkaḷ); tiṭukkiṭṭeḻutalum kaṇṇi mūṭutalum tulaṅkalkaḷ (etirūṉṟalkaḷ). kaṇṇi mūṭutalum tiṭukkiṭṭeḻutalum tacikaḷiṉ ceyalkaḷām. ivi nām vēṇṭumpōtu iyaṅki varum iyakkutacikaḷiṉ21 ceyal kaḷākum. kuruti, īral (itayam)22, irippi pōṉṟa uṟuppukaḷ eppoḻutum iyaṅkik koṇṭirukkum iyaṅku taci23 yālāṉavi. puliyik kāṇumpoḻutu accattāl itayam virivākat tuṭikkiṉṟatu; irippi picital kuṟikiṉṟatu. iṅku, pulittōṉṟal ūṉṟalākum; itayam virintu tuṭit talum irippi uṇavip piciyātu niṟṟalum etirūṉṟal kaḷākum. muṉṉatil ceyaṟpaṭuvatu tacik kiḷarcciyām24 etirūṉṟal; piṉṉavaṟṟil ceyaṟpaṭuvatu tacittaṭiyām25 etirūṉṟal.
irayil vaṇṭi payaṇattil karippori kaṇṇil viḻukiṉṟatu; uṭaṉē kaṇṇīrc curappi ap poriyik kaḻuvum nīriṉic curappikkiṉṟatu. iṉiya uṇaviṉik kaṇṭatum vāyil umiḻ nīr curakkiṉṟatu. ic ceyalkaḷiyum mēṟ kaṇṭavāṟu viḷakkalām. ivaṟṟil ceyaṟpaṭupavi, curappikaḷ.
(i) poruttuvāykaḷ26: ivi poruttum karuvikaḷām. ivi pukuvāy uyiraṇukkaḷiyum iyaṅkuvāy uṟuppu kaḷiyum iṇikkiṉṟaṉa. naramput tokutiyum kurutiyōṭṭamum poruttuvāykaḷākum. ivaṟṟuḷ naramput tokutiyē mukkiyamāṉatu. potuvāka māṉiṭa uṭalil pukuvāykkum iyaṅkuvāykkum nēr iṇippu illi; ivi mūḷi allatu mutuku naṭu narampu vaḻiyāka iṇikkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. pukuvāykaḷ tūṇṭalkaḷāl tākkap peṟṟu at tākkalkaḷi ēṟṟu avaṟṟi narampuc caktiyāka māṟṟip poruttu vāykaḷ mūlam iyaṅkuvāykaḷukkucceluttic ceyalkaḷ, cintaṉi, nīr curattal pōṉṟa tulaṅkalkaḷi uṇṭākkukiṉṟaṉa. paḷḷic ciṟuvaṉ oruvaṉ paḷḷit tōṭṭattil arunellik kaṉikaḷik kāṇkiṉṟāṉ. kaṉikaḷik kaṇkaḷ (pukuvāy) kaṇṭatum, it tākkal mūḷikkuc ceṉṟu mūḷiyiṉ naṭuviṭattiṉ (poruttuvāy) tuṇiyāl kikaḷiṉ tacikaḷi (iyaṅkuvāy) iyakkuvittuk kaṉiyip paṟikkac ceykiṉṟaṉa; vāyilum umiḻnīric curakkac ceykiṉṟatu.
kurutiyōṭṭamum oru mukkiyamāṉa poruttuvāyākum. ituvum uṭaliṉ pala tiṟac ceyalkaḷiyum iṇittut tiṭṭam ceykiṉṟatu. uṭaliṉ oru pakutiyil nikaḻum payaṉ kurutiyōṭṭattiṉmūlam ataṉ aṭutta pakutikku eṭuttuc cellappeṟukiṉṟatu. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, mitivaṇṭiyil27 pala mil tūram cellukiṉṟōm. kāltacikaḷ muṟṟum cōrvaṭikiṉṟaṉa; ippoḻutu uṭaliṉ maṟṟat tacikaḷum cōrvuṟum. kāraṇam, kāl taci iyaṅkuvatāl uṇṭākum vētiyiyaṟ28 poruḷkaḷ kurutiyāl uṭaliṉ ellāp pakutikaḷukkum eṭuttuc cellap peṟuvatēyākum.
kuruti yōṭṭattāl ēṟpaṭum iṇipput tiṭṭam ōraḷavu hārmōṉkaḷ29 eṉappaṭum uṭcurappic cāṟukaḷāl naṭipeṟukiṉṟatu. ic cāṟukaḷ tūmpilāc curappikaḷil30 uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟil curakkum nīrkaḷ kaṇṇīrc curappikaḷ, umiḻnīrc curappikaḷ, kallīral, kaṇiyam ākiyavaṟṟil curakkum nīrkaḷ tūmpu allatu kuḻal vaḻiyākac celluvatu pōlaṉṟi nērē kuruti yōṭṭattiṟkuḷ ceṉṟu uṭaliṉ ellāp pakutikaḷilum paravukiṉṟaṉa. aṭittalic curappi,31 puricic curappi32, māṅkāyc curappi33 pōṉṟavi tūmpilāc curappikaḷām. ivaṟṟil ūṟum cāṟukaḷ uḷḷak kiḷarccikaḷiṉ iṭiviṉikaḷukku nērāṉa toṭarpuḷḷavi; ivi uṭal niliyiyum uḷa niliyiyum atikamākap pātikkiṉṟaṉa.
uṭaliṉ vevvēṟu uṟuppukkaḷit taṉittaṉiyē pirittuk kaṟṟal uḷaviyalārukkup payaṉpaṭṭa pōtilum, avarkaḷ maṉita uyiriyi oru muḻu iyakkat tokutiyākavē karutukiṉṟaṉar.

poruttappāṭṭiṉ kōlaṅkaḷ34
oru kuḻanti cūḻniliyil iyaṅkuvataṟkum vaḷarnta oruvar cūḻniliyil iyaṅkuvataṟkum atika vēṟṟumi uṇṭu. kuḻantiyiṉ naṭatti eḷitāṉatu, vaḷarntavariṉ naṭattik kōlam mikavum cikkalāṉatu. ōr uyiri cūḻniliyiṉālō taṉakkuḷḷō ēṟpaṭum oru nilimikkut taṉṉip poruttamuṟac ceytu koḷḷum ceyaliṉ toṭarcciyē naṭattik kōlam eṉṟu vaḻaṅkap peṟukiṉṟatu. porutta muṟutal pala tiṟappaṭṭa ceyalākum. oru paḷiṅkil pala paṭṭikaḷ kāṇappeṟuvatupōl, kuḻantiyiṭamum pala kūṟukaḷ kāṇap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. kuḻanti palvēṟu cūḻnilikaḷil poruttappāṭu aṭiyumpoḻutu intak kūṟukaḷ oṉṟiyoṉṟu taḻuvik koṇṭum oṉṟiyoṉṟu pātittukkoṇṭum uḷḷaṉa. ovvoṉṟilum ēṟpaṭum māṟupāṭukaḷit taṉittuk kavaṉit tāltāṉ ivaṟṟi naṉku aṟiyalām. eṉiṉum, kuḻantiyō vaḷarntavarō ivi yaṉittiṉ cērkki eṉpati niṉivil irutta vēṇṭum. kuḻanti cūḻnilikkup porutta muṟutalil uṭaṟceyal vakikaḷiyum uḷḷac ceyal vakikaḷiyum kāṇkiṉṟōm. tummutal, cūṭāṉa poruḷiṉiṉṟum kiyi virivāka iḻuttukkoḷḷutal pōṉṟavi eḷitāṉa naṭattik kōlaṅkaḷ; ivi uṭal ceyal paṟṟiyavi. mūḷiyiṉ muḻu vēliyiṉṟiyē ivi naṭipeṟum. uḷḷac ceyalkaḷi uṟṟu nōkkuṅkāl avaṟṟil aṟivuk kūṟu35 uṇarccik kūṟu36, muyaṟcik kūṟu37 eṉṟa mūṉṟu kūṟukaḷ tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, kamparāmāyaṇattil nakar nīṅku paṭalattip paṭippatil cila ceytikaḷi aṟikiṉṟōm; iṉpamō tuṉpamō peṟukiṉṟōm; appaṭalattip paṭippatil muyaṟci koḷḷukiṉṟōm allatu atit taḷḷivittuviṭṭu vēṟu etilō īṭupaṭukiṉṟōm. ic ceyalil immūṉṟu kūṟukaḷil aḷavil ēṟṟattāḻvukaḷ irukkalām. itu cikkalāṉatoru kōlamākum. iṅṅaṉamē oru kaṇitak kōṭpāṭṭit tīrppatum cikkalāṉa kōlamē. ivi iraṇṭum uṇmiyilēyē mūḷiyiṉ ceyalākum, avvappoḻu tirukkum maṉanilikaḷuk kēṟṟavāṟu mūḷic ceyalkaḷil inta mūṉṟu kūṟukaḷil oṉṟu mītūrntum maṟṟa iraṇṭum tāḻntum irukkum. āṉāl, ovvoru naṭattik kōlamum, atu eḷitā yiruppiṉum, aṉṟi, cikkalāka iruppiṉum, tūṇṭaliṉ pukuvāykkum iyaṅku vāykkum uḷḷa narampup pātiyālēyē iyaṟṟappeṟal vēṇṭum. taṉiyāḷ vaḷaravaḷara in naṭattik kōlaṅkaḷ mikavum cikkalākiṉṟaṉa.
maṉitaṉ ōr uyarnta uyiri. ākavē, taṉittūṇṭaloṉṟu oru taṉippukuvāyil ceyaṟpaṭukiṉṟatu eṉṟu eṇṇuvatu tavaṟu. oru tūṇṭaṟ kōlattil orē camayattil pala tūṇ ṭalkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. kārikkuṭiyil vēṉiṟkālattil orunāḷ aḻakappā payiṟcik kallūri nūlakattilirukkum māṇākkaṉ oruvaṉ palavakiyil tūṇṭap peṟukiṉṟāṉ. avaṉ aṇ miyil naṭipeṟavirukkum tērviṉ poruṭṭu oru mukkiya viṉāviṟku viṭi tēṭukiṉṟāṉ. kiyiluḷḷa nūlip pārttu uṇarkiṉṟāṉ. oru mūliyil iraṇṭu pēr pēcuvatik kēṭ kiṉṟāṉ. cāḷarattiṉ vaḻiyākap paranta poṭṭaliliruntu vīcum veppakkāṟṟi uṇarkiṉṟāṉ. itaṟkiṭiyē kallūri mutalvar varukiyāl avarukku mariyāti celuttuvāṉ vēṇṭi eḻuntu niṟkiṉṟāṉ. it tūṇṭalkaḷ yāvum vevvēṟu aḷavukaḷil avaṉit tākkiyapōtilum avaṉ kavaṉam mukkiyamāka avaṉ muṉṉirukkum nūlilēyē uḷḷatu. avaṉ it tūṇṭalkaḷiyellām uṇarnta pōtilum paṭittal eṉṉum ceyalilēyē ciṟappāka īṭupaṭṭuḷḷāṉ. iv vitamākap palappala vakikaḷil nām tūṇṭappeṟṟuc ceyalāṟṟuvataṉāl poruttappāṭṭu uṟuppukaḷip paṟṟic caṟṟu ārāyvatu nalam payakkum. ivaṟṟi mēlē kāṇpōm.
mēlum, kuḻanti taṉittu vāḻvatilli. piṟaruṭaṉ kūṭiyē vāḻkiṉṟatu. ākavē, atu camūkac cūḻnilikkum poruttamuṟalvēṇṭum. poruttappāṭṭil uṭal vaḷarcci, iyakka vaḷarcci, uḷḷakkiḷarcci vaḷarcci, camūkap paṇpu vaḷarcci ākiyavaṟṟik kāṇkiṉṟōm. icceyalkaḷiṉ vakikaḷiyum naṉku aṟintāltāṉ āciriyar muḻukkuḻantiyiṉ vaḷarcciyil tuṇipuriyak kūṭum.

narampu maṇṭalam
kuḻanti eṉpatu uṭal, uḷḷam eṉṟa iraṇṭiṉ cērk kiyākum. ivi iraṇṭum neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyavi. oṉṟu maṟṟoṉṟi āṭci celutta vallatu. uṭal cōr vuṟṟirukkumpoḻutu maṉam vēli ceyya maṟukkiṟatu. aṅṅaṉamē, maṉam kavaliyuṟṟirukkumpoḻutu uṭalvēli aluppāka uḷḷatu. uḷḷattiṉ eṇṇaṅkaḷum viruppaṅkaḷum uṭaliṉ uṟuppukaḷi iyakkuvikkiṉṟaṉa.
maṉattiṉ ceyalkaḷāl iraṇṭu payaṉkaḷ uṇṭu. oṉṟu, taṉiyāḷi oṉṟupaṭuttuvatu. maṟṟoṉṟu, at taṉiyāḷic cūḻnilikkēṟṟavāṟu poruttamuṟac ceyvatu. inta iraṇṭu nōkkaṅkaḷiyum muṟṟuvippatu mikac cikkalāka amintuḷḷa narampu maṇṭalam. uṭaliṉ oru pakuti maṟṟoru pakutiyuṭaṉ toṭarpu koḷvataṟkum uṭaliṉ ellāp pakutikaḷum veḷi yulakattiliruntu ceytikaḷi aṟivataṟkum, ac ceytikaḷuk kēṟṟavāṟu ceyaṟpaṭuvataṟkum narampu maṇṭalam tuṇi purikiṉṟatu. eṉavē, nammuṭiya ceyalkaḷaṉittum narampu maṇṭalattiṉ nalattip poṟuttuḷḷatu. ākavē, narampu maṇṭalattippaṟṟi nām aṟintu koḷvatu iṉṟiyami yātatu. uḷaviyalārukku iṉṟiyamiyātaṉavāka uḷḷa oru cila ceytikaḷi maṭṭilum īṇṭu tokuttuk kūṟuvōm.
narampu maṇṭalattil mūṉṟu mukkiyap pakutikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avi: 1. naṭu narampu maṇṭalam;38 2. veḷi narampu maṇṭalam39; 3. taṉṉāṭci narampu maṇṭalam.40 ivi ovvoṉṟiyum terintu koḷvataṟku muṉ narampukaḷiṉ amippiyum, vakikaḷiyum, uṟuppukaḷiyum avaṟṟiṉ toḻilkaḷiyum aṟintukoḷḷa vēṇṭum.
paṭam-1 : narappaaṇu. itu mutuku naṭunarampiṉiṉṟu eṭukkappeṟṟa narappa aṇu; perukkik kāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu. itu pala narappak kiḷikaḷiyum oṟṟi narappa viḻutiyum  koṇṭuḷḷatu; narappa viḻutu taci variyilum ceṉṟu aṅkup pirintu cila taci nārkaḷuṭaṉ neruṅki iṇikiṉṟatu.
narampukaḷiṉ amippu: uyiraṇu iṉṉateṉpati nām naṉku aṟivōm. narampu maṇṭalattiṉ mikac ciṟu aṇu narampaṇu eṉpatu. itaṉi narappam41 eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvōm. tolipēcik42 kampiyil pala melliya kampikaḷ cērntu kaṭṭappeṟṟuk kiṭappatip pōlavē narampum pala iḻikaḷ cērntu ōr uṟiyil amikkappeṟṟuḷḷatu. iv viḻikaḷ tām narappaṅkaḷ. narampu maṇṭalam palakōṭi narappaṅkaḷāl āṉatu. namatu uṭalil 9,200,000,000 narappaṅkaḷ iruppatāka ārāycciyāḷarkaḷ nampukiṉṟaṉar.
paṭam-2 narappaṅkaḷ, narappa viḻutukaḷ narappak kiḷikaḷ  oru kūṭalvāy  ākiyavaṟṟik kāṭṭum narappaṅkaḷ. 
narappattil mūṉṟu pakutikaḷik kāṇalām. paṭattiluḷ ḷativiṭa narappa viḻutu iṉṉum nīḷamāṉatu. cāmpal niṟamākavuḷḷatu. aṇuvaṟi43 allatu aṇu uṭal. itu uḷḷaṇuvōṭu44 cērntuḷḷatu. itiliruntu tāṉ narampu ūṭṭam peṟukiṉṟatu. aṇuvaṟiyiliruntu nīṇṭa kampikaḷ pōṉṟa pakuti veṇṇiṟa uṟiyāl mūṭappeṟṟuḷḷatu. ovvoṉṟum iraṇṭiliruntu mūṉṟaṭi nīḷam vari irukkum. ip pakutiyi narappa viḻutu45 eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar. narappattiṉ melliya mayiriḻi pōṉṟa pakuti narappakkiḷikaḷ46 eṉa vaḻaṅkappeṟum. iraṇṭu narappaṅkaḷ neruṅkuvatil oru taṉit taṉmi uṇṭu. oru narappattiṉ narappakkiḷikaḷ maṟṟoru narappattiṉ narappa viḻutuṭaṉ neruṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu neruṅkumiṭam kūṭalvāy47 eṉa vaḻaṅkappeṟum. narap pakaḷiṉ muṭivup pakutikaḷ oṉṟu cērvatilli eṉpatu kavaṉittaṟpālatu. iḵtu oru nūtaṉappaṇpu. narampukaḷiṉūṭē cellum āṟṟali oruvita miṉṉāṟṟal eṉṟu kūṟuvar. iḵtu oru narappattiliruntu maṟṟoru narappattiṉi nōkkic cellukiṉṟatu. narappa āṟṟal orēpātiyil cellum; tirumpātu. narappa āṟṟali narappakkiḷikaḷ peṟṟu ataṉi narappa viḻutu vēṟu narappattiṟkuc celuttukiṉṟatu. narampi lēṟpaṭum uḷ tuṭippu kūṭal vāyi mikac cikkalāṉa kiṭṭat taṭṭa tiṭīreṉṟu ēṟpaṭum vētiyiyal ceykiyāl pālam pōl piṇikkiṉṟatu eṉṟum, intac ceyki naṭappataṟkuk kuṟippiṭṭa oru cila uyarnta nurippuḷiyaṅkaḷ48 tuṇi ceykiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum karutuvar.
narappattūṇṭal: ovvoru narappamum tūṇṭappeṟṟu maṟṟoṉṟit tūṇṭukiṉṟatu. narappattiṉ ceyal ituvē. narappamāṉatu oḷi, oli mutaliya veḷip poruḷkaḷāl tūṇṭap peṟalām. ivitām pukuvāykaḷ. pukuvāy narappaṅkaḷ aṭutta narappaṅkaḷit tūṇṭa, ivvāṟu toṭarntu cellukiṉṟatu. muṭivil it tūṇṭal taciyil allatu curappiyil muṭikiṉṟatu. ivitām iyaṅkuvāykaḷ. ivaṟṟiṉ kiḷarcciyē pukuvāy vaḻiyāka vanta tākkaliṉ muṭivākum. tacikaḷ curuṅkuvatālō, curappikaḷ cāṟukaḷic curappataṉālō uṭal muḻuvatum veḷipporuḷ vaḻiyākat tākkiya iyaṟkip parappuṭaṉ porutta muṟukiṉṟatu.
narampuk kiḷarcci: ovvoru narampu nārilum ōṭum narampuk kiḷarcci oru taṉmiyiṉatē eṉṟu āyvāḷarkaḷ aṟutiyiṭ ṭuḷḷaṉar. itic ciṟitu viḷakkuvōm. miṉṉāṟṟal pala kampikaḷ vaḻiyāka ōṭukiṉṟatu. oru kampiyil ōṭuvataṟkum maṟṟoru kampiyil ōṭuvataṟkum vēṟṟumi illi. oru kampiyil ōṭuvatō viḷakkerikkiṉṟatu; maṟṟoṉṟil ōṭuvatō viciṟukiṉṟatu. iv vēṟṟumi kampiyiṉ iyaṟki vēṟṟumiyaṉṟu; ovvoru kampiyum eṅkeṅkē pōy muṭikiṉṟatō antanta muṭiviṭattip poṟuttatākum ivvēṟṟumi. viḷakkil pōy muṭivatu viḷakkerikkiṉṟatu; viciṟiyil pōy muṭivatu viciṟukiṉṟatu. atupōla narampuk kiḷarccikaḷum tammil oru vēṟṟumiyiṉṟi muṭiviṭattāl vēṟṟumiyuṟukiṉṟaṉa. kaṇṇil pōy muṭivatu oḷiyi uṇarkiṉṟatu; kātil pōy muṭivatu oliyi uṇarkiṉṟatu. narampukaḷ ceyaṟpaṭuvatāl uyirp poruḷ erintu kariyamila vāyuviyum veppattiyum veḷiviṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷarkaḷ kaṇṭaṟin tuḷḷaṉar.
narampu vakikaḷ: narampukaḷil pala vakikaḷ uḷa. cila narampukaḷ ceytiyi veḷippuṟattiliruntu mūḷi, mutuku naṭunarampu pōṉṟa miyaṅkaḷukkuk koṇṭu cellukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟi uṭcel narampukaḷ49 allatu pulaṉuṇar narampukaḷ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvar. ivi pukuvāykaḷi mūḷiyuṭaṉō mutuku narampuṭaṉō iṇikkiṉṟaṉa. mūḷi allatu mutuku naṭunarampu pōṉṟa miyaṅkaḷiliruntu veḷi nōkkic ceṉṟu taci pōṉṟa uṟuppukaḷi iyaṅka vikkum narampukaḷ veḷic cel narampukaḷ50allatu iyakka narampukaḷ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappeṟum. ivaṟṟik kaṭṭaḷi narampukaḷ eṉṟum colva tuṇṭu, ivi mūḷi allatu mutuku naṭu narampiṉiṉṟu iyaṅku vāykaḷukkuc cellukiṉṟaṉa. orē miyattiluḷḷa narampukaḷi iṇippataṟkum, narampu maṇṭalattil oru pakutiyiliruntu piṟitoru pakutikkuc celvataṟkum iṇikkum narampukaḷ51 uḷḷaṉa. iṉi, narampu maṇṭalaṅkaḷip paṟṟic ciṟitu pakarvōm.
naṭu narampu maṇṭalam: itil perumūḷi, ciṟumūḷi, mukuḷam, narampukaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya mutuku naṭu narampu ākiyavi aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa (paṭam. 3). mutuku poruttap peṟṟa uṟuppukaḷ yāvum ivaṟṟiṉuḷ aṭaṅkum. inta narampu maṇṭalattip paṭimuṟi vaḷarccik koḷkippaṭi variciyākak kavaṉittal payaṉ aḷikkumām.
mutuku naṭunarampu: eḷiya ceyalkaḷik koṇṭa itutāṉ mika mikat toṭakka niliyil uḷḷatu; iḵtu oṉṟutāṉ kīḻniliyiluḷḷa mutukelumpikaḷiṉ52 narampu 
maṇṭalamākum. itu mukuḷattil53 toṭaṅki mutukelumpu varici yeṉṉum mūṭṭuḷḷa elumpuk kuḻaluḷ aminta naramput tokuti yākum. itu nīṇṭa kayiṟu pōṉṟatu. itil muṉṉum piṉṉumāka iraṇṭu veṭippukaḷ uḷa. itaṉ uṭpākam narampaṇukkaḷ iruppataṉāl cāmpal niṟamāṉatu; veḷippuṟam narampuk kampikaḷ aṭaṅkiyiruppatu paṟṟi veṇṇiṟamākavuḷḷatu. in naramput tokutiyil 30 iṇikku mēlāṉa mutuku veḷinarampukaḷ itaṉip pukuvāy kaḷōṭum iyaṅku vāykaḷōṭum iṇikkiṉṟaṉa. mēlē mūḷikkuc celpavi uṭcel narampukaḷākum; mūḷiyiliruntu kīḻē varupavi veḷic cel narampukaḷākum. innaṭunarampu taṉ valimiyiṉāl uṭcel narampukaḷiyum iṇikkum oru miyamu mākum. naṭunarampu maṇṭalattiliruntu namatu uṭal muḻuvatum paravi yuḷḷa narampukaḷip paṭattil kāṇka (paṭam-4).
mutuku naṭunarampiṉ valimiyāl maṭakkuc ceyalkaḷ allatu maṟiviṉikaḷ54 naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka, nam puṟaṅkiyil oruvar ūciyāl kuttiṉāl veṭukkeṉṟu kiyi iḻuttuk koḷḷu kiṉṟōm (paṭam.5). itaṟku yātoru yōcaṉiyum cey vatilli. itu mutuku naṭu narampiṉ ceyalāl naṭipeṟukiṉṟatu. cila uyir vakikaḷiṉ mūḷiyip peyarttu eṭut tuviṭṭa pōtilum avi cila ceyalkaḷic cey kiṉṟaṉa. oru tavaḷiyiṉ mūḷiyi ōr āyvāḷar aṟutteṭuk kiṉṟār. piṉ ataṉuṭaliṉ tōlil ciṟi taḷavu ōr amilattit teḷikkiṉṟār. ap pōtum antat tavaḷi taṉatu piṉṉaṅkālit tūkki anta amilatti akaṟṟa muyal kiṉṟatu. āyvāḷar ak kālip piṭittuk koḷ kiṉṟār. tavaḷi maṟṟoru kālit tūkki acceyali niṟivēṟṟa muyalkiṉṟatu. aṅṅaṉamē avar oru nāyiṉ mūḷiyi akaṟṟu kiṉṟār. piṉṉar ataṉ mutukil oru kaṭṭi eṟumpik kaṭikkaviṭukiṉṟār. kaṭipaṭṭa iṭattarukē taṉ kālit tūkkit tēyttuk koḷkiṟatu annāy.
mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu maṭakkuc ceyal naṭipeṟuvatic ciṟitu viḷakkuvōm. veḷipporuḷ tūṇṭukiṉṟatu; ikkiḷarcci pukuvāy vaḻiyākap pukuntu pulaṉuṇar narampu vaḻiyāka mutuku naṭunarampiṉi aṭikiṉṟatu. piṉṉar, iyakka narampukaḷiṉ vaḻiyāka iyaṅkuvāykaḷi aṭikiṉṟatu; aḵtā vatu taciyiṉi aṭikiṉṟatu. taciyiṉ iyakkattāl kāl āṭukiṉṟatu; kāl amilatti akaṟṟa muyalukiṉṟatu. eṟumpu kaṭitta iṭattic coriya muyalukiṉṟatu. narampiṉ iyaṟki yamippē ittuṇikkum kāraṇamāka uḷḷatu. tāmākac ceyaluṟum toṭarccikaḷ pala im mutuku naṭunarampil kāṇak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. nām ayarntu tūṅkumpōtum mayakkattāl nammi maṟantu kiṭakkumpōtum ittakiya maṭakkuc ceyal kaḷic ceytu muṭikkiṉṟōm allavā?
maṭakkuc ceyalkaḷi iyaṟṟuvatit tavira, mutuku naṭunarampu mūḷiyiliruntuvarum ceytikaḷik kaḻuttiṟkuk kīḻuḷḷa pakutikaḷukkum, ippakutikaḷiluḷḷa uṇrccikaḷi mūḷikkum aṉuppum vaḻi niliyamākavum ceyaṟpaṭukiṉṟatu; añcal niliyamāka amikiṉṟatu. (paṭam 5.) kiyiṉ piṉ puṟamiruntu puṟattaci variyilum uḷḷa oru narampiṉik kāṭṭukiṉṟatu. ki ēṟkum tūṇṭalukkēṟṟa tulaṅkali puyattaci niṟivēṟṟukiṟatu eṉpatip paṭam viḷakkukiṉṟatu.
namatu naṭattikaḷ yāvum immātiriyāṉa maṭakkuc ceyalkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvataṟkilli. maṭakkuc ceyalkaḷ perumpālum namatu naṉavu niliyiṉ āṭcikkuṭpaṭṭavi alla. eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka, namatu kaṇṇiṟku nērākak kūrmiyāṉa kucciyi yārāvatu vēkamāka nīṭṭiṉāl kaṇṇimikaḷ tāmāka mūṭikkoḷḷum. in naṭattiyil nam cintaṉi maṭakkuc ceyali naṭattuvatākak karutamuṭiyātu. kaṇṇit tiṟantu vittukkoṇṭirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu evvaḷavutāṉ nām karutiṉum nammiyaṟiyāmalēyē ammaṭakkuc ceyal nikaḻntu viṭukiṉṟatu. mēlum cuvācittal mutaliya maṟṟa ceyal kaḷil namatu niṉavu ciṟitum paṅku peṟuvatilli. ātaliṉ nam naṭattiyil veṟum maṭakkuc ceyalkaḷukkum mēlāṉa oruvaki uṇṭu. itil palavita maṭakkuc ceyalkaḷ oṉṟu cērntu iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ivi namatu naṉavu niliyiṉ āṭcikku uṭpaṭṭavi. immātiri naṭattiyi iyalpūkka naṭatti eṉpar.
mukuḷam: mutuku naṭunarampiṟku mēṟpuṟamākavum peru mūḷikku aṭiyil piṉpuṟattilum mukuḷam55 eṉappaṭum naṭu muṭiccu amintuḷḷatu. itu ciṟu kōḷavaṭivamāṉatu; mikavum mukkiyamāṉatu. mutuku naṭunarampip pōlavē ituvum oru mukkiyap poruttappāṭṭu miyamākum. āyiṉum, itaṉ mukkiya vēli mutuku naramput tokutiyi mēl miyaṅ kaḷōṭu iṇippatē. aṉṟiyum, cuvācittal, itayattuṭippu, kurutiyōṭṭam, cerimāṉam campantamāṉa maṟiviṉikaḷuk kellām mukuḷamē kāraṇamākum. taliyiluḷḷa pulaṉkaḷ, tacikaḷ paṟṟiya maṭakkuc ceyalkaḷukkum ituvē irup piṭamākum. itaṉ vaḻiyāka mēlum kīḻum cellum narampukaḷ valamiruntu iṭamākavum, iṭamiruntu valamākavum ceṉṟu uṭaliṉ irupākaṅkaḷiyum iṇikkiṉṟaṉa.
ciṟumūḷi56: itip piṉtali mūḷi eṉṟum vaḻaṅkuvar. itu perumūḷiyiṉ piṉpuṟattil pālattiṟkum mukuḷat tiṟkum uḷḷa iṭiveḷiyil amintuḷḷatu. iru arik kōḷa vaṭivamāṉatu. itu uṭaliṉ acivukaḷi oḻuṅku paṭutti ataṉ camaniliyik kaṇkāṇikkiṉṟatu. nām niṟkum pōtum, naṭakkumpōtum, kālāl utikkum pōtum cāyntu viḻātapaṭi kākkiṉṟatu; uṭaliṉ kōlattik57 kavaṉippatum ituvē.
ciṟumūḷikku mēlāka, pālam58 eṉṟatoru periya amippu uḷḷatu. itu narampu nārkaḷālum nuṭpamāṉa poruttappāṭṭu miyaṅkaḷālum ākiyatu. ciṟumūḷikkum pālattiṟkum mēlākap palavaki aṭukkukaḷ aminta ciṟanta iṇippu miyaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ivi uḷtuṭippup porut tappāṭṭilum uyiri cūḻniliyōṭu poruttamuṟum ceyalilum atikap paṅku koḷḷukiṉṟaṉa. im miyaṅkaḷip paṟṟi nām aṟintatu ciṟupakutiyē; aṟiyātatu perumpakuti uḷḷatu. uṭaṟpākupāṭṭu muṟiyil avaṟṟiṉ toṭarpukaḷi iṅkuk kūṟuvataṟkilli.
pālattiṉ mēṟpuṟamāka naṭumūḷi eṉa vaḻaṅkum mūḷiyiṉ oru pakuti uḷatu. avviṭattiltāṉ atikamāṉa maṇṭi narampukaḷ-ciṟappāka kaṇ nakarcciyuṭaṉ toṭarpu koṇṭirup pavi-toṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
