perumūḷikkuk kīḻē amintiruppatu pūttaṇṭu59 eṉṟa utavi miyam. itaṉ mukkiya vēli poṟikaḷiliruntu varum uḷtuṭippukaḷi mūḷikkuk kaṭattuvatākum. itaṉuṭaṉ iṇinta tuṇippūttaṇṭu, mēṟpūttaṇṭu60 ākiyavi uṭalvaḷarcciyil mukkiyap paṅku koḷḷukiṉṟaṉa. mēṟpūttaṇṭu tāṉ paci, tūkkam uṭal vaḻiyāka veḷippaṭakkūṭiya uḷḷak kiḷarcci ākiyavaṟṟi ōraḷavu kaṭṭuppaṭuttukiṉṟatu. mēlum taṉṉāṭci narampu maṇṭalattiyum uyar muṟiyil kaṭṭup paṭuttukiṉṟatu. itaṉuṭaṉ iṇintiruppatu aṭittaliccurappi61; eṇṭōkirīṉ maṇṭalattiṉ aracaṉpōl ceyaṟpaṭuvatu.
perumūḷi: māṉiṭa mūḷiyiṉ perumpakuti peru mūḷiyē62. itu maṇṭiyōṭṭiṉuḷ iraṇṭu arikkōḷaṅ kaḷākap pirivupaṭṭuk kiṭakkiṉṟatu. itu neruṅki amikkap peṟṟa perum narampaṇut tokutikaḷāl amintatu. caṟṟēṟak kuṟiya maṇṭiyōṭu muḻuvatiyum itu nirappukiṉṟatu. maṉitaṉiṉ mūḷi mirukaṅkaḷiṉ mūḷiyi viṭa (yāṉi, timiṅkalam nīṅkalāka)k kaṉamākavum peritākavum uḷḷatu. yāṉimūḷiyiṉ eṭi cumār 12 irāttalkaḷ; timiṅkala mūḷiyiṉ eṭi cumār 10 irāttalkaḷ, maṉita mūḷiyiṉ eṭi kiṭṭattaṭṭa 3 irāttalkaḷ. uṭal eṭiyuṭaṉ op piṭṭup pārttāl, maṉitamūḷitāṉ yāṉi, timiṅkalam ākiyavaṟṟiviṭap periteṉpatu pulaṉākum. peritākavuḷḷa perumūḷiyiṉ kāraṇamākavē,-ciṟappākac ciṟanta muṟiyil vaḷarntuḷḷa neṟṟippiriviṉ kāraṇamāka-maṉitaṉ ēṉiya pirāṇikaḷiviṭat tali ciṟantavaṉāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟāṉ.
perumūḷiyiṉ mēṟparappip puṟaṇi63 eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar. ataṉ veḷipparappu cāmpar niṟamāṉatu; aṅkē uyiraṇukkaḷ uḷa. kīḻappakuti veṇṇiṟamāṉatu; aṅkē narampuk kampikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. veḷipparappil pala maṭippukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. maṭippukaḷiṉ atika eṇṇikkikkēṟpa aṟivum atikamāka irukkum eṉpar. ovvoru arikkōḷamum veṭippukaḷāl vala pākaṅkaḷākap pirikkap peṟṟuḷḷatu. muṉpuṟamākavuḷḷatu neṟṟippirivu64; piṉpakkamākavuḷḷatu maṇṭip pakkappirivu65; taliyiṉ piṉpakkamuḷḷatu piṭarippirivu66; kātukku mēluḷḷatu poṭ ṭuppirivu67. ovvoru pirivum ovvoru vēliyic ceytu varukiṉṟatu. aimpoṟi kaḷākiya pārvi, kēḷvi, ūṟu, nāṟṟam, cuvi ākiya ovvoṉṟum mūḷiyil ovvoru pakutiyiyuṭiyatu. āyiṉum, mūḷi oṉṟākavē ceyaṟ paṭukiṉṟatu eṉpati nām aṟital vēṇṭum. eṭuttukkāṭ ṭāka? nām oṉṟiṉip pārkkiṉṟōm; atē camayattil kēṭkiṉṟōm, nukarkiṉṟōm, cuvikkiṉṟōm. eṉavē, pala pakutikaḷum oruṅku iṇintē ceyaṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu itaṉāl uṟutippaṭukiṉṟatu.
paṭam 6: perumūḷi. perumūḷiyiṉ
iṭappakutiyiṉ pakkavāṭṭut tōṟṟam.
mūḷiyiṉ pakutikaḷiyum iyakkattiṟkum
pulaṉkaḷukkum uriyapakutikaḷiyum kāṭṭuvatu
mēṟcuṭṭiya pulaṉ ellikaḷit tavira, iyakka elli eṉṟa oṉṟum uṇṭu. itu neṟṟippirivil uḷḷatu. kāl, uṭal, ki, mukam, tali eṉṟa variciyil iyakkaṅkaḷ avviṭattil amintuḷḷaṉa. mēlē tōṉṟuvatu kāliṉ iyakkam; kīḻētōṉṟuvatu taliyiṉ iyakkam; iṭiyē kāṇpatu uṭaliṉ maṟṟap pākaṅkaḷiṉ iyakkam.
ivi tavira cilapakutikaḷil iyipu narampukaḷ68 uḷḷaṉa. nam anupavaṅkaḷi iyipuṟuttalil in nampukaḷ īṭupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. perumūḷiyē naṉavukku69 iruppiṭam. itiyē mēlmiyam70 eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvar. ciṟumūḷiyiyum mutuku naṭunarampiyum kīḻmiyaṅkaḷ71 eṉpar.  viruppac ceyalkaḷ72 yāvum mēlmiyattālum, aṉiccic ceyalkaḷ yāvum73 kīḻmiyaṅkaḷālum āḷappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.
veḷinarampu maṇṭalam: itil maṇṭi narampukaḷ, mutukuvērkaḷ, narampu uṭalkaḷ ākiyavi cērntuḷḷaṉa, poṟiyiṉ ceyalkaḷ pala pātikaḷiṉ vaḻiyākap parantu naṭunarampu maṇṭalattiṉ pala pakutikaḷukkum cellukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu mēlē kaṇṭōm. ivvuṇarccikaḷē enta iyaṅku vāykaḷ ceyaṟpaṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟum, avi evvāṟu ceyaṟpaṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟum aṟutiyiṭukiṉṟaṉa. tulaṅkalkaḷ cemmiyāka amiya vēṇṭu māyiṉ tūṇṭaluṇarcci iyaṅkuvāykaḷukkuk koṇṭuvarappeṟutal vēṇṭum. veḷi narampu maṇṭalam tulaṅkal iyaṟṟuvataṟku vēṇṭiya alivukaḷ kūṭum potuvāṉa iṟuti narampup pātiyāka amintuḷḷatu.
tūṇṭaluṇarccikaḷum tuṭippukaḷum eṅkiruntu variṉum, tulaṅkal naṭipeṟuvataṟku avi ippāti vaḻiyākavē taci nārkaḷiyō curappi kaḷiyō aṭital vēṇṭum. puṟattē tākkum tūṇṭalkaḷēyaṉṟi uṭaliṉuḷḷiruntum tūṇṭalkaḷ eḻukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ ciṟcila tuṭippukaḷ oṉṟiyoṉṟu tāṅki valiyuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa. maṟṟum cila oṉṟiyoṉṟu taṭuttu niṟuttukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, maṉitaṉ ittakiya mikac cikkalāṉa poruttappāṭṭiṉ mūlamāka nuṇukkamum, vēṟṟu mikaḷum, tiṭṭamum poruntiya iyakkat tulaṅkalkaḷik kaṟṟuk koḷkiṉṟāṉ. poruttap pāṭum matinuṭpamum aminta naṭatti avaṉiṭam tōṉṟukiṟatu.
taṉṉāṭci narampu maṇṭalam: mēṟkūṟiya narampu maṇṭalam nām veḷi yulakattuṭaṉ toṭarpu koṇṭu āṟṟum ceyal kaḷil paṅku peṟukiṉṟatu. āṉāl, naṭatti eṉpatu eppoḻutum veḷic cūḻniliyi māṟṟuvatu maṭṭilum illi. cila camayaṅkaḷil nam uṭal uṟuppu kaḷuḷḷēyum māṟutalkaḷ nikaḻvaṉavāka irukkalām. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, ciṉam pōṉṟa uḷḷak kiḷarcciyāl kurutiyōṭṭam atikarikkiṉṟatu. inta naṭatti uṭpuṟa naṭatti (aiayīṭiuvi) eṉa vaḻaṅkappeṟum. immātiri naṭattikaḷil ceyaṟpaṭuvataṟkuriya maṇṭalamē taṉṉāṭci narampu maṇṭalamākum. itiluḷḷa narampukaḷ mutukelumpiṉ irupuṟaṅkaḷilum caṅkilit toṭarpōl ciṟiya uruṇṭiyāṉa narampaṇut tiraḷkaḷāka74 amin tuḷḷaṉa. ivviraṇṭu caṅkilikaḷum maṇṭiyōṭṭiliruntu mutuku narampiṉ aṭippākam variyilum cellukiṉṟaṉa.
uyir nūṟpaṭi im maṇṭalam narampu maṇṭalattiṉ mikap paḻamiyāṉa pakutiyākum. itil iru pakutikaḷ uḷa, (1) parivu narampu maṇṭalam75: itaṉu ṭiya narampukaḷ itayam, nuri yīralkaḷ, cila pirattiyēkamāṉa curappikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṟkuc cellukiṉṟaṉa. oru vipattil allatu kiḷarcci tarum cantarp pattil, uyiri mika atikamākat tūṇṭap peṟukiṉṟatu; itaṉāl atika itayattuṭippu, virivāṉa cuvācittal pōṉṟa ceyalkaḷ naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl vekuṇṭu eḻutalō allatu veruṇṭōṭutalō mikavum porut tamāka amikiṉṟatu. eṉiṉum, itu cerimāṉa maṇṭalattiṉ taṟkālikap pātippāl naṭipeṟukiṉṟatu. ip parivu narampu maṇṭalattuṭaṉ mikavum iṇintu niṟpatu tuṇipparivu maṇṭalam. (2) tuṇipparivu narampu maṇṭalam itu ceyalil muṟkūṟiya maṇṭalattiṟku nēraṭiyāka muraṇpaṭṭa tākum. iḵtu uyiriyip paḻiya nilikkē koṇṭuvaruvataṟkut tuṇiceykiṉṟatu.
tūmpilāc curappikaḷ
uḷḷak kiḷarccikaḷuṭaṉ toṭarpuḷḷa tūmpilāc curappikaḷi mēlē kuṟippiṭṭōm allavā? ic curappikaḷ narampu maṇṭalattuṭaṉ iṇintum, tammoṭu tām iṇintum ceyaṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ic curappikaḷ ov voṉṟip paṟṟiyum ōraḷavu terintu koḷḷutal uḷaviyal kaṟṟalukkut tuṇiyāka irukkum.
puracic curappi76: itu kaḻuttiṉ aṭippākattil kural vaḷi maṇi77kku iru puṟaṅkaḷilum amintuḷḷatu. mikavum cuṟucuṟuppāka iyaṅkakkūṭiya puricic curappiyik koṇṭavarkaḷ mikavum naramput taḷarcciyuṭaṉiruppar; avarkaḷ uṭal aṭikkaṭi viyarkkum; avarkaḷ mikavum palak kuṟivuṭiyavar kaḷākavum iruppar. avarkaḷ uṭaliṉ eṭi aṭikkaṭi kuṟiyum; cilacamayam atikak kavaliyuḷḷavarkaḷākavum iruppar.
tuṇip puricic curappikaḷ78: ivi puricic curappiyiṉ piṉpuṟamāka, pakkattiṟku iraṇaṭāka amintuḷḷaṉa. ic curappikaḷ uṭalil kālciyam, pāvaram ākiya iraṇṭiṉ uppukaḷiṉ aḷaviṉik kaṭṭup paṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. mēlum, cila naccup poruḷkaḷi nalla poruḷkaḷāka māṟṟavum, kurutiyiṉ kārat taṉmiyic cīrāka vittuk koḷḷavum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kālciyam elumpu vaḷarccikkup payaṉpaṭuvatit tavira, narampu maṇṭalam cariyākac ceyaṟ paṭuvataṟkum mikavum iṉṟi yamiyāta tākavuḷḷatu. tuṇip puricic curappikaḷi akaṟṟi viṭṭāl, kurutiyiṉuḷḷa kālciya nili kuṟivatuṭaṉ iyalpukku māṟāṉa muṟiyil narampu maṇṭalam ceyaṟpaṭṭu īrppu vātamākap pariṇamikkum.
aṭittalic curappi79: itu maṇṭiyōṭṭiṉ aṭiyil mēl aṇṇattiṟkum mūḷikkum iṭiyil maṅkalāṉa mañcaḷ niṟat tuṭaṉ ōr elumpuc cimiḻukkuḷ amintuḷḷatu. (paṭam9). iḵtu ēṉiya curappikaḷ iyaṅkuvatiyum kaṭṭuppaṭuttu kiṉṟatu. kuḻantipparuvattil iḵtu iyakkak kuṟivuṭaṉ viḷaṅkiṉāl, atu uṭal vaḷarcciyip pātikkum; kuḻanti kuḷḷaṉākiviṭuvāṉ; iyakka mikutiyuṭaṉ viḷaṅkiṉāl, avaṉ ēḻu aṭikku mēlum uyarntu vaḷarvāṉ; kālamallāk kālattil-muṉ kūṭṭiyē- pāluṇarcciyum avaṉiṭam tōṉṟum.
māṅkāyc curappikaḷ80: ovvoru ciṟunīrakattiṉ81 mēṟkōṭiyilum vayiṟṟiluḷḷa periya kurutik kuḻalkaḷukku irupuṟamum ovvoru curappiyāka oṭṭik koṇṭuḷḷatu. iccurappikaḷ nām uyir vāḻvataṟku mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātavi. ivi nīkkappeṟiṉum allatu citi vuṟiṉum irupattu nāṉku maṇi nērattiṟkuḷ iṟapput tāṉ muṭivu. kurutiyamukkam, itayat tuṭippu, kurutiyil caruk kari kalattal, mūccuk kuḻal virital pōṉṟa taṉṉāṭci narampu maṇṭala vēlikaḷil ivi toṭarpu koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉapperukka uṟuppukaḷiyum ivi kaṇkāṇik kiṉṟaṉa.
neñcukkuḻic curappi82: itu kuḻantip paruvattil kāṇappeṟum. mūccuk kuḻaliṉ muṉṉāl itayattiṟku mēlē amintuḷḷatu. itu maṟṟiya curappikaḷ muṟṟātapaṭi aṭakki yāḷkiṉṟatu. ataṉāl kuḻantiyiṉ uṭal vaḷarkiṉṟatu. kuḻanti nili nīṅkiyatum itu curuṅki viṭukiṉṟatu.
mēltalic curappi83: itu mūḷiyiṉ naṭuvil uḷḷatu. ic curappi pālaṟikuṟikaḷi vaḷarkkum māṅkāyc curappiyiṉik kaṭṭuppaṭuttukiṉṟatu. ataṉāl, iḷami nīṅkum vari uṭaṉ nilip pālaṟikuṟikaḷ tōṉṟuvatilli. kuḻantiyiṉ ēḻeṭṭu yāṇṭukaḷukkuppiṉ ic curappi curuṅkat toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu. appoḻutu puṇarcci eṉṟa iyaṟkic ceyal niliyum vaḷarntu muṟṟattoṭaṅkum.
pālnilic curappikaḷ84: āṇiṭam virikaḷākavum,85 peṇṇiṭam cūṟpikaḷākavum86 kāṇappeṟupavi. ivaṟṟi uṭaliyalār potuvāka iṉakōḷaṅkaḷ87 eṉṟum vaḻaṅkuvar. piṟanta nāḷ toṭṭuk kumarap paruvam variyilum ivaṟṟil yātoru ceyalum naṭipeṟuvatilli; ākavē, kuḻantiyiṉ naṭattiyi ivi pātippatilli.
potuvāka it tūmpilāc curappikaḷ maṉaveḻucci kaḷuṭaṉum uṭal vaḷarccikaḷuṭaṉum neruṅkiya toṭar puṭiyavi. uḷḷak kiḷarccip poruttappāṭum vaḷarcciyum ivaṟṟip poṟuttavi. ic curappikaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu iṇintē ceyaṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi yāvum aṭaṅkiya eṇṭō kirīṉ maṇṭalattip88 paṟṟiya uṟutiyāṉa uṇmikaḷ yāvum aṟitōṟum aṟiyāmi kaṇṭanili yiluḷḷaṉa.
poṟikaḷ
poṟikaḷiṉ mukkiyattuvam: ellā maṉac ceyalkaḷum poṟikaḷiṉ tūṇṭalkaḷāl naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉavātaliṉ, uḷaviyalil poṟikaḷmukkiyat tuvattip peṟukiṉṟaṉa. nām ceyvaṉayāvum nām peṟum niliyum poṟikaḷip poṟuttē amikiṉṟaṉa. nammuṭiya aṟivu muḻuvatum nērmuṟiyilō aṉṟi nēral muṟiyilō poṟi vāyilākap peṟum ceytikaḷiṉ mītu eḻuntatē yākum. veṟum pulaṉuṇarvu maṭṭilum aṟivaṉ ṟeṉiṉum, pulaṉuṇarviṉi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭē aṟivu malarkiṉṟatu. nām peṟṟuḷḷa poṟikaḷiṉ vakikaḷip poṟuttē ulaki uṇarkiṉṟōm; nammiyum uṇarkiṉ ṟōm. makkaḷ aimpoṟikaḷiviṭak kuṟivāṉa poṟikaḷip peṟṟiruppiṉ avarkaḷ ivvulakiṉip paṟṟik koḷḷum karuttiṉiyum avarkaḷē vāṉoli alikaḷiyum, putirk katir kaḷiyum89 aṇṭakkatirkaḷiyum90 uṇarakkūṭiya atikamāṉa poṟikaḷip peṟṟiruppiṉ avarkaḷ ivvulakiṉip paṟṟik koḷḷum karuttiṉiyum cintittuppāruṅkaḷ. nam vāḻkkik kōlaṅkaḷ pala poṟikaḷ iṇintiyaṟṟu vataṉālēyē cāttiyamākiṉṟaṉa.
poṟikaḷiṉ eṇṇikki: maṉitaṉukku ettaṉip poṟikaḷ uḷa eṉpati uḷaviyalār iṉṉum tiṭṭamāka variyaṟukkavilli, āyiṉum, vaḻivaḻi vantuḷḷa “cuvi, oḷi, ūṟu, ōci, nāṟṟam eṉṉum aimpulaṉkaḷukku mēlāka vēṟu cila pulaṉkaḷum uḷa eṉpatu maṭṭilum uṟuti. aimpoṟikaḷiṉ vāyilākac cūḻniliyip paṟṟi nām aṟintu koḷḷalām. āṉāl, nammippaṟṟi namakkuk kūṟum poṟikaḷum uḷa. oruvakip poṟikaḷ nam uṭaliṉ pala pākaṅkaḷiṉ acivu kaḷiyum, nilikaḷiyum namakkuk kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. vēṟu cila poṟikaḷ nam uḷḷuṟuppukaḷiṉ niliyi namakkuk kaṭṭa vēṇṭip pāluṇarcci, paci pōṉṟa iyalpūkkaṅkaḷi eḻuppukiṉṟaṉa.
maṉitaṉ poruttappāṭṭuc ciṟappu uṭiyavaṉ. porut tappāṭṭu muṟiyil oru poṟiyiṉ ceyali maṟṟoṉṟu mēṟkoḷḷavum iyalum. kuruṭar kiviralkaḷāl paṭikkak kaṟṟukkoḷkiṉṟaṉar; ceviṭar paṭippatil āṟutal peṟu kiṉṟaṉar. maṇam eṉṟa cuviyit tuykka iyalātavarkaḷ vāḻkkiyil vēṟu vaḻikaḷi iyaṟṟik koḷḷukiṉṟaṉar.
maṉita uṭampu eṉṟum ceyal niṟintuḷḷatu. poṟikaḷ aṉittum nammiṭam oru tākkali allatu atircciyi uṇṭākkukiṉṟaṉa. it tūṇṭalkaḷ namakkuḷḷō allatu namakku veḷiyilō cila takavalkaḷi namakku uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. tūṇṭalkaḷ poṟikaḷit tākki, pukuvāykaḷ mūlam narampu maṇṭalatti aṭikiṉṟaṉa; naramput tokutikaḷ vaḻiyāka (poruttuvāykaḷ) naramput tuṭippukaḷ mūḷiyi aṭintu aṅkup pulappāṭukaḷi uṇṭākkukiṉṟaṉa. piṟaku ittuṭippukaḷ taci allatu curappikaḷil (iyaṅkuvāykaḷil) ceyalkaḷi eḻuppukiṉṟaṉa. tūṇṭalkaḷ uṟippuṭaṉ (aivēnaḷēvila) iruntāltāṉ pulaṉuṇarcci ēṟpaṭum. maṅkalāṉa niṟam, mikat tāḻntakural, teḷivaṟṟa maṉam mutaliyavi nammiṭam uṇarc ciyi uṇṭākkuvatilli. aṉṟiyum tūṇṭalkaḷ caṟṟu nēramāvatu taṅkiṉāltāṉ pulaṉuṇarcci91 uṇṭākum.
iṅku nām aṟiyavēṇṭiyatu poruttappāṭṭiṉ mutal niliyip paṟṟiyē. atāvatu, nam uṭalilum cūḻ niliyilum eḻum tūṇṭalkaḷik koḷvatippaṟṟiyē aṟintu koḷḷa vēṇṭum. poṟikaḷ illiyēl porut tappāṭu muṟṟilum naṭipeṟātu; nammiyum ulakittiyum paṟṟiya aṟivu ciṟitēṉum nammiṭam uṇṭākātu. ākavē, avaṟṟippaṟṟi naṉku aṟintu koḷvōm.
kāṭcip pulaṉ-kaṇ
kāṭcippulaṉ maṉitaṉ peṟṟuḷḷa pēṟukaḷuḷ tali ciṟantatu. ataṟku uṟutuṇiyāka iruppatu kaṇ eṉṉum uṟuppu. kaṇṇiṟ ciṟanta uṟuppilli eṉpatu āṉṟōr vākku. itaṉāl kaṇtāṉ taliciṟanta uṟuppu eṉṟa uṇmi pulaṉākiṉṟatu. irāmaṉ kāṉāḷac cellavēṇṭum eṉṟa iraṇṭāvatu varattik kēṭkavēṇṭā eṉṟu kikēyiyi vēṇṭum tayarataṉ kaṇṇē vēṇṭum eṉṉiṉum īyak kaṭavēṉ eṉṟu kūṟum vākkiṉi eṇṇik kaṇṇiṉ ciṟappiṉi uṇarka.
kaṇṇiṉ amippu: nām kaṇ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatu kaṇṇuṇṭi yiyum, ataṉi iyakkivikkum kaṇ taci kaḷiyum, aḵtu īram pularātapaṭi niṟkum kaṇṇīrc curap pikaḷum kūṭiyatōr uṟuppēyākum. ēṟak kuṟiya muṟṟu uruṇṭi vaṭivāyiruttaliṉ ataṉik kaṇṇuṇṭi92 eṉal takum. aḵtu ōraṅkulak kuṟukkaḷavuḷḷatāyk kaṭkuḻiyil93 potintu niṟkiṉṟatu. itaṉuṭiya muṉpuṟattē maṟṟoru ciṟiya uṇṭiyi uḷḷaḻuttip potiya vittāṟpōlak karuviḻi94 pituṅkit tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. kaṇṇuṇṭi mup pōrvi pōrttu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. kaṇṇuṇṭiyiṉ kāvaṟkup payaṉpaṭum veḷḷi viḻiyē95 ataṉatu tiṇṇiya mēṟpōrvi yākum. ivveḷḷi viḻiyō kaṇṇuṇṭi muḻutum cuṟṟi niṉṟu, karuviḻiyiṉ viḷimpuvari vantu aṅkē niṉṟuviṭukiṉṟatu. karuviḻiyō oḷi nuḻi96 poruḷākum. kaṇṇuṇṭiyiṉ iṭiniṉṟa pōrviyō kurutik kuḻāykaḷum ciṟa uyiraṇukkaḷum tacikaḷum cērnta iruṭpaṭāmākum. akappōrviyō kaṇ narampu paṭarntu viriyum pulappaṭā mākum. itu kaṇ-tiri97 eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟum (paṭam 11).
kaṇ oru oḷippoṟi: kaṇṇi oru niḻaṟ poṟiyuṭaṉ98 oppiṭṭuk kūṟalām. oḷippoṟiyiṉ mēṟpeṭṭi pōṉṟatē veḷḷiviḻi. uḷ tōṉṟum kaṟuppup pūccup pōṉṟatē kaṇṇuṇṭiyiṉ iṭip pōrvi yākiya karumpaṭām; ap peṭṭikkuḷ piṉpuṟattē tōṉṟuvatāki ōviyam poṟikkum pilim pōṉṟatē kaṇṇuṇṭiyiṉ akap pōrviyāy viḷaṅkum narampu vaṭivamāṉa pulappaṭām. kaṇṇuṇṭiyilum villi yuṇṭu. atu karuviḻiyiṉ piṉṉē irupuṟam kuvinta vaṭivil amin tuḷḷatu. atutāṉ kaṇ-maṇi99 eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟuvatu. aḵtu oḷinuḻi poruḷ. āṟu maṇit tacikaḷ100 ataṉic cuṟṟi niṉṟu ataṉ vaṭivit taṭṭiyākkiyum parumaṉākkiyum vēṇṭiyapaṭi tiruttiyamikkiṉṟaṉa. kaṇ-maṇikku muṉṉiṭattum karuviḻikkup piṉṉiṭattum karuviḻip puṉal niṟintuḷḷatu. itaṉi muṉ-kaṇīr101 eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar. itu nīr pōṉṟa teḷivāṉa pāymam102; uppunīr pōṉṟatu. itu oḷi nuḻi poruḷēyākum. itu nīr vaṭivāy irup patāl kaṇ-maṇi taṭṭiyāy nīḷavum, taṭittuk kuṟukavum iṭaṅ koṭukkiṉṟatu. kaṇ-maṇikkup piṉṉiṭattuḷḷa perum pakutiyil kūḻ pōṉṟa poruḷ nirampiyuḷḷatu. itu piṉ-kaṇīr103 eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappeṟum. ik kuḻamputāṉ kaṭiṉamāṉa veḷḷi viḻiyuṭaṉ cērntu kaṇmaṇiyit taṉṉiliyiṉiṉṟum viḻātapaṭi kākkiṉṟatu. muṉ-kaṇīrukkum kaṇmaṇikkum iṭiyē vaṭṭappuḻiyōṭu kūṭiya taci yoṉṟu uḷatu. intap puḻiyē pāvi104 eṉappaṭuvatu: itu karumpuḷḷi pōl kāṇappaṭum intap pāvit taciyi viḻittiri105 eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvar. viḻittiriyiluḷḷa puḻi, oḷiyiṉ aḷaviṟkēṟṟa vāṟum aṇmiyiluḷḷa poruḷukkēṟṟavāṟum cēymiyi luḷḷa poruḷukkēṟ ṟavāṟum maṭakkuc ceyalāl iṟukiyum taḷarntum kaṭṭuppaṭuttappeṟukiṉṟatu. mikak kuṟinta aḷavu oḷiyil ataṉ kuṟukkaḷavu 8 milli mīṭṭarukku virintum, mika atikamāṉa oḷiyil 2 millimīṭṭarukkuc curuṅkiyum ceyaṟ paṭuvatākak kaṇakkiṭṭuḷḷaṉar.
kaṇ-tiri106: itaṉik kaṭpulappaṭām eṉṟum vaḻaṅkuvar. iḵtu ōr aṅkulattil nūṟṟil oru paṅku parumaṉē irunta pōtilum, itil pattup purikaḷ aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa. itu pala narampaṇukkaḷik koṇṭa aṇic cavvāl āṉatu. itil pārvip pulaṉuṇar uṟuppukaḷiṉ muṭivup pakutikaḷ uḷa. kāṭcip pulaṉukku iṉṟiyamiyātaṉavākavuḷḷa kōlkaḷ107 kūmpukaḷ108 eṉṟa iraṇṭu vita uyiraṇukkaḷ ic  cavvil aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa. kōlkaḷ nūl nūṟkum katirkaḷ pōṉṟavi; ciṟu kōlkaḷ pōl nīṇṭiruttaliṉ ivaṟṟik kōlkaḷ eṉṟaṉar. ivi pimpattiṟku oḷirvit tarupavi. kumiḻkaḷ paruttuk kuṟiyaṉavāyp piṉpuṟattē kuvintu muṭintu viḷaṅ kukiṉṟaṉa. pimpattiṟku niṟattit tarupavi ivi. ivi iraṇṭum pukuvāykaḷ eṉappaṭum narampuṟuppukaḷ. kōlkaḷum kūmpukaḷum kaṇ-tiriyiṉ piṉpuṟam kūṭalvāyiṉ mūlam narampu muṭiccaṇukkuṭaṉ cērkiṉṟaṉa. ivvaṇukkaḷiṉ narappa viḻutukaḷ kaṇṇic cuṟṟilum paravip pārvi narampāka māṟukiṉṟatu. pārvi narampiṉ mūlam uḷ tuṭippukaḷ mūḷikkuk koṇṭu cellappeṟukiṉṟaṉa (paṭam 12). kōlkaḷum kūmpukaḷum kaṇṇukku muṉ puṟamiruntu taḷḷi appāl uḷḷaṉa. ampuk kuṟikaḷ kāṭṭuvatu pōl, oḷi, kaṇ-tiriyiṉ aṇuk kaḷiṉūṭē pāyntu ceṉṟu avaṟṟiṉit tūṇṭa vēṇṭum. oḷi kōlkaḷum kūmpukaḷum mikavum neruṅkiyuḷḷa oru niṟappuriyi aṭintatum, atu avi nāriṉ nuṉikaḷit tūṇṭi naramput tuṭippukaḷi eḻuppukiṉṟaṉa. kaṇ-tiriyik kaṭantu cellum narappa viḻutukaḷ oṉṟu cērntu oḷi narampāka (ṭīyīviu nēcena) māṟukiṉṟatu. innarampē kaṇṇiṉ ceytiyi mūḷikkuk koṇṭu cellu kiṉṟaṉa.
paṭam 12-aiyum paṭam 13-aiyum uṟṟu nōkkiṉāl kaṇ tiriyiṉ palvēṟu pakutikaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭarpu koṇṭiruppatu teriya varum. kōlkaḷip poṟuttavari itu ciṟappākap poruntum. avi iraṭṭit turuva uyiraṇukkiḷiṉmītu109 oṉṟu cērvatōṭaṉṟi (paṭam. 13) kuṟukkuveṭṭu narampu nuṇmattiṉālum110 oṉṟōṭoṉṟu cērkkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa (paṭam. 12). paṭam 13-l periya vaṭṭam kaṇ-tiriyiṉ parappik kuṟikkiṉṟatu. atiluḷḷa ciṟiya vaṭṭaṅkaḷ koḷvāy aḷavikaḷ.111 ivaṟṟiliruntu iḻikaḷ112 kṣa eṉṟa iraṭṭit turuva uyiraṇukkaḷi aṭi kiṉṟaṉa. iraṭṭit turuva uyiraṇukkaḷē ḻu eṉṟa narampaṇuttiraḷil oṉṟu cērkiṉṟaṉa. narampaṇuttiraḷiṉ iḻikaḷ naramput tuṭippukaḷi mūḷiyiluḷḷa pūt taṇṭu113 eṉṟa pakutikkuk koṇṭu cellukiṉṟaṉa.
kāṭcip pimpaṅkaḷ: puṟavulakiluḷḷa oru pimpam ōr oḷik kōlamākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. mutalāvatāka oḷi karu viḻiyi aṭikiṉṟatu. atu viḻittiri eṉṉum puḻiyiṉūṭē celkiṉṟatu. viḻittiri eṉpatu karu viḻiyiṉ naṭuvil karumpuḷḷi pōl kāṇappaṭuvatu. ataṉ aḷavu maṭakkuc ceyalāl curuṅkiyum virintum uṭcellum oḷiyiṉ aḷavik kaṭṭup paṭuttukiṉṟatu. ataṉmūlam maṅkalum pirakācamumāṉa pimpaṅkaḷ kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. itaṉ piṉṉar oḷikkatirkaḷ kaṇ-maṇi eṉappaṭum villiyi ūṭuruvic ceṉṟu kaṇ-tiri eṉappaṭum kaṭpulappaṭāmil viḻukiṉṟaṉa; uṭaṉē aṅkat tuṭippukaḷ uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa. attuṭippukaḷ pārvi narampukaḷiṉ mūlam mūḷikkuk koṇṭu cellap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. oḷikkatir kaṇṇukkuḷ ceṉṟu pimpam viḻuvatik kāṭṭum paṭattil (paṭam-14) ip pimpam talikīḻāka iruppatupōl tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. āyiṉum mūḷikku ivvāṟu mēl kīḻ eṉṟu illi. atu veḷiyil poruḷ enniliyil uḷḷatō an niliyilēyē kāṇac ceykiṉṟatu. aṅṅaṉam kāṇpataṟkēṟṟa narampu amippukaḷ kaṇṇil amintuḷḷaṉa. kaṟṟalāl nam  pārvippulak kāṭcikaḷ tiṭṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
pimpatti miyappaṭuttutal: māṉiṭak kaṇṇiṉ villi (kaṇ-maṇi) oḷippaṭap peṭṭiyiṉ villiyi viṭa nekiḻvāṉatu. oḷippaṭap peṭṭiyil teḷivāṉa pimpam peṟa vēṇṭumāṉāl atiluḷḷa turutti pōṉṟa pākam muṉṉum piṉṉumākat taḷḷappeṟutal vēṇṭum. āṉāl, kaṇṇiṉ villi pārkkappeṟum poruḷiṉ tūrattiṟ kēṟṟavāṟu tāṉāka uṭaṉukkuṭaṉ poruttamuṟukiṉṟatu. villiyip paṟṟiyuḷḷa maṇittaci kaḷ taḷarntu atit taṭṭiyākac ceytāl toliviluḷḷa poruḷ kaḷik kāṇalām; attacikaḷ curuṅki villiyik kuviyac ceytāl aṇmip poruḷkaḷ naṉku tulakkamuṟum.
pārvit tuṭippukaḷ mūḷikkuc cellum vitam: oḷiyiṉ kōlam kaṇṇiṉ uyarcci mikunta koḷvāy muṭivup pakutikaḷit tūṇṭum poḻutu aṅku vētiyiyal etirviṉi toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu. iv vetir viṉi kaṇ-tiriyiṉuḷ narampaṇuvukkuk kaṭattappeṟṟup pārvi naram piṉmūlam mūḷiyi vantaṭikiṉṟatu. kaṇ-tiriyiṉ ov voru koḷvāyilumiruntum mūḷiyiṉ pārvip pakutikku oru narampu nārt tokuti cellukiṉṟatu. ippakuti mūḷiyiṉ piṉpuṟattil amintuḷḷatu. pārkkum poruḷiṉ ovvoru pakutiyum kāṭcip pākattiṉ parappil vīcappeṟukiṉṟatu. aḵtāvatu, poruḷiṉ ovvoru miyamum ataṟkiyin tavāṟu kaṇ-tirik koḷvāykku oḷiyi etiroḷik kiṉṟatu115. it tūṇṭaṟ kōlam narampu nārkaḷāl mūḷikku eṭuttuc cellappaṭa aṅkum ataṟkiyintatoru narampaṇuk kōlam tūṇṭappeṟukiṉṟatu. narampaṇuviṉ vētiyiyal etirviṉit tuṭippukaḷ ēṟakkuṟiya maṇikku 150 mil vēkattil cellukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ atirvu-eṇ116 koḷvāy niliyi yum oḷittūṇṭaliṉ uṟippiyum117 poṟuttatu. atikamāyiruntāl it tuṭippukaḷ virivil oṉṟiyoṉṟu toṭarkiṉṟaṉa. atika oḷiyiṉāl tuṭippukaḷ peritākā; avaṟṟiṉ toṭarcci vēkamē atikamākum. oru pulaṉuṇarcci perun tūṇṭutalāl vaṉmi peṟak kāraṇam narampaṇukkaḷ atikamākat tulaṅkutalāl aṉṟu; virivākat tulaṅku talālēyākum.
kuruṭṭiṭamum oḷiyiṭamum: kaṇ narampu kaṇṇiṉ piṉ vaḻiyāka vantu paravukiṉṟatu. atukaṇṇukkuḷḷē pukumiṭattil kaṭpulappaṭām ciṟitum illi. ātaliṉ ataṉik kuruṭṭiṭam118 eṉpar. kaṭpulap paṭāttilē uḷḷa naṭuviṭam, pāviyiṉ naṭuviṭattiṟkum kaṇ-maṇiyiṉ naṭuviṭattiṟkum nērē amin tuḷḷatu. avviṭam mañcaḷ niṟamāka uḷḷatu. atu ciṟitu uṭkuḻintum viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. aṅkuttāṉ kūmpukaḷ mikac ceṟintuḷḷaṉa. aṅkuk kōlkaḷ illi. ākavē, atutāṉ nām poruḷiṉ pimpatti viḷakkamāka ēṟṟuk koḷḷum iṭamākum. ituvē oḷiyiṭam119 eṉappaṭuvatu. inta iṭattil tāṉ nām oru poruḷi nēraṭiyākap pārkkuṅkāl oḷik katirkaḷ cātāraṇamākak kuvikiṉṟaṉa. oḷiyiṭattil kurutik kuḻalkaḷum iṇikkum iḻiyaṅkaḷum illi.
kāṇappaṭum poruḷ nērē kaṇṇiṉ naṭuviṭattil viḻuvatāltāṉ pala niṟaṅkaḷiyum pirittaṟikiṉṟōm. naṭuviṭattilaṉṟi ōrattil viḻu māṉāl pala niṟaṅkaḷ tōṉṟu vatilli. viḷimparukē varavara niṟat tōṟṟam kuṟikiṉṟatu. tōṉṟum niṟamum niṟivu niṟamākāmal kuṟivu niṟamākiṉṟatu. viḷimpilē veḷiṟittāṉ kāṇkiṉṟōm; niṟattik kāṇpatilli. kūmpukaḷ naṭuviṭattē neruṅkiyuḷḷaṉa; viḷimpiṟku arukuvariyil ciṟitu ciṟitākak kuṟintu koṇṭē vantu iṟutiyil oṉṟumillāmaṟ pōkiṉṟatu. kōlkaḷ viḷimparukē moyttuk koṇṭuḷḷaṉa; naṭuviṭattiṟkup pōkap pōkak kuṟintu koṇṭē pōy iṟutiyil avi illāmaṟ pōkiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru kaṇ-tiriyilum 10,00,00,000 kōlkaḷum 60,00,000 kūmpukaḷum uḷḷaṉavākak kaṇakkiṭ ṭuḷḷaṉar. iv vamippiṉik koṇṭu kūmpukaḷē niṟattāl tākkappeṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, kōlkaḷē veḷiṟṟoḷiyāl  tākkappeṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum cilar muṭivu kaṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. niṟaṅkaḷ pala vēṟupāṭṭi aṭivatu pōlavē, kūmpukaḷum palavāka vēṟupaṭṭu viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. pakaṟkāṭci kūmpukaḷāl nērkiṉṟateṉṟum oḷi kuṟintu mālik kāṭci kōlkaḷāl nērkiṉṟateṉṟum vēṟu cilar eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar.
niṟakkuruṭu: kaṇṇiṉ mukkiyamāṉa toḻilkaḷuḷ oṉṟu niṟaṅkaḷip pirittaṟivatum avaṟṟip pārppatumākum. cātāraṇamāṉa oru carācari maṉitaṉ 3,00,000kku mēṟpaṭṭa niṟaṅkaḷip pirittaṟiyak kūṭum eṉṟu matippiṭṭuḷḷaṉar. itaṉāl ovvoru niṟattiṟkum avaṉ peyariṭak kūṭum eṉpatu poruḷaṉṟu.
nammil perumpālōr ellā niṟaṅkaḷiyum avaṟṟiṉ kalavi kaḷiyum pārttaṟital kūṭum; cilarāl aṅṅaṉam kāṇa iyalātu. avarkaḷ cila niṟaṅkaḷi maṭṭilumtāṉ kāṇutal kūṭutal. pala niṟaṅkaḷi avarkaḷ pala nilikaḷiluḷḷa veḷiṟṟoḷikaḷākavē kāṇpar. ittakiya kuṟiyi niṟakkuruṭu120 eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvar. makkaḷ tokiyil iruvita niṟak kuruṭukaḷ iruppatākak kaṇṭaṟintuḷḷaṉar. cilar veḷḷi-kaṟupput toṭar niṟaṅkaḷi yum mañcaḷ-nīlam toṭar niṟaṅ kaḷiyum kāṇakkūṭum; āṉāl, avarkaḷ cemmi-paccit toṭar niṟaṅkaḷik kāṇa iyalātu. ivarkaḷukkuḷḷa kuṟiyi ciṟitu-niṟakkuruṭu121 eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar. vēṟu cilar veṇmi-karumit toṭar niṟaṅkaḷi maṭṭilumē kāṇaval lavarkaḷ; ivarkaḷuṭiya kuṟi muḻu niṟakkuruṭu122 eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟum. ciṟitu-niṟakkuruṭuṭiyōr irumi-niṟamuṭiyōr123 eṉṟum, muḻuniṟakkuruṭuṭiyōr orumi niṟamuṭiyōr124 eṉṟum vaḻaṅkappeṟuvar. makkaṭṭokiyil ēṟakkuṟiya 4 catavītam āṇ pālārum, ēṟakkuṟiya 1 catavītam peṇ pālārum irumi-niṟamuṭiyarkaḷāka uḷḷaṉar eṉṟu matippiṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu. āyiṉum, orumi - niṟamuṭiyōriṉ eṇṇikki mikak kuṟintē kāṇap peṟukiṉṟatu.
iṉṟiya ulaka vāḻvil niṟak kuruṭik kaṇṭaṟivatu mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatākiṉṟatu. pōkkuvarattil, niṟa aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ kiyāḷap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. accāḷar, kōlam puṉivōr, amippāḷar, necavāḷar ākiyōr tam toḻilil tiṟamiyaṭiyavēṇṭumāyiṉ nalla niṟap pārviyip peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum. niṟakkuruṭic cōtittaṟivataṟku iṉṟu uḷaviyalār āyvukaḷik kaṇṭaṟintuḷḷaṉar, avaṟṟuḷ peruvaḻakkāka iruppatu iṣihārā āyvu (aiḷāihyacaya vanaḷava) eṉpatu. 
pārvik kuṟikaḷ: uḷaviyal payilvōr kaṭpulaṉil ēṟpaṭak kūṭiya uṭaṟ kūṟupaṟṟiya pārvik kuṟikaḷi aṟintu koḷvatu cāttiyamilli. atu maruttuvam payilvōruk kuriya taṉippaṭṭa tuṟiyākum. eṉiṉum, cātāraṇamāka ēṟpaṭakkūṭiya pārvik kuṟikaḷip paṟṟi ōraḷavu āciriyar aṟintukoḷḷa vēṇṭuvatu mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatu. kiṭṭappārvi125 eṉpatu cātāraṇamākak kāṇappeṟum oru kuṟi. itu piṟaviyilēyē ēṟpaṭṭa kuṟiyaṉṟu; piṟaku ēṟpaṭuvatu. intak kuṟiyi uṭiyavarkaḷ aṇmiyiluḷḷa poruḷ kaḷittāṉ naṉku pārttal iyalum; caṟṟut toliviluḷḷa poruḷkaḷ ivarkaḷukkut teḷivaṟṟaṉavākavē kāṇap paṭum. kuḻivilli126kaḷik koṇṭa mūkkuk kaṇṇāṭikaḷi aṇintu ikkuṟikaḷi nivartti ceytu koḷḷalām. turappārvi127 eṉpatu maṟṟoru kuṟi. itu piṟaviyilēyē ēṟpaṭum kōḷāṟu. intak kuṟi yuṭiyavarkaḷ toliviluḷḷa poruḷkaḷi naṉku kāṇpar; aṇmi yiluḷḷa poruḷkaḷi ivarkaḷ naṉku pārttal iyalātu. kuvivilli kaḷik128 koṇṭa mūkkuk kaṇṇāṭiyi aṇintu ik kuṟiyi nivartti ceytu koḷḷavēṇṭum. kuṟik kāṭci129 eṉpatu piṟaviyilēyē ēṟpaṭum piṟitoru kuṟi. paṭikavilli allatu karuviḻi oḻuṅkaṟṟa taṉmiyil amintiruppatē itaṟkuk kāraṇam. ikkuṟiyi yuṭiyavarkaḷ poruḷkaḷi ōraḷavu teḷivuṭaṉum ōraḷavu teḷivaṟṟum kāṇpar. uruḷi villikaḷik 130 koṇṭa mūkkuk kaṇṇāṭikaḷi aṇintu ikkuṟiyic camāḷikka vēṇṭum iraṭṭippārvi131 eṉpatu iṉṉoru kuṟi. pārkkappaṭum poruḷiṉ pimpam kaṭpulappaṭām iraṇṭilum ottuḷḷa puḷḷikaḷil132 miyap paṭuttap peṟātatālitu nēriṭukiṉṟatu. itaṉ viḷivāka iraṇṭu pimpaṅkaḷ uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa. iraṭṭip pārvi, narampukaḷilum tacikaḷilum ēṟpaṭum nōynilikaḷiṉ kāraṇa mākavum nēriṭalām; mitamiñciya kuṭiyiṉālum palavita nañcukaḷālum nōykaḷālum ēṟpaṭalām. nañcu nīkku muṟi kaḷālum palavakippaṭṭa tacip payiṟcikaḷālum inniliyik kuṇamākkalām. takka villikaḷik koṇṭa mūkkuk kaṇṇāṭiyi aṇintum itaṉic camāḷikka muṭiyum.

kēḷvippulaṉ-kātu
pārviyip pōlavē kēḷviyum toliviluḷḷa uḷtuṭippukaḷi ēṟpatāl nēriṭukiṉṟatu. pārviyip pōlavē kēḷviyum nammic cuṟṟiyum nikaḻvati aṟiya utavukiṉṟatu. iraṇṭu pulaṉkaḷum toli viluḷḷa tūṇṭalkaḷi mēṟkoḷ vatāl, inta iraṇṭu pulaṉ uṟuppu kaḷi mēṟkoḷvatāl, inta iraṇṭu pulaṉ uṟuppukaḷum tolivup pukuvāykaḷ133 eṉa vaḻaṅkappeṟum pārviyippōla, cevippulaṉ nām vāḻ vataṟku avvaḷavu atimukkiyamāka irāviṭiṉum, kēḷvi purintu koḷvataṟkum pātukāppiṟkum oru mukkiyamāṉa cātaṉamāka amin tuḷḷatu. kāṭcip pulaṉukku oḷiyalikaḷ tūṇṭalkaḷāka amivaṉa pōlavē, kēḷvippulaṉukkut tūṇṭalkaḷāka amipavi kāṟṟuttuṇukku kaḷiṉ134 atirvu kaḷākum135 inta atirvukaḷ alivaṭiyil naṭipeṟu kiṉṟaṉa. ōr aliyiṉ vīccukkum136 atirvu-eṇṇukkum137 ēṟṟavāṟu muṟiyē oliyuṇarcciyiṉ uṟippiyum138 curutiyiyum139 peṟu kiṉṟōm. olikkuc curakuṇam140 eṉṟa mūṉṟāvatu paṇpum uṇṭu. curakuṇam atirviṉik koṭukkum aliyiṉ amippiṉip poṟuttatu. yāḻil eḻum curakuṇattiṟkum kuḻalil eḻum curakuṇattiṟkum vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. eṉavē, orē curutiyuṭaṉum uṟippuṭaṉum cīṟiyāḻ narampiliruntum mūṅkiṟ kuḻaliliruntum uṇṭākkappeṟum curam141 curakuṇa vēṟu pāṭṭiṉ kāraṇamāka vēṟupaṭṭa iraṇṭu viḷivukaḷip peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
iṉi, kēḷvip pulaṉukku iṉṟiyamiyātatākavuḷḷa kātiṉi ārōyvōm. oliyāṉatu nammuḷ pukuvataṟku vāyiṟpaṭiyāka viḷaṅkuvatē cevippoṟiyākum. vaḷḷiyip pōlat taliyiṉ iru puṟattum tōṉṟum uṟuppiṉi maṭṭum kātu eṉṟu ulakavaḻakkil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟōm. āṉāl, cevippoṟi ik kātu maṭṭumaṉṟu; aḵtu uḷḷē maṇṭikkuḷḷum ceṉṟu amintuḷḷatu. ac cevippoṟi mūṉṟu pirivukaḷāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. avaṟṟi nām puṟaccevi, iṭiccevi, uṭcevi eṉa vēṟu pirittu vaḻaṅkuvōm.
puṟaccevi: veḷiyē tōṉṟum kātumaṭalum142, veḷi yiliruntu cevippaṟivari cellum mukkāl aṅkula nīḷa muḷḷa puṟaccevikkuḻalum cērnta amippē puṟaccevi eṉpatu. avi maṇṭikkuḷḷē pōy muṭi kiṉṟaṉa. inta iraṇṭu uṟuppukaḷum veḷiyiluḷḷa oli alikaḷit tiraṭṭi cevippaṟikku aṉuppat tuṇi ceykiṉṟaṉa. cevip paṟiyē puṟacceviyiṉ uḷ elliyākum.
iṭiccevi: cevippaṟikkum uṭcevikkum iṭiyē yuḷḷa oru kuṟukalāṉa aṟiyākum itu. itaṟkup puṟelliyākac cevippaṟiyum, aka elliyākac ciṟiyatōr eṉpuc cuvarum tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. ivviraṇṭu ellikaṭkum iṭiyē mūṉṟu ciṟiya elumpukaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu piṇintu toṭar pōl viḷaṅkic cevippaṟiyiyum avveṉpuc cuvariyum piṇikkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ uruva amippiṉik koṇṭu avi cutti 143, paṭṭaṭi,144. aṅkavaṭic145 ciṟṟelumpukaḷ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ iṇippip paṭattil kāṇka. (paṭam 15). iṭic ceviyiṉ uḷ elliyāka uḷḷa eṉpuc cuvaril iraṇṭu tuḷikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. oṉṟu vaṭṭa vaṭiviṉatu; ātaliṉ atu vaṭṭap puḻi eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappeṟum. maṟṟoṉṟu muṭṭi vaṭiviṉatu; ātaliṉ atu muṭṭip puḻi eṉap peyar peṟum, ovvoru puḻiyiyum cavvu mūṭikkiṭak kiṉṟatu. cutti elumpiṉ kippiṭi cevippaṟi yilum aṅkavaṭi elumpiṉ piṟi vaḷiviṉ irumuṉiyum muṭṭip puḻiyiṉ cavvilum oṭṭik kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. veḷi oliyāl cevippaṟi aciyumāṉāl ataṉōṭu oṭṭiyuḷḷa eṉput toṭarum aciyum; avveṉputtoṭar aciyum poḻutu muṭṭippuḻic cavvum acikiṉṟatu. inta nuṭpamāṉa eṉpukaḷi iṇikkum pantakaṅkaḷ iṟukippōkumāyiṉ atircci pātikkappeṟṟu aricceviṭu nēriṭalām. cila atircci maṭṭilum kāṟṟiṉ vaḻiyāka aṉuppap peṟalām.
iṭiccevi, naṭuccevik kuḻal146 eṉṉum nīṇṭa meṉmiyāṉa kuḻaliṉāl toṇṭiyuṭaṉ iṇikkap peṟṟuḷḷatu. nām viḻuṅkum poḻutu allatu koṭṭāvi viṭumpoḻutu kāṟṟi intak kuḻalukkuḷ palamākac celuttik kātil kāṟṟi niṟivikkiṉṟōm. itaṉāl cevippaṟiyiṉ irupuṟamum kāṟṟiṉ amukkam camappaṭukiṉṟatu. cila camayam nām caḷi piṭippāl pīṭikkappeṟṟirukkumpoḻutu muṉ toṇṭi vāyilkaḷic caḷi aṭittuk koḷḷukiṉṟatu. innili namakkuc ciṟitu acukaryattit taruvatuṭaṉ iṭicceviyiluḷḷa kāṟṟiṉ amukkak kuṟiviṉālum cevippaṟi uṭpuṟamāka, uppik koṇṭiruppatālum kēḷviyum mantappaṭukiṉṟatu. toṇṭiyil ēṟpaṭum toṟṟiṉiyoṭṭi iṭic ceviyilum toṟṟu ēṟpaṭṭuk kāṟṟiṟkup patilākac cīḻ nirampi viṭum. cevippaṟiyiluḷḷa mikacciṟiya cantuvaḻiyāka intac cīḻ veḷiyēṟi cevippaṟiyum cukappaṭṭuviṭum. eṉiṉum, aṭikkaṭi nēriṭum toṟṟu nōyiṉāl cevip paṟiyiṉ atircciyum elumpukaḷiṉ atircciyum kuṟintu pōkak kūṭum.
uṭcevi: iṭiccevikku uṭpuṟattē viḷaṅkuvatutāṉ uṭcevi. avvuṭcevi cuṟṟuc cuḻaṟci vaṭivāka amintuḷḷatu. aṅkē mūṉṟu vaḷiyaṅkaḷ cikkik kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. aviyē ataṉ piṉpuṟam. aviyē nilippulap poṟiyākavum 
viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, muṉpuṟattē uḷḷavi tām olippulap poṟikku uyiriṭamākum. muṭṭip puḻikkum vaṭṭap puḻikkum aṭuttāṟ pōl iraṇṭu uṇṭi vaṭivamāṉa pitukkaṅkaḷ tōṉṟuvatāl iṭiyē oru paḷḷam kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. iḵtu uṭceviyiṉ oru pirivu. maṟṟoru pirivu nattiyōṭu pōl viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. pirikayiṟṟuttali muṭipōla uḷḷē puriyākavum, aṭiyil akalamākavum, mēlē pōkappōka akalam kuṟintu nuṉiyil ciṟuttatākavum, cuṟṟiṉāṟpōl amintatākavum itu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu; ātaliṉ itaṉip purimuṭi eṉalām. ippurimuṭiyiṉ uṭpuṟamāṉatu neṭukkē mūṉṟu pirivukaḷākap pirintu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. im mūṉṟu kuḻikaḷilum pāymam nirampiyuḷḷatu. immūṉṟu kuḻikaḷum aṭiyiliruntu nuṉivariyilum antap purimuṭi pōlavē cuṟṟic cuṟṟi mēṟcellukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟilē oru kuḻiyāṉatu muṭṭip puḻiyiṉiṉṟum varukiṉṟatu; ātaliṉ ataṉi muṭṭip puḻikkuḻi eṉalām. maṟṟoṉṟu vaṭṭap puḻiyiṉiṉṟu varukiṉṟatu. ātaliṉ ataṉi vaṭṭappuḻik kuḻi eṉalām. purimuṭiyiṉ nuṉiyil ivviraṇṭu kuḻikaḷum oṉṟākak kalakkiṉṟaṉa. mūṉṟāvatu kuḻiyō ivviraṇ ṭiyumviṭa mikamikac ciṟiyatu. itaṉip purimuṭikkuḻal eṉṟu vaḻaṅkalām. itaṉatu mēṟpuṟattōl ikkuḻaliṉi muṭṭik kuḻiyiṉiṉṟu vēṟu pirikkiṉṟatu; itaṉatu kīḻppuṟattōl itaṉi vaṭṭappuḻikkuḻiyiṉiṉṟum vēṟu pirikkiṉṟatu.
mayirk karuvamippu: itaṉ kīḻppuṟattōlil tāṉ kōl vaṭivamāṉa narampamippukaḷum mayirkkaru amippukaḷum uḷḷaṉa. iviyē olit tūṇṭalāl tākkuṟupavi. iviyē cevippulanarampōṭu toṭarpu paṭṭu uḷḷavi. oliali purimuṭiyiṉuḷ cellumpōtu ivi av alikkēṟpa acintu narampuk kiḷarcciyi uṇṭu paṇṇukiṉṟaṉa. puri muṭiyiṉ aṭippuṟattiliruntu nuṉivari, immayirkaḷ ciṟukac ciṟuka muṟiyē nīṇṭukoṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. oliyāl aciyak kūṭiya taṭṭik kampikaḷ palavaṟṟip palavakiyāka ōriṭattē niralē putittu vittu, vevvēṟu vakiyāṉa olikaḷi eḻuppuvōmāṉāl, ovvōr oli ovvoru vakiyāṉa kampikaḷi acippatik kāṇpōm. avvāṟē, vevvēṟu oliyalikaḷ iyaṅkumpoḻutu vevvēṟu nīḷamāṉa am mayirkkaru amippukaḷum iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. oliyalikaḷiṉ uyarccikkēṟṟavāṟu im mayirkaḷiṉ nīḷamum vēṟupaṭṭu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. āyiram narampukaḷ koṇṭa civaperumāṉ yāḻiṉiviṭa 24,000 narampukaḷ koṇṭa nam ceviyāḻ ciṟantatu aṉṟō?
āciriyarukkuk kuṟippu: kēḷvippulaṉil kuṟiyuḷḷa māṇākkarkaḷ cātāraṇamākap paḷḷikaḷil kāṇappaṭuvar. cila camayam oru kuṟippiṭṭa māṇākkaṉ taṉ kēḷvip pulaṉil uḷḷa ciṟukuṟiyi uṇarāmal irukkalām. avaṉ orukāl uṇarntālum pala kāraṇaṅkaḷāl akkuṟiyi āciriyarukkut terivikkāmal irukkalām. itaṉ viḷivu avaṉip paḷḷi vēḷiyil piṟpōkkāḷaṉāka ākkiviṭukiṉṟatu. ākavē, ittakiya māṇākkarkaḷi āciriyar uṉṉippākak kavaṉittu āvaṉa ceyyavēṇṭum.
piṟa pulaṉkaḷ
kāṭcip pulaṉ, kēḷvip pulaṉ ākiya iraṇṭaṉuṭaṉ ettaṉip pulaṉkaḷ nammiṭam amintuḷḷaṉa? ariṭāṭṭil kālattiliruntu tiruvaḷḷuvar kālattiliruntu-mēlum mūṉṟu pulaṉkaḷ uḷḷaṉa eṉṟu collappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. avi: cuvip pulaṉ, ūṟṟup pulaṉ, nāṟṟap pulaṉ eṉpaṉa. eṉiṉum, vaḻivaḻiyē kūṟappeṟum cuvi oḷi ūṟu ōci nāṟṟam eṉṟa aintiṉukku mēṟpaṭṭa pulaṉkaḷum uḷḷaṉa eṉpatu veḷippaṭi, avi ovvoṉṟiyum ciṟitu ārāy vōm.
vētiyiyaṟ pulaṉkaḷ
cuvip pulaṉum nāṟṟap pulaṉum payaṉilum ceyaṟpaṭuvatilum mika neruṅkiya uṟavu koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. inta iraṇṭilum poruttamāṉa tūṇṭalkaḷāl vētiyiyal māṟṟaṅkaḷ nikaḻ kiṉṟaṉa. naṭimuṟiyil ivi iraṇṭum iṇintē ceyaṟ paṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpati nām aṟiyalām. caḷi piṭittirukkum poḻutu nām uṇaviṉ cuviyi uṇarātatu itaṟku ōr eṭuttuk kāṭṭākum. nām uṇṇum uṇaviṉit tērnteṭuppataṟkum, kēṭṭiṉi viḷivikkum poruḷkaḷi vilakku vataṟkum ivviṇṭum oṟṟumiyuṭaṉ tuṇipurikiṉṟaṉa, ākavē, ivi iraṇṭiyum oṉṟupaṭutti vētiyiyaṟ pulaṉkaḷ147 eṉa vaḻaṅkalām.
cuvippulaṉ-nākku: ip pulaṉukkup pukuvāyāka amin tavi cuviyarumpukaḷ148 eṉa vaḻaṅkum uyiraṇukkaḷiṉ tokuti yākum. kuḻantikaḷiṭam atikamāṉa cuviyarumpukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. vayatu āka āka, mutirntavarkaḷiṭam pukuvāykaḷiṉ eṇṇikki pala nūṟukaḷiliruntu eḻupatiliruntu eṇpatu variyilumāka kuṟintu viṭukiṉṟatu. ivi nāliliruntu pattu vari tokuti kaḷāka amintuḷḷaṉa. ivi nāvilum, aṇṇam, kaṉṉam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ aṇiccavvilum (īḻittōl) amin tuḷḷaṉa.oru cuviyarumpu (paṭam-16) kiṭṭattaṭṭa kaḻuttuk kuṟukiya kūcāvip pōṉṟu uruḷi vaṭivamāṉa narappa aṇukkaḷ cērnta tokutiyākum. kaḻuttu vaḻiyāka mēṟ parappiṟku varum uyiraṇukkaḷil mika melliya urōma amippukaḷ uḷḷaṉa; avitām cuviyuḷḷa tiravattuṭaṉ campantappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ellāc cuviyarumpukaḷum oṉṟu pōlirup patilli. cila kūcāvaṭiviṉavākavum cila kuḻivaṭiviṉavākavum uḷḷaṉa. cuviyaṟitaṟkut tūṇṭaṟ poruḷkaḷ tiravap poruḷ kaḷāka māṟavēṇṭum. tiṭap poruḷkaḷum umiḻnīril karintu tiravap poruḷāka māṟum pōtu tāṉ cuvi tarukiṉṟaṉa. mūṉṟu maṇṭi narampukaḷiliruntu narampu nārkaḷ ōṭivantu nāviṉ pala iṭaṅkaḷilu muḷḷa cuviyaṟiyum uyiraṇuk kaḷic cērkkiṉṟaṉa. innarampu nārkaḷ vaḻiyākattāṉ kiḷarcci, mūḷiyiluḷḷa cuvip pula elliyic cērkiṉṟatu.
paṇṭiyōr cuvikaḷi aṟucuvi eṉa vakuttirunta pōtilum ārāycciyāḷarkaḷ cuvikaḷi nāṉku vaki kaḷākavē pirittuḷḷaṉar. avi: iṉippu, puḷippu, uvarppu (uppu) kippu (kacappu) eṉpaṉa. cila kuṟippiṭṭa vētiyiyal poruḷik koṇṭu ivaṟṟil oṉṟu allatu palavaṟṟic ceyaṟpaṭātu ceyyavum kūṭum; eṉavē, ivi yāvum ovvoru kuṟippiṭṭa cuviyarumpukaḷuṭaṉ uṟavu koṇṭuḷḷaṉa eṉpati aṟikiṉṟōm. nākkiṉ nuṉi ellāc cuvikaḷiyum aṟital kūṭum; āṉāl atu kippiyum iṉippiyum kūrmiyāka aṟiyakkūṭiyatu. nākkiṉ piṉpakuti uvarppuc cuviyi aṟi kiṉṟatu. nākkiṉ iraṇṭu pakkappakutikaḷum kippiyum puḷippiyum naṉku uṇarkiṉṟaṉa. nākkiṉ mēṟpakutiyil kūrmiyāṉa uṇarcci illi; avviṭattil ciṟitu nēram kacappu maruntiyum vittirukkalām. nām marunti viḻuṅkum poḻututāṉ antak kacappi aṟiya muṭiyum. ētāvatu ōr uṇavi uṇṇumpoḻutu nām peṟum cuviyanupavam inta nāṉku mutal nilic cuvi uṇarvukaḷuṭaṉ-palvēṟupaṭṭa vēṟu cuvip pulaṉuṇarkaḷuṭaṉ-kalantē peṟukiṉṟōm. 
(e-ṭu.) cūṭāṉa kāḵpi. kāḵpiyiṉ cuvi uvarppu; carukkari cērntiruppatāl iṉikkiṉṟatu. ēṉiya anupavaṅkaḷ yāvum cuṭupulaṉ, toṭupulaṉ, nāṟṟap pulaṉ ākiyavaṟṟāl ēṟpaṭu paviyē.
ellārum ellāc cuvikaḷiyum cama aḷavil aṟintu anupavittal illi. evvaḷavu paṭṭaṟivu peṟṟiruntālum, oru ciṟu pakutiyiṉariṭam cuviyaṟiyum taṉmi cariyāka amivatē illi; palvēṟu nilikaḷil avarkaḷ cuviyi aṟiya iyalātavarkaḷāka uḷḷaṉar. ivarkaḷi niṟak kuruṭarkaḷuṭaṉ oppiṭalām. ōrukāl avarkaḷiṭam piṟaviyilēyē cila kuṟippiṭṭa pukuvāykaḷ vaḷarcciyuṟāmal iruttalum kūṭum. cuvip pulaṉum tūṇṭalik koḷḷum palacātaṉaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. ulaka aṟitalukku itu mutal aṭiyākum.
nāṟṟappulaṉ-mūkku: maṇam aṟiyum uṇarcci maṉitaṉukku mikavum kuṟivāṉa aḷavil vēṇṭap peṟuvatu; āṉāl, aḵtu avaṉ cūḻnilikaḷic cariyāka aṟintu koḷvataṟkut tuṇi ceykiṉṟatu. makkaḷiṭi ippulaṉ ciṟappu niliyuṟṟu viḷaṅkavilli. nāyum eliyum mōppam piṭippatil tali ciṟantaṉa. avaṟṟiṟku makkaḷ av vaḻiyil tōṟṟuppōkiṉṟaṉar. nāṟṟappulaṉ uṇavupporuḷit terinteṭuppataṟkup payaṉ paṭukiṉṟatu; ātaliṉ itu vāykkarukē iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu. mōppattiṉālēyē kumaṭṭum poruḷkaḷi aṟikiṉṟōm. makkaḷ uyarnili aṭiya aṭiya ip pulaṉ taṉ perumiyi iḻantu varukiṉṟatu; eṉṟālum, maṇṭiyil mutalmutal pirinta narampu ippula narampē eṉpati maṟattalākātu. nāṟṟap pulaṉiṉ aṇṇaṉē cuvip pulaṉ. ivi iraṇṭum toṭak kattil uṇavup pulaṉ allatu maruntup pulaṉ eṉa oṉṟāka viḷaṅkiṉa. kāṭcip pulaṉ, kēḷvip pulaṉ ākiyavi pōṉṟē ituvum tolivuppulaṉē149. navīṉa vāḻviṟku ituvum mikavum vēṇṭappaṭuvatu.
mukartalāl viḷaṅkum nāṟṟap pulaṉukkēṟṟa poṟimūkku. uyirppōṭu toṭarpupaṭṭiruppatāl uyirppeṉṟum ip pulaṉi vaḻaṅkuvar. kaṇṭu kēṭṭu uṇṭu uyirttu uṟṟu aṟiyum aimpula iṉpam eṉa vaḷḷuvapperumāṉ vākku varutal kāṇka. itaṟkup pukuvāyō cila uyiraṇukkaḷ ākum. āvi mūkkiṉuḷ aminta īḻittōlil (caḷic cavvu) potintuḷḷaṉa (paṭam-17). avi kiṭṭattaṭṭak kaṇ maṭṭattil amintuḷḷaṉa. amippilum ceyalilum ivi nāk kiluḷḷa cuvi yarumpukaḷi ottuḷḷaṉa; vaṭivil vaṭik katir pōṉṟu viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ mēṟparappiluḷḷa atinuṭpamāṉa urōma amippukaḷ maṇattuṭaṉ toṭarpukoḷḷukiṉṟaṉa.
uyirpporuḷkaḷ, uyiral poruḷkaḷ eṉpavaṟṟi liruntu eḻum nuṇ poṭikaḷ kāṟṟil paravukiṉṟaṉa. ikkāṟ ṟi nām uyirkkum poḻutu atu nam mūkkiṉuṭ pukuntu urōma amippukaḷ mūlam īḻit tōlil putintuḷḷa uyiraṇukkaḷit tākki vētiyiyal viṉiyip puri kiṉṟaṉa. ataṉāl avi kiḷarcciyuṟukiṉṟaṉa. ik kiḷarcci narampu vaḻiyāka mūḷiyiluḷḷa nāṟṟap pula elliyi aṭikiṉṟatu.
mukarum poṟi cikkaliṉṟi iruntālum nāṟṟap pulaṉiṉ vakikaḷō palavākavē tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. niṟavaki, olivaki mutaliyaviyāl nāṟṟavakikaḷ oṟṟumippaṭṭup paṭip paṭiyāka oru toṭarcciyāka viḷaṅkavilli. nāṟṟappulaṉ makkaḷiṭiyē ciṟantu viḷaṅkāmiyāl atippaṟṟi ārāy vōrum ataṉ vēṟṟumikaḷi vaki ceytu ituvari muṭivu kaṭṭavilli. putupputu maruntukaḷum, putupputu paḻaṅkaḷum putupputu pūkkaḷum putupputu maṇattit terivikkiṉṟaṉa.
ūṟṟuppulaṉ-tōl
puṟa ulakit terintu koḷvataṟku ūṟṟuppulaṉkaḷ150 iṉṟiyami yāta maṟṟoru mūlam ākiṉṟatu. tēvip paṭuṅkāl, ivi pārvip pulaṉukkup patilākat tiṟaṉuḷḷa uṟuppu kaḷākavum amikiṉṟaṉa. kuruṭar kaṟkum muṟiyiṉi uṟṟu nōkkiṉāl ivvuṇmi pulaṉākum. ārāycciyāḷarkaḷ ūṟṟuppulaṉi nāṉkākap pirittuḷḷaṉar. avi: toṭupulaṉ (amukkam), cuṭupulaṉ, kuḷirpulaṉ, noppulaṉ (vali) eṉpavi. ivikaḷukkup pukuvāykaḷ palavakiyāka amintu kiṭak kiṉṟaṉa. ivi orē pulaṉāka viḷaṅkiṉāl, nōyiṉāl ivaṟṟuḷ yātēṉum oru pulaṉ kēṭuṟumpōtu maṟṟiya pulaṉkaḷum kēṭuṟutal vēṇṭum. āṉāl, naṭimuṟiyil avvāṟilli. nōyāḷikaḷiṭiyē ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu keṭiṉum maṟṟiya pulaṉkaḷ keṭātu viḷaṅkak kāṇkiṉṟōm. eṉavē, ivi vēṟu vēṟu pulaṉkaḷ eṉpatu teḷivākiṉṟatu. nam tōliṉ cila iṭaṅkaḷ veppatti maṭṭilum aṟiyum; cila kuḷiri maṭṭum aṟiyum; cila nōtali maṭṭum aṟiyum.
ūṟṟuppulaṉiṉ pukuvāykaḷil cila tōliṉ puṟattilēyē uḷḷaṉa; cila uḷtōlil uḷḷaṉa. kuḷiri virivil aṟikiṉ ṟōm; veppattic ciṟitu kālan tāḻttē aṟikiṉṟōm; ātaliṉ kuḷirpulaṉiṉ pukuvāy tōliṉ mēlum, cuṭupulaṉiṉ pukuvāy tōliṉ uḷḷum iruppatu teḷivākiṉṟatu. kuḷirpulaṉ pōlat toṭupulaṉum mīntōṟpulaṉēyākum. cuṭupulaṉ pōla uṟuttup pulaṉum uḷpōla pulaṉām. ip pulaṉkaḷiṉ pukuvāykaḷ uṭaliṉ puṟattē muḻutum paraviyuḷḷaṉa. mayirk kālkaḷtōṟum toṭupulaṉiṉ pukuvāy uṇṭu. āṉāl, avvuṟuppukaḷ veppamum kuḷirum aṟivatāka ēṟpaṭavilli. tōlil paraviyuḷḷa pukuvāykaḷiṉ amippip paṭattil (paṭam-18) kāṇka.
toṭupulaṉ: itaṉ vaḻiyāka vaḻuvaḻuppu, cuṟacuṟappu, tivaral, (taṭavutal), īram oṭṭutal mutaliya vēṟṟumiyi aṟikiṉṟōm. ūṟal eṭuttal, cuḷḷiṭutal (vayapēṭana), kilukilut tal (viumaṭana) mutaliyaṉavum toṭupulaṉiṉ vakiyēyākum. toṭupulaṉ kaṭpulaṉ pōlap palavakip paṭātu eṉṟālum ciṟappuṭiyatēyākum. nammic cuṟṟilum uḷḷa poruḷkaḷip paṟṟi ataṉ vāyilākavē uṇarkiṉṟōm. iṭavuṇarvu intat toṭupulaṉāltāṉ ēṟpaṭu kiṉṟatu.
cuṭu pulaṉ: kotinīriṉ veppattiṉ uṟippi 100 pirivukaḷākap pirikkiṉṟaṉar. ovvoṉṟiyum ovvoru cuḻi  (ṉunapacanana) eṉṟē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. ivvāṟu pirittup pala vakiyāṉa cūṭṭu nilikaḷi aḷantaṟikiṉṟaṉar. nam uṭaliṉ veppattiṟkumēl arikkāṟ cuḻi (1/8u) aḷavu cūṭumikiṉum kuṟiyiṉum, cuṭupulaṉum kuḷir pulaṉum anta vēṟṟumiyi aṟikiṉṟaṉa. arikkāṟ cuḻikkum kuṟinta vēṟṟumikaḷ pulaṉāvatilli. tōliṉmītuḷḷa veppap pukuvāykaḷ151 oru kuṟippiṭṭa vīccaḷaviṟkē tulaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. veppam allatu kuḷircciyit tūṇṭum ittūṇṭalkaḷiṉ motta vīccaḷavu-10ukkum 70ukkum iṭiyē amintu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. inta veppanilikaḷukku mēlō kīḻō nām peṟum tulaṅkal valiyuṇarcciyē; appoḻutu iḻiyaṅkaḷiṉ152 citivu nēriṭukiṉṟatu. kuḷirpulaṉum cuṭupulaṉum toṭupulaṉ aḷavukkuc ciṟantaṉa alla eṉṟālum, avi nam tuyttuṇarvip palavakic cuviyākkuvataṟkup payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
noppulaṉ: kiṭṭattaṭṭa uṭaliṉ ellāp pakutikaḷiṉ ellāvita iḻiyaṅ kaḷuṭaṉ ip pulaṉ uṇarcciyi eḻupputal kūṭum. tōliṉmītu noppulap pukuvāykaḷ atikamāka uḷḷaṉa. tōliṉ arukē muṭiyum narampukaḷi nērē tīṅku viḷivikkak kūṭiya epporuḷum (tūṇṭalkaḷ) tākkum poḻutu nōvu eṭukkiṉṟatu. ik kiḷarcci mūḷiyiluḷḷa nōvelliyil ceṉṟu muṭikiṉṟatu. nōvāṉatu taṉiyāka eḻuvatilli; vēṟu pulaṉkaḷōṭu cērntē eḻukiṉṟatu. mikavum cuṭṭālum, mikavum kuḷirntālum, mikavum uṟuttiṉālum nōveṭukkiṉṟatu. cuṭupulappukuvāy mutaliyavaṟṟil tūṇṭal tākkumpoḻutu ap pukuvāykaḷ koḷḷa muṭiyātu mikunta kiḷarcci cuṟṟilum paravit taṉiyē kiṭakkum narampukaḷit tākkukiṉṟatu; ataṉāl nōveṭukkiṉṟatu. ākavē, nōvukaḷ avi eḻum iṭattālum, nīṭikkum kāla aḷavālum, paravum iṭa aḷavālum, uṭaṉ eḻum pulaṉkaḷālum, vēṟupaṭṭup pala peyarkaḷip peṟu kiṉṟaṉa. kīṟutal kuttal, veṭṭu, kaṭi, puṇ eṉpaṉa toṭupula vakiyāl peyar peṟṟa nōvukaḷ. cuṭupuṇ eṉpatu cuṭupula vakiyāl peyar peṟṟa nōvu. vayiṟṟu nōy, kuṭarnōy eṉpaṉa avi eḻum iṭavakiyāl peyar peṟṟaṉa. cuḷukku, taciyiṟukkam eṉpaṉa tacippulaṉ vakiyāl peyar peṟṟaṉa.
ip pulaṉ tuṉpamākavē viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. itaṉ ciṟappiyalpukaḷip pirittaṟivatu arumiyiṉum arumi. kaṇṇōykkum tōlmēl uḷḷa puṇṇōykkum vēṟṟumi tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. āṉāl, avvēṟṟumi noppula vēṟṟumi aṉṟu; ataṉōṭeḻum toṭuppulaṉ tacippulaṉ ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟṟumiyākum. curīleṉak kuttum nōviṟkum parantu maḻuṅkalāṉa nōviṟkum uḷḷa vēṟṟumiyum noppula vēṟṟumiyaṉṟu. kuttal eṭukkum nōvil cila narampukaḷil nōvu orumukappaṭukiṉṟatu; paranta nōvil narampukaḷilum kiḷarcci paravukiṉṟatu. tuṭikkum nōviṟkum nīṇṭanāḷāyt toṭarntu varum nōviṟkum uḷḷa vēṟṟumi kālattāl eḻunta vēṟṟumiyākum. mīttōlmēl eḻum nōvukaḷō kaṇṇellām miṉṉip pōvatupōla nōkiṉṟaṉa uḷḷuṟuppil eḻuvaṉavō, eṅkō tolivil eḻuvaṉa pōla maṅkalākat tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa.
nōvu maṉattiṟkup piṭikkamāṟ pōyiṉum, maṟṟiya pulaṉkaḷippōl perum payaṉit taruvatoṉṟēyākum. oruvakiyil nōkkumiṭattu in noppulaṉ avaṟṟiṉum ciṟntatoṉṟu eṉalām. namakku nēriṭum tīṅkiṉi muṉṉatāka aṟivittu at tīṅkiṉip pōkkik koḷḷa nammi ūkkuvatu in noppulaṉ allavā? uyarnili aṭinta uyirikaḷiṭiyē innoppulaṉ taliciṟantu viḷaṅku kiṉṟatu.
iyakkap pulaṉkaḷ153
nammuṭiya uṭaliṉ palvēṟu uṟuppukaḷiṉ cila iyakkaṅkaḷ catā naṭipeṟṟuk koṇṭiruppiṉum avviyakkaṅkaḷi nām aṟivatilli. inta uṇarvukaḷ atimākat tiraṇṭum paraviya niliyilum kāṇappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. uṭalil palvēṟu vaki iyakkap pukuvāykaḷ uḷḷaṉa. perumpālāṉavāya iyakkap pukuvāykaḷ vaṭikkatir vaṭivāka uḷḷaṉa. ivi tacikaḷilum eṉpup pūṭṭukaḷilum amintuḷḷaṉa. cila iyakkap pukuvāykaḷ kumiḻvaṭivāṉa amippukaḷ; avi palvēṟu tacinārkaḷilum154 eṉpup pūṭṭukaḷic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷilum amintuḷḷaṉa. iyakkap pukuvāykaḷ tūṇṭap peṟuṅkāl, naramputtuṭippukaḷ mutuku naṭunarampu155 vaḻiyākac ciṟumūḷikkum perumūḷiyiṉ puṟaṇikkum156 kaṭattap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. cātāraṇamāka, vaḷarntavar oruvar tammiṭam amintuḷḷa iyakkap pukuvāykaḷiṉ tiṟami kāraṇamākavum ciṟumūḷi cariyāka iyaṅkuvataṉ kāraṇamākavum mika nērttiyāṉa poruttappāṭukaḷi ēṟpaṭutta muṭikiṉṟatu. taṭṭaccuppoṟiyiṉi157 iyakkuvōrum piṉṉal vēli ceyvōrum, vīṇi allatu yāḻiṉi miḻaṟṟuvōrum mika nuṭpamāṉa acivukaḷic ceyvataṟkuk kāraṇam avarkaḷiṭam iyakkap pukuvāykaḷ ciṟanta muṟiyil amintiruppatēyākum. nām cātāraṇamāka ūṟṟuppulaṉ eṉṟu kūṟuvatil iyakkap pulaṉum toṭuppulaṉum amintuḷḷaṉa. paṭṭiṉit taṭavum pōtum upputtāḷit taṭavumpōtum uṇṭākum uṇarcci vēṟṟumi amukkattāl maṭṭilumalla, iyakkattālum uṇṭāvatākum. toḻil mūlam kalvipukaṭṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟum ikkālattil ippulaṉkaḷukku mukkiyattuvamuṇṭu.
nilippulaṉ
nām niliyāy iruppataṉiyum, iyaṅkuvataṉiyum, nilipiṟaḻ vataṉiyum aṟivataṟku vāyilāka iruppataṉi nilippulaṉ158 eṉṟu vaḻaṅ kalām. mikac cikkalāṉa ivvulaka vāḻvil ciṟappāka ōṭumpōtu allatu viḷiyāṭumpōtu, nām toṭarntāṟpōl nērttiyāṉa poruttap pāṭukaḷic ceytavaṇṇam irukkiṉṟōm. ittakiya poruttap pāṭu kaḷip purivataṟkuc ciṟu mūḷiyē mikavum poṟup puḷḷatāka amikiṉṟatu. itaṟkēṟṟa pulappoṟi eṅku uḷḷatu? uṭ ceviyil purimuṭiyiṉi aṭuttu mūṉṟu piṟi vaṭivamāṉa vaḷiyaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa eṉak kaṇṭōm aṉṟō? aviyē nilippoṟiyākum. avitām oṉṟuk koṉṟu nēr kuṟukkāka amintu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. oṉṟu nīḷaniliyilum, oṉṟu akalaniliyilum oṉṟu uyaraniliyilumāka niṉṟu avi mūṉṟu iṭaniliyiyum kuṟittu varukiṉṟaṉa. im mūṉṟu piṟi vaḷiyaṅkaḷum muruntu elumpiṉāl ākiya kuḻikaḷākum. ivaṟṟil niṟiya oru vakip pāymam159 taḷumpukiṉṟatu. nām talimik kōṇalāka iyakkumpōtu iv vaḷiyaṅkaḷil uḷḷa pāymam acikiṉṟatu, av vacivāl vaḷiyattil oru mūliyil pāymam pāyntu mikukiṉṟatu. uyaram, nīḷam, akalam eṉṟa mūṉṟu nilikaḷuk kēṟṟavāṟu av vavvaḷiyaṅkaḷil nīr acikiṉṟatu. avaṟṟiṉ oru muṉiyil pāymac ceṟivum, maṟṟoru muṉiyil pāymak kuṟivum uṇṭāvatāl nili māṟutali uṇarkiṉṟōm.
palanāḷ variyilum iv vaḷiyaṅkaḷic cevip pulaṉukkup payaṉpaṭuvaṉa eṉṟē palar karutiṉar. cevip pulaṉukkum ivaṟṟiṟkum ōriyipum illi eṉpatu ippōtu teḷivākiviṭṭatu. iviyē nilippulaṉippaṟṟiya poṟi eṉpatum uṟutiyākiyatu. niliniṟpataṉiyum nili māṟṟat tiyum uyirkaḷ aṟintālaṉṟi avi iyaṅka iyalātu. ātaliṉ innilippulaṉ iṉṟiyamiyāc ciṟappuṭiyatākum. uyirikaḷiṭipē mutalil tōṉṟiyatu ip pulaṉē; mīṉāyk kaṭalil vāḻnta kālattil tōṉṟiyatu atu. tariyil vāḻavantapōtu kāṟṟiṉ nili māṟṟaṅkaḷi aṟiya vēṇṭiya tāyiṟṟu. cevippoṟi muṟṟi vaḷarntatu. inta nilippulaṉum iyakkappulaṉum uḷḷamiyāltāṉ naṭattal, iyaṅkutal mutaliyaṉa paḻakkamākiṉṟaṉa.
uṭpulaṉ
paci, nīrviṭāy, vānti, pāl-uṇarvukaḷ pōṉṟa pulaṉu ṇarccikaḷ yāvum uṭpulaṉkaḷ160 eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. ivi yāvum uṭaliṉ uṭpakutikaḷil eḻukiṉṟaṉa. ippoṟi yuṇarccikaḷ yāvum uṭaliṉ aṭippaṭiyāṉa tēvikaḷuṭaṉ mika neruṅkiya uṟavu koṇṭuḷḷaṉa; ākavē, ivi ceyaluk kuriya uḷtūṇṭalkaḷ161 allatu untu nilikaḷi162 uṇ ṭākkukiṉṟaṉa, ciṟappāka ivi uyiriyiṉ nalattiyum ataṉ kuruti naṉṉiliyil iruttaliyum nili niṟuttuvatil paṅku peṟukiṉṟaṉa. cila camayam iyakkappulaṉ, nilippulaṉ, ūṟṟup pulaṉ ākiyavaṟṟuṭaṉ uṭpulaṉ cērntu uṭaṟpulaṉkaḷ163 eṉavum vaḻaṅkappeṟukiṉṟatu.
pulappayiṟci
pulaṉkaḷē aṟiviṉ vāyilkaḷ eṉpati nām aṟivōm. ātaliṉ, kuḻantik kalviyil pulappayiṟcikkuc ciṟappiṭam aḷikkap peṟṟuḷḷatu.
pulaṉkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttip poruḷkaḷi aṟiyāviṭil, kuḻantikaḷ tam eṇṇaṅkaḷukkum cintaṉikaḷukkum takunta aṭippaṭiyiṉṟi, pala tavaṟāṉa karuttukaḷiyuṭiyavarāvar. nāṭṭuppuṟac cūḻniliyē aṟiyāta nakarppuṟac ciṟuvarkaḷil nel kāyci maram eṉpōrum uṇṭu. ētāvatu oru poruḷi (e-ṭu-pantu) oru kuḻantiyiṭam koṭuttāl, aḵtu iyalpākavē atippārkkiṟatu; ōcippaṭuttukiṉṟatu; toṭṭuttaṭavukiṟatu; amukku kiṟatu; kaṭittuc cuviyaṟikiṟatu; mukarntu maṇam aṟikiṟatu; uṭikkavum muyalkiṟatu. atu ellāp pulaṉkaḷiyum payaṉpaṭuttip poruḷiṉ taṉmiyi aṟiya muyalkiṉṟatu. paḷḷikku varumuṉ ciṟuvaṉ kaṟkum muṟiyum ituvē. paḷḷikku vantavuṭaṉum avaṉ immuṟi yiyē mēṟkoḷvāṉ eṉpati maṟakkalākātu. iṅṅaṉam pulaṉkaḷiṉ payiṟciyē iyaṟkiyaṉṉiyiṉ kalvimuṟi eṉpati māṇṭicōri ḵpirāpel pōṉṟa kalvi vallunarkaḷ naṉku aṟintu pulappayiṟcikku mutaliṭam aḷittuḷḷaṉar. māṇṭicōrik kalvi muṟi pulap payiṟciyi vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟatu. paḷḷiyil eḻutavum paṭikkavum maṭṭil vāyppukaḷ koṭuttal pōtātu. coṟkaḷukku muṉ poruḷkaḷ eṉṟa koḷki kuḻantik kalviyiṉ uyirnāṭiyāka iruttal vēṇṭum.
pulap payiṟciyiṉ nōkkam: pulappayiṟciyāl pulaṉkaḷiṉ tiṟamiyi atikarikkak kūṭumā? eṉṟa viṉā eḻutal kūṭuma. iyaṟkiyākak kuḻanti yiṭam ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷa tiṟamiyi, aḵtāvatu poṟikaḷiṉ kūrmiyi, atikarikka muṭiyātutāṉ; eṉiṉum, poṟikaḷāl aṟintu koḷḷakkūṭiya āṟṟalip payiṟci yiṉāl vaḷarkkalām; kāṭcikaḷi vēṟu pirittu aṟintu koḷḷum tiṟaṉi atikarikkavum ceyyalām. iyaṟkiyil ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷa tiṟamikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttum vāyppukaḷ ellāp pulaṉkaḷukkum kiṭippatilli. ākavē, palatiṟappaṭṭa payiṟcikaḷiṉ mūlam ellāp poṟikaḷukkum cuṟucuṟuppākac ceyalāṟṟum vāyppukaḷ tarutal vēṇṭum. uṇmiyil, pulappayiṟci eṉpatu pulattiṉ vāyilāka maṉattiṟku aḷikkap peṟum payiṟciyē eṉpatu teḷivākum. kaṇṇiyō cevi yiyō paḻakkuvateṉpatu uṇmiyil maṉattip paḻakku vatākavē koḷḷa vēṇṭum. takka pulap payiṟciyiṉāl mūḷiyiṉ miyaṅkaḷ iyipu peṟṟu maṉamum paṇpaṭi kiṉṟatu. mūṉṟu vayatiliruntu ēḻu vayatu vari kuḻantikaṭkup pulappayiṟci aḷippataṟkuriya kālam eṉṟu uḷaviyal aṟiñarkaḷ kaṇṭaṟintuḷḷaṉar. māṇṭicōri muṟiyil pulappayiṟcikku vēṇṭiya cātaṉaṅkaḷ aḻakāka amintuḷḷaṉa. pulap payiṟciyuṭaṉ moḻiyaṟiviyum toṭarpupaṭuttappeṟṟuḷḷa ammiyāriṉ pulappayiṟcit tiṭṭam ciṟantu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu.
toṭakkanili, uyarnilip paḷḷikaḷilum poruḷkaḷip pulaṉkaḷ vāyilāka aṟiyum vāyppukaḷik koṭukkalām. poruṭkāṭciccāli, pūṅkā, kaṭaṟkari, mirukak kāṭcic cāli, malikaḷ, toḻiṟcālikaḷ mutaliya iṭaṅkaṭkuk kuḻantikaḷi aḻittuc ceṉṟu poruḷkaḷi nērāka aṟiyum vāyppukaḷ aḷikkalām. kittoḻilkaḷ mūlam aḷikkappeṟum kalvi ōraḷavu itaṟkut tuṇi ceykiṉṟatu. uruvaṅkaḷ varitalum, amittalum, pāykaḷ piṉṉutalum, mikkūṭu, kappal, vimāṉam, kuruvi pōṉṟavikaḷik kākitaṅ kaḷāl ceytalum, kaḷimaṇ vēlikaḷum, nūṟṟal, neytal pōṉṟa toḻilkaḷum pulattiṉ iyakkattiṟkut tuṇi ceypavi. ivvāṟu kāṭcip poruḷkaḷi uṇartal karuttup poruḷit teḷivuṭaṉ aṟivataṟku vāyppu aḷip patākum. vakuppaṟiyil kaṟpikkumpoḻutu, paṭaṅkaḷ, tuṇik karuvikaḷ, karumpalaki mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ tuṇikoṇṭu palapulaṉ kaḷukku muṟiyīṭu ceyyum vāyppu aḷikkalām.
uṟṟu nōkkal
aṟivu pulaṉkaḷiṉ vāyilākavē ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu eṉpati mēlē kaṇṭōm. eṉavē, mutaṉmik karuttukkaḷ yāvum poruḷkaḷiṉ nērttoṭarpu mūlamē uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa. aḵtāvatu, pulaṉkaḷiṉ mūlamē nām aṟiviṉip peṟukiṉ ṟōm. pulappayiṟci uṟṟunōkkalik kuḻantikaḷiṭam uṇṭākku vatiyē nōkkamāka uṭiyatu. uṟṟu nōkkal eṉpatu eṉṉa? oru poruḷik kūrntu nōkki ataṉ pakutikaḷiyum vivaraṅ kaḷiyum kuṟippākak kavaṉittalē uṟṟu nōkkal ākum; oru poruḷi nōkkamaṟṟup pārttal uṟṟu nōkkal ākātu. oru poruḷukkum atupōṉṟa piṟaporuḷukkumuḷḷa oṟṟumi vēṟṟumikaḷi ārāyntu pārttāltāṉ uṟṟu nōk kalākak koḷḷap peṟum.
uṟṟunōkkal eṉṉa eṉpatic cila eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ teḷivu paṭuttum. nām poruḷkaḷ viṟkum cantikkuc cellukiṟōm. aṅkup palvēṟu kaṭikaḷik kāṇkiṟōm; ivvāṟu kāṇpatu uṟṟu nōkkal ākātu. karuṇikkiḻaṅku vāṅka vēṇṭum eṉṟāl ati niṉivil vittuk koṇṭu, tēṅkāyk kaṭikaḷ, tāṉiyak kaṭikaḷ, paḻakkaṭikaḷ, ilik kaṭikaḷ ivaṟṟiyellām otukkiviṭṭu, kāykaṟikkaṭikaḷi aṭintu avaṟṟilum kiḻaṅku vakikaḷ viṟkum iṭattiṟkuc ceṉṟu namakku vēṇṭiya karuṇikkiḻaṅku uḷḷatā eṉṟu ūṉṟik kavaṉittalē uṟṟu nōkkal ākum. uṟṟu nōkkalil oru nōkkam irukkum; pulaṉkaḷum maṉamum iyintu oru mukappaṭṭuc ceyalpuriyum; tiṭṭamākavum oru toṭarpuṭaṉum avi toḻiṟpaṭum.
uṟṟunōkkil payiṟci taruvatu taṟkālak kalviyiṉ mukkiya nōkkam ākum. ivviṭattil nām mukkiyamāka maṉattilirutta vēṇṭiyatu eṉṉa veṉil, uṟṟu nōkkal eṉpatu, oruvita taṉi maṉavaṉmi alla. aṅṅaṉam aḵtu oru taṉi maṉavaṉmiyāka iruppiṉ ētāvatu oru cilavaṟṟil uṟṟunōkkal payiṟciyaḷippiṉ, piṟavaṟṟilum attiṟaṉ kivarappeṟum, āṉāl, uṇmiyil oru tuṟiyil tiṟamiyāka uṟṟunōkkupavarkaḷ piṟavaṟṟil tiṟamiyāka atic ceyyamuṭivatilli. (e-ṭu). kūlavāṇikattil tiṟami yākap poruḷkaḷi uṟṟunōkkupavariṭam viraṅkaḷi uṟṟu nōkkum tiṟaṉum irukkum eṉṟu colla muṭiyātu. eṉavē, uṟṟu nōkkalil māṇākkarkaḷukkup payiṟci aḷikka vēṇṭu māyiṉ avarkaṭkup palatuṟikaḷil kavarccikaḷi eḻupputal vēṇṭum. aḵtuṭaṉ vevvēṟu tuṟikaḷilum kūṭiyamaṭṭil nēraṭiyāṉa anupavaṅkaḷi ēṟpaṭum vāyppukaḷ tarutal vēṇṭum. mēlum, ittuṟikaḷ palavaṟṟilum palavitat takavalkaḷ terintiruttal vēṇṭum. iti niṟivēṟṟutaṟ poruṭṭē iṉṟu paḷḷikaḷil aṭikkaṭic cuṟṟulākkaḷ164 mēṟkoḷḷap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. nakarppuṟa māṇākkarkaḷ nāṭṭup puṟattiṟkum, nāṭṭuppuṟa māṇākkarkaḷ nakarppuṟattiṟkum iruvarum varalāṟṟup pukaḻpeṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷ, malikaḷ, āṟukaḷ, aruvikaḷ, aṇittēkkaṅkaḷ, miṉṉāṟṟal niliyaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷukkum ceṉṟu palavaṟṟi uṟṟunōkki aṟital peritum payaṉ viḷivikkum. cuṟṟulāviṉnōkkam muṉṉarē urikkap poṟṟu, cuṟṟulāviṉ poḻutu pārkka vēṇṭiyavaṟṟi viḷakki, māṇākkarkaḷik kuṟippukaḷ eṭukkac ceytu, cuṟṟulā muṭintapiṉṉar kaṇṭum kēṭṭum aṟintavaṟṟi āciriyarkaḷ māṇākkarkaḷuṭaṉ kuṟippu kaḷuṭaṉ kalantu pēci, niṉippūṭṭi, aṉittiyum tiraṭṭi niralpaṭak kaṭṭuriyākavō, coṟpōr mūlamākavō, viṉāviṭi mūlamākavō veḷippaṭac ceytāl cuṟṟu lāviṉ muḻuppayaṉiyum naṉku aṭiyalām.



iyal-3
kuḻantikaḷiṉ vaḷarcciyum tulakkamum

kuḻantikaḷi naṉmuṟiyil payiṟṟa vēṇaṭumāyiṉ, avi vaḷarum palvēṟu kōlaṅkaḷiyum, avvaḷarccikku ātaravu tarum muṟikaḷuḷ ententa muṟikaḷil tuṇi puriyalām eṉpatiyum nām aṟintu koḷḷutal vēṇṭum. potuvāka vaḷarccipaṟṟiya potu vitikaḷi aṟintu koṇṭāl maṭṭilum pōtātu; ovvoru kuḻantiyiṉ vaḷarcciyiyum taṉittaṉiyākavum kavaṉittal vēṇṭum
cātāraṇamāka, nam vāḻnāḷil mūṉṟil oru paṅku vaḷarcciyil celavākiṉṟatu eṉpati nām aṟital nalam. uṭal vaḷarcciyippaṟṟiya variyilum ivvuṇmiyi yāvarum oppukkoḷvar. āṉāl iyakkam, aṟivu, uḷḷak kiḷarcci, camūkap paṇpu, āḷumi pōṉṟaviyum vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu avvaḷavu veḷippaṭiyākap pulaṉāvatilli yātaliṉ avaṟṟi eḷitāka aṟintu koḷḷa iyaluvatilli. ivaṟṟiṉ vaḷarccippōkku kōlaṅkaḷāka amikiṉṟatu. aḵtāvatu, ivi paṭippaṭiyākap paṇpāṭaṭikiṉṟaṉa.
ovvoru maṉitaṉukkum taṉip paṇpukaḷ uḷa eṉpati ōraḷavu nām aṟivōm. ip paṇpukaḷtām oruvari virumpat takkavarākavum veṟukkattakkavarākavum ceykiṉṟaṉa eṉpatiyum aṟivōm. iti nām āḷumi1 eṉak karutalām. uṇmi atuvaṉṟu; āḷumi eṉpatu mikavum cikkalāṉatu. oru maṉitaṉuṭiya naṭatti, vaḻakkam, maṉappōkku, eṇṇaṅkaḷ, nōkkaṅkaḷ, karuttukaḷ, nampikkiḷ, maṉappāṉmikaḷ mutaliyavi aṉittum kalantu niṟkum maṉappāṅkē āḷumi eṉpatu. aḵtu avaṟṟiṉ veṟuñ cērkki maṭṭum aṉṟu; accērkkiyil orumippāṭu uṇṭu. oru makaṉō, makaḷō āḷāka viḷaṅkum taṉmiyē āḷumiyākum. uyirp poruḷ, uyiril poruḷ eṉṟavaṟṟiṭam kāṇappeṟum taṉmiyik kuṟikka iyalpu eṉṟa col vaḻaṅkappeṟutaliṉ īṇṭu nām kuṟikkum taṉmiyi yuṇartta āḷumi eṉṟa col mēṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
irāmaṉik kampaṉ āḷumip paṇpuk kūṟukaḷ2 aṉit tiyum koṇṭa oru perumakaṉākap paṭittuḷḷāṉ. anumaṉ mūlam cukkirīvaṉ irāma ilakkumaṇarkaḷiṉ ciṟappiyellām aṟikiṉṟāṉ. irāma ilakkumaṇar tolivil varum poḻutē avarkaḷiṉ puṟattōṟṟaṅkaḷil īṭupaṭṭu neṭitu nōkku’kiṉṟāṉ. nāṉmukaṉ paṭippuk kālantoṭṭu aṉṟuvari ceyta nalviṉikaḷ yāvum tiraṇṭu iru vaṭivaṅ kaḷāki viṭṭaṉa pōlum eṉṟu eṇṇukiṉṟāṉ. iṟutiyāka,
tēṟiṉaṉ amarark kellām
	tēvarām tēvar aṉṟē
māṟiyip piṟappil vantār
	māṉiṭa rāki maṉṉō;
āṟukoḷ caṭilat tāṉum
	ayaṉum eṉ(ṟu) ivarkaḷ āti
vēṟuḷa kuḻuvi yellām
	māṉuṭam veṉṟa taṉṟē. 3
eṉṟu oru muṭivukku varukiṉṟāṉ. āḷumiyiṉ paṇpuk kūṟukaḷ avarkaḷiṭam oṉṟēṉum kuṟiviṉṟi yiruntamiyāl cukkirīvaṉ avarkaḷip puruṭōttamaṉatu avatāramē eṉṟu karutukiṉṟāṉ. iṅṅaṉam irāma ilakkumaṇarkaḷ āḷumiyiṉ paṇpuk kūṟukaḷ niṟintavarkaḷākak kāṭciyaḷikkiṉṟaṉar
camūkattil kalantu paḻakumpoḻututāṉ, nām mēṟkūṟiya iv āḷumip paṇpukaḷik kāṇa muṭikiṉṟatu. nām oruvarik kuṟippiṭuṅkāl ottup pōkum iyalpuṭiyavar eṉkiṉ ṟōm. ivvaḷavu maṭṭilum coṉṉāl pōtātu a eṉpavarum ā eṉpavarum ottup pōkiṟārkaḷ eṉpatāl a eṉpavarum, i eṉpavarum ottuppōvar eṉpatilli. ottuppōtal eṉpatu iruvariṭiyē ēṟpaṭum ōr uṟavumuṟi, iḵtu āḷumikaḷ iṭiviṉi iyaṟṟuvatip poṟuttatu. ivvulakil naṭi peṟum makiḻvāṉa tirumaṇaṅkaḷiyum makiḻ vaṟṟa tirumaṇaṅkaḷiyum ārāyntu entap paṇpukkūṟukaḷ makiḻccikku allatu makiḻcciyiṉmikkuk kāraṇam eṉpati aṟiya muṉintāl, oru muṭiviṟkum varamuṭiyātu. palvēṟu vitamāṉa makkaḷ makiḻcciyuṭaṉum makiḻcciyillāmalum tirumaṇam purintukoḷvatittāṉ kāṇkiṟōm; cila nalliṇakkamāṉa paṇpuṭi yavarkaḷ makiḻcciyillāmalum, appaṇpu amiyap peṟātavarkaḷ maṉaniṟi vuṭaṉum vāḻvatip pārkkiṉṟōm. valuvanta māṇavarkaḷum. viṭṭuk koṭuppōrum, eḷitil ciṉaṅkoḷvōrum cuṟaṇiyaṟṟōrum, niliyāṉavarum niliyaṟṟavarum-maṇa vāḻkkiyil oṉṟi makiḻvuṭaṉ irukkiṉṟaṉar; aṉṟi, makiḻcciyaṟṟavar kaḷākavum uḷḷaṉar. itaṟkuk kāraṇam āḷumikaḷ mōtu talip poṟuttatu eṉṟutāṉ colla vēṇṭum, ōraḷavu piṟa kāraṇaṅkaḷum irukkalām.
paḷḷiyil payilum māṇākkarkaḷiṭiyē āḷumi vēṟṟumikaḷik kāṇalām. ōraḷavu kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷ aminta vakuppaṟiyilum ivaṟṟip pārkkalām. cilar tiṭṭamāṉa karuttuṭiyavarkaḷākavum cilar vāy tiṟakkak kūṭa añcuparvakaḷākavum, cilar piṭivāta muṭiyavarkaḷākavum, cilar taṉṉam pikkiyaṟṟavarkaḷākavum ivvāṟu palvēṟu vitamāy irukkak kāṇalām.
vakuppaṟikku veḷiyē, ōraḷavu kaṭṭuppāṭaṟṟa niliyil inta āḷumiyiṉ kōlaṅkaḷ iṉṉum teḷivākap pulaṉākum. aṅku māṇākkarkaḷiṉ iyaṟki nili veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu. oruvaṉiṭam taṉ ṉeṭuppu mītūrntu niṟkiṉṟatu: avaṉ pēcciṉāl piṟari āḷamuyaṉ kiṉṟāṉ. iṉṉoruvaṉ eḷitākap piṟaruṭaṉ ottup pōkiṉṟāṉ; viṭṭuk koṭukkiṉṟāṉ. intap paṇpukaḷ ellā nilikaḷilum muṟṟilum veḷip paṭuvatilli. iṟumāppuṭiyavaṉ eṉṟu palarālum karutap peṟum oruvaṉuṭaṉ neruṅkip paḻakumpoḻutu avaṉ aṉpum ārvamum uṭiyavaṉāka iruttal terikiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam ovvoruvaṉum palavakiyāṉa cūḻnilikaḷil eṅṅaṉam tulaṅkukiṉṟāṉ eṉpatik koṇṭē avaṉuṭiya āḷumiyi aṟital kūṭum.
āḷumiyil pala kūṟukaḷ amintuḷḷaṉa eṉpati mēlē kaṇṭōm. āṉāl, camūkak kūṟukaḷ taṉic ciṟap puṭiyavi. cila uḷaviyalār makkaḷi akamukar4 eṉṟum puṟamukar5 eṉṟum iru kūṟiṭṭup pēcuvar. yuṅ6 pōṉṟa uḷaviyal aṟiñarkaḷ ivarkaḷuṭiya paṇpukaḷi ārāyntu kuṟittu vittuḷḷaṉar. akamukar eṉpōr uḷnōkkiyavar: catā kaṉavu kaṇṭu koṇṭiruppar; camūkat toṭarpukku añcuvar; ivarkaḷ eḷitil oru muṭivukku vārār. puṟamukar eṉpōr tam uṇrccikaḷi eḷitāka veḷi yiṭuvar; puṟamukar eṉpōr tam uṇarccikaḷi eḷitāka veḷiyiṭuvar; nikaḻkāla vāḻvil karuttuṭi yavarkaḷ: kikkuk kiṭikkak kūṭiyavaṟṟil nāṭṭam celuttupavarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ eḷitil muṭivukku varuvar; camūkattilum naṉṟākap paḻakuvar. āṉāl, ulakiluḷḷa perumpālāṉa makkaḷ akamukamallar. avarkaḷi irumukar7 eṉpatē poruttamuṭiya tākum.
anupavattil āḷumi: ivvulakil uṭali aṭip paṭiyākak koṇṭu makkaḷic cilar taramiṭuvar. kuṭṭi, neṭṭi, parumaṉ, melivu pōṉṟa uṭalamippukkut takkavāṟu makkaḷiṉ maṉappaṇpu amintirukkum eṉṟu karutuvar. melivāka uḷḷavar cūḻccit tiṟam vāyntavar eṉṟum, uṭal paruttiruppavar nampat takuntavar eṉṟum āṅkila nāṭaka āciriyar cekappiriyar kūṟip pōntati āṅkila ilakkiyam paṭittōr aṟivar. cila uḷaviyalārum aṅṅaṉamē karutukiṉṟaṉar. etu eppaṭiyāyiṉum tūmpilāc curappikaḷiṉ8 āṭcikkut takkavāṟu makkaḷiṉ mīppaṇpu9 amintuḷḷatu eṉpatu maṭṭilum uṇmi.
āḷumiyi aḷattal: āḷumiyi aḷakkumpōtu mēṟkūṟiya paranta kūṟukaḷēyaṉṟi, taṉippaṭṭa maṉappāṉmik kūṟukaḷiyum kavaṉikkalām. ātikkam-paṇivu. viṭāmuyaṟci-muyaṟciyiṉmi pōṉṟa kūṟukaḷil taramiṭalām. oruvaruṭiya uṭalamippu, maṉanili, oḻukkam, eṇṇaṅkaḷ, maṉappōkku, mīppaṇpu, iyalpu, paṇpu, maṉappāṉmi, maṉappāṅku, nāppaḻakkam, maṉappaḻakkam, kippaḻakkam, mutaliya aṉittum oruṅku tiraṇṭu ententa vakiyil iyipu peṟṟu viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa eṉpataṉik kāṇalvēṇṭum. ittakiya kūṟukaḷ aṉitti yum kāṭṭum paṭam āḷumip paṭam10 eṉappaṭum. ivaṟṟiṉi aḷappataṟku(1) taṉiyāḷ muṟi; (2)taramiṭal11; (3)peṉcil-tāḷ āḷumi aḷakkum utirikaḷ; (4) naṭattic cōtaṉikaḷ12; (5)kaṇṭu pēcal13 allatu pēṭṭi nilimic cōtaṉikaḷ14; (7) puṟattēṟṟu muṟikaḷ15 eṉṟa muṟikaḷi mēṟkoḷḷalām. ivaṟṟippaṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷi uḷanūlkaḷil kaṇṭu koḷka. oruvaruṭiya āḷumiyi aḷappataṟku kuḻavipparuvamē ciṟantatu eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ aṉivarum karutuvar.
āḷumi vaḷarccikkuk kuṭivaḻiyum cūḻniliyum aṭip paṭik kūṟukaḷākum. kuṭumpam, viḷiyāṭṭukkuḻu, uṟaviṉar, paḷḷit tōḻarkaḷ, caṅkam pōṉṟa camūka niliyaṅkaḷ yāvum āḷumi vaḷarcciyil paṅku peṟukiṉṟaṉa. vīṭṭil maṭṭukku miñciya celvam koṭuttal, māṇākkarkaḷip puṟakkaṇittal, uṭkuṟipāṭukaḷ mutaliya kāraṇaṅkaḷāl oruvaruṭiya āḷumi pātikkap peṟalām. āciriyar ittakiya paḻaṅkuṟikaḷik kaṇṭaṟital vēṇṭum. paḷḷiyil ittakiya nōyūṭṭum nilikaḷ ēṟpaṭātavāṟu kaṇkāṇikkavum vēṇṭum. ov voruvarukkum taṉittaṉi uḷḷak kiḷarcci etirviṉikaḷ uṇṭeṉṟum, avaṟṟi avaravarē kaṇṭaṟintu vāḻa vēṇṭum eṉṟum nām aṟital vēṇṭum. ituvē āḷumi vaḷarvataṟkuc ciṟanta vaḻiyākum.
oru kuḻantiyiṉ kalvi atu piṟantuṭaṉēyē toṭaṅki viṭukiṉṟatu. kuḻanti mūttōruṭaṉ koṇṭuḷḷa toṭarpu, avarkaḷ ataṉip pātukākkum vitam, kuḻanti kāṇum kāṭcikaḷ, kēṭkum olikaḷ, ivi pōṉṟa piṟatūṇṭalkaḷ ākiyavi kuḻantiyiṉ pāṭattiṭṭattil amintuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟit takka muṟikaḷil kiyāṇṭu kaṟpippatiṉālēyē piyaṉatu kuṇatti amikkum pala anupavaṅkaḷ uṇṭā kiṉṟaṉa. kuḻantiyiṉ tiṟamikaḷ mutirntāl avaṉatu kalvikkuriya ceyalkaḷum vāyppukaḷum atikarikkiṉṟaṉa. ivi atikap payaṉaḷikka vēṇṭumāyiṉ ellā nilikaḷilum kuḻantiyiṉ kalvi avaṉ vaḷarum muṟikaḷukkēṟpa amital vēṇṭum. iṉi, kuḻantiyiṉ vaḷarccipaṟṟiya ciṟappiyal pukaḷik16 kāṇpōm.
vaḷarcciyiṉ ciṟappiyalpukaḷ
kaṟṟaliyum kaṟpittaliyum cariyākap purintu koḷḷa vēṇṭumāyiṉ vaḷarcciyiṉ iyalpiṉit teḷivāka aṟintu koḷḷa vēṇṭum vaḷarcciyiṉ oru kuṟippiṭṭa kūṟukaḷip paṟṟiya ārāyccikaḷiyellām icciṟu nūlil curukki yurittal eṉpatu iyalāta ceyal. eṉiṉum, vaḷarccikku aṭippaṭiyāka irukkum oru cila ciṟappiyalpukaḷi āciriyarkaḷ aṟintu koḷḷutal cālap payaṉ tarum.
(1) ōr uyiriṉ vaḷarcci ataṉ kuṭivaḻi17 cūḻnili18 ākiya iraṇṭum iṭiyaṟātu iṭiviṉi19 iyaṟṟuvataṉ viḷiviṉāl ēṟpaṭuvatākum: kuṭivaḻi eṉpatu piṟaviyilēyē uyirikkuk kiṭitta iyaṟki āṟṟal20. cūḻnili eṉpatu paḷḷiyilum veḷiyilum kuḻantikkuk kiṭikkum anupavaṅkaḷ; āciriyar, peṟṟōr, maṟṟōr aḷikkum ūkkam. kuḻantikkuk kiṭikkum veṟṟi, tōlvikaḷākiyavi aṉittum aṭaṅkiyatoru nili. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟillāmal piṟitoṉṟāl maṭṭilum vaḷarcci ēṟpaṭa muṭiyātu. iḵtu eṉpōlavō eṉiṉ, vitiyum maṇṇum iṇaṅkuvatu pōla eṉka. vitiyiṉṟi maṇ, ceṭiyi uṇṭākka muṭiyātu; aṅṅaṉamē maṇṇiṉṟi viti vaḷarātu.
(2) vaḷarcci, aḷavilum paṇpilum naṭipeṟukiṉṟatu: vaḷarcciyil iru kūṟukaḷ aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa. oṉṟu, aḷavil (parumaṉilum maṉattilum) ēṟpaṭum mikutiyik kuṟippatu.(e-ṭu.) mūḷi peritākiṟatu; kaṉam peṟukiṟatu. aṅṅaṉamē tacikaḷum peritākik kaṉam peṟukiṉṟaṉa. maṟṟoṉṟu, taṉmi māṟu pāṭṭik21 kuṟippatu. (e-ṭu.). piṟakkum poḻutē kuḻantiyiṉ mūḷi pallāyiram uyiraṇukkaḷik koṇṭatu. ivi periya viyāka vaḷarkiṉṟatu. āyiṉum, ivvaṇukkaḷil cila vētiyiyal māṟṟaṅkaḷum22 nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa; narampuk kampikaḷ uṟikaḷāl mūṭap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. kuḻantiyiṉ cerimāṉap pātiyilum ivvita māṟṟaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. atu vaḷarvatuṭaṉ amippilum māṟukiṉṟatu. itaṉāltāṉ vaḷarccik kēṟṟapaṭi palvēṟuvita uṇavukaḷic cerikka muṭikiṉṟatu. inta iraṇṭā vatu kūṟu tulakkam23 eṉa vaḻaṅkappeṟum. kuḻanti vaḷara vaḷara, taliyiṉ vītam kuṟivatiyum, tacikaḷiṉ vītam atikarip patiyum kāṇalām. ivi yāvum tulakkattik kuṟippavi.
(3) vaḷarcci iṭiyaṟātu toṭarntu metuvāka naṭipeṟukiṉṟatu: ellāk kuḻantikaḷukkum oḻuṅkāṉa vaḷarccik kōlaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. (e-ṭu.) kuḻanti naṭappataṟku muṉ niṟkap paḻakukiṟatu; cariyākap pēcuvataṟkumuṉ maḻalic coṟkaḷip pēcukiṉṟatu. kecal eṉṟa aṟiñar itaṉi aḻakākak kūṟuvar: ovvoru kuḻaviyum niṟpataṟkumuṉ uṭkārap paḻakukiṉṟatu; pēcu vataṟkumuṉ maḻaliyāṭukiṉṟatu; uṇmiyurippataṟku muṉṉar poyyip puṉikiṉṟatu; caturam varivataṟku muṉṉar vaṭṭam varikiṉṟatu. potu nalap paṇpik kāṭṭuvataṟku muṉṉar taṉṉalap paṇpi veḷippaṭuttu kiṉṟatu; taṉṉi nampiyiruppataṟku muṉṉar piṟari nampiyē irukkiṉṟatu.” perumpālāṉa kuḻantikaḷiṭam vaḷarcciyiṉ inta milkaṟkaḷivaḷarccik kōlaṅkaḷi24niliyāṉa oḻuṅkil naṭipeṟṟu varuvatik kāṇalām.
(4) vaḷarcciyiṉ viṟuviṟuppu25 (vēkam) orē cīrāka iruppatilli: vaḷarcci toṭarntu naṭipeṟṟālum, ataṉ vēkam aṭikkaṭi māṟukiṉṟatu; viṟuviṟupput taṉmiyil māṟṟam kāṇappeṟukiṉṟatu. kuḻavip paruvat tilum26 paḷḷi celvataṟkut toṭakka niliyilum vaḷarcci virivāṉatu. paḷḷicelvataṟku iṟuti niliyilum paḷḷik kālattilum vaḷarcci metuvākap pōkiṉṟatu. tiṭīreṉṟu oru ceyalil uyarvu kāṇappeṟalām; piṉpu avvēkam kuṟintu viṭalām.itiliruntu vaḷarcci naṭipeṟavilli eṉṟu koḷḷutal tavaṟu. vaḷarcci nām kāṇa muṭiyāta niliyil naṭipeṟṟuk koṇṭutāṉ uḷatu.
(5) vaḷarccikkut tiṭṭamāṉa neṟikaḷum vakikaḷum uḷa: (I) kuḻantiyiṉ vaḷarcci taliyiliruntu toṭaṅki, kālil muṭivuṟukiṉṟatu. (e-ṭu.) kuḻanti uṭkāruvataṟku muṉṉar taliyi nimirttukiṉṟatu. (ii) vaḷarcci naṭuvil toṭaṅkik kaṭiyil muṭikiṉṟatu. nuṉiyiluḷḷa uṟuppukaḷ tulakka maṭivataṟku muṉṉar naṭu uṟuppukaḷ tulakkamuṟukiṉṟaṉa. (e-ṭu.) naṭunarampu maṇṭalam aminta piṉṉarē veḷi narampu maṇṭalam naṉkamikiṉṟatu. (iii) vaḷarcci mottac ceyalil27 toṭaṅkit taṉic ceyalil28 muṭikiṉṟatu. uṭalil entap pākattil tūṇṭal ēṟpaṭṭālum maṟṟa ellāp pākaṅkaḷum tulaṅkukiṉṟatu. (e-ṭu.) kālik kiḷḷiṉāl kuḻanti uṭal muḻuvatiyum acikkiṉṟatu. nāḷaṭivil acivukaḷ taṉip paṭṭut teḷivāka vēṟupaṭṭut tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. mutalil kiyacivum, nāḷaṭivil viral acivu nuṭpaṅkaḷum paṭip paṭiyākat tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa.

(6) vaḷarcciyiṉ palvēṟu kūṟukaḷ palvēṟu vītaṅkaḷil tulaṅku kiṉṟaṉa: vaḷarcciyiṉ palvēṟu kūṟukaḷ orē vēkattilum orē camayat tilum naṭipeṟuvatilli. (e-ṭu.) toṭakka āṇṭukaḷil narampu maṇṭalam vēkamākat tulakkamuṟukiṉṟatu. mutal aintu āṇṭukaḷil kuḻantikaḷ putiyavaṟṟik kaṟṟuk koḷvatupōl avaṟṟiṉ vāḻnāḷil entap paruvattilum kaṟṟuk koḷvatilli. pūppeytum paruvattil avarkaḷ piṟappuṟuppu maṇṭalam mika virivāka vaḷarcci peṟukiṉṟatu.
(7) vaḷarcci vītamum vaḷarccik kōlamum uṭalukku uḷḷum puṟampum uḷḷa nilimikaḷip poṟuttavi: kuḻantikaḷiṉ vaḷarccikku aṭip paṭit tēviyākavuḷḷa cūḻnili amiyāviṭil vaḷarcci vītamum vaḷarccik kōlamum pātikkappeṟum. uṇavūṭṭam, ceyal, ōyvu, nalla maṉanili, tēviyāṉa oḻuṅku nili mutaliya kūṟukaḷ kuḻantiyiṉ vaḷarccikku iṉṟiyamiyātavi. ivi naṉṉiliyilamin tāl nalla vaḷarcci ēṟpaṭum.
(8) ovvoru kuḻantiyum taṉakkē uriya ciṟappāṉa muṟiyil vaḷarkiṉṟatu: cila kuḻantikaḷ uyaramākavum cila kuṭṭiyākavum, cila olliyākavum, cila taṭittum irukkiṉṟaṉa. ovvoruvaruṭiya vaḷarcci yum ovvoruvitamākavē irukkum. itaṉāl peṟṟōrkaḷ kuḻanti vaḷarcciyiṉit turitappaṭut tuvataṟkāka evvita muyaṟciyum eṭuttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭiya tilli. kuḻantikkuk kuḻanti vaḷarcci vēṟupāṭukaḷ irukkum eṉpati avarkaḷ aṟital vēṇṭum.
(9) vaḷarcci iyakkaṅkaḷiṉ orumippāṭṭik kuṟik kiṉṟatu: ciṟuciṟu pakutikaḷiṉ vēlikaḷ orumippāṭu29 peṟṟup putiya ceyalāka ami kiṉṟaṉa. perumpālum nam muṭiya tiṟaṉuḷḷa ceyalkaḷ yāvum muṉṉarē aminta tulakkaṅkaḷ tērnteṭukkappeṟṟup putiyavikaḷ cilavaṟ ṟuṭaṉ cērntu tirumpavum putiya kōlaṅkaḷāka amipaviyē.
(10) vaḷarcci cikkalāṉatu; ataṉ ellāk kūṟukaḷum neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyavi: kuḻantiyiṉ uṭal vaḷarcci aṟivu vaḷarcciyōṭum, uḷḷakkiḷarcci vaḷarcciyōṭum, camūka vaḷarcciyōṭum, toṭarpuṭiyatu. uḷḷak kiḷarc cikaḷil nēriṭum amitikkalivu uṇpatil, allatu tūṅku vatil kuḻantiyiṉ naṭattiyit tīrmāṉittatil kūṭum. kuḻantiyiṉ uṟuppukkuṟi cila maṉappōkkukaḷi uṇṭākkavum, camūkap poruttappāṭṭi ēṟpaṭuttavum toṭakka niliyāka iruttal kūṭum. it toṭarpukaḷic cariyākap purintu koṇṭāl kuḻantiyiṉ vaḷarcciyil nām etaṉi etirpārkkalām eṉpati aṟiyalām. itaṉāl piṉṉar nikaḻum vaḷarcci muṟikaḷi muṉṉarē aṟintu, ataṟkēṟpap poruttappāṭuḷi eyta ētuvākiṉṟatu.
mēṟkūṟiya vaḷarccipaṟṟiya uṇmikaḷi āciriyarkaḷum peṟṟōr kaḷum aṟintu kuḻantikaḷiṉ vaḷarcciyil takka cūḻnilikaḷi nalki ēṟṟa payiṟcikaḷiyum aḷittal vēṇṭum. kuḻantikaḷiṉ tiṟaṉkaḷ vaḷarcciyiṉ koṭumuṭikaḷi aṭiyum muṉṉar kuḻantiyiṉ naṭattiyil ēṟpaṭum māṟṟaṅkaḷ perumpālum mutircciyiyum kaṟṟaliyum poṟuttavi. mutirccikkuppiṉṉar naṭipeṟum naṭatti māṟṟaṅkaḷ yāvum kaṟpataṉ viḷivukaḷē. inta vaḷarcciyiṉ aṭippaṭik kūṟukaḷ kuṭivaḻiyāka amintukiṭappiṉum, cūḻniliyāl peṟak kūṭiyavi yāvum āciriyar, peṟṟōrkaḷ kiyiltāṉ uḷḷaṉa.
uṭal vaḷarcci
uṭalukkum uḷḷattiṟkum neruṅkiyatōr iyipu uṇṭu. uṭal muṟṟi vaḷarnta niliyiltāṉ, muṟṟivaḷarnta uḷḷattiṉ ceyalum pulappaṭattōṉṟukiṟatu. kuḻantiyiṉ uṭalōṭu kuḻantiyiṉ uḷḷamum vaḷarntu varutaliṉ, uṭal vaḷarcciyi ārāyvatu iṉṟiyamiyāta tākiṉṟatu. uṭal vaḷarcci uṭal nalattiṟkum naṭattiyiṉ poruttap pāṭṭiṟkum mukkiya māṉatu.
piṟappu, vaḷarcciyiṉ toṭakkam aṉṟu; āṉāl ataṟku oṉpatu tiṅkaḷkaṭku muṉṉar karuttarittalē vaḷacciyiṉ toṭakkamākum. oru kuṇṭūciyiṉ taliyiviṭac ciṟiya ōr uyiraṇuviliruntu oru maṉitaṉ tōṉṟuvatu vintiyiṉum vintiyeṉṟē collavēṇṭum.
uyaram eṭi mutaliyavaṟṟil: potuvāka vaḷarcciyiṉ pōkku kuṭi vaḻiyāl aṟutiyiṭappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. vaḷarcciyi iṭiyaṟāta toṭarpu eṉṟu mēlē kūṟiṉōm allavā? karuvulakil oṉpatu tiṅkaḷvari ōr uyiraṇuviliruntu ēṟak kuṟiya eṭṭu irāttal eṭiyuḷḷatum, 20 aṅkula nīḷamuḷḷatumāṉa kuḻanti vaḷarkiṉṟatu. karuvaṟiyil iruntataṉiviṭa, piṟakkum poḻutu uyiraṇuviṉ eṇṇikki arikkōṭi maṭaṅku mikutiyākiviṭukiṉṟatākak kaṇakkiṭṭuḷḷaṉar. 
potuvākak kūṟumiṭattu, āṇ kuḻanti peṇ kuḻantiyi viṭac caṟṟu uyaramākavum kaṉamākavum irukkum. piṟantavuṭaṉ kuḻanti uḷḷirukkum vāḻkkit tēvikaḷ paṟṟi iṭi viṭātu ceyal puriyum; cila nāṭkaḷil veḷit tūṇṭalkaḷum tulaṅkat toṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. nāḷaṭivil kuḻanti uyarattilum eṭiyilum mikkuvaruvatik kāṇalām. uṭaliṉ pala uṟuppukaḷ vevvēṟu vēkattil vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl kuḻantiyiṉ naṭatti pātikkap peṟukiṉṟatu.
vaḷarcci kuḻantikkuk kuḻantimāṟuvatu iyalpāyiṉum, cila potuvāṉa takavalkaḷik kuṟippiṭalām. toṭakkattil vaḷarcci mika atikamāka uḷḷatu. iraṇṭāvatu āṇṭuvari ivvāṟu virivāka irukkum. 11-ām āṇṭuvari vaḷarcci vītam caṟṟuk kuṟivākavē irukkum. āṉāl, iḷaṅkumarap paruvattil (11-14) vaḷarccivēkam mīṇṭum atikarikkiṉṟatu. kuḻantip paruvattil uḷḷa aḷavupōl vaḷarccivēkam ipparuvattil illi. uyaramāka vaḷarumpoḻutu cuṟṟu vaḷarcciyil kuṟintum, cuṟṟuvaḷarcci mikumpoḻutu uyarum vaḷarcci kuṟintum varuvati nām kāṇalām.
uruvattil vaḷarccikkōla māṟupāṭukaḷ: vaḷarccik kōlam ovvoru kuḻantikkum vēṟupaṭṭatāka irukkum. eṉiṉum, cila potup paṇpukaḷik kuṟippiṭalām. uṭaliṉ pala pākaṅkaḷilum vaḷarccik kōlam māṟupaṭukiṉṟatu. (e-ṭu) piṟanta piṉ kikāl vaḷarcci taliyiṉ vaḷarcciyik kāṭṭilum atikam. ataṉāltāṉ uṭaliṉ ovvōr uṟuppum vevvēṟu vītattil vaḷarntu varukiṉṟatu eṉṟu aṟikiṉṟōm. oru cila pakutikaḷ muṟṟiya niliyil irukkattakka aḷaviṉi ēṟakkuṟiya kuḻavi niliyilēyē eṭṭip pārkkiṉṟaṉa. ēṉiya uṟuppukaḷ vayatiṟku ēṟṟavāṟu aḷavil māṟivarukiṉṟaṉa.
kuḻanti-mutiyavar oppu: kuḻantiyiṉ uṭaluṟuppukaḷiṉ aḷavu vikitaṅkaḷukkum mutirntavariṉ uṟuppukaḷiṉ aḷavu vikitaṅkaḷukkum mikunta vēṟupāṭukaḷ uḷa. nāḷaṭivil ivvēṟupāṭukaḷ māṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kuḻantiyiṉ taliyum uṭalum kālkaḷiviṭap periyavi. kālkaḷ kuṭṭiyāṉavi; mukam periyatu. mukam pala māṟutal aṭivatu paṟṟik kuḻanti evar mukac cāyaliyuṭiyatu eṉṟu collutal muṭiyātu.
āṇ-peṇ vaḷarcci: oru paruvattil peṇ piḷḷikaḷ āṇ piḷḷikaḷiviṭa uṭalniliyil mutircciyuṟṟu, atē vayatu āṇ piḷḷikaḷiviṭa mūttōrpōl kāṇap paṭuvatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. kumarap paruvam30 peṇkaḷukkup paṉ ṉiraṇṭām yāṇṭilum, āṇkaḷukkup patiṉmūṉṟāmyāṇṭilum toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu. ip paruvattiltāṉ āṇ kaḷukkup piṉṉaṇiyil niṉṟa peṇkaḷ muṉṉaṇiyil vantu niṟkiṉṟaṉar. 12-14 yāṇṭuvari peṇkaḷ uyarattil ciṟantu viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉar; 15-ām yāṇṭuvari eṭiyilum ciṟantu viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. itaṟkuḷ āṇkaḷ inta ōṭṭattil peṇkaḷip piṭikka vantu peṇkaḷukku muntiyum ōṭi veṟṟi aṭikiṉṟaṉar. āṇkaḷ pālmutirvu peṟuvataṟku 1ṅū  yāṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē peṇkaḷ pūppeytip pāl-mutirvu peṟṟuviṭukiṉṟaṉar.
pulaṉkaḷ vaḷarcci: piṟanta kuḻantiyiṉ tulakkaṅkaḷ perumpālum nōkkamaṟṟaviyākavē irukkum; muḻu uṭal paṟṟiyaviyākavē irukkum. mutalil iraṇṭu kaṇkaḷum cērntu ceyaṟpaṭuvatilli. iraṇṭu kaṇṇum orumukappaṭṭu oru poruḷip pārkkum nili 15 nāṭkaḷil varukiṉṟatu. poruḷkaḷ tōṉṟuvatiyum maṟivatiyum kāṇamuṭikiṉṟatu. pala kuḻavikaḷ piṟantavuṭaṉ oḷikaḷi aṟiyā; cila nāṭkaḷ ceṉṟa piṉṉarē aṟiyum. aḻum kuḻanti tālāṭṭup pāṭṭik kēṭṭu vāḷā iruntāl, olikaḷi aṟivatāka ūkikkalām. kuḻantikkup piṟantavuṭaṉē cuvi yuṇarcci uṇṭākiṉṟatu. āyiṉum, cila tiṅkaḷ vari cuviyuṇarcciyil atika vaḷarcci ēṟpaṭuvatilli.
vāḻkkikku vēṇṭiya narampu nuṇmaṅkaḷ31 yāvum piṟakkum poḻutē iyaṟkiyāka amintu viṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, muḻu vaḷarcciyum peṟac cila yāṇṭukaḷākum. mūḷiyiṉ perumpakuti ceyaṟpaṭa āṟṟaliṉṟik kiṭakkiṉṟatu; kīḻnilip pakutiyē tērcciyuṭiyatu. karuvulaka vāḻvilēyē cila narampup
pātikaḷ ēṟpaṭṭu, tēviyāṉapōtu aṉiccic ceyalkaḷāka iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. (e-ṭu) cuvācittal, uṟiñcutal, viḻuṅkutal, tummal, irumal, pōṉṟa maṟiviṉikaḷ32 nāḷaṭivil narampuk kampikaḷ uṟikaḷāl mūṭappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. icceyal piṟakkumuṉpē toṭaṅkiviṭukiṉṟatu. āṉāl, narampu miyaṅkaḷ muḻuvaḷarcciyaṭiya nāḷākiṉṟatu. illā viṭṭāl piṟantavuṭaṉ pala tākkalkaḷāl kuḻantikku nerukkaṭi uṇṭākum. putiya tūṇṭalkaḷiṉ payaṉāka mūḷip puṟaṇiyi aṭiyum pala pātikaḷ uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa. mutal yāṇṭil mūḷiyiṉ parumaṉ nāḷukku nāḷ atikarikkiṉṟatu; kuḻantikkuk kullāy virivil pōtāmal pōvatu tāymārkaḷ kāṇum anupavam. taṉip pulaṉkaḷ anupavam ēṟpaṭat toṭaṅkiya vuṭaṉ iyipu miyaṅkaḷ ceyaṟpaṭum. nāṉkām vayatil mūḷi 100-kku 75 paṅku kaṉattiyum, 8-ām vayatil 90 paṅku kaṉattiyum 20-ām vayatil muḻuk kaṉattiyum aṭikiṉṟatu.
iyakka vaḷarcci: mutalil kuḻanti taṉ uṭaliṉmēl āṭci puriyak kaṟkiṉṟatu. uṇṇutal, parukutal, kaḻivup poruḷkaḷi akaṟṟutal pōṉṟavaṟṟiṟku iyakka āṭci tēviyallavā? mutal mutalil kikaḷ ivaṟṟiṉ acivu, oru poruḷik kiyāḷutal, naṭattal, niṟṟal, ōṭutal, tavaḻtal, pēcutal, eḻututal pōṉṟa ovvoru ceyalum iyakkattiyē nāṭukiṉṟatu. piṟaku vāḻvil nām mēṟkoḷḷum toḻil kaḷum iyakkaṅkaḷip poṟuttaviyē. nalvāḻkkikku iyakkaṅkaḷiṉ mēl takunta ātikkam iṉṟiyami yātatu. kalviyilum iyakkampaṟṟiya tērnta aṟivum, iyakkaṅ kaḷik kaṇkāṇikkum tiṟaṉum tēvi. 
iyakka vaḷarcciyilum ōr oḻuṅkumuṟi kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. iyakkaṅkaḷ muṟiyāka vaḷarntu paravukiṉṟaṉa; kōlaṅkaḷāka amikiṉṟaṉa. mutalil kaṇkaḷiṉ iyakkaṅkaḷē āḷappeṟṟu oruṅkiyiya varukiṉṟaṉa. aṭuttu, kaḻuttut tacikaḷiṉ iyakkaṅkaḷ oṟṟumi peṟṟu viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. mūṉṟān tiṅkaḷukkuḷ kuḻanti appuṟamum ippuṟamum kaḻuttit tiruppukiṉṟatu. piṉpu taliyiyum mārpiyum tūkkik koḷkiṉṟatu. nāṉkām mātattiṟkuḷ kaṇ, tali, kaḻuttu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ iyakkaṅkaḷ oruṅkiyiya varukiṉṟaṉa. oruṅkiyiya varukiṉṟa nili piṉṉart tōḷukkāka iyaṅkukiṉṟatu. tōḷkaḷum kikaḷum  oruṅki yintu iyaṅku kiṉṟaṉa. kiyāl toṭuvatum paṟṟuvatum mutalāṉa ceyal kaḷ nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. kuḻavikaḷ 15ām vārattil viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷi vittu viḷiyāṭukiṉṟaṉa. 38-ām vārattil kuḻavi kaviḻntu koḷkiṟatu. piṉpu, piṉpuṟamāka nakarkiṟatu. nāḷaṭivil tavaḻtal, niṟṟal, naṭattal, ōṭutal, muṟiyāka nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, tacikaḷiṉ iyakka vaḷarcciyum taliyiliruntu iṟaṅkivarak kāṇkiṉṟōm.
iyakkat tulakkattil nām kāṇum uṇmi eṉṉa? tūṇṭaliṉ33 piṉ tulakkaṅkaḷ34 eḻum vēkam virintu vaḷartalē vaḷarcciyiṉ mutal iyalpu. putiyaputiya tulaṅkal kaḷik kuḻavikaḷ kaṇṭu koḷkiṉṟaṉa. iḵtu iraṇṭāvatu iyalpu. taṟceyalāka eḻum poruḷaṟṟa iyakkaṅkaḷ tāmāka maṟikiṉṟaṉa. itu mūṉṟāvatu iyalpu. kuḻavi urimiyuṭaṉ iyaṅki varumpoḻututāṉ ittakiya vaḷarcci vaḷam ati karikkiṉṟatu. ātaliṉ, kuḻavikaḷiṉ iyakkaṅkaḷit taṭutta lākātu. mutalil kuḻantikaḷiṉ ceyalkaḷ tiruttamuṟāmalum paṇpaṭiyāmalum irukkalām. itu paṟṟik kuḻantikaḷik kuṟikūṟalākātu.  kuḻantikaḷ pala vēlikaḷic ceyya muṟpaṭum; itaṟku nām iṭantara vēṇṭum. uḷḷiruntu tuṭittuk koṇṭu varum āṟṟalukkut taṭi pōṭakkūṭātu; avarkaḷ ceyalil kuṟukkiṭavum kūṭātu. takunta vāyppukaḷ koṭuttāl aṉittum tāmākavē cīrpaṭum, oḻuṅkāka amiyum. vaḷarccikkut tiṭṭamāṉa neṟikaḷ uḷḷaṉa eṉṟu mēlē kūṟiyati īṇṭu niṉivu kūrka.
kittiṟaṉiyum kiyeḻuttut tiṟaṉiyum kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷ mēṟkūṟiya uṇmikaḷi niṉivil vittuk kaṟpittal vēṇṭum. pāṭattiṭṭam amippatilum ivi kavaṉam peṟavēṇṭum. aṟivuttuṟikku matipput taruvatip pōl iyakkat tiṟaṉukkum takka matipputtarutal vēṇṭum. itaṉiyē taṟkālak kalvi ēṟpāṭu mikavum vēṇṭuvatu.



uḷḷak kiḷarcciyiṉ tulakkam
uḷḷak kiḷarcci eṉpatu eṉṉa eṉpati variyaṟip paṭuttik kūṟamuṭiyātu. ovvoruvarum itaṉi naṉku uṇar vārāyiṉum, coṟkaḷāl uṇarttuvatu mikak kaṭiṉam. vekuḷi, accam, makiḻcci, iṉpam, veṟuppu ivi pōṉṟa meyppāṭṭu nilikaḷik kuṟippatē uḷḷak kiḷarcci eṉpatu. tīvira uṇarcci, uḷtuṭippu, uṭaliṉ ceyalkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ cērkkiyē uḷḷakkiḷarcciyākum. uṇarcci35 iṉṉatu eṉpati ovvoruvarum tāmē aṟitalkūṭum. vekuḷiyāl muṉceṉṟu tākkutal, accattāl veruṇṭōṭutal, veṟuppāl vānti paṇṇutal, makiḻcciyāl kutittal, tuyarattāl mukam veḷuttal pōṉṟavi uṭtuṭippukaḷiyum36 uṭaliṉ ceyalkaḷiyum37 kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, uṇarccikaḷ, tuṭippukaḷ, pūtuṭalnili, etirviṉi ākiyavi aṉittum uḷḷakkiḷarcciyiṉuḷ aṭaṅkum eṉṟākiṉṟatu. tiṭīreṉṟu eḻum veṭippu nilikaḷil maṭṭilum uḷḷakkiḷarcci tōṉ ṟum eṉṟu karututal tavaṟu; atu nam cintaṉikaḷ, ceyalkaḷ mutaliya aṉittiyum paṟpala aḷavu kaḷil aṭakkiyāḷu kiṉṟatu. uḷḷakkiḷarccikaḷippaṟṟi aintām iyalil viḷakkap peṟṟuḷḷatu; āṇṭuk kaṇṭu koḷka.
mutalil tōṉṟum etirviṉikaḷ38: vāḻkkiyiṉ toṭakkattil kuḻanti aḻutu kāliyum kiyiyum aṭittu  uḷḷakkiḷarcci vāynta ceyalkaḷi iyaṟṟukiṉṟatu. ēṉiya naṭattik kūṟukaḷil tōṉṟu vatippōlavē, uḷḷak kiḷarcciyilum taṉippaṭṭa vekuḷi, accam, makiḻcci, tuyaram pōṉṟa vēṟṟumikaḷi uṇarttum etirviṉikaḷ tōṉṟu vatilli. mutalil tōṉṟum etirviṉikaḷ potuttuṭippu kaḷākavē tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. nāḷaṭiviltāṉ mēṟkuṟippiṭṭa pākupāṭukaḷ teḷivuṟukiṉṟaṉa.
kuḻantiyiṉ āṟṟalkaḷ muṟṟamuṟṟa, ataṉatu paṭṭaṟivu mikamika, uḷḷakkiḷarccikaḷit tūṇṭum ētukkaḷil māṟu pāṭukaḷ nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. mutalil nērākat tākkum tūṇṭalkaḷē uḷḷakkiḷarccikaḷi eḻuppum. (e.ṭu) pēroli, miṉṉal, mukattil vēkamākak kāṟṟu tākkutal, tiṭīreṉa ātaravi iḻattal pōṉṟavi accatti viḷivikkum. uriya kālattil pāl koṭukkappeṟāmi, tāṉāka eḻum kikālkaḷiṉ iyakkaṅkaḷukkut taṭi pōṉṟavi ciṉatti uṇṭākkum. vāḻkkit tēvikaḷ niṟivēṟutalum, maṉaniṟivu tarum iyakkaṅkaḷum makiḻcciyi viḷivikkiṉṟaṉa.
mutalāṇṭil uḷḷakkiḷarcci veḷippāṭṭil ēṟpaṭum māṟṟam: kuḻanti piṟantu cila tiṉaṅkaḷil tōṉṟum ciṟappāṉa veḷippāṭoṉṟu āḷmukattik kaṇṭu muṟuvalittal ākum. iṉṉum ciṟitu kālattil mukattil naki tōṉṟukiṟatu. nāṉku vāraṅkaḷil paci, vekuḷi, vali eṉpavaṟṟiṟku vevvēṟu vakiyāṉa aḻuki tōṉṟukiṟateṉṟu kecal39 eṉṟa uḷaviyalaṟiñar kaṇṭaṟintuḷḷār. inta aḻukip paṇpu kuḻantikkuk kuḻanti vēṟupaṭum. eṉavē, taṉ kuḻantiyiṉ aḻukip paṇpi naṉkuṇarnta oru tāy maṟṟoru kuḻanti aḻuvataṉ kāraṇatti aṟital aritu. mutalāṇṭu niṟi vēṟuvataṟku muṉṉar accam, aṉpu, iṉpam ākiyavaṟṟi veḷikkāṭṭum aṟikuṟikaḷikkāṇalām.
vayatu muyaṟciyāl māṟṟam: vayatu mutiramutira uḷḷak kiḷarccikaḷi veḷiyiṭum ciṉṉaṅkaḷ māṟṟam aṭikiṉṟaṉa. (e.ṭu) kuḻantip paruvattil kālik kiḷḷiṉāl kāli māttiram veṭukkeṉṟu iḻukkāmal uṭal muḻuvatiyum acitta kuḻanti, ippoḻutu kāli maṭṭilum iḻukkiṉṟatu. uḷḷakkiḷarcci veḷippāṭu ivvāṟu kuṟintu varukiṉṟatu. ēḻu allatu eṭṭut tiṅkaḷ niṟiveytiya kuḻantikkup paci eṭukkumpōtu pāl koṭukkat tāmatam ceytāl, ataṉ uṭal ellām ceyalāka amintu viṭukiṟatu. utittu neḷintu, kattik karikiṉṟatu; veṟi koṇṭu alaṟukiṉṟatu; ciṉattik kakkukiṉṟatu. kuḻanti vaḷara vaḷara ic ceyalkaḷ kuṟikiṉṟaṉa. ēṟakkuṟiya ēḻu āṇṭil kuḻanti uṭal muḻuvatiyum alaṭṭik koḷvatilli. uḷḷak kiḷarcciyiṉ āṭamparam kuṟintu accamum vekuḷiyum aṭaṅkiyē veḷip paṭukiṉṟaṉa. ippoḻutu kuḻanti etaṟkeṭuttālum aḻuvatu milli.
iṅṅaṉam māṟṟaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭuvataṟkup pala kāraṇaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. kuḻantikku atika anupavam ēṟpaṭṭu viṭukiṉṟatu.  moḻiyālum piṟa kuṟiyīṭukaḷālum taṉ uṇarccikaḷi nuṭpamākak kūṟum tiṟamiyik kuḻanti peṟukiṉṟatu.  vaṉceyalkaḷālum muraṭṭuttaṉattālum piracciṉi kaḷit tīrkka muṭiyātu eṉpati aṟikiṉṟatu. peṟṟōrum piṟarum pālami40 tavirti41 yeṉṟum, periya piḷḷiyāka irukkavēṇṭum eṉṟum kuḻantiyi iṭiviṭātu vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟaṉar. ciṟuvaṉum nāḷaṭivil puṇpaṭṭa maṉattiyum kaṇṇīriyum maṟikkak kaṟṟuk koḷkiṉṟāṉ. taṉṉi accuṟuttavum puṇpaṭuttavum ciṉappaṭuttavum kūṭiya nilimikaḷ uṇṭeṉ patit tāṉum oppuk koḷḷāmal, piṟarukkum veḷip paṭuttāmal vāḻat toṭaṅkukiṉṟāṉ.
paḷḷikkuc celvataṟkuḷ ciṟuvaṉiṉ uṇarccikaḷil perumpāṉmi yāṉavi maṟikiṉṟaṉa; allatu māṟupaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉum uḷḷakkiḷarcci kaḷiṉ veḷippāṭu kuṟiyiṉum, uḷḷirukkum uḷḷakkiḷarcci anupavaṅkaḷ kuṟivatilli. nēr veḷippāṭu kuṉṟutalālum, ellā uḷḷakkiḷarcci kaḷiyum collāl uṇartta muṭiyātatālum, kuḻantiyiṉ naṭattiyi accam, vekuḷi, veṟuppu pōṉṟavi evvāṟu āḷukiṉṟaṉa eṉpati aṟintu koḷvatu aritākiṉṟatu. eṉavē ciṟuvaṉi ūṉṟik kavaṉikka vēṇṭum.
nāḷaṭivil aṟivu iyakkattiṟaṉkaḷ, kavarccikaḷ, kaṟpaṉi yāṟṟalkaḷ ākiyavi mutircci peṟukiṉṟaṉa. taṟ pōtiya tēvikaḷi viṭa nīṇṭa varuṅkālat tēvikaḷum paḻiya niṉivukaḷum, putiya tiṭṭaṅkaḷum uḷḷakkiḷarcci kaḷi aṟutiyiṭukiṉṟaṉa. muṉṉar makiḻcciyūṭṭiya māṭippaṭi ēṟutal, cakkaram uruṭṭutal, pommi vittu viḷiyāṭutal pōṉṟavi ippoḻutu makiḻcci taruvatilli. paṭṭam viṭutal, pamparam viṭutal pōṉṟavi maṉaniṟivu tarukiṉṟaṉa muṉṉāl anniyarik kaṇṭu añciṉavaṉ, ippoḻutu taṉṉi maruṭṭiyavaṉiyum aṭittavaṉiyum kaṇṭu añcu kiṉṟāṉ. kumarapparuvattil pañcampōr pōṉṟa camūka nerukkaṭiyāṉa ceytikaḷik kaṇṭu añcukiṉṟāṉ. 
kuḻantiyiṉ kiḷarcci naṭattiyip purintu koḷḷutal: kuḻantikku nēriṭum ceyalkaḷāl, avaṉiṭam kiḷampum uḷḷakkiḷarccikaḷ avaṉuṭiya maṉaniliyip poṟut tavi. kuḻantiyiṉ maṉaniliyi aṟintāl, niyāyamaṟṟa naṭatti eṉa nām karutum palavaṟṟi viḷakka muṭiyum. apāya millāta poḻutu kuḻanti añciṉālum, kuṟi collu vataṟku muṉ maṉam nontālum, accattiṟkum vekuḷikkum kāraṇamilliyeṉṟu ālōcaṉi yiṉṟik kūṟuvatāl payaṉ illi. avaṉ muṉṉamē alla luṟṟiruppatāl eḷitil añcukiṟāṉ eṉṟum, taṉṉampikki iṉmi yālum maṟṟavarkaḷ pāl koṇṭa etirppālum avaṉ kuṟi kūṟuvatāl puṇpaṭu kiṉṟāṉ eṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum; avaṟṟiṟkuriya kāraṇaṅ kaḷik kaṇṭaṟintu avaṉik kuṇappaṭutta muyala vēṇṭum
kuḻantikaḷiṉ uḷḷakkiḷarccikaḷ paḷḷicceyalkaḷuṭaṉum kalantu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. pāṭattiṭṭam naṉmuṟiyil avaṉuṭiya āṟṟalukkēṟṟavāṟu amintiruppatāl, kuḻanti tāṉ cātippatil makiḻcciyaṭikiṉṟāṉ. mēlum uṭaṉpayilvōr, uṭaṉviḷiyāṭuvōr avaṉiṭamum, avaṉ avarkaḷiṭamum kāṭṭum aṉpu, parivu, veṟuppu pōṉṟaviyum avaṉ vāḻkkiyil mukkiyamāṉavi. āciriyariṉ pāṭam payiṟṟum tiṟamiyi viṭa, avar kāṭṭum aṉpiyum pariviyum kuḻanti atikamākak kavaṉik kiṉṟatu. kaṭumiyum uṟutiyum iṉṟiyami yātaviyē; eṉiṉum, kuḻantiyiṉ āṟṟalkaḷ vaḷarvataṟku aṉpuṭaṉ tarum vāyppukaḷē peritum maṉaniṟivu tarak kūṭiyavi. kuḻantikaḷi vāḷā irukkac ceytalālum, paḻakiya iyakkattiyē tirumpattirumpac ceyyumpaṭi ēvu talālum, avarkaḷiṉ tiṟaṉkaḷukku ovvāta ceyalkaḷic ceyyumpaṭi ēvutalālum, kuḻantikaḷiṭam aluppum etirppu maṉappāṉmiyum tōṉṟum eṉpati āciriyarkaḷum peṟṟōr kaḷum uṇartal vēṇṭum.
camūkappaṇpu vaḷarcci 42
kuḻavi cuṟṟupuṟac cūḻalāl māṟi varuvataṉip palamuṟi kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷōm. piṟarōṭu kūṭi vāḻvatē makkaḷ iyalpu.  camūkatti viṭṭup pirittu taṉiyāḷi uṇarvatu iyalātu. uṇavupporuḷ viḷivippōr, uṭi neyvōr, vaṇikar pōṉṟa palaruṭiya tuṇiyāl nam vāḻvu naṭipeṟukiṉṟatu. eṉiṉum nāṉ eṉatu eṉ urimi pōṉṟa eṇṇaṅkaḷ makkaḷiṭam ōyāmal kuṭikoṇṭuḷḷaṉa. camūkam, taṉiyāḷ eṉṟa iraṇṭu eṇṇaṅkaḷiṭiyēyum ōyāmal pōrāṭṭam naṭipeṟṟa vaṇṇamirukkiṉṟatu. eṉavē, kuḻavi camūkattiṟku iṇaṅkat tiruntip poruttappāṭu aṭintu varuvataṉiyum aṟintālaṉṟi nām kuḻanti vaḷarcciyippaṟṟi muḻumiyum aṟintavarkaḷātal ilōm. tavira, camūka vaḷarcci uṭal vaḷarcci, iyakka vaḷarcci, uḷḷakkiḷarcci vaḷarcci pōṉṟa vaṟṟuṭaṉ neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyatu eṉpati eṉṟum niṉivil iruttal vēṇṭum. eṉavē, kuḻantiyiṭam camūkap paṇpu vaḷarcci naṭipeṟu vataṉik kavaṉippōm.
iv vaḷarcciyiṉ pōkku: camūka vaḷarcciyil tiṭṭamāṉa nilikaḷ iṉṉavi eṉṟu variyaṟi ceytu kūṟuvataṟkilli. iṅkuc cila potuvāṉa pōkkukaḷē kūṟappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.
