piṟanta kuḻaviyiṭam camūkuṇarcci iruppatākac colla muṭiyātu. aḵtu uḷatu eṉṟu kūṟukiṟavarkaḷ kaṟpaṉiyulakil vāḻkiṉṟavarkaḷē. piṟantavuṭaṉ tāy pālūṭṭi iṉpamākap pārāṭṭiyum, uṟaṅkutal, malam, ciṟunīr kaḻittal pōṉṟa paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷi muṟippaṭuttiyum utavukiṉṟāḷ. kuḷip pāṭṭutal, tālāṭṭutal pōṉṟavaṟṟāl pārāṭṭip pēci vaḷarkkiṉṟāḷ; iṉṉum palvēṟu muṟikaḷil kuḻantikku maṉaniṟivu tarukiṉṟāḷ. kuḻantiyum taṉakku maṉaniṟivu tarupavarkaḷiṭam aṉpu koḷkiṉṟatu. ituvē camūkappaṇpu vaḷarccikku aṭippaṭi. kikālkaḷiṉ iyakkaṅkaḷālum, puṉmuṟuvalālum kuḻanti maṉaniṟivi veḷippaṭuttu kiṉṟatu. mukamalarcci, kural, ūṟṟuṇarcci mutaliya pala vēṟu pāṭukaḷi uṇarntu kuḻanti tāyiṉ pārāṭṭip peritum virumpukiṉṟatu.
iraṇṭām tiṅkaḷil kuḻavi taṉṉip pārppavarik kaṇṭu puṉmuṟuval koḷkiṟatu. periyōrkaḷi ivvāṟu kaṇṭaṟinta piṟakē taṉṉippōṉṟa kuḻavikaḷik kaṇṭu puṉṉaki koḷḷukiṟatu. cātāraṇamāka 7,8 tiṅkaḷ vari piṟakuḻavikaḷiṭam akkaṟi koḷvatilli. mutal vayatiṉ iṟutiyil piṟa kuḻavikaḷiṉ viḷiyāṭṭupporuḷkaḷil maṭṭilum akkaṟi koḷkiṉṟatu; atu kāraṇamākac caṇṭiyum iṭukiṉṟatu; āṉāl kuḻantikaḷiṭam avvaḷavu kavaṉam celuttuvatilli. ēṟakkuṟiya 15 tiṉaṅkaḷukkup piṟakē piṟa kuḻavikaḷiṭam aṉpu kāṭṭukiṉṟatu. āṉāl, iraṇṭām vayatil, uṇmiyāṉa kūṭṭuṟavu arumpuviṭṭu malarkiṉṟatu. iraṇṭām yāṇṭiliruntē piṟa kuḻantikaḷiṉ taṉi viḷi yāṭṭip pārkkiṉṟatu. atippōlavē tāṉum cey kiṉṟatēyaṉṟi, ak kuḻanti yuṭaṉ cērntu atika nēram orē viḷiyāṭṭi viḷiyāṭu vatilli. iraṇṭu kuḻavikaḷ cērntu viḷiyāṭum nēram āṇṭutōṟum atikarik kiṉṟatu. mūṉṟām vayatil kuḻavikaḷ orē poruḷkaḷik koṇṭu arukiliruntu viḷiyāṭukiṉṟaṉa; āṉāl nāṉkām vayatil vīṭukaṭṭi viḷiyāṭutal pōṉṟa periya viḷiyāṭṭil pala kuḻantikaḷ cērntu viḷiyāṭukiṉṟaṉa. yāṇṭutōṟum viḷiyāṭṭuk kuḻukkaḷil atikamāka oṟṟumip pāṅkiyum vēliyip paṅkiṭṭuk koḷḷum taṉmiyiyum kāṇkiṉ ṟōm. ivvāṟu vaḷarum camūka vaḷarcci kuḻantikaḷiṉ aṟitiṟaṉ43 vaḷarcciyiyum aṟivu44 vaḷarcciyiyum poṟuttuḷḷatu.
kuṭumpamē camūka uṇarcciyi mutaṉmutalāka uṇṭākku kiṟatu. kuṭumpamē kalvi niliyaṅkaḷil mutaṉmiyāṉatu eṉpati nām aṟivōm. ciṟuvaṉ piṟar pārāṭṭi vēṇṭu kiṉṟāṉ; ikaḻcciyit tavirkkiṉṟāṉ. piṟaruṭiya urimi kaḷiyum oruvāṟu uṇarkiṉṟāṉ. piṟa niliyil taṉṉi vittuk koṇṭu taṉṉippōl piṟari eṇṇavum ciṟuvaṉ kuṭumpattiltāṉ kaṟkiṉṟāṉ. mutalil kuṭumpattiluḷḷārōṭu maṭṭum paḻaki varum ciṟuvaṉ piṟaku kuṭumpattiṟku appāṟ paṭṭavaruṭaṉum paḻakat toṭaṅkukiṉṟāṉ. ivvāṟu ciṟuvaṉiṉ camūka elli akaṇṭukoṇṭē varukiṉṟatu.
paḷḷikku varum vayatuṭiya piḷḷikaḷ kuḻuttaṉmi peṟṟa45 uyirikaḷāka uḷḷaṉar. toṭakkanilip paḷḷiyil  pukumpoḻutu aṟivum moḻiyum vaḷarcci peṟukiṉṟaṉa. iviyum camūkavaḷarcciyil ciṟanta paṅku peṟukiṉṟaṉa. ciṟuvaṉ palaporuḷkaḷippaṟṟi aṟiya viḻikiṉṟāṉ; pala viṉākkaḷi viṭukkiṉṟāṉ. viṉavutal piṟariṭam toṭarpu koḷḷum vaḻiyeṉpati nām aṟiya vēṇṭuvatoṉṟu. viṉākkaḷ mūlam piṟarip pēccukkiḻukkiṉṟāṉ; avarkaḷ naṭpiyum peṟukiṉṟāṉ. mēlum, viḷiyāṭṭu camūkappaṇpu vaḷarcciyil ciṟantatōr iṭampeṟukiṉṟatu. atu viṭṭuk koṭukkum maṉappāṉmi, piṟar urimi, taṉmatippupōṉṟa paṇpukaḷi vaḷarppatil iṇiyaṟṟatākum. kuṭumpattil piṟar urimiyi ōraḷavu aṟintālum, paḷḷiyil orē vayatuṭiya taṉṉip pōṉṟa pala ciṟuvarkaḷuṭaṉ paḻakukiṉṟāṉ. vīṭṭil ēṟkāta poṟuppukaḷi ēṟkum vāyppukaḷ aṅkuk kiṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. vakuppiluḷḷōr etirpārkkiṟapaṭi naṭantukoḷḷap paḻaku kiṉṟāṉ. etu cari, etu tavaṟu eṉpati naṉku uṇarkiṉṟāṉ. kuḻuvēli, kuḻu viḷiyāṭṭuppōṉṟavaṟṟāl kuḻu vuṇarcci uṇṭākiṉṟatu; kuḻuvuṭaṉ aikkiyamāki viṭukiṉṟāṉ. kuḻuviṟku muṉintu pāṭupaṭutalē kaṭami eṉpatāka aṟikiṉṟāṉ. itil pōṭṭi maṉappāṉmi46 ciṟantatōr iṭam peṟukiṉṟatu. kūṭṭu viḷiyāṭṭu pattuvayatil tōṉṟukiṉṟatu eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. naṭatti kuḻanti nilikkut takkavāṟu māṟu vatālum kūṭṭu viḷiyāṭṭut tiṭīreṉṟu tōṉṟum oru ciṟappuc ceyalaṉṟu ātalālum, iv vayatit tiṭṭamākak kūṟa iyalātu. uṇmiyil iraṇṭām vayatiliruntē kūṭṭu viḷiyāṭṭu oruvāṟu toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu eṉṟu tāṉ colla vēṇṭum.
orē vakiyāṉa viruppa niliyum akkaṟiyum uḷḷa kuḻavikaḷ nērukku nērākak kūṭi viḷiyāṭi varukiṉṟaṉa. ittakiya kuḻuvil caṭṭatiṭṭam oṉṟum illi. viḷi yāṭṭiṉpōtu oṉṟu kūṭum kūṭṭattilumkūṭa oṟṟumiyum karuttu vēṟṟumiyum uṇṭu. āṉāl pēccuk kūṭṭāḷikaḷāka irukkumpoḻutu evvitak kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷum illi. pāl vēṟṟumi, poruḷātāra vēṟṟumi, camaya vēṟṟumi eṉṟavaṟṟiṟkellām iṭamiṉṟi ivi amikiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟi ṉiṉṟē tōḻami nili paḻukkiṉṟatu. paḷḷiyiṉiṉṟum oruṅkē vīṭu tirumpumpoḻutum viḷiyāṭṭil oruṅkē āṭi varumpoḻutum tōḻami muḷiviṭat toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu. ellā vayatu nilikaḷilum ittōḻami viḷaṅkak kāṇalām. 
piṉpaṟṟal47 allatu cāyalpiṭittal paṇpum camūka uṇarcciyi vaḷarkkiṉṟatu. camūkattiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ nammuṭiya paḻakka vaḻakkaṅ kaḷākiṉṟaṉa. ottuṇarvu vaḷarkiṉṟatu. cila kuḻavikaḷ taṉimaraṅkaḷpōl piṟaruṭaṉ paḻakuvatilli. ivvakik kuḻavikaḷ akamukarkaḷāka48, aḵtāvatu tameṇ ṇaṅkaḷ uṇarccikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiyē kavaṉipparvakaḷāka, ākiṉṟaṉar. āciriyarkaḷ camayōcitamākavum paṭippaṭiyākavum avarkaḷip piṟa kuḻavikaḷuṭaṉ viḷiyāṭavum kūṭi vēli ceyyavum paḻakavittu, oraḷavu puṟamukar49 kaḷākac ceyya vēṇṭum.
āṇ-peṇ toṭarpu: ciṟuvarkaḷum ciṟumikaḷum mutaṉ mutalākac camavayatiṉarōṭu cērntu viḷiyāṭuṅkāl avarkaḷukkuḷ ēṟṟattāḻvu talikāṭṭuvatilli. paḷḷiyil cērvataṟku muṉṉum toṭakka nilippaḷḷi mutalāṇṭilum iru pālārum cērntu viḷiyāṭukiṉṟaṉar. paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷō periya piḷḷikaḷō taṭuttālaṉṟi oru kuḻanti perum pālum etirpālārāl amikkap peṟṟa kuḻuviṉil cērntu viḷiyāṭat tayaṅkuvatilli. toṭakkattilēyē ciṟuvarkaḷum ciṟumiyar kaḷum takka eṇṇikkikaḷil iruntāl ciṟuvarkaḷ ciṟuvarkaḷōṭum ciṟumikaḷ ciṟumiyarkaḷōṭum cērntu taṉittaṉiyākavē viḷiyāṭa muṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉar eṉpatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
toṭakkanilip paḷḷikaḷiṉ mutalāṇṭilum iṭi yāṇṭukaḷilum ittakiya pirivu naṉku tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. āyiṉum, kumarap paruvam neruṅkuṅkāl irupālārum iru tiṟattiṉariyum oṉṟākak kūṭṭum ceyalkaḷi nāṭukiṉṟaṉar. inta etirppāl kavarccip perukkam piyaṉkaḷiviṭap peṇpiḷḷikaḷukkē muṉṉatāka ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. peṇ piḷḷikaḷ piyaṉkaḷukku ēṟattāḻa oṉṟariyāṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē kavarcciyil vaḷarkiṉṟaṉar. piyaṉkaḷukkuḷḷē im māṟutalkaḷ tōṉṟuvatil nāṉku yāṇṭukaḷ varikkum vēṟṟumi kāṇappeṟalām.
mūttōrkaḷukku: mūttōrkaḷākiya āciriyar, peṟṟōr ivarkaḷiṉ mukkiya kuṟippiṉi ivaṇ kuṟittal vēṇṭum. kuṭumpattilāyiṉum cari, paḷḷiyilāyiṉum cari, kuḻantikaḷ oru tali ātaravu50 kāṭṭalākātu; taṉiccelvam kāṭṭa lākātu; āciriyarum peṟṟōrum cila camayaṅkaḷil avarkaḷ aṉumatiyāta ceyalkaḷip piṟariṭam ceykiṉṟaṉar eṉpatic ciṟuvarkaḷ aṟikiṉṟaṉar. cariyāṉa camūkappaṇpu vaḷarccikku ivi ēṟṟaṉa alla.
ēṟakkuṟiya 12 vayatil kumarapparuvam talikāṭṭukiṉṟatu. ip paruvattil camūkappaṇpu vaḷarcci ciṟantōṅkum. ip poḻutu piṟar urimiyaṟital, etirpālil kavarcci, camūkat toṇṭu pōṉṟavi taṉittu viḷaṅkuvatik kāṇalām. ip paruvattil viḷiyāṭṭukaḷ, viḻākkaḷ, kuḻukkaḷ, kaḻakaṅkaḷ, cuṟṟulākkaḷ, cāraṇarpaṭi pōṉṟavi taṉṉalam karutātu kuḻukkiḷarcciyi51 uṇṭākkip paranta nōkkatti vaḷark kiṉṟaṉa. nāṭṭuppaṟṟu, aṉittu nāṭṭuppaṟṟu eṉṟavi nāḷaṭivil tōṉṟukiṟatu.
talimip paṇpi vaḷarttal: iḷiñarkaḷit takunta talivarkaḷāka paḻakkum nōkkattiyum kalvi niṟivēṟṟa vēṇṭum. talimikku uṭalkavarcci, uṭalnalam mukkiya māṉavi. kuṟippiṭṭa tuṟiyil tiṟami iruttalvēṇṭum. tōḻami, pakimi ēṟpaṭuttikkoḷḷāmi, palvēṟupaṭṭa makkaḷuṭaṉ palavakiyil paḻakak kaṟṟuk koḷḷutal, viṭṭuk koṭukkum iyalpu, kuḻuviṉ viruppattiṟku matippu tarutul, ēmāṟṟa millāmal maṉamāra uḻittal, ottuḻippu, uyarnta kuṟikkōḷkaḷ, mariyāti pōṉṟa paṇpukaḷ talimi pūṇpataṟkut tuṇiceypavi, paḷḷi ivaṟṟil kavaṉam celutti ivi māṇākkarkaḷiṭam vaḷara vāyppukaḷi ēṟpaṭuttit tarutal vēṇṭum.
maṉavaḷarcci: aṟivu vaḷarcci
maṉam aṟivukkūṟu, uṇarccikkūṟu muyaṟcikkūṟu eṉṟa muk kūṟukaḷikkoṇṭatu. muyaṟcik kūṟiṉi uḷḷakkiḷarcci kaḷiṉ vaḷarcci yilum kaṇṭōm. īṇṭu eñciyuḷḷa aṟivu vaḷarcciyiṉik kāṇpōm.
mēlē uṭal vaḷarcciyiṉik kuṟippiṭuṅkāl narampu maṇṭalattiṉ vaḷarcciyiyum mūḷiyiṉ vaḷarcciyiyum kuṟippiṭṭōm. mutal nāṉku vayatil mūḷi eṭiyil ciṟanta vaḷarcci peṟukiṉṟatu eṉṟum, caṟṟēṟakkuṟiya 20-ām vayatil muḻu vaḷarcciyiyum eyti viṭukiṉṟatu eṉṟum kaṇṭōm. maṉavaḷarcci mūḷi vaḷarcciyiyum anupavattiyum cārntatu. uṟṟunōkkumiṭattu pulaṉ uṇarcci52, pulaṉkāṭci53, kaṟpaṉi54 cintaṉi55 pōṉṟa pala ceyalkaḷ tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. ivi taṉippaṭṭa paṭikaḷalla; āṉāl, toṭarpāka nikaḻpavi cila nilikaḷil cila kūṟukaḷ ciṟappākat tōṉṟum. ēṉiya vaḷarccikaḷip pōlavē iṅkum pākupāṭum orumippāṭum uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa. nāḷaṭivil aṟivu āḻattilum virivu aṭikiṉṟatu. maṉa vaḷarcciyil cila potuppaṇpukaḷi īṇṭuk kuṟippiṭuvōm.
aṟivu elli virital: putitākap piṟakkum kuḻantikku ulakam mikavum ciṟiyatē. uṭal tēvikaḷukkum uṭal nalattuṭaṉ oṭṭiya toṭarkaḷukkum uriya tūṇṭalkaḷukkum ēṟṟavāṟu maṭṭilumē kuḻanti tulaṅkukiṉṟatu. ataṉuṭiya viḻippu vāḻkkiyiṉ perum pakuti ivvaḷavē. piṟantu cila nāṭkaḷ kaḻinta piṉṉarē kāṭcip pulaṉum kēḷvippulaṉum ceyaṟpaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. aciyum poruḷkaḷum kural olikaḷum muṟiyē kaṇkaḷiyum kātukaḷiyum tākkukiṉṟaṉa. nāḷaṭivil kāṭcip pulaṉum kēḷvippulaṉum tūraccūḻniliyuṭaṉ atika mākat toṭarpu koḷḷukiṉṟaṉa.
kāla aṟivu perukutal: kuḻanti vaḷaravaḷara iṭattāl cēymiyiluḷḷa poruḷkaḷip pōlavē, kālattiṉāl cēymiyiluḷḷa nikaḻccikaḷum kavaṉam peṟukiṉṟaṉa. muṉṉiya anupavaṅkaḷum varuṅkāla eṇṇaṅkaḷum kuḻantiyiṉ aṟivil paṅku koḷḷukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṟkēṟṟavāṟu ceyalpuriyum āṟṟaliyum kuḻanti aṭikiṉṟatu. 
kuṟiyīṭukaḷip payaṉpaṭuttum tiṟaṉ vaḷartal: nāḷāka nāḷāka nikaḻcci kaḷiṉ pakutikaḷum kuṟiyīṭukaḷum kuḻantiyiṭam tulaṅkalkaḷi eḻuppu kiṉṟaṉa. pāl uruḷiyil karaṇṭiyiṉ oli kēṭṭavuṭaṉ kuḻanti uṇavi etirpārkkiṉṟatu. muṉṉāl ivvoli pālkuṭikkumpoḻutu eḻuntatu; annikaḻcciyiṉ pakutiyākiya oliyiliruntu muḻu nikaḻcciyiṉ poruḷ tōṉṟu kiṉṟatu. moḻi vaḷarcci tulaṅkiyatum kuṟiyīṭukaḷikkiyāḷum tiṟaṉ vaḷarat toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu.
piṟa māṟutalkaḷ: muṉṉiya anupavaṅkaḷip payaṉ paṭuttum tiṟaṉi yoṭṭip pala tiṟaṉkaḷ tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. nāḷaṭivil kuḻanti uṭaṉē payaṉtarum nōkkaṅkaḷit tavirttu nīṇṭa payaṉ tarum viḻupporuḷ  kaḷik kuṟikkōḷ kaḷākak koḷḷukiṟatu. kālappōkkil ciṟuvaṉ kuṟiyīṭukaḷit tāṉākavē kiyāḷum tiṟaṉip peṟukiṉṟāṉ. oru ciṟṟūriṉ varipaṭattip pārttō, māvaṭṭattiṉ vari paṭattip pārttō aṇmiyil mēṟkoḷḷavirukkum cuṟṟulāviṟkuriya tiṭṭattip pōṭukiṉṟāṉ; atiyē maṉattālum iyaṟṟit tiṭṭamiṭu kiṉṟāṉ.
maṉa vaḷarcciyil mēṟkūṟiya māṟutalkaḷ nikaḻuṅkāl, aṅkut taṉittaṉi nilikaḷ illi eṉpatu uṇartaṟpālatu. āyiṉum, cila vaḷarccip pōkkukaḷ oru camayam teḷivākavum piṟitoru camayam teḷivaṟṟum tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, maṉattil nikaḻum ceyalkaḷi īṇṭu ōraḷavu ārāytal iṉṟiyamiyāta tākiṉṟatu.
mutalāṇṭil pulaṉkaḷ ceyaṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa; avaṟṟiṉip payaṉpaṭuttum tiṟaṉ mikutiyāka vaḷarkiṉṟatu. iraṇṭām yāṇṭil pēccu mēṉmiyaṭikiṉṟatu. uṟumutal, maḻalic col, coṟkaḷ eṉṟa variciyil moḻi vaḷarkiṉṟatu. mutaliraṇṭu yāṇṭukaḷil kuḻanti pulaṉkaḷiṉ tuṇiyāl cūḻniliyi naṉku ārāykiṉṟatu. mūṉṟām yāṇṭil yār? etu? ēṉ? eṉṟu pala viṉākkaḷik kuḻanti viṭukkiṉṟatu. anta yāṇṭi viṉānili eṉalām. in niliyil moḻi vaḷarcci ciṟappeytukiṉṟatu. kuḻantiyiṉ nāvil mutaṉ mutal col eḻuntu oḷirvatu pattām tiṅkaḷilākum. 54-ām vārattiṟkuḷ iraṇṭu coṟkaḷum 66-ām vārattiṟkuḷ 7 coṟkaḷum viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. 86-ām vārattiṟkumēl ic coṟkaḷiṉ eṇṇikki virintu vaḷarkiṉṟatu. iraṇṭām yāṇṭil 250 coṟkaḷum, mūṉṟām yāṇṭil 850 coṟkaḷum, nāṉkām yāṇṭil 1,500 coṟkaḷum, aintām yāṇṭil 2,000 coṟkaḷum, āṟām yāṇṭil 2,500 coṟ kaḷum viḷaṅku kiṉṟaṉa. aṟivup parappilum āḻattilum vaḷarcci peṟu kiṉṟatu. ciṟuvaṉ coṟkaḷik kiyāḷuvatiliruntu avaṉukku avaṟṟiṉ tiṭṭamāṉa aṟivu ēṟpaṭṭuviṭṭatu eṉṟu karutalā kātu; poruḷ viḷaṅkāta pala coṟkaḷik kiḷip piḷḷi pōl colluvāṉ. mutalil avaṉ poruḷkaḷip paṟṟik kūṟum ilakkaṇam avaṟṟiṉ payaṉit taḻuviyatākavē irukkum. (e-ṭu.) katti-veṭṭukiṟatu; tāy-pālūṭṭupavaḷ. muḻumiyāṉa ilakkaṇam nāḷaṭiviltāṉ tōṉṟum.
aṟivuc ceyalkaḷil mutalāvatu pulaṉ uṇarcci 56 yākum. aḵtu oli, oḷi pōṉṟa paṇpukaḷip pulaṉkaḷik koṇṭu aṟitalākum. āṉāl, pulaṉkāṭci (ñanacunayīviṭin) eṉpatu ippaṇpukaḷiyuṭiya poruḷkaḷi aṟital ākum. (e-ṭu.) iḵtu oru elumiccampaḻam eṉpatu pulaṉkāṭci. muṉṉiya anupavattik koṇṭu pulaṉ uṇarcciyi viḷakkuvatu pulaṉ kāṭciyākum. uruṇṭi vaṭivattiyō mañcaḷ niṟattiyō pārttal pulaṉ uṇarcciyākum. iṉṉoru eṭuttukkāṭṭu: oruvar taṉ aṟiyil tuyilkiṉṟār. yārō oruvar katavit taṭṭukiṉṟār. oliyalikaḷ uṇṭāki tuyil pavariṉ kāti aṭikiṉṟaṉa. avar eḻuntirātatāl, av voliyalikaḷi aṟiya muṭiyavilli. tirumpat tirumpa varum oliyalikaḷ avari eḻuppukiṉṟaṉa; ippoḻutu ōraḷavu oli kēṭkiṉṟatu. atu pulaṉ uṇarcciyākum. yārō taṭṭum oli eṉṟu aṟintāl atu pulaṉ kāṭciyākiṉṟatu. potu vākak kūṟiṉāl, pulaṉ uṇarcci eṉpatu tūṇṭalkaḷ pukuvāykaḷi aṭivatu; pulaṉ kāṭci eṉpatu nām pulaṉkaḷik koṇṭu poruḷkaḷi aṟivatu.
aṟivuc ceyalkaḷil maṟṟoru vaki niṉivu57 eṉpatu. muṉṉar naṭipeṟṟa nikaḻcciyip piṉṉar vēṇṭumiṭattu aṟivatu niṉivākum. nām pārtta poruḷkaḷ, mukaṅkaḷ, iṭaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟiyum, kēṭṭa coṟkaḷ, aṟinta takavalkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiyum niṉivil vikkāviṭil vāḻkki eṅṅaṉam naṭipeṟum? niṉivuc ceyal kaṟṟal58 iruttutal59, niṉivūṭṭutal60 eṉṟa mukkūṟukaḷik koṇṭatu. ciṟuvarkaḷukku veku virivil niṉivu uṇṭākiṉṟatu. oru camayam aṇṭi vīṭṭiṟkup pōṉa pōtu mūliyil ōriṭattil muṟukku vittiruntatip pārtta ciṟuvaṉ cila nāṭkaḷukkup piṟaku aṅkup pōka nēriṭṭāl, atē mūliyil muṟukku irukkum eṉa niṉittuk kēṭkiṉṟāṉ.
kavaṉamum61 ōr aṟivucceyalē. atu tērnteṭutta uṟṟu nōkkal. ṭuṭ62 eṉpār kūṟṟuppaṭi kavaṉam eṉpatu, oru poruḷ allatu karuttiṉmītu toṭarntu allatu muḻuvatum paṭittal niliyil maṉattiṉ iyaṟṟi niliyic63 celuttuvatu. ciṟuvarkaḷiṉ kavaṉam oṉṟiliruntu piṟitoṉṟiṟkup pāyum; piṟakutāṉ niliyāṉa kavaṉam uṇṭākiṉṟatu. oru ceyal allatu poruḷi neṭunēram kāṇpatu payiṟṟaliṉ payaṉ ākum. atu poruḷiṉmītu acciṟuvaṉ koṇṭa kavarcci64 allatu akkaṟiyip poṟuttatu.
maṉattiṉ āṟṟalkaḷil ciṟantatu cintaṉi65 yākum. cintaṉiyilum muṟiyāṉa vaḷarcciyuṇṭu. oru paṭattip pārttu mūṉṟāṇṭuc ciṟuvaṉ atiluḷḷavaṟṟi ovvoṉṟākac collukiṉṟāṉ; āṟāṇṭup pālaṉ appaṭattiluḷḷa poruḷkaḷi varuṇikkiṉṟāṉ; oṉpatāṇṭup piyaṉ paṭatti viḷakkukiṉṟāṉ. kuḻantip paḷḷikaḷ, toṭakkanilip paḷḷikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟil payilum ciṟuvarkaḷikkoṇṭu itaṉi aṟiyalām. itaṉik karuttil koṇṭutāṉ mutal mūṉṟu vakuppukaḷukkuriya moḻippāṭa nūlkaḷiṉ mutaliluḷḷa cila pāṭaṅkaḷ amikkappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. cintaṉikkuk karuttup poruḷkaḷ tēvi. pulaṉkāṭciyiliruntu potuuṇarvu66 uṇṭākiṟatu. nāy eṉṟa potu uṇarvu evvāṟu uṇṭā kiṉṟatu eṉṟu kāṇpōm. oru kuḻanti oru nāyi muḻu vaṭivamākavē kāṇkiṉṟatu; atippaṟṟiya ōr anupavam peṟukiṉṟatu. ati naṉku kavaṉikkumpoḻutu atippaṟṟiya aṟivu kuḻantiyiṭam naṉṟāka vaḷarkiṉṟatu. mutalil aṭiyum pulaṉkāṭciyāl nāyiṉ ōṭutal, kurittal, ataṟku nāṉku kālkaḷiruttal, taṉṉiviṭap peritāka iruttal, veṇmi niṟam ākiyavaṟṟi maṭṭilum aṟital kūṭum. aṭuttamuṟi oru vēṭṭi nāyik kāṇum poḻutu ataṉ ōṭṭam, kurittal, nāṉku kālkaḷiruttal, taṉṉi viṭap peritāka iruttal, karuppu niṟam ākiyavaṟṟi aṟiyum. inta iraṇṭu pulaṉkāṭci kaḷiliruntu kuḻanti mutal nāṉku paṇpukaḷi maṭṭilum koṇṭu nāyippaṟṟiya oruvita eṇṇattip (cūṭiviṭin) peṟukiṉṟatu. iṉṉoru muṟi iṉṉoru vaki vēṭṭi nāyip (ḷuyīyanniṭa) pārkkumpoḻutu an nāy ōṭuvatiyum, kurippatiyum, ataṟku nāṉku kālkaḷiruppatiyum, taṉṉiviṭac ciṟitāka iruppatiyum kāṇkiṉṟatu. ippoḻutu māṟupaṭṭa paṇpukaḷ maṟikiṉṟaṉa; oṉṟupaṭṭa paṇpukaḷ maṭṭilum oruṅkiṇintu urampeṟukiṉṟaṉa. nāyippaṟṟiya muṉṉiya eṇṇattiviṭa ipputiya eṇṇam valuvākavuḷḷatu. itil mūṉṟu paṇpukaḷē iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa; mūṉṟu paṇpukaḷ-pulaṉkāṭcikaḷ-oru potu uṇarvāka amintuḷḷatu. eṉavē, potuuṇarvu eṉpatu taṉippaṭṭa poruḷkaḷi vakikaḷākavum, ciṟappup poruḷkaḷip potup poruḷākavum, pala poruḷkaḷi oru poruḷākavum aṟiyavalla āṟṟalākum. pūṉiyiṉam eṉṟa oru potu uṇarvu pūṉikaḷ, ciṅkaṅkaḷ, pulikaḷ, ciṟuttip pulikaḷ, kāṭṭup pūṉikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ pulaṉkāṭcikaḷiṉ ōr uttamap potukkāraṇiyākum; avaṟṟiṭam potumiyākavuḷḷa paṇpukaḷ yāvum potu uṇarvā kiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, pulaṉkaḷiṉ muṉṉart taṉittut tōṉṟuvatu pulaṉkāṭci; maṉattiṉkaṇ potuvākak kāṇpatu potu uṇarvu. potu uṇarvi māṉatak kāṭci eṉṟum vaḻaṅkuvar.
kaṟpaṉi67 eṉpatu iṉṉoru aṟivuc ceyalākum. kaṟpaṉi eṉpatu pulaṉkaḷukku eṭṭāta poruḷkaḷi aṟivatu. pulaṉkaḷi uṟuttum poruḷkaḷi aṟivatu pulaṉ kāṭci. eṉ vīṭṭiṟku etirēyuḷḷa kōviliṉ kōpurattik kāṇpatu pulaṉkāṭci; irācarācacōḻaṉ eṭutta tañcik kōpuratti maṉattiṉkaṇ pārppatu kaṟpaṉi. pulaṉkāṭciyil veḷip poruḷkaḷ maṉattiṉ ceyali eḻuppukiṉṟaṉa; kaṟpaṉiyil maṉattiṉ toḻil uḷḷiruntē eḻukiṉṟatu. ciṟuvarkaḷiṉ viḷiyāṭṭukaḷil tōṉṟum oruvitak kaṟpaṉi pāvaṉi68 eṉpatu. ciṟuvarkaḷiṉ viḷi yāṭṭil nāṟkāli kappalākiṉṟatu; ceṅkaṟpoṭi veṭimaruntākiṉṟatu. ūcal palaki irayil vaṇṭiyākiṉṟatu. avarkaḷ viḷiyāṭṭil vilaṅkukaḷum pēcukiṉṟaṉa. cila camayam avarkaḷ vaṇṭiyōṭṭikaḷākavum, pāṟkārarkaḷākavum, āciriyarkaḷākavum iṉṉum tam maṉam pōṉa pōkkil palavāṟākavum uṇmi ulakuṭaṉ ottu naṭikkiṉṟaṉar. iṅṅaṉam maṉattiṉ aṟivuk kūṟu pulaṉ uṇarcci, pulaṉkāṭci, māṉatak kāṭci, niṉivu, cintaṉi ākiyavaṟṟiṉ mūlam tulaṅki vaḷarukiṉṟatu.
kuḻantiyiṉ vaḷarcciyic cariyāka aṟivataṟku ati uṭal vaḷarcci, uḷḷakkiḷarcci vaḷarcci, camūkap paṇpu vaḷarcci, aṟivu vaḷarcci eṉa nāṉku pakutikaḷākak kūṟiṉa pōtilum avi aṅṅaṉam taṉittaṉiyāka vaḷarccipeṟa villi. kuḻanti ellāt tuṟikaḷilum cērntē vaḷar kiṉṟatu eṉpati nām avaciyam aṟitalvēṇṭum.


vaḷarccip paruvaṅkaḷ
kuḻantiyiṉ vaḷarcci toṭarcciyāka iruntālum atil cila mukkiya māṉa paruvaṅkaḷi uḷaviyalaṟiñarkaḷ pakuttup pēcuvar. avi varumāṟu:
kuḻavip paruvam69 0-2ṅū allatu 3 vayatu.
muṉ-piḷḷip paruvam70 3-6 allatu 7vayatu: maṉavaḷarccik kuriyatu.
nilimāṟu paruvam71 6 allatu 7-8 vayatu: uṭalvaḷarccik kuriyatu.
piṉ-piḷḷip paruvam72 8-12 vayatu: maṉavaḷarccikkuriyatu.
muṉ-kumarap paruvam73 12-14 vayatu: uṭalvaḷarccikkuriyatu.
piṉ-kumarap paruvam74 14-18 vayatu: maṉavaḷarccikkuriyatu.
cila camayam nilimāṟu paruvatti naṭup piḷḷip paruvam75 eṉavum kuṟippiṭuvatuṇṭu.
iṅṅaṉam kuḻantiyiṉ vaḷarcciyip pala paṭikaḷākap pirittālum vaḷarcci āṅkāṅku ovvoru paṭiyilum muṭi veytip piṉpu putitākat toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu eṉṟu karututal tavaṟu; aḵtu orē toṭarcciyākavē pōkiṉṟatu. oru paruvattiṟkuriya ciṟappiyalpukaḷ piṟa paruvaṅkaḷil kāṇap peṟā eṉṟō, ovvoru paruvattiṟkuriya ciṟappiyalpukaḷ muḻuvatum maṟinta piṟakutāṉ ataṟkaṭutta paruvattiṟkuriya ciṟappiyal pukaḷ tōṉṟum eṉṟō karutavēṇṭā. mēlum, ovvoru paruvattiṟkuriya vayatu elliyiyum maṟimoḻi yākak koḷḷavum vēṇṭiyatilli. ivi yāvum kuḻantikkuk kuḻanti māṟak kūṭiyavi; ivi yāvum kuḻantikaḷiṉ kuṭivaḻiyiyum avi vaḷarntuvarum cūḻniliyiyum poṟut tēyirukkum. ovvoru paruvattiṉ ciṟappiyalpukaḷic ciṟitu kāṇpōm.*
kuḻavip paruvam (0-3) vayatu
ipparuvattil ēṟpaṭum vaḷarcci vivaraṅkaḷi āṅkāṅku muṉpē kuṟittuḷḷōm. itu kuḻanti perumpālum peṟṟō ric cārntirukkum paruvam. ip paruvattilum kuḻanti aṉṉiyiṉ parāmarippilirukkum. itil kuḻanti virivāṉa vaḷarcci peṟukiṉṟatu. uṭal vēkamāka vaḷarkiṟatu. iyakka āṟṟalum tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. kuḻanti paṭippaṭiyākat taci iyakkaṅkaḷi oṉṟupaṭuttavum taṉṉuṭiya uṟuppukaḷi oru nōkkattiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttavum kaṟṟuk koḷkiṉṟatu. ip paruvattil kuḻantiyiṉ nōkkam yāvum taci, narampu iyakkaṅ kaḷiyum uṟuppukaḷiyum orumippaṭuttalēyākum. iyakkat tūṇṭalkaḷiyuṭiya poruḷkaḷ yāvum kuḻantikkuk kavarcci tarum. uṭalum uṟuppukaḷum ceyaṟpaṭuvataṟku vāyp piṉit tarum viḷiyāṭṭukaḷ yāvum kuḻantiyik kavarum. cātāraṇamāka ōṭutaliyum tāṇṭutaliyumkūṭa kuḻanti atikamāka virumpum.
ip paruvattil pulaṉkaḷ tulakkamaṭikiṉṟaṉa. putiya poruḷkaḷip pārppatālum, putiya olikaḷik kēṭpatālum, putiya poruḷkaḷik kiyāḷuvatālum kuḻanti toṭarntu putiya anupavaṅkaḷip peṟṟa vaṇṇamuḷḷatu. itaṉāltāṉ kuḻanti turuturu veṉṟu irukkiṉṟatu, taṉakku muṉṉuḷḷa ulakic catā ārāykiṉṟatu. iti yaṟiyāta mūttōr kuḻantikku kuṟumpuc ceyal atikam eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. kuḻanti puriyum ceyalkaḷāl ataṟkut tīṅku nēriṭāvaṇṇam atu cuṟucuṟuppāka irukkum vāyppukaḷit tarutal vēṇṭum; atu ceyaṟpaṭavēṇṭum eṉṟa viruppattic citittal kūṭātu. kuḻantiyiṉ vayatiṟkum āṟṟalukkum ēṟṟavāṟu eḷitil iyakkakkūṭiya poruḷkaḷ ataṟkuk kiṭikkac ceyvatu mūttōr kaṭamiyākum.
kuḻantiyiṉ viruppaṅkaḷum uḷḷakkiḷarccikaḷum tīvira māṉavi; āṉāl virivil māṟak kūṭiyavi. tāṉ uṇar vatiyellām ārppāṭṭaṅkaḷuṭaṉtāṉ veḷiyiṭum. eṉavē, nām atu vekuḷkiṉṟatā, poṟāmippaṭukiṉṟatā, makiḻkiṉṟatā, aṉpu koḷkiṉṟatā, añcukiṉṟatā eṉpati aṟintu koḷvatil ciramam oṉṟumilli. ipparuvattil uṇṇutal, uṟaṅkutal, kaḻivup poruḷkaḷi akaṟṟutal pōṉṟa uṭal nalattuṭaṉ toṭarpu koṇṭa ceyalkaḷil naṟpaḻakkaṅkaḷi vaḷarkkavēṇṭum.
muṉ-piḷḷip paruvam (3-6 allatu 7) vayatu
ip paruvattilum ciṟappiyalpākak kāṇap peṟupavi uṭaliyakkamē. ciṟu kuḻaviyiṭam tēṅkik kiṭakkum vaṟṟāta āṟṟalik kaṇṭu nām viyappaṭiyattāṉ ceykiṉṟōm. viḷiyāṭṭil tīviramāṉa kavarcciyuṇṭākiṉṟatu; tirumpat tirumpa naṭipeṟum ceyalkaḷik koṇṭa viḷiyāṭṭu kaḷiyē ciṟuvaṉ atikamāka nāṭukiṉṟāṉ. ip paruvac ciṟuvaṉ piṟaruṭiya kūṭṭuṟavil viḷiyāṭa virumpiṉālum, avaṉ viḷiyāṭṭu taṉiyāka viḷiyāṭak kūṭiyatākavē uḷḷatu. kutittal, ōṭutal, viraṭṭutal pōṉṟa viḷiyāṭṭukaḷ iṉpam tarupavi. uruḷi viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷmēl ciṟuvaṉukku viruppam atikam. ciṟuvaṉ tāṉākavē kuḷip pāṭṭik koḷḷa virumpukiṉṟāṉ.
pala piḷḷikaḷuṭaṉ kūṭi viḷiyāṭum viḷiyāṭṭu iṉpam tarum. āṉāl ciṟu kuḻukkaḷē amikiṉṟaṉa. ciṟuvarkaḷum ciṟumiyarum cērntu viḷiyāṭukiṉṟaṉar. ciṟuvarkaḷ tam naṇpar kaḷuṭaṉ maṟpōr toṭuttuc caṇṭiyiṭukiṉṟaṉar; tam āṇmiyi nilināṭṭa muyalukiṉṟaṉar. viruntiṉiyum kuḻuk kūṭṭaṅkaḷiyum virumpukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl, aṅku mūttōriṉ vitippaṭi naṭantu koḷvatilli. āciriyariṉ karuttukaḷukkum eṇṇaṅkaḷukkum atika matipput tarukiṉṟaṉar. naṉṉaṭattiyum tīya naṭattiyum māṟi māṟit tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa.
ip paruvattil kuḻantikaḷiṉ āṟṟal veḷippaṭuvataṟkum kaṟpaṉi ceyaṟpaṭuvataṟkum ēṟṟa vāyppukaḷi nalkutal vēṇṭum. maṉaviruppam, pāvaṉi viḷiyāṭṭu, pakaṟkaṉavu, mōkiṉik katikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟic ciṟuvarkaḷ atikamāka virumpuvar. tantiyākavum, pōr vīraṉākavum, āciriyarākavum, kutiriyākavum taṉṉip pāvittu viḷiyāṭuvatil ciṟuvaṉ elliyaṟṟa makiḻcciyaṭivāṉ; naṉavulakil taṉṉāl aṭiya muṭiyātavaṟṟiyellām kaṉavulakil peṟṟu iṉpuṟuvāṉ. cātāraṇamākak kāṇum iyakkaṅkaḷiyum olikaḷiyum tirumpattirumpa uṇṭākkuvatil ciṟuvaṉ atika viruppattik kāṭṭukiṉṟāṉ. inniliyil pāppā pāṭalkaḷ, cevilip pāṭalkaḷ, āṭṭap pāṭalkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟi mikavum virumpu kiṉṟāṉ. paṇpaṭiyāta ikkaḷiyāṭṭap pāṭṭukaḷē piṟ kālattil paṇpaṭintu kūttākavum iciyākavum, katipoti pāṭalkaḷākavum, piṟa ilakkiyaṅkaḷākavum mukiḻkkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu car. persinaṇ76 eṉṟa aṟiñar karutuvar. tirumpac ceytalum canta iyakkamum āḻntatōr uṇarvit tiruptip paṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. āciriyar ivaṟṟic cariyākak kiyāṇṭāl avi murukuṇar kaliyākavum, nalla naṭattiyākavum, variyaṟiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya paḻakkaṅkaḷākavum vaḷarum. ip paruvattil nīṇṭa pāṭa vēḷikaḷ kūṭā; iruppunili (ñaṭiḷavaracana)yum iyakka niliyum aṭikkaṭi māṟakkūṭiya vāyppukaḷ iruttal vēṇṭum.
ip paruvattiṉ ciṟappiyalpukaḷil oṉṟu viṭuppūkkam77 kuḻantikaḷiṭam atikamākat tulaṅkuvatākum. eṉṉa? eppaṭi? ēṉ? eṉṟa viṉākkaḷāl kuḻantikaḷ nammit tuḷittu viṭum. peṟṟōrkaḷum āciriyarkaḷum ivvūkkattic citikkātu takkavāṟu viṭiyaḷittuc camāḷikkavēṇṭum. perumpālum iv viṭuppūkkam poruḷkaḷi uṭippatil paṅku peṟu kiṉṟatu. poruḷkaḷic citittal avi eṉṉa eṉpatiyum eppaṭic ceyaṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatiyum kaṇṭaṟital paṟṟiyē nikaḻum. atē camayattil ciṟuvarkaḷ poruḷkaḷic citit talilum makiḻcci koḷḷukiṉṟaṉar eṉpatum uṇmiyē. viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷic citippatāl ciṟuvaṉiṉ narampu iṟukkam78 nīṅkukiṉṟatu eṉṟu karutalām.
ciṟuvaṉ vaḷara vaḷara tacikkaṭṭuppāṭu atikarikkiṉṟatu; kuḻanti poruḷkaḷip paṭippatilum kaḷippaṭikiṉṟatu. ip paruvattil kaḷimaṇ-vēli naṉmi payakkum; itaṉāl ciṟuvaṉukku iyakka vēlikaḷil vāyppu kiṭikkiṉṟatu. ciṟuvaṉukku maṉaniṟivu ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu; kaṟpaṉi vaḷarkiṉṟatu; mutirntōr ceyyum vēliyit tāṉ niṟivēṟṟi viṭṭatākap perumitam koḷḷukiṉṟāṉ. piṉpaṟṟal79 ip paruvattil perumpaṅku koḷḷum; itaṉāl paḻakkaṅkaḷ uṇṭā kiṉṟaṉa; piṉpaṟṟum ceyalum vāḻkkiyiṉ oru pakutiyāki viṭukiṉṟatu. eṉavē, nām ciṟuvarkaṭku nalla camūkac cūḻ niliyi amittut tarutal vēṇṭum; kuṟippākak kuḻanti atika nēram irukkakkūṭiya vīṭṭil nalla cūḻnili nilavum paṭic ceytal vēṇṭum.
uḷḷak kiḷarccikaḷip poṟutta variyil, ciṟuvaṉ taṉṉic cuṟṟiyē ellāc ceyalkaḷum naṭipeṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟum, tāṉ ceyvatip piṟar mecca vēṇṭum eṉṟum, virumpukiṉṟāṉ. curukkamākak kūṟiṉāl ciṟuvaṉ taṉ niṉi vākavē iruppāṉ. avaṉ uṇarccikaḷ ciṟu kāraṇattiṟkum  eḷitākak kiḷarnteḻum; virivākavum aṭaṅkiviṭum. ciṟuvaṉiṭam uḷḷap pakimiyē80 ēṟpaṭātu; piṟar ceyyum tīṅkukaḷi virivāka maṟakkiṉṟāṉ; maṉṉittum viṭukiṉṟāṉ.
nilimāṟu paruvam (6 allatu 7-8) vayatu
uyarattil oḻuṅkāṉa, metuvāṉa vaḷarcciyuḷḷatu. ipparuvac ciṟuvaṉ cuyēcciyāka irukkap paḻakukiṉṟāṉ. kuḻantip paḷḷiyi viṭṭut toṭakkanilip paḷḷikku varukiṉṟāṉ. etiyum peṟṟōriṭamiruntu etirpārppatilli; vīṭṭiyum atikamāka virumpukiṟatilli. paḷḷiyil kaṭṭup pāṭṭiṟku aṭaṅki oḻuṅkāka naṭantu koḷḷukiṉṟāṉ. ciṟuvaṉiṉ maṉattip paḷḷi atikamākak kavarkiṉṟatu; paḷḷi yilēyē atika nērattik kaḻikka virumpukiṉṟāṉ. ciṟuvaṉ poṟuppiṉi ēṟka viḻikiṉṟāṉ; caṟṟukkaṭiṉamāka vēli kaḷiyum ceyyum āṟṟalip peṟukiṉṟāṉ; avaṉuṭiya naṭattiyum uyarniliyi aṭikiṉṟatu.
ipparuvattil ciṟuvaṉukkup palavita nōykaḷ ēṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa; nōykaḷiṉāl atikam pātikkap peṟukiṉṟāṉ. kaḻinta paruvatti viṭavum iṉi varappōkum paruvatti viṭavum kaḷippiṉāl atikam tākkappaṭukiṉṟāṉ. ipparuva iṟutiyil mūḷiyum muḻu vaḷarcciyuṟṟu irukka vēṇṭiya eṭiyip peṟukiṉṟatu.
iṉṉum viḷiyāṭṭil taṉittaṉmi81 kāṇappeṟu kiṉṟatu; iṉṉum taṉ niṉivu māṟukiṟatilli. kaṟpaṉi mikavum vīṟuṭaṉ viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu; nāṭakak kaṟpaṉi maṉattik kavarkiṉṟatu. ippoḻutu ciṟunāṭakattip paṭittu naṭikkat tūṇṭalām. uṇarcci nilikaḷukkum kaṟpaṉikkum iḵtu ēṟṟa paruvamākum. ipparuvattil ciṟuvarkaḷukkuk kūṟappeṟum katikaḷ nalla muṭivuṭiyaṉavāka iruttal vēṇṭum.
eṭṭām āṇṭu avāvuḷḷa āṇṭākum. ulakameyp poruḷil viruppam uṇṭākiṉṟatu. kaṟpatil ārvam eḻu kiṉṟatu. ciṟuvaṉ uḷḷati uḷḷapaṭi aṟiya viḻikiṉṟāṉ. eṇṇuvataṟkuk kaṟṟuk koḷḷukiṉṟāṉ. kūṭṭalum kaḻittalum avaṉukku viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. kālam colvāṉ; oru tiṅkaḷ kūṭac colvāṉ. ovvoru iṭaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟu poṟup peṭuttup poruḷ vāṅkum tiṟaṉ ivvayatiliruntu peruku kiṉṟatu. taṉakku iṭum paṇikaḷukku maṟuppu kūṟukiṉṟāṉ. āṇ peṇ vēṟṟumiyippaṟṟiya ārāycciyum, kuḻantikaḷ evvāṟu vaḷarukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟa viṉāvum avaṉatu uṇmi uṇarum avāviṉik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. mūttōrkaḷ avaṉ viṭukkum viṉākkaḷukku uṇmiyāṉa viṭiyaḷikka vēṇṭum.
itaṟku muṉṉar kavarcciyuṟṟatāka irunta pāvaṉi ulakamum makiḻcciyaḷitta vaṉatēvatik katikaḷum ippoḻutu makiḻcci taruvatilli ippoḻutu avaṟṟik kūṟiṉāl atu uṇmiyā? eṉṟu viṉavukiṉṟāṉ; cintaṉiyāṟṟalum niṉivum ōṅkukiṉṟaṉa. payaṉpaṭum poruḷkaḷic ceyvatil tuṭippu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. takkaḷi, irāṭṭi, kattirikkōl, maṇveṭṭi, irampam pōṉṟa karuvikaḷik kiyāḷa virumpu kiṉṟāṉ. kaṭṭūkkam,82 tiraṭṭūkkam,83 piṉpaṟṟal84 pōṉṟavi ciṟantu viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. kāraṇa kāriyat toṭarpi aṟiya virumpukiṉṟāṉ. kavaṉikkum āṟṟal atikamākiṉṟatu; atika nēram oru poruḷik kavaṉikkiṉṟāṉ.
piṉ piḷḷipparuvam (8-12) vayatu
ipparuvam kiṭṭattaṭṭa māṟāta niliyiṉiyuṭiyatu. uṭal vaḷarcci yil vēkam kuṟikiṉṟatu. pal varicikaḷ tiruntukiṉṟaṉa. kuḷittal, tali vārutal pōṉṟa uṭal tēvikaḷukku atikak kavaṉam tarappeṟukiṉṟatu. palvēṟu viḷiyāṭṭukaḷ viḷiyāṭi palatiṟac ceyalkaḷ purivatāl nalla ōyvum tūkkamum iṉṟiyamiyātavi. apāyatti acaṭṭi paṇṇu vatāl aṭikkaṭi kāyappaṭukiṉṟāṉ.
ip paruvattil mūḷivaḷaravēṇṭiya aḷavukku vaḷarntu viṭukiṟatu. uṭalum naṉku vaḷarcciyuṟṟu etiyum poṟukkum āṟṟal kivarap peṟukiṉṟatu. narampu iṇippukaḷ naṉku ēṟpaṭṭu, tacikaḷil ottu iyaṅkutal uṇṭāki, elumpukaḷum valivip peṟukiṉṟaṉa. cerikkum uṟuppukaḷ, kuruti maṇṭalam, mūccu maṇṭalam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ uṟuppukaḷ yāvum nalla vaḷarc ciyuṟukiṉṟaṉa. vaḷarcciyil atikamāṟṟam terikiṟatilli; āṉāl vaḷarcciyuṟa vēṇṭiya niliyi eytiyiruppati aṟikiṉṟōm.
ciṟuvaṉiṉ nēram perumpālum paḷḷikaḷil kaḻikiṉṟatu. ciṟuvaṉiṉ maṉa vaḷarcciyik kuṟittum avaṉ camūkattil paḻakuvatik kuṟittum āciriyar kavaṉikka vēṇṭiyavarākiṉṟār. taṉ kuṭumpattiluḷḷavarkaḷit tavira, veḷiyār kaḷuṭaṉ ciṟuvaṉ mutal mutalākap paḻakum vāyppukaḷip peṟukiṉṟāṉ. itaṉāl palaruṭaṉ paḻakum akaṉṟa anupavam ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. eṇṇaṅkaḷum karuttukaḷum moḻi vaḷarc ciyum ciṟitu ēṟpaṭavē, ciṟuvaṉiṉ viṭuppūkkam ceyaṟpaṭat toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu. etiyum terintu koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟa avā ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. eṉavē, mūttōrkaḷiṭam viṉāmārikaḷip poḻinta vaṇṇamuḷḷāṉ. āciriyarkaḷum peṟṟōr kaḷum ciṟuvaṉukkut takka viṭiyaḷittu avaṉ aṟivi vaḷarkka vēṇṭum. ipparuvattil nacukkap peṟuvataṉālō aṉṟi, kavaṉak kuṟiviṉālō ciṟuvarkaḷ viṭuppūkkatti iḻakkum paṭi nēriṭṭāl, piṟkālattil avarkaḷiṉ aṟivu vaḷarcci taṭippaṭum; putuppōkkuṭimiyum85 aṟivu nāṭṭamum illātu pōyviṭum.
ipparuvattil ciṟuvarkaḷ piṟa piḷḷikaḷuṭaṉ kūṭik kulāvuvatil ārvaṅkāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. muṉ paruvattip pōl ciṟuvaṉ taṉṉantaṉiyaṉāka viḷiyāṭa virumpuvatilli. piṟaciṟārkaḷuṭaṉ cērntē viḷiyāṭu kiṉṟāṉ; palaruṭaṉ cērntu oru kuḻuvāka iṇintu viṭukiṉṟāṉ. perumpālum orē pālic cārnta ciṟuvarkaḷē ik kuḻukkaḷ amittuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar. ovvoru kuḻuviṟkum talivaṉ oruvaṉē iruppāṉ; perumpālum taṟcātippum uṭal vaṉmiyum mikkuḷḷavaṉē talivaṉāka amivāṉ. ip paruvattiluḷḷa ciṟuvarkaḷ tam collik kēṭpatilli yeṉṟum, catā viḷiyāṭac ceṉṟu viṭukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟum perumpālāṉa peṟṟōrkaḷ kuṟi kūṟuvati nām aṟiyāmalilli. ivvāṟu avarkaḷ eṇṇuvatum kuṟi kūṟuvatum tavaṟu. ippoḻutu tāṉ kuḻantikaḷ veḷiyulakippaṟṟit terintu koḷḷa viḻikiṉṟaṉar eṉpati avarkaḷ aṟital vēṇṭum. veḷic ciṟārkaḷuṭaṉ kūṭi viḷiyāṭuvatāl ciṟuvar kaḷiṭam camūka campantamāṉa cila paṇpukaḷ amikiṉṟaṉa. talivaṉukkuk kīḻppaṭital, kuḻuviṉ nampikkikkup pāttiramātal pōṉṟa kuṇaṅkaḷ ivarkaḷiṭam vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa. cila camayam cila takavalkaḷit talimiyāciriyarukkut taruvataṟkuk kūṭa ciṟuvarkaḷ maṟuppar; oru naṇpaṉik kāppāṟṟuvataṟkākavō allatu tām ēṟpaṭuttik koṇṭuḷḷa oḻukka neṟikaḷiṉ poruṭṭō ivvāṟu ceyyakkūṭum. inniliyil paḷḷi āṭciyiṉar ivarkaḷip pārāṭṭa vēṇṭumēyaṉṟi ivarkaḷit taṇṭikkac kūṭātu. virumpat takāta naṭattiyuḷḷa poḻutu ivarkaḷi nuṭpamākak kiyāṇṭu ataṉi nācūkkākakkaḷiya muṟpaṭa vēṇṭum. ētāvatu kaṭṭāyappaṭuttit tolli tantāl, an naṭatti avarkaḷiṭam nirantaramākavē paṭintuviṭum.
cāraṇar iyakkam, kuṭimip payiṟci, ilakkiyak kaḻakaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa vaṟṟiyum, kuḻuvāka iṇintu ceyalāṟṟum piṟavaṟ ṟiyum amittu ivarkaḷic ceyalāṟṟat tūṇṭavēṇṭum. tam vēliyit tāmē tiṟaṉāyum tiṟaṉip86 peṟṟiruppatāl, cila ciṟiya poṟuppukaḷit taralām. taṉṉampikki naṉ muṟiyil ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. kuḻuvuṇarcci ciṟantōṅkik kāṇap peṟukiṉṟatu. takkamuṟiyil ikkuḻu uṇarccikku vēli tarāviṭil atu peruṅ kēṭṭil koṇṭu celuttiviṭum. palvēṟu viḷiyāṭṭukaḷ, viḷiyāṭṭup pōṭṭikaḷ, kaḷiyāṭṭaṅkaḷ, nāṭakaṅkaḷ mutaliyavi ip paruvac ciṟuvar kaḷukku mikavum ēṟṟavi.
ip paruvac ciṟuvarkaḷiṭam kāṇum iṉṉoru ciṟappiyalpu paṭippil87 ārvaṅkāṭṭutal ākum. ettaṉi nūlkaḷik koṭuttālum avaṟṟi virivil paṭittu muṭittuviṭuvar. tāymoḻikkuc ciṟitaḷavu ēṟṟaṅ koṭuttiruppatāl ip poḻututāṉ pala nūlkaḷ veḷivarukiṉṟaṉa. nam nāṭṭip poṟuttamaṭṭilum innūlkaḷiyellām ciṟuvarkaḷ vāṅkum niliyil illi; peṟṟōrkaḷiṉ vaṟumi niliyē itaṟku mutaṟkāraṇamākum. paḷḷikaḷ tām ōraḷavu nalla nūlkaḷi atika eṇṇikkiyil vāṅkivittup paṭikkum vāyppukaḷi ēṟpaṭuttit tarutal vēṇṭum. ipparuvattil paṭippil cuvi vaḷarttalilum āciriyarkaḷ kavaṉamāka iruntal vēṇṭum.
ipparuvattiltāṉ ciṟuvarkaḷiṭam vīra-vaḻipāṭu88 talikāṭṭukiṉṟatu. ivartāṉ mikavum virumpattakkavar eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭavarikkāṭṭi avarip piṉpaṟṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttutal peruntavaṟu. itil āciriyarkaḷ vaṟ puṟuttum aḷavukkuk kavaṉam celuttalākātu. ceyalālum cintaṉiyālum makkaṭkum ulakiṟkum peruntoṇṭāṟ ṟiyavarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷik kuṟippiṭalām; avarkaḷiṉ vāḻkki varalāṟukaḷip paṟṟiya nūlkaḷip paṭikkum vāyppukaḷi ēṟpaṭuttalām; cila nikaḻccikaḷi nāṭakaṅkaḷāka eḻuti naṭikkavum tūṇṭalām.
kumarap paruvam (12-18) vayatu
muṉ-kumarap paruvamum piṉ-kumarap paruvamum cērntē ārāyap peṟukiṉṟatu. piḷḷip paruvattiṟkum muḻu vaḷarcci peṟṟa mutirnta paruvattiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa paruvamē kumarap paruvam eṉpatu. ipparuvattiṉ toṭakkattiyum muṭiviyum aṭiyāḷaṅ kāṭṭit tiṭṭamākac cuṭṭik kūṟuvatu iyalātu. ipparuvamē peṟṟōrkaḷukkum āciriyarkaḷukkum tali valiyit taruvatu. avarkaḷ māṇākkarkaḷip paṟṟiya, avarkaḷ tarum pala piracciṉikaḷic camāḷikka vēṇṭiyavar kaḷākiṉṟaṉar.
uṭal vaḷarcciyum tiṟaṉkaḷum: ipparuvattil virivāṉa uṭal vaḷarcci tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. uyaramum, uṭiyum ucca niliyi aṭikiṉṟaṉa. tacikaḷ vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa. uṭal uṟuppu māṟupāṭukaḷ 12 vayatiliruntu 14 vayatiṟkuḷ toṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ipparuvat toṭakkattil ciṟuvaṉuṭiya uṭaluṟuppukaḷiṉ amippukaḷum avaṟṟiṉ toḻilkaḷum naṉku oḻuṅkupaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. uṭaluṟuppukaḷ palavum tammaḷavil porutta muṟṟut tacivalimiyiyum vēli ceyyum tiṟamiyiyum peṟukiṉṟaṉa. mutircci peṟṟavaṉuṭiya uyarattil pattil oṉpatu paṅku uyaramum, mūṉṟil iraṇṭu paṅku eṭiyum patiṉāṟām vayatil kāṇappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. elumpukaḷ nīḷkiṉṟaṉa; mārpukkūṭu virikiṉṟatu. itayam, nuriyīral pōṉṟa akavuṟuppukaḷ vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa. mikunta virivil uṭal vaḷarcci ēṟpaṭuvatāl, pala uṟuppukaḷum oṉṟupaṭṭuc ceyaṟpaṭuvatil taṭumāṟṟam ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. iḷiñaṉuṭiya ceyalkaḷum caṟṟut tāṟu māṟākak kāṇappeṟalām. kālappōkkiltāṉ ceyalkaḷ oḻuṅkupaṭṭut tiṟamiyākavum cemmiyākavum amiyum. āṇ piḷḷikaḷiṉ kural oṭintu melliya kural valiya kuralāka māṟukiṉṟatu; iḷiñaṉ mellavō, urakkavō, iṉimiyākavō pēca iyalavilliyeṉṟu niṉikkiṉṟāṉ. eṭiyilō aḷavilō mūḷi māṟāviṭiṉum, amippil māṟutal aṭikiṉṟatu. eṇṭōkirīṉ curappikaḷ vēkamāka vēli ceyyattoṭaṅki āṇ, peṇ taṉmikaḷiṉ vaḷarccik ēṟṟaṉavāyutavukiṉṟaṉa. ciṟuvaṉukku mīci arumpukiṉṟatu. ciṟumikku iṭikkīḻpakuti virivaṭikiṉṟatu; pūppum aṭi kiṉṟāḷ. curukkamākak kūṟiṉāl āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum pakkuva niliyi eytukiṉṟaṉar. kālam, pāl (ḷano), taṭpaveppa nili, iṉam, vāḻkkic cūḻnili, pakuttaṟivu mutaliya pala pakutikaḷic cārntu innili ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. perumpālum āṇpiḷḷikaḷiviṭap peṇpiḷḷikaḷiṭiyē ēṟakkuṟiya iraṇṭāṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarākavum tiṭṭamākavum ipparuvam tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. cātāraṇamāka nam nāṭṭil peṇpiḷḷi kaḷiṭam 12-ām vayatilum āṇpiḷḷikaḷiṭam 14-ām vayatilum innili tōṉṟukiṉṟatu eṉalām.
aṟivu vaḷarcciyum ceyalkaḷum: ivi ipparuvattil putiyaṉavāka ēṟpaṭuvatilli. cintittal, caṅkaṟpittal, kaṟpaṉi ceytal, kavaṉittal, niṉivukkuk koṇṭu varutal pōṉṟa tiṟaṉkaḷ toṭakkattiliruntē kāṇappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉum, ipparuvattil ivi virivākat toḻilpurikiṉṟaṉa. kāraṇa kāriyam kāṇum tiṟaṉum naṉkuvaḷarkiṉṟatu. ākavē, pulaṉuṇarc cikaḷiṉāl karuttiyuṇarvatilum cintippatilum tiṟami mikutiyākat tōṉṟum. veṟṟuṇarvut tiṟaṉ atikarikkiṉṟatu. cevviyākac cintittuc ceyalkaḷil tōṉṟum payaṉkaḷi eṇṇi naṭattiyē mēṟkoḷvatil kūrtta mati kāṇap peṟalām. viḷiyāṭṭukaḷilum nūlkaḷip paṭippatilum maṉam mikutiyāka īṭupaṭum. kaṟpaṉiyāṟṟalum perukit tōṉṟum. pakaṟkaṉavukaḷ tōṉṟalām. poṟuppi ēṟkum maṉappāṅkum uṇṭākum. camūkap piracciṉikaḷil maṉam cellum. mutirntōr ceyal kaḷil ūkkam kāṭṭum nili tōṉṟum. etirkālattil tām mēṟkoḷḷavirukkum toḻilkaḷilum maṉa vūkkatti veḷippaṭuttuvar.
ipparuvattil iḷiñarkaḷ uṇmik katikaḷiyum tuṇikarac ceyalkaḷik kūṟum nūlkaḷiyum virumpuvar. putiyaṉa paṭittal, aṟiviyal ivaṟṟil ūkkam kāṭṭuvar. varalāṟu, vāḻkki varalāṟu, payaṇanūlkaḷ ivaṟṟilum viruppam uṇṭākum. peṇpiḷḷikaḷ ciṟitu vēṟupaṭṭa vakiyil ceyyuḷ, nāṭakam, putiṉam ivaṟṟil viruppattic celuttuvar. cila nāṭkaḷil irutiṟattārum vāḻkki muḻutum cuvi tarum orē vakiyāṉa ilakkiyattil nāṭṭam celuttuvar. eṉavē, ipparuvattil ivarkaḷukku nalla vaḻikāṭṭi, ek kālattiṟkum ennāṭṭiṟkum uriya ciṟanta ulaka ilakkiyaṅ kaḷil cuvitōṉṟumāṟu ūkkuvatu nalam payakkum.
ipparuvattil pakuttaṟivu vaḷarkiṉṟatu. maṉa uṟuti, aṭakkiyāḷum viruppam, ulakuṭaṉum kaṭavuṭ koḷki yuṭaṉum naṉku vāḻtal ākiyavaṟṟil nōkkam ēṟpaṭalām. taṉṉiyaṭakkum āṟṟal, eṇṇittuṇital mutaliyavaṟṟi mēṟkoḷḷutal, uyariya nōkkam, taṉviruppappaṭi naṭakka ūkkam, uṟutiyāṉa aṟivu, naṉṉaṭatti ivi mukkiyamāṉavi. tūyati aṟintu ataṉpaṭi nalloḻukkatti mēṟ koḷḷutal, camayappaṟṟu iviyum vaḷaravēṇṭum. ivaṟṟippaṟṟi eṭutturip pativiṭa naṭantu kāṭṭal vēṇṭum. mēlum kīḻppaṭiyāmi, taṉṉi mikamukkiyamāka niṉittal, piṟar taṉṉip pārāṭṭa virumputal, maṭimi, ūkkamiṉmi ākiyaviyum ipparuvattil kāṇappeṟalām, peṟṟōr viḻippāka irāviṭil kuṟṟac ceyalkaḷ, tīya naṭatti mutalāṉavi tōṉṟa ētuvuṇṭu.
camūka muṉṉēṟṟam: ipparuvattil camūka maṉappāṉmiyum puttuyir peṟukiṉṟatu. kuṭumpam, paḷḷi ivaṟṟip paranta camūkam oṉṟiṉ uṟuppiṉar eṉṟu ipparuvattiṉar uṇar kiṉṟaṉar. putiya ūkkaṅkaḷum, uṟavukaḷum, nōkkaṅkaḷum ēṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. camūkatti nāṭutal, tammiyotta vayatiṉaru ṭaṉ cēravirumputal, kaḻakam amittal, camūkattiṟkākat taṉṉi oṭukkik koḷḷutal, camūkattiṟkum caṅkattiṟkum pāṭupaṭutal ākiyavi yāvum talimi, naṭpu, poṟuppu. ottuḻippu, tiyākam ākiyavaṟṟi vaḷarkkum. ottuḻikka virumpuvatiyum, piṟarukkuḻippatiyum, piṟaruṭaṉ kūṭivāḻa muṉvaruvatiyum naṉku payaṉpaṭuttikkoḷḷalām. payaṉ paṭutta vaki yaṟiyāmal ivaṟṟi vīṇēviṭṭāl ivi neṟipiṟaḻvāṉa naṭattikaḷil koṇṭuceluttiviṭum. naṉku payaṉpaṭuttiṉāl ciṟantapayaṉi aṭiyalām. peṟṟōrkaḷ takkavarkaḷiṉ cērkkiyi uṇṭākkik kūṭā naṭpi vilakkutal vēṇṭum.
uṭalmāṟupāṭukaḷāl eḻum uḷaviḷivukaḷ: perum pālum ivviḷivukaḷ uḷḷak kiḷarccikaḷip paṟṟiyavi. avi mutircci peṟum pāluṇarcciyuṭaṉ ōraḷavu toṭarpu koṇṭavi. ipparuvattil eḻum uḷḷakkiḷarccikaḷ perumpālum uṟutiyaṟṟu irukkum; āṉāl, avi valiyuṟ ṟukkiḷarnteḻum. makiḻcci, pāluṇarcci, accam, ciṉam, poṟāmi, pēravā ākiya ivi poṅkiyeḻuntu oṉṟō ṭoṉṟu mōtum iyalpilum aḷavilum ivi kuḻanti yuṇarccikaḷiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭavi. ikkuḻappamāṉa kālat til palvēṟu tūṇṭalkaḷāl ēṟpaṭum eṇṇaṅkaḷiyum ceyalkaḷiyum māṟṟum uṇarcci nili uṟutiyillātatu. iḷiñaṉ oru camayam taṉṉikkaṇṭu maṉaniṟivōṭu makiḻ kiṉṟāṉ; maṟucamayam tīrāttuyarattil mūḻkiyavaṉāykkāṇap peṟukiṉṟāṉ. oru camayam vīṇperumi pārāṭṭukiṟāṉ; uṭaṉē eḷitil maṉattaḷarcci peṟukiṉṟāṉ. oru kaṇam atikārap piriyaṉākak kāṇappeṟukiṉṟāṉ; aṭutta kaṇam mikat tāḻmiyuṭiyavaṉākak kāṇappeṟukiṉṟāṉ. oru camayam ākkiramikkum iyalpu mītūrntu niṟkiṉṟatu; aṭuttu kōḻit taṉmi mēlōṅkukiṉṟatu. oru maṇittuḷi ullāca mākavum, piṟaku ūkkamaṟṟavaṉākavum ākiṉṟāṉ. orucamayam viṭutaliyuṇarcci mēlōṅki niṟkiṉṟatu; aṭuttu muṟṟilum piṟaric cārntavaṉākac kāṇappeṟukiṉṟāṉ. oru camayam piṟar nalattip pōṟṟukiṉṟāṉ; maṟucamayam. taṉṉalam pēṇukiṉṟāṉ. mottattil putitāyttōṉṟum uṇarcci kaḷiyum uḷtuṭippukaḷiyum aṭakkipporuttamuṟamuṭiya villi. innilimi palvēṟu vaḻikaḷil veḷiyākiṉṟatu. iḷiñaṉ evvitak kaṭṭuppāṭṭiyum mīṟa virumpum puraṭcik kāraṉākat tōṉṟukiṉṟāṉ.
ipparuvattil iṉapperukka uṟuppukaḷiṉ toḻil virivaṭikiṉṟatu. kātal uṇarcci piṟakkiṉṟatu. aḻakilum vaṉappilum kavarcciyilum ipparuvattiṉar īṭupaṭukiṉṟaṉar. takunta cūḻniliyil takka kaṇkāṇippuṭaṉ iḷiñarkaḷ piṟapālārōṭu iṇaṅkuvatu ōraḷavu naṉmi payakkum. oruva rōṭu oruvar aḷavaḷāvutalāl pala naṟpayaṉkaḷi aṭi kiṉṟaṉar. makaḷir nitāṉattiyum taṉṉampikkiyiyum perun taṉmiyiyum aṭikiṉṟaṉar. mutirntavar peṇkaḷiṭam kāṭṭum mariyātiyiyum naṉṉayattiyum kāḷiyar ip paruvattil kāṇpikkiṉṟaṉar. taṅkaḷuṭiya tōṟṟattiyum naṭiyiyum peṇkaḷ maṉattil patiyac ceyya muyalkiṉṟaṉar. irupālārum tamakkuḷ cariyāṉa, peruntaṉmiyāṉa, ēṟṟa maṉōpāvaṅkaḷik kāṭṭik koḷḷukiṉṟaṉar. inniliyil pālkalvi89 kaṟpittal iṉṟiyamiyātatu eṉpar aṟiñar.
uṭalmāṟṟaṅkaḷukkēṟpap poruttappāṭu: ipparuvat til uṭalil ēṟpaṭum māṟupāṭukaḷ viyakkat takkavi. iḷiñaṉ taṉ uṭal vaḷarcci māṟṟattit tāṉē uṇarntavaṉāki, uṭal valimi yiyum āṇmiyiyum peṟuvatil ūkkaṅkoṇṭu palavaki viḷiyāṭṭukaḷ, pōṭṭip pantayaṅkaḷ, vēlikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟil īṭupaṭukiṉṟāṉ. ataṉāl toḻil purikiṟa tiṟamiyum cemmiyum mikukiṉṟaṉa. ātalāltāṉ paḷḷikaḷil naṟpayaṉ viḷikkum palavaki vēlikaḷ, viḷiyāṭṭukaḷ, cāraṇar iyakkam, kuṭimip payiṟci, makiḻccic celavu mutaliyavikaḷil ciṟuvar ciṟumiyarik kalantu koḷḷumāṟu ceykiṉṟaṉar. āyiṉum, aḷavukku mīṟiṉa vēliyaḷippatu tīṅku payakkum. pōtiya aḷavu ōyvu, nalla uṇavu, uṭalnalam ivi tēviyākum.
makaḷiriṭattilum ittaṉṉuṇarvu tōṉṟi, uṭalip paṟṟiya ūkkam ōraḷavu kāṇappaṭum. avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ uṭal tōṟṟattil mikka kavaṉam celuttuvar. uṭal nalattiyum uṭalaḻakiyum vaḷarkkumeṉat tāṅkaḷ karutum uṭaṟpayiṟci kaḷ avarkaḷatu maṉattik kavarum. vaṭivaḻakilum āṭiyilum avarkaḷ kāṭṭum kavaṉattip peṟṟōr uḷḷavāṟu uṇartal iṉṟiyamiyātatākum. taṅkaḷuṭiya peṇ maṇikkaṇakkil kaṇṇāṭiyiṉ muṉ niṟpatikkoṇṭu avar keṭṭu viṭṭāḷ eṉṟu karututal ākātu. utaṭukaḷukku niṟamūṭṭi, iru caṭikaḷ pōṭṭu āṭiyaṇi mutaliyavaṟṟāl pakaṭṭuc ceytu koḷḷutalō allatu ataṟku nērmāṟāṉa nākarikamaṟṟa muṟiyil āṭiyaṇintu iruttalō ivviraṇṭum tavirkka vēṇṭiyaviyākum. viruntu, viḻā pōṉṟa nāṭkaḷukkut takunta āṭikaḷ taṉakku illiyeṉṟō, maṟṟap peṇkaḷ aṇintirukkum āṭi kaḷippōl taṉakku illiyeṉṟō oru peṇ varuntakkūṭum.
iḷiñaṉum ipparuvattil taṉtōṟṟattil kavaṉam celuttak kūṭum. kuṟukiviṭṭa kālcaṭṭikaḷi aṇiya maṟuppāṉ. avvāṟu aṇintu koṇṭu veḷiyil cella nērumpōtu varuntuvāṉ. maṟṟa iḷiñarkaḷ uyarnta āṭi aṇintirukkiyil tāṉmaṭṭilum avvāṟu aṇiyavilliyē eṉṟu tuyarappaṭuvāṉ. itaṉip periyavarkaḷ parivōṭu kavaṉikkavēṇṭum.
kalvi niliyaṅkaḷil ellōrum orē vakiyāṉa āṭikaḷaṇiyac ceyyum kaṭṭuppāṭu ivvakiyāṉa ēkkaṅkaḷit tavirppatumaṉṟi, eḷiya peṟṟōrkkut tuṇiyākavum irukkum.
āciriyar, peṟṟōr poṟuppu: inta nerukkaṭiyāṉa paruvattiṉariṭam āciriyarum peṟṟōrum aṟivuṭaṉ kūṭiya ottuṇarcciyik kāṇpikka vēṇṭum. taṅkaḷ kumarapparuva vaḷarccikaḷiyum ceyalkaḷiyum niṉiviṟkuk koṇṭu varavēṇṭum. kīḻkkaṇṭa kuṟippukaḷ payaṉ tarupavi.
(1) uṭalukkō allatu uḷattiṟkō mitamiñciya vēli tarakkūṭātu. nalla uṇavu, pōtumāṉa aḷavu ōyvu, tūkkam iṉṟiyamiyātavi. ip paruvattil ciṟuvarkaḷum ciṟumiyarum mitamiñciya aḷavukku viḷiyāṭṭukaḷil īṭupaṭu talākātu. aḷavuṭaṉ ceyalkaḷil īṭupaṭavēṇṭum.
(2) kaḷippiyum maṭimiyiyum pākupaṭutti aṟital avaciyam. virintu vaḷarum kumaraṉ atika nēram aṟivu vēliyil īṭupaṭuvatu cāttiyamāṉataṉṟu. vīṭṭuvēliyit taruvatil āciriyar ikkūṟiṉi niṉivil vittut tarutal vēṇṭum.
(3) iḷiñaṉiṉ camūka vaḷarccikkum aṟavāḻkki vaḷarccikkum ip paruvam mikavum mukkiyamāṉatu. ip paruvattiṉar aṭiyum putiya nilikkut takka matippu tarutal vēṇṭum. ip paruvattil nuḻiyuṅkāl pālarkaḷāka iruntavarkaḷ ip paruvattik kaṭakkuṅkāl pālarip peṟṟeṭukkum āṟṟalu ṭiyavarkaḷākiṉṟaṉar. ip paruvattiṉariṭiyēyuḷḷa tavaṟāṉa kūccatti akaṟṟip pāl kalviyi añcāmal pukaṭṭa vēṇṭu meṉpatu taṟkālak koḷki. illāviṭil tavaṟāṉa eṇṇaṅ kaḷiyum paḻakkaṅkaḷiyum takāta vaḻikaḷil iḷiñarkaḷ peṟa muyaluvārkaḷ. pāl paṟṟiya ceytikaḷi mūṭivippatālum maḻuppuvatālum payaṉilli. āṉāl, pālaṟivuṭaṉ taṉṉaṭakkamum90 avaciyam eṉpatu namakkut teriyāmal illi. taṉṉaṭakkamillāviṭil pālkalvi apāyakaramāṉatu.
(4) ip paruvattil poṅki eḻum iyalpūkkaṅkaḷiyum uḷḷak kiḷarccikaḷiyum takka muṟiyil tūymi ceyya vēṇṭum. ilakkiyak kaḻakaṅkaḷ, nuṇkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷ, cāraṇar iyakkam, iḷiñar ceñciluvic caṅkam, camūkat toṇṭuk kaḻakaṅkaḷ, paḷḷi viḻākkaḷ pōṉṟavi itaṟkut takka vāyppukaḷi nalkum. ip paruvattil ici, ōviyam, vaṇṇavēli, kaṟpaṉi vaṉmi, tiyal, ilakkiyam, cēvi pōṉṟa ākka vēlikaḷil iḷiñarkaḷi īṭupaṭutti avar kaḷiṉ āṟṟali uyarmaṭimāṟṟam91 ceyyalām. ciṟuvarkaḷi maṭṭantaṭṭik kuṟikūṟāmal, avarkaḷ piṉniṉṟu ēṟṟa aṟivuriyum vāyppukaḷum nalkip poruttamuṟa utavutal mūttōr kaṭamiyākum. evvitattilum iḷiñarkaḷiṉ taṉ matippukkuk kuṟivāṉa muṟiyil mūttōr naṭantu koḷḷutal ākātu.
kuḻantikaḷi uṟṟunōkkum muṟikaḷ
payiṟṟutal tiṟampaṭa amiya vēṇṭumāṉāl, payiṟṟum poruḷiyum payiṟṟap peṟum kuḻantiyiyum naṉku aṟital vēṇṭum eṉpatu iṉṟiya uḷaviyal namakku uṇarttuvatu. kuḻantikaḷippaṟṟi nūlkaḷum aṟiñarkaḷum eṭuttuk kūṟupavi ōraḷavu vaḻikāṭṭikaḷākavē payaṉpaṭum; āciriyarkaḷē kuḻanti kaḷi uṟṟunōkki eḻuti vikkum kuṟippukaḷē naṉku payaṉpaṭum. nūlil kūṟappeṟupaviyum aṟiñarkaḷ urippaviyum cariyā eṉpatiyum cōtikkalām. cariyāka iruppiṉ koḷḷalām; illātavaṟṟit taḷḷalām.
kuḻantikaḷi uṟṟu nōkkum āciriyarkaḷ kuḻantikaḷ pāl aṉpuṭiyavarkaḷāka iruntalvēṇṭum. kuḻantikaḷit taṉittaṉiyākavum kuḻukkaḷilum kavaṉikkavēṇṭum. pāṭa nēram, viḷiyāṭumiṭam, ōyvu nēram, tōṭṭam, pūṅkā, kaṭi, kōvil, vīṭu, teru, kuḷakkari, nāṭakakkoṭṭaki, paṭak kāṭci, makiḻccic celavu pōṉṟa pala cūḻnilikaḷilum kuḻantikaḷi uṟṟu nōkkutal vēṇṭum. kuḻantikaḷ uṭalnili, aṟivu nili, uḷḷakkiḷarcci nili, camūkap paṇpu nili pōṉṟa pala kūṟukaḷi uṭiyavarkaḷātaliṉ, avi yaṉittiyum avaṟṟiṉ vaḷarccippaṭikaḷiyum uṟṟu nōkka vēṇṭum. avvappoḻutu kuṟippukaḷ eḻuti vittuk koḷḷutal mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatu.
ciṟappākak kuḻantikaḷ tāmākak kaṭṭuppāṭiṉṟip paḻakum iṭaṅkaḷil avarkaḷi uṟṟu nōkkal vēṇṭum. avarkaḷiṭam aṉpākavum parivākavum paḻaki mika vinayamāka naṭantu koḷḷa vēṇṭum. ivarkaḷ ceyalkaḷilum viḷi yāṭṭukaḷilum taliyiṭāmal taṟceyalākak kavaṉikka vēṇṭum. aṟiviyal cōtaṉikaḷil kuṟippukaḷ eḻutuvatu pōl naṭantati naṭantapaṭiyē eḻutul vēṇṭum. iṅkuc contak karuttiṟkē iṭamilli. ēṉiya āciriyarkaḷuṭaṉum kuḻanti kaḷip paṟṟi uriyāṭutal perum payaṉtarum.
uṟṟu nōkkalil iru muṟikaḷ uḷa. oṉṟu: orē kuḻantiyi nīṇṭa kālam uṟṟu nōkki avaṉiṭam putiya naṭattikaḷum kavarccikaḷum tōṉṟum kālam, avi vaḷarum virivu, paḻiya naṭattikaḷum kavarcci kaḷum maṟiyum kālam mutaliyavaṟṟik kavaṉittal. itu neṭukkāka uṟṟu nōkkal eṉa vaḻaṅkappeṟum. maṟṟoṉṟu: orē vayatuṭiya kuḻanti kaḷiṉ potup paṇpukaḷi uṇaralām. oru kuṟippiṭṭa vayatuṭiya kuḻantikaḷukkut teriyātavi, terintavi, avarkaḷ virumpum pāṭaṅkaḷ, veṟukkum pāṭaṅkaḷ, avarkaḷ mēṟkoḷḷumpoḻutu pōkkuc ceyalkaḷ, avarkaḷiṭam uḷḷak kiḷarccikaḷ tōṉṟum muṟi, avarkaḷ tīṭṭum ōviya vakikaḷ, viṭukkum viṉākkaḷiṉ vaki mutaliyavaṟṟik kavaṉittuk kuṟippukaḷ eḻuti vittal. itu kuṟukkāka uṟṟu nōkkum muṟi eṉa vaḻaṅkappeṟum.
kīḻē kāṇkiṟapaṭi acciṭṭa tāḷil kuḻantikaḷi uṟṟu nōkkuvatāl aṟinta takavalkaḷik kuṟittuk kuṟippukaḷ eḻutivikkalām.



taṉik kuḻantiyi uṟṟunōkkal
kuḻantiyiṉ peyar..............   vayatu................	kavaṉitta tēti...........................
paḷḷiyiṉ peyar...................   payilum vakuppu. ..............................
ai.kuḻantiyiṉ kuṭumpa nili
1. kuṭumpa uṟuppiṉariṉ toki:
(ya)	tantiyiṉ peyar:
(b)	toḻil:
(u)	varumāṉam:
(ṉa)	tāyiṉ peyar:
(na)	toḻil:
(ka)	varumāṉam:
(pa)	kuṭumpattiṉ potup poruḷātāra nili:
(h)	vāḻkkittiṟaṉ:
(i)	mūtta cakōtararkaḷ (peyar, vayatu)
(ta)	mūtta cakōtarikaḷ (peyar, vayatu)
(ma)	iḷiya cakōtararkaḷ (peyar, vayatu)
(ṭa)	iḷiya cakōtarikaḷ (peyar, vayatu)
kuṟippu: tirumaṇam āṉavarkaḷā, ākātavarkaḷā eṉṟa vivaraṅkaḷiyum ovvoruvarukkum kuṟippiṭuka.
2. vīṭṭuc cūḻnili:
(ya)	kuḻantiyiṉ uṇavu muṟikaḷ:
(b)	kuṭumpattil aṉpu - kaṇṭippu:
(u)	kuṭumpattil kavaṉam - kavaṉakkuṟivu:
(ṉa)	vīṭṭiṉ potuniliyum tōṟṟamum:
(na)	vīṭṭiṉ amippum aḷavum kuḻantiyi 						evvāṟu pātikkiṉṟaṉa?
(ka)	vīṭṭil kuḻantiyiṉ naṭatti:



aiai.kuḻantiyiṉ uṭalnili
1. potu:
(ya)	tūymi:
(b)	uyaram:
(u)	niṟam:
(ṉa)	parumaṉ:
(na)	eṭi:
(ka)	potuttōṟṟam:
(pa)	naṭamāṭṭam:
(h)	uṭaṟkōlaṅkaḷ:
(i)	paḷḷikku oḻaṅkāka varukiṉṟāṉā? aṅṅaṉam varāviṭṭāl kāraṇam? (uṭal nalakkuṟivu pōṉṟavaṟṟik kuṟippiṭuka.)

2. pulakkuṟikaḷ:
(ya)  	pārviyiṉ taṉmi: kaṟpalaki, nūlkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi payaṉpaṭuttumpoḻutu eṅṅaṉam vittukkoḷ kiṉṟāṉ?
(b)	cevippulaṉiṉ taṉmi: viṭuttaviṉāvukku uṭaṉuk kuṭaṉ patilirukkiṉṟāṉā? illāviṭṭāl, kāraṇam eṉṉa?

3. uṭalnalam:
(ya)	potut tūymi (kaṇ, kātu, paṟkaḷ, nakam, tali mayir, uṭi, pottāṉ):
(b) 	nakam eḻutukōl ivaṟṟik kaṭikkum paḻakkam:
(u) 	miṭṭāy mellum paḻakkam:
(ṉa) 	naṭiyiṉ tōṟṟam (nimirntā, āṭṭi āṭṭiyā, kuṉintā):
(na) 	kaḷippiṉ aṟikuṟikaḷ:
aiaiai. kuḻantiyiṉ aṟivu nili

1. moḻit tiṟaṉ:
(ya)	patiṉintu maṇittuḷi nērattil kuḻanti pēciyavaṟṟi yellām nāṭkuṟippu muṟiyil eḻutivikkavum.
(b)	coṟperukkam: vayatukku miñciya coṟkaḷip payaṉpaṭuktukiṉṟāṉā? avi yāvi?
(u)	pēcum muṟi: pēccu metuvāka uḷḷatā? viri vākavā? kuṟinta pēccā? nirampap pēccā? tirumpat tirumpac collum muṟi uḷḷatā? pēccil taṭumāṟ ṟam uḷḷatā? (vivaramākak kuṟip piṭavum).
(ṉa)	coṟkaḷit toṭarcciyākap payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟāṉā?
(na)	aṭiyiṟkaṇṭavaṟṟiṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ taruka.
	(i)		putuccoṟkaḷiṉpāṟ kavarcci:
	(ii)		peyarkaḷi eḷitil paṭittal:
	(iii)		palvēṟu poruḷkaḷippaṟṟip pēcukiṉṟāṉā, 		cilavaṟṟippaṟṟi māttiramā?
	(e)	pala viṉākkaḷ viṭukkiṉṟāṉā? yāvum porut 	 tamāṉaviyā? taṉ yōcaṉiyiṉmītu kēṭṭaṉavā? piṟar collik koṭuttaṉavā?
	(e)	potu uṇmikaḷik kūṟuntiṟaṉ:
	(e)	ūṉṟik karuttaṟiyum tiṟaṉ:

2. kaṟkum āṟṟal:
(ya) 	niṉivu nalla niliyil uḷḷatā?
(b) 	tērcciyiṉ aḷavu eppaṭiyuḷatu?
(u) 	kavaṉam eppaṭiyuḷatu? parākkup pārkkiṉṟāṉā?
(ṉa) 	kavaṉattik kalippavi yāvi?
(na)	kuḻanti ceyyum ciṟu kuṟumpukaḷ yāvi?
(ka)	pāṭavēḷiyil veḷiyē pōvataṟkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ yāvi?
(pa) 	cintaṉit tiṟaṉ:
(h) 	kaṟpaṉit tiṟaṉ:
(i)	mantamatiyuḷḷavarkaḷiṉ taṉittiṟaṉkaḷ, kavarccikaḷ:

3. ceyalālum pēccālum ēṟpaṭum pulavaḷarcci:
(ya)	niṟaṅkaḷik kāṇutal:
(b)	kaṭiṉattuvam uṇartal:
(u)	makkaḷik kavaṉittal:
(ṉa)	aḷavik kaṇṭaṟital:
(na)	amippik kāṇal:
(ka)	tolivi matippiṭal:
(pa)	kālattiṉpāṟ kavarcci:
(h)	cuṟucuṟuppum ūkkamum:
(i)	peyarik kūppiṭṭavuṭaṉ patilaḷittal:
(ta)	uṇaviṉ cuviyiyum maṇattiyumpaṟṟi avaṉ urippatu:

4. anumāṉat tiṟaṉ:
oru piracciṉikkut tīrvukāṇuṅkāl kīḻkkaṇṭa muṟippaṭi avaṉ ceyaṟpaṭukiṉṟāṉā?
(ya)	pirackiṉiyi uṇartal:
(b)	ataṟkiyinta vaḻikaḷi ārāytal:
(u)	cariyākat tōṉṟum viṭiyit terital:
(ṉa)	iv viṭiyic cōtittal:
(na)	muṭivukaḷ:

5. maṉap paṇpukaḷ: (eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ taravum)
(ya)	purintu koḷḷuvatil virivu:
(b)	purintu koḷḷuvataṟku vēṇṭiya ūkkam:
(u)	muṉṉiya nikaḻccipaṟṟiya niṉivu:
(ṉa )	kati, ici mutaliyaṉa:
(na)	aḻakuccuvi:
(ka )	aṟiviyal kiḷarcci:
(pa)	makkaḷiyum nikaḻccikaḷiyumpaṟṟiya kavarcci:

aiē. kuḻantiyiṉ uḷḷakkiḷarcci nili

1. eḻucci nili: (eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ taruka.)
(ya)	ciṟappāṉa naṭattiyi vivarikkavum:
(b)	iṉpa niliyilum tuṉpa niliyilum avaṉatu tōṟṟam:
(u)	kuḻantikku iṉpam payappaṉa yāvi?
(ṉa)	kuḻantikkut tuṉpam tarupavi yāvi?

2. etirūṉṟal nili: (eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ taruka.)
(ya)	kuḻanti aṭiyiṟ kaṇṭavarkaḷuṭaṉ veṟuppaṭinta pōtu eṉṉa ceykiṉṟāṉ?
	(i)	poruḷkaḷōṭu:
	(ii)	mutiyōruṭaṉ:
 	(iii)	piṟa kuḻavikaḷuṭaṉ:
(b) 	avaṉ aṉpi evvāṟu veḷiyiṭukiṉṟāṉ?
(u) 	accam, ciṉam, veṭkam pōṉṟavi eppoḻutu tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa?
(ṉa)	paricil, taṇṭaṉi, pārāṭṭu, ninti-ivi ov voṉṟiṉ etirviṉi ettakiyatu?
(na)	ottuṇarcci tōṉṟum vaḻikaḷ yāvi?
(ka)	cuvi yaṟivu ettakiyatu?
(pa)	maṉattaṭumāṟṟattik kāṭṭum paḻakkaṅkaḷ avaṉiṭam uḷḷaṉavā? avi eppoḻutu tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa?
ē. kuḻantiyiṉ camūkap paṇpunili
1. ēṉiya kuḻavikaḷmuṉ ivaṉatu ciṟappu naṭatti:
 (eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ taruka)
(ya)	kūṭṭuṟavu, naṭpu, uḷḷaṭakkam, kūccam, etirppu, ātik kam:
(b)	cama vayatuṭiyōr, mūttōr, iḷiyōr ivarkaḷiṭam eṅṅaṉam paḻakukiṉṟāṉ? (piṭivātam, ottuḻippu, ākkiramippu)
(u)	oru kuḻantiyōṭu viḷiyāṭutal, cila kuḻanti kaḷuṭaṉ viḷiyāṭutal, pala kuḻantikaḷuṭaṉ viḷi yāṭutal:
(ṉa)	oru kuḻantiyip parāmarittal:
(na)	tōḻami nāṭutal:
(ka)	kūṭṭattil cērtal, kuḻucceyalil paṅkeṭuttal, kuḻuvippaṟṟi akkaṟiyiṉṟi iruttal, tāṉākavē ceyalāṟṟutal:
(pa)	kūṭṭattil talimi vakittal, talimiyi naṉṉōkkaṅkaḷukkākap payaṉpaṭuttal, tīya nōkkaṅkaḷukkākap payaṉpaṭuttal, tīya muṟikaḷik kiyāḷutal, iṉimiyāl piṟari naṭattutal allatu valimiyāl āḷutal:
(h)	nīti uṇarcciyik kāṭṭutal:

2. periyōrmuṉ kāṭṭum ciṟappu naṭatti:
(ya) 	naṭpu, utavi, uḷ vāṅkutal, kūccam, etirppu, tōḻami, uṭimi:
(b) 	piṟaric cārntuḷḷāṉā? viṭutali uḷḷavaṉā?
(u) 	piṟaruṭaṉ eppaṭip paḻakukiṉṟāṉ? (oruvaruṭaṉā? cilaruṭaṉā? palaruṭaṉā?)
(ṉa) 	pārāṭṭu, kuṟṟaṅkūṟal mutaliyavaṟṟip peritum karutukiṉṟāṉā? pukaḻi nāṭukiṉṟāṉā?

3. piṟa naṭattikaḷ:
(ya)	poruḷātāra nili vēṟṟumi pārāṭṭap peṟukiṉṟatā?
(b)	anniyarkaḷiṭam kāṭṭum aṉpu, mariyāti, parikācam, upakārac cinti:
(u)	piṟar tuṉpattil irakkam, karvam, cirippu:
(ṉa)	tāḻvuṇarcci, uyarvuṇarcci, kōḷ collum paḻakkam:

4. kuḻantiyiṉ āṭikaḷ:
(ya)	pātukāvalukkum eḷiya iyakkaṅkaḷukkum taṉ utavikkum ēṟṟavāṟu uṭi amintuḷḷatā?
(b)	kuḻanti tuṇiyippaṟṟi akkaṟi koṇṭuḷḷāṉā?
(u)	uṭi avaṉ toḻilukku utavukiṉṟatā? taṭi ceykiṉṟatā?
ēai. vēlic ceyalkaḷum viḷiyāṭṭuc ceyalkaḷum

1. kivēliyil:
(ya)	ārvam irukkiṉṟatā?
(b)	kavaṉam irukkiṉṟatā?
(u)	cuyaputtiyuṭaṉ ceykiṉṟāṉā? piṟarip piṉpaṟṟic ceykiṉṟāṉā?
(ṉa)	vēlipaṟṟiya viṉākkaḷi aṭikkaṭi viṭukiṉṟāṉā?
(na)	piṟar utaviyi aṭikkaṭi nāṭukiṉṟāṉā? ōroru taruṇattilā?
(ka)	tāṉākap poruḷkaḷit terinteṭukkiṉṟāṉā? āciriyar naṭattutal tēviyākiṉṟatā?
(pa)	pala tiṟappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷit terikiṉṟāṉā? allatu oṉṟiyēyā? (eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ taruka)
(h)	poruḷkaḷiyō uṇmi niliyiyō payaṉ paṭuttuṅkāl paṭipputtiṟaṉ allatu puṉivuttiṟaṉ kāṭṭukiṉṟāṉā?
(i)	piṟarik kuṟṟam cāṭṭukiṉṟāṉā?
(ta) 	maṉaccōrvu ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatā?

2. tōṭṭa vēliyil:
(ya)	tōṭṭa vēliyil karuttic celuttukiṉṟāṉā?
(b)	ili, koṭi, ceṭi, pū-ivaṟṟil viruppam kāṭṭu kiṉṟāṉā? allatu utācīṉam ceykiṉṟāṉā?
(u)	tāṉāka aṟiyum āṟṟal uḷatā?
(ṉa)	pala viṉākkaḷi viṭukkiṉṟāṉā?
(na)	mēlum pārkkavum cintikkavum āval uḷatā?
(ka)	ōviyam variya viruppam uḷatā?
(pa)	cōtaṉi ceyya āval uṇṭā?

3. viḷiyāṭṭil:
(ya)	terinteṭutta viḷiyāṭṭuc ceyal, celaviṭṭa nēram, payaṉpaṭuttiya poruḷ:
(b)	kaṭṭaḷikkuṭpaṭukiṉṟāṉā? mīṟukiṉṟāṉā?
(u)	kaṭci nalaṉ nāṭukiṉṟāṉā?
(ṉa)	paraparappu, taṟperumi-uḷavā?
(na)	piṟar utavi atikam uḷatā?
(ka)	nērmi, orutalic cārpu uḷatā?
(pa)	alaṭciyam kāṇappeṟukiṉṟatā?
(h)	veṟṟiyilum tōlviyilum orē maṉappāṉmi yuṇṭā?
(i)	tuyarattilum makiḻcciyilum orē maṉappāṉmi teṉpaṭukiṉṟatā?

ēaiai. piṟa kavarccikaḷ
1. poruḷkaḷit tiraṭṭutal:
(ya)	epporuḷkaḷit tiraṭṭukiṉṟāṉ?
(b)	avaṟṟi eṅṅaṉam payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟāṉ?
(u)	avaṟṟi eppaṭip pātukākkiṉṟāṉ?
(ṉa)	tiraṭṭuvatil ārvam, uṟuti, viṭāmuyaṟci kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatā?
(na)	nāṭōṟum kācu kēṭkum paḻakkam uṇṭā?
(ka)	etil viruppam kāṭṭukiṉṟāṉ? (tiṉpaṇṭam, po ruḷ, vēli.)
(pa)	atikamākap payaṉpaṭuttuvatu viḷiyāṭṭuc cāmāṉ kaḷā? nulkaḷā? toḻiṟkaruvikaḷā?

2. poḻutu pōkku:
(ya) 	etil naṉku poḻutu pōkkukiṉṟāṉ?
 	(i)	viḷiyāṭṭu:
 	(ii)	kittoḻil:
 	(iii)	tōṭṭam:
 	(ie) naṇparkaḷ:
(b)	paṭakkāṭciyi atikam virumpukiṉṟāṉā? pōki ṉṟāṉā?
(u)	makiḻccic celavil atikam paṅku koḷkiṉṟāṉā?
(ṉa)	vīṇpēccil atikam kālaṅkaḻikkiṉṟāṉā?



iyal-4
kuṭivaḻiyum cūḻniliyum

kuḻantiyip paṟṟiya ārāycciyilum avarkaḷ kalviyilum eḻum mukkiya piracciṉikaḷuḷ oṉṟu kuṭivaḻi1, cūḻnili2 ākiyavi peṟum paṅku ākum. itil iru vēṟu karuttukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. oru cārār, kuḻanti kaḷiṉ vaḷarcci,
oḻukkam naṭatti mutaliyavi avarkaḷ peṟṟōri ṭamiruntum mūtātiyariṭamiruntum peṟum niliyāṉa cila kūṟukaḷāl muṟṟilum aṟutiyiṭappeṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kūṟuvar; avaṟṟi evvāṟṟāṉum acikka muṭiyāteṉṟu vātippar. piṟitoru cūḻniliyil peṟum vāyppukaḷip poṟuttavi eṉṟu cāṟṟuvar; cūḻniliyiṉāl aṉittayum kivarac ceyyak kūṭum eṉṟu cātippar. iṅṅaṉam kuṭivaḻikkum cūḻnilikkum-iyaṟkikkum3 vaḷarppukkum4-iṭiyē naṭipeṟṟuvarum etir vaḻakkil5 iru cārār kūṟum kāraṇaṅkaḷiyum ciṟitu ārāyvōm.
kuṭivaḻik kaṭciyiṉar kūṟuvatu: kuṭivaḻiyeṉpatu oru kuḻanti taṉ muṉṉōrkaḷiṭamiruntu peṟum meyppaṇpu kaḷum maṉappaṇpukaḷum ākum. itaṉāl kuḻanti uṭal amippil tantiyippōliruppataṉṟi, viruppukaḷilum veṟuppukaḷilum, tiṟaṉkaḷilum6 aṟitiṟaṉilum7, paḻakkaṅ kaḷilum oḻukkattilumkūṭa tantiyi ottuḷḷatu eṉpar. tāyippōla piḷḷi, nūlippōla cēli,’ makaṉu rikkum tanti nalatti tantiyar oppar makkaḷ eṉṟa kūṟṟukaḷiyum cāṉṟukaḷākak kāṭṭuvar. piṟavivaḻiyāka varum inta iyalpē kuḻantiyiṉ vaḷarcciyil mukkiya paṅkiṉip peṟukiṟateṉṟum, atuvē kuḻantipeṟum kalviyiṉ cāttiyak kūṟukaḷi aṟutiyiṭukiṉṟatu eṉṟum kūṟuvar. eṉavē, kuḻanti taṉ peṟṟōr kaḷiṭamiruntu peṟum cila tiṟaṉkaḷum kavarcci kaḷum ataṉ kalviyi varampukaṭṭi viṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, avi malarum taṉmiyum neṟiyum akkuḻanti vāḻum cūḻnili yāl eññāṉṟum celvākkup peṟuvatilli eṉṟum pakarvar. ivvāṟu kuṭivaḻikkut tarum mukkiyattuvattāl kuḻantik kalviyil cūḻniliyip paṟṟiya eṇṇam, muyaṟci, varuntiyuḻikkum kaṭṭuppāṭu ākiyavi yāvum payaṉaṟṟavi eṉak karutuvatāka muṭikiṉṟatu. avarkaḷ pāpar, civāji, irañciṭciṅ pōṉṟavarkaḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭi avarkaḷ evvaḷavu viṭāmuyaṟciyuṭaṉ tamatu vāḻkkiyil nēriṭṭa etirāṉa cūḻniliyi evvāṟu camāḷittaṉar eṉṟum, tammuṭiya cūḻniliyil ūkkam koṭukkum caktikaḷi evvāṟu muṟiyaṭittaṉar eṉṟum, tām cellum vaḻiyil kaṭumiyāṉa taṭikaḷum tollikaḷum iruntapōtilum evvāṟu āṭcipīṭattik kippaṟṟic ciṟappuṟṟaṉar eṉṟum cāṉṟukaḷi oṉṟukku mēloṉṟāka aṭukkuvar. āyirak kaṇakkāṉa iḷiñarkaḷ immātiriyāṉa cūḻniliyil iruk kattāṉ ceykiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl avarkaḷ yāvarum uṟcākat tuṭaṉ ceyalāṟṟuvatumilli; appaṭi āṟṟiṉālum tōlvi yiyē aṭi kiṉṟaṉar. oru cilar maṭṭilum iṅṅaṉam aruñ ceyalkaḷiṉ koṭumuṭi kāṇpataṟkum piṟa ciṟappukaḷi eytuvataṟkum kāraṇam, avarkaḷ piṟaviyuṭaṉ peṟṟa iyalpum mētit taṉmiyumē8 ākum. eṉavē, kuṭivaḻiyākap peṟum paṇpuk kūṟukaḷum9 piṟaviyilē yaminta iyaṟkip pēṟumē oru kuḻantiyiṉ ōḻukkam, naṭatti, āḷumi ākiyavaṟṟi iṟutiyāka aṟutiyiṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttuvar. viti yoṉṟu pōṭṭāl curi yoṉṟu muḷikkumā’. eṉpatu ivarkaḷ viṭukkum viṉā. itic cariyeṉa oppukkoṇṭāl paḷḷiyum āciriyarum vīṇ eṉṟu koḷḷa vēṇṭiyatāka muṭiyum.
cūḻnilik kaṭciyiṉar kūṟuvatu: uḷaviyal muṟippaṭi cūḻnili eṉpatu oruvaṉ karuvākiyatu mutal kālaṉ kavvum vari peṟṟuḷḷa tokutikaḷākum. uṇavu, vaḷarkkum muṟi, kalvi, ulaka anupavam mutaliyaṉa ellām itaṉuḷ aṭaṅkum. curappikaḷāl ēṟpaṭum māṟutal kaḷikkūṭac cilar cūḻniliyuṭaṉ cērttup pēcuvar. cūḻniliyippaṟṟi ik kūṟiya karuttu ceyaltiṟaṉ vāyntatu. oru poruḷ muṉṉē niṟpatāl māttiram cūḻniliyākiviṭa muṭiyātu. aḵtu oruvarit tūṇṭiṉāltāṉ cūḻniliyākum. aḵtāvatu, taṉiyāḷiṉ uṭalukkup puṟampēyuḷḷa, āṉāl avaratu pulaṉkaḷiṉ ellikkuḷ irukkum, ellāp poruḷkaḷum vicikaḷum aṭaṅkiya tokutiyē cūḻnili eṉpatu. ivvilakkaṇam cūḻniliyippaṟṟip potumakkaḷ koṇṭuḷḷa karutti viṭa mikap parantatu. vāḻkkic cuḻaṟciyiluḷḷa evvakit tūṇṭalkaḷum itaṉuḷ aṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
oru māṉiṭak kuḻanti palvēṟu cāttiyak kūṟukaḷuṭaṉ piṟakkiṉṟatu eṉṟum, atu maṉitattiṟaṉ ellikkuḷ aṭaṅkiya enta vitamāṉa tulaṅkalukkum uṭpaṭak kūṭiyatu eṉṟum cūḻnilik kaṭciyiṉar kūṟuvar. cātakamāṉa vāyppukaḷ iruppiṉ, maṉitaṉ itukāṟum cātittati yellām oruvaṉ cātikka muṭiyum. mētit taṉmi eṉpatu muṭṭāḷtaṉattip pōlavē cūḻniliyiṉ viḷivēyākum. kuḻantiyiṉ maṉam kaḷi maṇṇip pōṉṟatu; cūḻnili ataṟku enta vaṭivatti vēṇṭumāṉālum amikkalām. lākkē10 eṉpār karut tuppaṭi itaṉi oru tūymiyāṉa kaṟpalakikku oppiṭalām; anupavaṅkaḷ yāvum atil patikiṉṟaṉa. ik kaṭciyiṉar pāpar, civāji mutaliyōriṉ eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷil avaruṭiya muṉṉōrkaḷum vaḻit tōṉṟalkaḷum ēṉ avarkaḷip pōla aruñceyalkaḷiyum tiṟamikaḷiyum11 kāṭṭa villi eṉṟu viṉavuvar. ivarkaḷ yāvarum tāṅkaḷ vāḻnta kālattiluḷḷa camūka, poruḷātāra, araciyal celvākku kaḷāl tākkuṇṭavarkaḷ; avarkaḷ tikaḻntiruttal muṭiyātu. cūḻnili, payiṟci, kalvi ākiyavi maṉitaṉi eṅṅaṉam ākkukiṉṟaṉavō aṅṅaṉamē avaṉ vaḷarkiṉṟāṉ eṉpatu ivarkaḷ kūṟum vātamākum.
ākavē, kalvi eṉpatu oruvaratu uḷa, oḻukkac ceyalkaḷiṉ tiraṭci yākum. oru kālattil kāṭṭumirāṇṭiyāka vāḻnta maṉitaṉ perum pukaḻ vāḻnta paṇpāṭu, kali, aṟiviyal, meypporuḷiyal, camayam, camūkam pōṉṟa tuṟikaḷi anupavattālum, kaṟṟalālum, kalviyālum payiṟci yālumē vaḷarttuḷḷāṉ eṉpataṟku nākarikattiṉ varalāṟē cāṉṟāka niṟkiṉṟatu. avaṉuṭiya naṟpēṟṟiṟkuk12 kuṭivaḻiyē mutaṟ kāraṇamāka iruntiruntāl avaṉ irupatu nūṟṟāṇṭukaṭku muṉṉirunta niliyilēyē iruntirukkavēṇṭum. mēlum, vāḻkkiyil paḻikaḷicceytu ceytu pāḻākap pōṉavar kaḷum, takka ēṟpāṭukaḷālum vaḻiyamippukaḷālum putiya muṟiyil vāḻkkiyit toṭaṅki naṉṉiliyi eytiyuḷḷaṉar eṉpataṟkum pala cāṉṟukaḷ illāmal illi. eṉavē, cūḻniliyē oru maṉitaṉi ākkukiṉṟatu allatu aḻikkiṉṟatu; kuṭivaḻi eṉpatu kaṇakkiṟku eṭuttukkoḷḷa vēṇṭāta niḻalākiviṭukiṉṟatu.
iraṇṭu karuttukaḷum kalviyil celvākkup peṟṟiruttal: iru vēṟuturuva ellikaḷiluḷḷa inta iraṇṭu karuttukaḷum potumakkaḷi ṭiyē vērūṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. ik karuttukaḷ kalviyip paṟṟiya āciriyar kaḷiṉ maṉappōkkiyum aṟutiyiṭuvatāl, avarkaḷ kuṭivaḻi, cūḻnili ākiya iraṇṭippaṟṟiyum teḷi vākap purintukoḷḷutal iṉṟiyami yātatākiṉṟatu. kuṭivaḻiyē ciṟantateṉak karutuvōr kalviyik kiviṭu kiṉṟaṉar; ataṉi aṟavē puṟakkaṇikkiṉṟaṉar. tām etirpārttataṟku māṟākak kuḻantikaḷ kāṇappeṟiṉum, avarkaḷiṭam naṟpaḻakkaṅkaḷi uṇṭākka vēṇṭum eṉkiṟa tam muyaṟci tavaṟippōyiṉum, avarkaḷ cūḻniliyē ak kuḻantikaḷiṉ oḻukkam, kaṟṟal, naṭatti ākiyavaṟṟi muṉṉarē aṟutiyiṭṭuḷḷatu eṉṟum, varakkūṭiya muṭivi māṟṟa muyalvatu vīṇ eṉṟum paḻam pāṭṭiyē pāṭukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ tammiṭam payilum māṇākkarkaḷiṉ mēmpāṭṭil13 nampikki iḻakkiṉṟaṉar; avarkaḷ vakuppup pōtaṉiyil ētāvatu peṟṟut toli yaṭṭum eṉṟu vāḷā iruntu viṭukiṉṟaṉar. cila āciriyarkaḷ avarkaḷ tam vakuppiṉ niliyiyē14 kuṟittu viṭukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟa kuṟiyiyum kūṟi, avari pōṭṭāl tuvari muḷik kātu eṉpatu uṟuti eṉṟa camātāṉamum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. avari pōṭṭāl tuvari muḷikkātu eṉpatu uṇmitāṉ. āṉāl takka muṟiyil cūḻniliyik kavaṉittāl ciṟanta avariyiyē uṇṭākku tal muṭiyum; takkavāṟu kavaṉittāl tuvari viḷiccaliyum atikarikkac ceytal iyalum. māṇāk karkaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷ vevvēṟāka uḷḷaṉa eṉpatum, avaṟṟiṉ vaḷarcci oru kuṟippiṭṭa tiṭṭamāṉa pōkkilēyē vaḷar kiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum uṇmiyē; āṉāl, appōkkil vaḷarcci peṟuvatil mēmpāṭu aṭiyac ceyvatum kuṭivaḻi vanta mutalic ciṟanta muṟiyil kiyāḷuvatum nam kiyil tāṉ uḷḷatu. kavaṉamāka eruviṭutal, pulattit takka muṟiyil paṇpaṭuttal, nīrppāyccum muṟi pōṉṟa aṟiviyal muṟic cākupaṭiyāl vitikaḷ ceḻittu vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa; uyarnta palaṉiyum tarukiṉṟaṉa. kuṭivaḻi aṟutiyiṭṭirukkum ellikku mīṟiya ceyalkaḷil kuḻantikaḷi īṭupaṭuttuvatu vīṇē; āṉāl, kuḻantikaḷ kuṭivaḻip peṟṟa tiṟaṉkaḷi muṟiyil vaḷarvataṟkuc cātakamāṉa vāyppukaḷ uḷḷa cūḻniliyi uṇṭākkuvatil āciriyar eppoḻutum tuṇi ceyyalām eṉpati uṇaravēṇṭum. takka cūḻniliyi amittuk koṇṭatāl aṇmikkālattil jappāṉ viyattaku muṟiyil puttuyir peṟṟup polivati ulaka varalāṟu namakku eṭut turikkiṉṟatu.
vēṟucilar kuṭivaḻiyi aṟavē puṟakkaṇittu, kalvi, payiṟci, cūḻnili mutaliya cātakamāṉa vāyppukaḷāl maṉitat tiṟamikkuḷḷaṭaṅkiya aṉittiyumē eytuvikkalām eṉṟu varampikantu karutukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ iṉpattiyē etir nōkkum iṟumānta maṉappāṉmiyāl tavaṟiḻittup peṟa muṭiyātatip peṟamuyalvatil ērāḷamāṉa muyaṟci, kālam, paṇam ākiyavaṟṟip pāḻpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar. tammuṭiya āṟṟa lukkum tiṟaṉukkum appāṟpaṭṭa poṟiyiyaṟ kalviyiyum maruttuvak kalviyiyum mēṟkoṇṭu-palvēṟu celvāk kukaḷiṉāl akkallūri kaḷil nuḻiyavum iṭam peṟṟu-tiṇṭāṭuvati nām kāṇāmal illi. evvaḷavu ciṟanta muṟiyil kalviyip pukaṭṭiṉālum, payiṟciyaḷittālum, takka cūḻniliyi uṇṭākkiṉālum , kuṭivaḻiyāl ki varap peṟāta kavarccikaḷiyum tiṟaṉkaḷiyum uṇṭākka muṭiyātu. ōviyam tīṭṭum tiṟaṉō iciyil īṭupaṭum tiṟaṉō vāykkap peṟāta taṉiyāḷi eṉṉatāṉ muyaṉṟālum, ak kalikaḷil ciṟanta vallunarkaḷālum akkalippulamiyi avaṉiṭam uṇṭākka muṭiyātu; cilariṭam tammuṭiya celvākkāl kaṇicamāṉa aḷavukūṭa palaṉi uṇṭākka muṭivatilli. itu kaḻutiyikkaṭṭi ōmam vaḷarppatu pōṉṟa ceyalāka muṭiyum. karuppu nāyi veḷḷi nāyākka muṭiyātu eṉpati irāyarukku eṭuttuk kāṭṭat teṉālirāmaṉ mēṟkoṇṭa cōtaṉi īṇṭu niṉivu kūrtaṟpālatu. āṉāl, kalviyāl ēṟkeṉavē amintu kiṭakkum tiṟaṉit tērnteṭuttu, ataṉit tūṇṭi vaḷarkka muṭiyum; atu muḻuvaḷarcciyiyum peṟuvataṟkup pātakamāṉa vāyppukaḷ yāvaṟṟiyum kaḷintu cātakamāṉa vāyppukaḷ aṉittiyum tantu tulakkamaṭiyac ceyyalām. inta ulakil ovvoru vakit tiṟaṉukkum ovvoru niliyiluḷḷa tiṟaṉukkum ōr iṭam uṇṭu. aṟivuṭiya kalvi muṟiyāl avaṟṟi iḷamiyilē ārāyntu avi ciṟanta muṟiyil tulakkamuṟuvataṟkuriya ellā vāyppu kaḷiyum aḷittal āṭciyāḷariṉ kaṭami; ataṟku iṇintu tuṇi niṉṟu paṇiyāṟṟuvatu āciriyariṉ poṟuppu. āḷumi vaḷarcciyē nāṭṭu vaḷarcci.
uṇminili: mēṟkūṟiya iruvēṟu karuttukaḷum kuṭi vaḻiyiyum cūḻniliyiyum oṉṟōṭoṉṟu muraṇpaṭṭa vicikaḷ eṉṟa tavaṟāṉa muṭivukaḷik koṇṭavi. avi vicikaḷumalla, tammuḷ tām muraṇi yaviyum aṉṟu. kuṭivaḻi vaṉmiyuṭiyatā cūḻnili vaṉmiyuṭiyatā eṉpatu piracciṉi aṉṟu. inta iraṇṭu coṟṟoṭarkaḷ kuṟikkum vāḻk kiyiṉ iraṇṭu kūṟukaḷum pirikka muṭiyātavi; piṇintu niṟpavi. oṉṟip puṟakkaṇittāl maṟṟoṉṟukkup poruḷē illi. mutalil nām aṟintu koḷḷa vēṇṭuvatu yāteṉil, attakiya āyappaṭu poruḷ15- piracciṉi16- illi eṉpatē. vaḷarum ovvoru uyiriyum-tāvaram, pirāṇi, maṉitaṉ ākiyavaṟṟuḷ etuvāyiṉum-kuṭivaḻiyum alla; allatu cūḻniliyum alla; atu kuṭivaḻiyum cūḻniliyum kalanta toṉṟu. atu viṭutaliyuṭaṉ iyaṅkum paṭippuc ceyal; atu tāṉē uruvāki tāṉāka vaḷarvatu. atu vaḷarvataṟkuk kāraṇam kuṭivaḻiyākap peṟṟa iyalpāṉa tiṟaṉum cūḻnili yumē. atu vaḷarvataṟkuk kuṭivaḻiyum cūḻniliyum iṇintu celvatuṭaṉ iṭi viṉiyum purikiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, kalvikkum camūkattiṟkum muṉṉiṟkum piracciṉi vāḻkkiyiṉ inta iru kūṟukaḷil etaṉit tērnteṭuppatu eṉpatu aṉṟu; ovvoru kuḻantik kum enta aḷavukkuc cūḻniliyi amittuk koṭukka muṭiyum eṉpatē. itaṉāl akkuḻanti kuṭivaḻip peṟṟa taṉṉuṭiya iyaṟkip pēṟu muḻuvatilum amintu kiṭakkum matippu aṉittiyum peṟuvataṟku vāyppu ēṟpaṭu kiṉṟatu. ituvē kuṭiyaracu nāṭukaḷiṉ mukkiya poṟuppu eṉpati nām maṟattalākātu.
oru tāvarattiṉ vaḷarcciyil viriyiṉ17 paṅkiyum maṇṇiṉ18 paṅkiyum aṟintāl kuṭivaḻikkum cūḻnilikkum uḷḷa uṟavumuṟi teḷivākum. viriyiṉuḷ atu oruvakit tāvaramāka vaḷarum āṟṟal aṭaṅkiyuḷḷatu; atu nalmuṟi yil vaḷarvatum naṉṟallāta muṟiyil vaḷarvatum aḵtu aṭiyum maṇṇip poṟuttuḷḷatu. atu pāṟiyil viḻuntālum veyilil viḻuntu nacukkuṇṭālum muḷippatilli. aḵtu atikamāṉa nīruḷḷa allatu vaṟaṭciyiyuṭiya vaḷamaṟṟa maṇṇil viḻuntāl muḷikkum; āṉāl nīṇṭakālam vāḻātu, allatu palaṉtarātu; kōyilkaḷ pōṉṟa kaṭṭiṭa iṭukkukaḷil viḻuntāl eṉṉa nēriṭum eṉpati nām aṟivōm. atu nalla nilattil vīḻntu uram, nīr, veyil pōṉṟa cātakamāṉa nilimikaḷip peṟṟāl, aḵtu oru ciṟanta tāvaramāka vaḷarum; niṟinta palaṉiyum tarum. tāvara vaḷarccikku viriyum vēṇṭum; maṇṇum vēṇṭum. oṉṟillāmal maṟṟoṉṟāl vaḷarcci eṉpatē illi. iraṇṭum taṉit taṉiyākavum ceyaṟpaṭa muṭiyātu; iraṇṭum oṉṟi yoṉṟu taḻuvi niṟkiṉṟatu. ivvāṟē oru kuḻantiyiṉ vaḷarcciyilum  kuṭivaḻiyum cūḻniliyum iṉṟiyamiyāta paṅkiṉip peṟu kiṉṟaṉa.
tāvara ulakiviṭṭu makkaḷ ulakiṟku varuvōm. oruvaṉukku ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷa ētāvatoru tiṟami kuṭivaḻiyāka vantatā, cūḻniliyāl amintatā eṉpati uṟutiyākac colla iyalātu. ōr icik kaliñaṉiṉ makaṉ icip pulavaṉākat tikaḻntāl, kuṭivaḻitāṉ kāraṇam eṉṟu uṟutiyākak kūṟuvatu cariyaṉṟu. avaṉuṭiya tantiyip pōlavē avaṉum cūḻnili yil iruntu pala vāyppukaḷip payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭatāl attiṟaṉip peṟṟirukkalām. icippulami oru tali muṟiyil ciṟitu kāla vāḻkkiyil paḻakiyataṉāl kuṭivaḻiyil kiṭikkum eṉṟu eṇṇuvatu ciṟitum poruntātu. kaṇakkaṟṟa talimuṟikaḷil pallāyiram yāṇṭukaḷiṉ paḻakkattāl pala paṇpukaḷ kuṭivaḻiyākak kiṭik kiṉṟatu eṉṟu karutuvatil ōraḷavu poruttam irukkalām. itaṉi maṟantu kaṇita āciriyariṉ makaṉ kaṇitattil tiṟamiyāḷaṉāka iruppāṉ eṉṟō, varalāṟṟu āciriyariṉ makaṉ aṟiviyal vallunaṉāka āka muṭiyātu eṉṟō karutuvatu tavaṟu kulavittik kallāmal pākam paṭum eṉṟa mēṟkōḷukku uḷaviyal karuttuppaṭi ciṟitum poruḷ illi.
kuṭi vaḻi oruvaṉukkuc cāttiyamākakkūṭiya kūṟukaḷik kuṟikkiṉṟatu; cūḻnili avaṟṟic ciṟanta muṟiyil vaḷarkkum vāyppiṉit tarukiṉṟatu; avvaḷavutāṉ. cūḻnili maṉitaṉi ākkukiṉṟatu; maṉitaṉum cila camayam cūḻniliyi ākkukiṉ ṟāṉ. vaḷḷal aḻakappariṉ vāḻkki yum aṟiviyal mēti ci.vi.irāmaṉiṉ vāḻkkiyum ivaṟṟiṟkuc ciṟanta eṭuttu kāṭṭukaḷāka amiyalām. vaḷḷal aḻakappar veṟum āli mutalāḷiyāka maṭṭilum iruntu kalviniliyaṅkaḷi ēṟpaṭut tiyirā viṭṭāl kalvit tuṟiyil ivvaḷavu cēvi cey tirukka muṭiyātu; vaḷḷalākavum malarntirukka muṭiyātu. kaṇakkut tuṟiyil paṇiyāṟṟiya car. ci.vi.irāmaṉukku accūḻnili ciṟitum poruntavilli. avar putika āyvakattiṟku vantatum accūḻnili avarit takkamuṟiyil tūṇṭi aṟiviyal mētiyākkiyatu. āṉāl poruḷ tuṟiyil atika nāḷ paṇiyāṟṟiya car. ār. kē. caṇmukam ceṭṭiyāri accūḻnili oru poruḷiyal vallunarākkiyatu. takka cūḻnili amiyātataṉ kāraṇamāka poṟiyiyal mēti ji. ṭi. nāyuṭuviṉ tiṟaṉ nāṭṭiṟku vēṇṭumaḷavukkup payaṉpaṭa vaḻiyillātu pōyviṭṭatu. itaṉāl ettiṟaṉum ēṟṟa cūḻ niliyil vaḷarum eṉpatu peṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
kuṭivaḻiyip paṟṟiya viḷakkam
kuṭivaḻipaṟṟiya teḷivaṟṟa karuttukaḷuṭaṉ nām maṉaniṟivu peṟalākātu. itukāṟum kuṭivaḻipaṟṟi nām kūṟiyavi yāvum, avi kāraṇakāriyat toṭarpuṭaṉ iruppiṉum, karuttiyal19 paṟṟiyaviyē. eṉavē, kuṭivaḻiyippaṟṟi pulaṉīṭāṉa20 uyiriyal uṇmi eṉṉa eṉpatic ciṟitu ārāyvōm.
kuḻantikaḷ kuṭivaḻiyākap peṟupavi yāvi? cakōtari kaḷum cakōtararkaḷum orē kuṭivaḻiyip peṟṟuḷḷaṉarā? cila kuṭumpaṅkaḷil uḷappaṇpukaḷ kuṭivaḻiyāka iṟaṅkukiṉṟaṉavā? kuḻantikaḷ peṟṟōril ētāvatu oruvarippōl maṭṭilum iruppataṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṉa? kuṟikaḷum kuṭivaḻippaṇpu kaḷāka vaḻivaḻic cellukiṉṟaṉavā? ivi pōṉṟa viṉākkaḷ pala peṟṟōrkaḷiyum āciriyarkaḷiyum maṉa vētaṉik kuḷḷākkukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟku avarkaḷ nāṭōṭi ilak kiyaṅkaḷ mūlamō, tiṟaṉāyvaṟṟa tammuṭiya kaṟpaṉi mūlamō uṇṭākkum viṭikaḷ kuḻantikaḷippaṟṟit tavaṟāṉa eṇ ṇaṅkaḷi uṇṭākki viṭukiṉṟaṉa.
uṭaliluḷḷa uyiraṇukkaḷil kuṭivaḻippaṇpu: taṉippaṭṭa ovvoru tāvaram allatu pirāṇiyum oṟṟi uyiraṇu21vākavē taṉ vāḻkkiyit toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu. taṉi maṉitaṉuṭiya vāḻkki karuvuṟṟa22 1/200 aṅkulam kuṟuk kaḷavuḷḷa oru ciṟiya muṭṭiyiṉiṉṟu toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu. inta oṟṟiyaṇu tāyiṉ cūṟpi23yiluḷḷa oru muṭṭiyum tantiyiṉ viriyiṉiṉṟum24 veḷippōnta oru vintaṇuvum25 (paṭam-19.) cērntataṉāl uṇṭāṉatu. muṭṭi kaṇṇukkut teriyak kūṭiyatu. vintaṇukkaḷ kaṇṇukkut teriyā. avi muṭṭiyit tēṭivarukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟil ētāvatoṉṟu muṭṭiyi aṇukiṉāl uṭaṉē ataṟkuḷ pāykiṉṟatu. appoḻutu ataṉuṭiya vāl aṟupaṭṭut talip pākam maṭṭilum uṭcellukiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu karuvuṟṟa muṭṭi mikap pātu kāppuṭaṉ amintuḷḷa cūḻniliyil, tāyiṉ karuppiyil, vaḷarntu iraṇṭu aṇukkaḷākap pirikiṉṟatu. inta iraṇṭu aṇukkaḷum nāṉkākavum, nāṉku eṭṭākavum immātiri toṭarntu pala ilaṭcakkaṇākkāṉa uyiraṇukkaḷākap palkip peruki vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa (paṭam-20). ivi yāvum karuvuṟṟa orē muṭṭiyiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiṭiṉum, ivi palvēṟu vitamākat tulakkamaṭi kiṉṟaṉa26. ivaṟṟuḷ cila taciyaṇuk kaḷākavum, cila curappiyaṇukkaḷākavum, cila narampu aṇuk kaḷākavum, cila elumpu aṇukkaḷākavum pirintu vaḷar kiṉṟaṉa.
uḷḷaṇu: ovvōr uyiraṇuviṟkum uḷḷaṇu27 eṉṟa oru pakuti uṇṭu. itu vētiyiyal muṟiyilum28 uṭaliyal muṟiyilum aṇu uṭaliṉ ēṉiya pakutiyiliruntu māṟu paṭukiṉṟatu. aṇu uṭaliṉ29 piṟa pakutiyiṉ ceyalvēṟu; uḷḷaṇuviṉ ceyalvēṟu. taciyaṇuviṉ piṟpakuti curuṅkum ceyaliyum, curappiyaṇuviṉ ippakuti curattal ceyaliyum, narampaṇuviṉ ippakuti ceytikaḷik kaṭattal30 ceyaliyum, mēṟkoḷḷu kiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl uḷḷaṇu, vaḷarcciyilum aṇuppirivilum perumpaṅku koṇṭuḷḷatu; uyiraṇuviṉ vāḻviyum urattiyum niliniṟuttukiṉṟatu. karuvuṟṟa muṭṭi yiluḷḷa uḷḷaṇu vaḷarntu iraṇṭu uḷḷaṇukkaḷākap pirikiṉṟaṉa; ivi iraṇṭum taṉittaṉiyāka iraṇṭu uyiraṇuk kaḷilum cērkiṉṟaṉa. ōr uyiraṇup pirivilum itē ceyal tāṉ naṭipeṟukiṉṟatu. itaṉāl nammuṭiya uṭaliluḷḷa ovvoru uyiraṇuvilum ōr uḷḷaṇu uḷḷatu eṉṟum, inta uḷḷaṇuvē karuvuṟṟa muṭṭiyiṉ uḷḷaṇuviliruntu pirintatu eṉṟum nām aṟikiṉṟōm. eṉavē, oru taṉiyāḷiṉ kuṭivaḻiyil ilaṭcakkaṇakkāṉa uḷḷaṇukkaḷ aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa eṉṟum, inta uḷḷaṇukkaḷ yāvum karuvuṟṟa muṭṭi yiṉiṉṟu pirintavi eṉṟum, intak karuvuṟṟa muṭṭi tanti vaḻi yoṉṟum tāyvaḻi yoṉṟumāka vanta iraṇṭu uḷḷaṇuk kaḷiṉ cērkkiyāl uṇṭāṉatu eṉṟum teḷivākap peṟap paṭukiṉṟatu.
itukāṟum aṟintavaṟṟiliruntu kuṭivaḻiyip paṟṟi mūṉṟu mukkiya karuttukaḷ teḷivākiṉṟaṉa: (1) oru kuḻantiyiṉ kuṭivaḻi peṟṟōr iruvariṭamiruntu ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu; (2) atu karuvuṟum poḻutē niliyāka aṟutiyiṭap peṟukiṉṟatu; tāy ak karuvi oṉpatu tiṅkaḷ cumantu uṇa vūṭṭattiṟkuk kāraṇamāka iruntālum atikamāṉa kuṭivaḻiyoṉṟiyum avaḷiṭamiruntu peṟuvatilli; (3) kuḻantiyiṉ kuṭivaḻi ataṉ uṭal muḻuvatum paraviyuḷḷatu; ataṉ ovvōr uyiraṇuvilum atu aṭaṅkik kiṭakkiṉṟatu. 
niṟakkōlkaḷ31: ovvoru uyiraṇuviluḷḷa uḷḷaṇuvilum kōl pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷ aṭaṅki yuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ cila nīḷamākavum, cila kuṭṭiyākavum, cila nērākavum cila vaḷintum irukkum. āṉāl, oru taṉiyāḷiṉ ov voru uyiraṇuvilum orē vaki uyiriṉaṅkaḷiṉ uṭali luḷḷa uyiraṇuvilum orē eṇṇikkiyuḷḷa niṟakkōl kaḷ uḷḷaṉa. irap paril mika nuṇṇiya nūliḻuttu ati nīḷamākavum kuṭṭi yākavum ciṟuciṟu tuṇṭukaḷāka veṭṭiṉāl eppaṭiyiruk kumō avvāṟu in niṟak kōlkaḷ tōṟṟam aṭikiṉṟaṉa (paṭam-21).
ovvoru uyiriyiṉ uyiraṇuvilum oru kuṟippiṭṭa eṇṇikki yuḷḷa niṟakkōlkaḷē uḷḷaṉa. iti mukkiyamāka niṉivilirutta vēṇṭum. īyiṉ uyiraṇuvil 4 iṇi32 niṟakkōlkaḷum, eliyiṉ uyiraṇuvil 20 iṇikaḷum uḷḷaṉa. cōḷattiṉ uyiraṇuvil iruppavi 10 iṇi; takkāḷiyiṉ uyiraṇuvil iruppavi 12iṇi. māṉiṭa uyiraṇuvil 23 iṇi niṟakkōlkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. inta niṟak kōlkaḷi 23 iṇikaḷākap pirittu vittāl ovvōr iṇiyum pārvikku orē mātiri uruvamuṭiyatāka iruppatu teriyavarum (paṭam-22). uyiraṇup pirivil, ovvoru niṟak kōlum taṉ nīḷatti accāka vittu iraṇṭāka uṭi kiṉṟatu. ākiyāl uyiraṇu iraṇṭākap piriyuṅkāl ovvōr uyiraṇuvilum 23 iṇi niṟakkōlkaḷ amikiṉṟaṉa. ivi yāvum karuvuṟṟa muṭṭiyiṉiṉṟē tōṉṟiyavi yātaliṉ, ovvoru uyiraṇu vilum kuṭivaḻi aṭaṅkiyuḷḷatu.
ivviṭattil iṉṉoru mukkiya ceytiyiyum niṉi vilirunta vēṇṭum. ovvoru uyiraṇuvilum 23 iṇiniṟak kōlkaḷ uḷḷaṉa eṉṟu mēlē kaṇṭōmallavā? ataṉāl karuvuṟuvataṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa muṭṭiyilum vintaṇuvilum 23 iṇi niṟakkōlkaḷē irukkumeṉṟu nām karutuvōm. āṉāl, uṇmiyil avvāṟu illi. oruvar pāl mutircci33 aṭiyuṅkāl avar uṭalil iṉṉoru vakiyāṉa uyiraṇup pakuppu34 naṭipeṟukiṉṟatu. itaṉ mūlamākattāṉ iṉap perukka uyiraṇukkaḷ35 (peṇṇiṭattil muṭṭiyum āṇiṭat til vintaṇuvum) kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. itu kuṟittup pakuttal36 eṉa vaḻaṅkappeṟum. itil iṉapperukka uyiraṇu ovvoṉ ṟilum mutaliluḷḷa uyiraṇukkaḷil pātiyē kiṭikkiṉṟatu. cātāraṇa uyiraṇuppakuppil niṟakkōlkaḷ iraṭṭikkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, kuṟittup pakuttal ceyalil avviṇi niṟakkōlkaḷ pirintu ovvoṉṟum cēy uyiraṇuviṟkuc cellukiṉṟatu. piṉṉar, āṇ peṇ iṉapperukka uyiraṇukkaḷ iṇiyuṅkāl tirumpavum muḻuniṟakkōlkaḷiṉ tokuti koṇṭa uyiraṇu piṟakkiṉṟatu. ituvē karuvuṟutal eṉpatu. ivvāṟu uruppeṟṟa uyiraṇu mīṇṭum pirintu vaḷarntu kuḻantiyākiṉṟatu. itaṉip paṭam (paṭam-23) viḷakkukiṉṟatu. mēṟkūriya ceytikaḷiyum ivaṟṟiyum oṉṟu cērttu ārāyntāl, taṉicciṟappukkuriya ōr uṇmi namakkup pulaṉākiṉṟatu. ovvōr iṇiyilumuḷḷa oru niṟakkōl vintaṇuviliruntu vantatu: maṟṟoṉṟu karuvuṟu vataṟkumuṉ muṭṭiyilēyē iruntatu. eṉavē, oru kuḻanti taṉ uṭaliluḷḷa uyiraṇuviṉ niṟakkōl iṇiyil oṉṟiṉit tantiyiṭamiruntum maṟṟoṉṟiṉit tāyiṉiṭamiruntum peṟukiṉṟatu eṉpatu peṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
jīṉkaḷ37: niṟakkōlkaḷippaṟṟi mēlē kaṇṭōm. ovvoru niṟakkōlilum kuṭivaḻik kūṟukaḷi38 tāṅkik koṇṭirukkum mika nuṇṇiya uṟuppukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟi jīṉkaḷ eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar. jīṉkaḷiṉ tokutiyālēyē niṟak kōlkaḷ amintirukkiṉṟaṉa. uyarnta nuṇṇaṇup peruk kiyālum39 jīṉit taṉiyākak kāṇpatu aritu. āṟṟalmikka nuṇṇaṇup perukkiṉāl oru niṟakkōli ārāyntatil atu kayiṟṟil kōkkappeṟṟa uruṇṭi maṇikaḷpōl kāṇappeṟukiṉṟatu; mukkiyamāka, aḵtu oru tiṭṭamāṉa oḻuṅkil amikkappeṟṟa jīṉkaḷiṉ kōviyē. māṉiṭa jīṉkaḷ kiṭṭattaṭṭa āyirattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭavi; avi 46 niṟakkōlkaḷilum camamiṉṟi viṉiyōkikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
iṇiyāka uḷḷa niṟakkōlkaḷiṉ ōr iṇiyiluḷḷa oru niṟakkōliṉ oru jīṉ anta iṇiyiluḷḷa maṟṟoru niṟakkōliṉ oru jīṉuṭaṉ iṇintuḷḷatu. inta jīṉkaḷtām orē mātiriyāṉa muṟiyil allatu māṟupaṭṭa muṟiyil orē ceyalip purikiṉṟaṉa. ōr iṇiyi luḷḷa jīṉkaḷ tam ceyalil orē mātiriyāṉa muṟiyil ceyaṟpaṭṭāl, avaṟṟāl ākkappeṟum ciṟappiyalpu40 tali kāṭṭum. ivvāṟu kāṭṭu vataṟku vēṟu iṇi uyiraṇukkaḷiṉ taliyīṭu41 illātirukka vēṇṭum. avi māṟupaṭṭa muṟiyil ceyaṟpaṭṭāl inta iraṇṭu jīṉkaḷiḷ vēṟupaṭṭa celvākkukaḷ oṉṟāka iṇintu iṭippaṭṭa ciṟappiyalpiṉi viḷivikkalām; allatu oru jīṉ piṟitoṉṟiṉ celvākki maṟittum viṭalām. vēṟoru jīṉi muṉṉiliyil, vaṉmiyāṉa jīṉ oṉṟu oru ciṟappiyalpiṉi uṇṭākkumēyāṉāl atu ōṅkiniṟkiṉṟatākap42 pēcap peṟum; maṟinta uyiraṇu piṉtaṅki niṟkiṉṟatākak43 kūṟappeṟum. ivi paṟṟiya muḻu vivaraṅkaḷi uyir nūlkaḷil kaṇṭu teḷika.
iṉap perukka uyiraṇukkaḷ uṇṭākum poḻutu tāyiṭamuḷḷa muṭṭiyil 23 niṟakkōlkaḷum tantiyiṭamuḷḷa vintaṇuvil 23 niṟakkōlkaḷum amikiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu mēlē kaṇṭōm. oru taṉiyāḷiṭa muḷḷa muṭṭikaḷ (allatu vintaṇukkaḷ) niṟakkōlkaḷiṉ palvēṟu vitamāṉa iṇippuk kaḷip peṟukiṉṟaṉa. 46 niṟakkōlkaḷil enta 23 niṟakkōlkaḷ oru kuṟippiṭṭa muṭṭi allatu vintaṇuviṟkup pōkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatit taṟceyal44 tāṉ aṟutiyiṭukiṉṟatu. karuvuṟutalāl mīṇṭum 46 niṟakkōlkaḷ amikiṉṟaṉa. enta vintaṇu (āṇiṭamuḷḷa enta 23 kōlkaḷiṉ tokuti) enta muṭṭiyuṭaṉ (peṇṇiṭamuḷḷa enta 23 niṟakkōlkaḷiṉ tokuti) cērntu oru putiya taṉiyāḷi uṇṭākkukiṉṟatu eṉpatiyum taṟceyali aṟutiyiṭukiṉṟatu. eṉavē, kuṭivaḻic caṭṭaṅkaḷ eṉpavi (1) muṭṭiyilum vintaṇuvilum uḷḷa niṟakkōl kaḷ taṟ ceyalāka iṉam iṉamākap piritaliyum (2) karuvuṟutalil kuṟippiṭṭa vintaṇukkaḷum muṭṭikaḷum taṟceyalākat toṭarpu koḷvatiyum poṟutta caṭṭaṅkaḷēyākum.
ik kūṟiyavaṟṟālum paṭam 23-ālum ovvoru kuḻantiyum tāyiṭamiruntum tantiyiṭamiruntum orē mātiri kuṭivaḻiyip peṟukiṉṟatu eṉpatu peṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. inta ovvoru peṟṟōrum tattam peṟṟōr kaḷiṭamiruntu camanilikaḷil kuṭivaḻiyip peṟukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟum, avarkaḷum aṅṅaṉamē tam peṟṟōrkaḷiṭamiruntu camamākap peṟukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟum terikiṉṟatu. eṉavē, ovvoru kuḻantiyum taṉṉuṭiya kuṭivaḻip paṇpukaḷil pātiyi nēr peṟṟōriṭamiruntum ṅu pakutiyi pāṭṭaṉ-pāṭṭimāriṭattum 1/8 pakutiyi mup pāṭṭaṉ-muppāṭṭimārkaḷiṭat tiruntum, iṅṅaṉamē toṭarn tum vaṭiva kaṇita vikitattil45 peṟukiṉṟatu. vāḻkki eṉṉum nīrōṭṭam toṭarntu pōykkoṇṭēyuḷḷatu; kuḻanti taṉṉuṭiya mūlataṉattip peṟṟōrkaḷiṭamiruntu allatu peṟṟōrkaḷ mūlam peṟukiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam kuḻantiyiṉ kuṭivaḻippaṇpukaḷ muṉṉōr aṉivariyum poṟut tatātaliṉ, oru kuḻantiyiṉ tāṭi (mōvāy) taṉ tāyiṉ tāṭiyip pōlavum, ataṉ neṟṟi tantiyiṉuṭiyatip pōlavum, kaṇṇiṉ nīla niṟam ataṉ pāṭṭaṉuṭiyatip pōlavum, ataṉ talimayir ammāṉuṭiyatip pōlavum, ataṉ mūkku ataṉ attiyiṉuṭiyatip pōlavum uḷḷaṉa. kuḻantikaḷ ippaṇpuk kūṟukaḷi46 orē iruppuc carakkiliruntu47 peṟṟaṉa eṉṟum, avi appaṇpuk kūṟukaḷip palvēṟu vitamākappirittaṭukkiṉavaṟṟik kāṭṭu kiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum kūṟalām. pala kuḻantikaḷ aṇmiyiluḷḷa enta mūtāti yariyum pōṉṟiruppatilli; avi kuṭivaḻiyil neṭun toliviluḷḷa oruvariṭamiruntu cila paṇpukaḷip peṟu kiṉṟaṉa; pala kuḻantikaḷ paṇpuk kūṟukaḷiṉ kalaviyākat tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa.
itukāṟum aṟintavaṟṟiliruntu orē kuṭumpattiluḷḷa pala kuḻantikaḷ vevvēṟu vitamāka iruppataṟkum ōriṉattiṉ ovvoru uṟuppiṉarum vintiyāṉa taṉiyāḷāyt tikaḻ vataṟkum oruvāṟu kāraṇam teriyum. taṉiyāḷ vēṟṟumi48 eṉpatu taṟkāla uḷaviyalilum, kalvi iyalilum oru cātāraṇa ceyti; anta uṇmiyiliruntu cūḻnili māṟṟattālō, kalvi muṟiyiṉālō, takka payiṟciyiṉālō ellāk kuḻanti kaḷiyum orē kuṟippiṭṭa nili aḷavukkuk koṇṭu celutta muṭiyātu eṉpati aṟikiṉṟōm. āṉāl, kalvi muṟi ovvoru kuḻantiyiṉ tiṟamik kēṟṟavāṟu amital vēṇṭum; ciṟanta muṟi yilum vāyppukaḷ nalka vēṇṭum. kuḻantiyiṉ ciṟanta tulakkam āciriyarum peṟṟōrum tiṭṭap paṭutti vittirukkum kuṟikkōḷ niliyi aṭivatil aṉṟu; tāṉ kuṭivaḻiyākap peṟṟa mūlataṉattiṟ kēṟpa ciṟantatoru niliyi eyta vēṇṭum eṉpatutāṉ.
cūḻniliyiṉ iyalpu
cūḻniliyippaṟṟiya cila karuttukaḷi mēlē kūṟiṉōm. īṇṭu mēlum cilavaṟṟik kāṇpōm. kuḻanti vaḷarcciyil cūḻnili palvēṟu vaḻikaḷil taṉatu celvākkic celuttu kiṉṟatu. vaḷarccic ceyalkaḷ yāvum uyiriyam49, nīr, uṇavu, cātakamāṉa taṭpa veppa nilikaḷ mutali yavi tēviyāṉa aḷavu kiṭippatip poṟuttuḷḷaṉa. oruvaruṭiya paḻakkaṅkaḷum tiṟaṉkaḷum50. avar cūḻniliyiliruntu peṟum payiṟci yit tarakkūṭiya nilimikaḷip poṟuttuḷḷaṉa. taṉi yāḷiṉ aṟivu avar peṟṟa payiṟṟaliṉ51 aḷaviyum avaruṭiya camūka maṉappāṉmikaḷiyum avar paṅku koṇṭa camūka nikaḻccikaḷiyum poṟuttavi.
poruḷkaḷ tūṇṭalkaḷi viḷivittāltāṉ avi cūḻnili yākum eṉṟu mēlē kūṟiṉōm allavā? ati mēlum viḷakkuvōm. paccippaṭṭu virittālotta paccip pacēleṉat tōṉṟum paranta pacumpulveḷi paciyōṭu tuṭikkum nāykku vaṟaṭciyāṉa cūḻniliyākum. aṅṅaṉamē, kuḻimuyalkaḷ niṟintu kiṭakkum pulam pacuviṟku ēlāta cūḻ niliyākum. taṉiyāḷiṉ uṭal tēvi allatu uḷḷak kavarccikku muṟiyīṭu ceyyumpoḻututāṉ cūḻnili payaṉuṭiya tākiṉṟatu; appoḻututāṉ ētāvatu oru vaḻiyil etirviṉi puriya vāyppu ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. etu payaṉuṭiyatākum eṉpatu taṉiyāḷ, avaruṭiya kuṭivaḻi, avaruṭiya muṉṉanupavam, avaruṭiya uṭal vayatu52 maṉavayatu53 ākiyavaṟṟip poṟuttatu. marutanilak kāṭcikaḷi ettaṉiyō muṟi kaṇṭiruk kiṉṟōm. atu nam uḷḷattit toṭuvatilli. cintiyik kiḷarvatilli; āṉāl, atik kāṇum kampaṉ pōṉṟa kaviñarkaḷiṉ uḷḷattil oru vitat tūṇṭali uṇṭākku kiṉṟatu. avariṭamiruntu veḷippaṭum tulaṅkal,
taṇṭali mayilka ḷāṭat
	tāmari viḷakkam tāṅkak
koṇṭalkaḷ muḻaviṉ ēṅkak 
	kuvaḷikaṇ viḻittu nōkkat
teṇṭiri eḻiṉi kāṭṭat
	tēmpiḻi makara yāḻiṉ
vaṇṭukaḷ iṉitu pāṭa
	marutamvīṟ ṟirukkum mātō? 54
eṉṟa kavitiyaracu malarkiṉṟatu. ōr aṟi niṟiyak kuḻumiyuḷḷa mutirntōrkaḷ55 araciyalpaṟṟik kalantu āyntu koṇṭirun tāl, aḵtu oru kuḻantiyiṉ aṟivuc cūḻnilikkuc ciṟitum ovvātu. kuḻantiyi nīkkiviṭṭu, atu vatiyum vīṭṭiyum kuṭumpattiyum ārāyvatāl yātoru payaṉum illi. kuḻantiyi vīṭṭil kāṇavēṇṭum; atu puriyum etir viṉikaḷik kavaṉittalvēṇṭum. orē vīṭṭil vatiyum iru kuḻavikaḷ tēviyāṉa orē vita cūḻniliyip peṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu collutal iyalātu. iru kuḻavikaḷiṭiyē kāṇap peṟum taṉiyāḷ vēṟṟumikaḷiṉ aḷaviṟkēṟpa, avarkaḷ vāḻum cūḻniliyāl avarkaḷ tākkam peṟuvatilum atika vēṟṟumikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
piṟappataṟkumuṉ mutircci56
oru maram peritāka vaḷarvataṟku muṉṉar ataṉ viri tarikkuḷ pulaṉākāmal vaḷarkiṉṟatu. tariyiṉ kīḻ eḻunta ataṉ vāḻkkiyum tariyiṉmēl vaḷarnta ataṉ vāḻkkiyum orē toṭarpuṭiyavi. aṅṅaṉamē, makkaḷiṭiyēyum avar kaḷuṭiya piṟappiṉ piṉ eḻuntu viḷaṅkum vāḻkki piṟappiṉmuṉ uḷḷaṭaṅkik kiṭanta vāḻkkiyiṉ toṭarcciyākum. karu eṉṉum arumpu malarnta niliyē kuḻanti piṟappatu. eṉavē, karuvulaka vāḻkkiyi ārāyvatu iṉṟiyamiyātatākiṉṟatu. antak karuvūriliruntaṉṟō yāvarum uṟiyum inta uṟiyūrukkuḷ pukuntu aṉivarum kūṭi vāḻum kūṭal ulakiṟ pukukiṉṟōm?
karuvuṟṟa uyiraṇu eṅṅaṉam pirintu vaḷarkiṉṟatu eṉpati muṉṉark kaṇṭōm. ūṉakkaṇṇukkup pulaṉākāta inta nuṇṇiya oṟṟiyaṇu eṅṅaṉam kālkaḷum kikaḷum, kaṇkaḷum, kātukaḷum, kurutiyīralum nuriyīralum, mūḷiyum, piṟa uṟup pukaḷum koṇṭa eṭṭu irāttal kuḻantiyāka vaḷarukiṉṟatu? itippaṟṟi ārāyvatē karuvulaka ārāycci; piṟappataṟku muṉṉuḷḷa mutircciyippaṟṟi ārāyvatu.
taṉiyāḷkaḷiṉ vēṟṟumikaḷukkuk kuṭivaḻiyum cūḻ niliyumē kāraṇam eṉṟu mēlē kūṟiṉōm. karuvulaka vāḻkkikkum akkāraṇam poruntum. atip porutti viḷakka muyaluvōm.
māṉiṭak karuviṉ vaḷarcci niliyi aṟiñarkaḷ mūṉṟu pirivukaḷākap pirittu ārāykiṉṟaṉar. karuvuṟṟatu toṭaṅki 15 nāṭkaḷ variyilum muṭṭi karuppiyil.57 oṭṭāmal vaḷarntu varukiṉṟatu. apparuvam mūḷinili58 allatu mutaṟcūḻnili eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappeṟukiṉṟatu. ippoḻutu uyiraṇu iraṭṭittup pirintu vaḷartal toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu; icceyal toṭarntu naṭi peṟṟukkoṇṭē uḷḷatu. cumār iraṇṭu vārattiṟkup piṟaku muṭṭi karuppiyil oṭṭik koḷḷukiṉṟatu. oṭṭikkoḷḷum iṭattil tāṉ koppūḻkkoṭi59 vaḷarkiṉṟatu; ik koppūḻk koṭiyiṉ mūlantāṉ karu tāyiṉiṭamiruntu uṇavūṭṭam60 peṟukiṉṟatu (paṭam 24). iṅṅaṉam muḷiccūl uruvam peṟātu iraṇṭu tiṅkaḷ vari vaḷarntu varukiṉṟatu. ipparuvam piṇṭa nili allatu iḷañcūlnili61 eṉap peyar peṟum.
uyir aṇukkaḷ ilaṭcak kaṇakkil palkip perukuvatil viyap poṉṟum illi; avi palvēṟu vitamāṉa uyir aṇukkaḷāka māṟu vatum, aviyē palvēṟu uṟuppukaḷāka māṟivaḷarvatumē nammi viyappuk kaṭalil āḻttukiṉṟaṉa. ellā uyiraṇukkaḷum karuvuṟṟa muṭṭi yiṉiṉṟē tōṉṟiya viyātaliṉ, avaṟṟiṉ kuṭivaḻiyil yātoru vēṟṟumiyum illi. ākavē, cūḻniliyik koṇṭē itaṉi viḷakka muyalavēṇṭum. ciṟappaṭiyāta kuḻaviyiṉ cūḻniliyākiya tāyiṉ karuppi pātukākkappeṟṟa oru cūḻnili; veḷiyulakiṉ taṭpamum, taḷḷalum iḻuttalum, oḷiyum maṇamum, piṟa vicikaḷum aṅku illi. avviṭam vaḻuvaḻuppākavum orē mātiriyākavum uḷḷatu. atu vaḷarum uyiriṉ ellā uyiraṇuk kaḷukkum orē cūḻniliyittāṉ peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. appaṭiyāṉāl uyiraṇukkaḷ palvēṟu vakiyāṉa uyiraṇukkaḷāka māṟu vataṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṉa?
mūḷi mikap parumaṉāka iruntapōtilum, iḷañcūl niliyiluḷḷa mūḷi ceyaṟpaṭuvatilli. ataṉāl uṇ ṭākkap peṟṟuḷḷa uyiraṇukkaḷ narampu aṇukkaḷ alla. aviyāvum vaḷarcciyaṭiya vēṇṭiya iḷañcūl uyiraṇukkaḷ.
