poṟāmiyik kiḷappiviṭum nikaḻcci camukampaṟṟiyatu; kuḻanti atikamāka aṉpukoḷḷum makkaḷē annikaḻcciyil paṅku peṟupavarkaḷ.
poṟāmi nilimikkut takkavāṟu vaṭivam eṭukkum.  etirāḷiyi nēraṭiyākat tākkuvatu, nintittal, piṭivātam ākiyavi pōṉṟa vaṭivaṅkaḷi atu eṭukkalām.  cilariṭam atu tuyilil ciṟunīr kaḻittal, peruviral cuvittal, uṇavu uṇṇa maṟuttal pōṉṟa kuḻantic ceyalkaḷiyum uṇṭākkalām.  caṟṟu vaḷarnta kuḻantikaḷiṭam poṟāmi ēṟpaṭuṅkāl vāyppēccuc caṇṭi, vīṇpēccu, kati kaṭṭi viṭutal, ciṉamūṭṭum kuṟippukkaḷi veḷiyiṭutal, eḷḷutal, ikaḻtal, kalakam mūṭṭutal pōṉṟa ceyalkaḷi atu viḷivikkum.  cila camayam pakaṟkaṉavu, taṟperumi, utācīṉam, kiṇṭal pēccu pōṉṟa tūṇṭalkaḷiyum cilariṭam uṇṭākkakkūṭum.  vēṇṭumeṉṟē oru kuḻaviyip poṟāmip paṭac ceytal allatu ciṉam mūḷac ceytal oru tīya ceyalākum.
kāraṇaṅkaḷ: perumpālum poṟāmi ēṟpaṭuvataṟku vīṭṭu nikaḻcciyē kāraṇamākiṉṟatu.  tāy kuḻavikaḷiṭam kavaṉam celuttuvatit tavirttal kuḻantikaḷiṭam poṟāmi eḻukiṉṟatu.  atikamāṉa cellam koṭukkum tāymārkaḷum kuḻavikaḷiṭam poṟāmiyi vaḷarppataṟkuk kāraṇamākiṉṟaṉar.  cila peṟṟōr kuḻavikaḷi aṭakkuvatālum, pārapaṭcam kāṭṭuvatālum kuḻantikaḷiṭam attīya paṇpu ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu.

oru kuḻanti taṉṉuṭiya taṉ-matippi vaḷarppatālum, maṟṟak kuḻavikaḷiṉ nalaṉil uṇmiyāṉa akkaṟi kāṭṭat tūṇṭuvatālum, peṟṟōr āciriyarkaḷuṭiya aṉpum akkaṟiyum uṟutiyākat tammiṭam iruppatākak kuḻavikaḷ kaḷippatālum kuḻavikaḷiṭam poṟāmi uṇṭākiṉṟatu.
taṭukkum vitam :  pārapaṭcatti vilakkavēṇṭum.  peṟṟōrō āciriyarō oru kuḻaviyip pirattiyēkamāṉa aṉpuṭiyavaṉ eṉṟu ēṟṟuk koḷḷalākātu.  ciṟuvarkaḷiṭam pārapaṭcam kāṭṭuvatu tīṅku viḷikkum.  oru kuḻantiyiṭam veṟuppuk kāṭṭuvatum, piṟa kuḻavikaḷi viṭa atu kuṟivuḷḷatu eṉṟu uṇarac ceyvatum ati aṭippati viṭak kēṭṭiṉi viḷivippavi.
cila ceyalkaḷil ēṉiyavarkaḷiviṭa oru kuṟippiṭṭa ciṟuvaṉ mēmpaṭṭavaṉ eṉṟu oppukkoḷḷa vēṇṭiyatāka irukkum.  mēlvakuppu māṟṟam ceyyumpoḻutum, cila taṉippaṭṭa calukikaḷ tara nērum poḻutum piṟa ciṟuvarkaṭkut taṅkaḷ kuṟikaḷ uṇarttap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  oruvaruṭiya tiṟamiyik kuṟittuc cariyāṉa matippi avar aṟiyac ceyvatum, atippaṟṟic cariyāṉa maṉappāṉmiyi avariṭam vaḷarac ceyvatum kalviyiṉ mukkiya nōkkamākum.  tammāl ceyya muṭiyāta ceyalkaḷum uḷa eṉṟum, avaṟṟic ceyyak kūṭiyavarkaḷ pāṟ poṟāmi koḷvatāl tamakku yātoru payaṉum illiyeṉṟum, avvāṟu koḷvatāl tammiṭam attiṟaṉ vaḷarātu eṉṟum avarkaḷ aṟiyac ceytal vēṇṭum.  iṅṅaṉam ceytāl, ciṟuvarkaḷiṭam nalla maṉappāṉmi uṇṭākum.


kūccam (nāṇum taṉmi)
kūccam46 allatu piṉvāṅkum47 naṭatti, pala kuḻavikaḷiṭam pala paruvaṅkaḷilum palavitamākak kāṇappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.  aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkiyil oru kuḻanti toṭarntu tōlviyik kāṇpatālum, taṉṉampikkik kuṉṟuvatālum kūccam ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu.  kūccamuḷḷa kuḻanti cātāraṇamākat taṉimiyiyē virumpum.
kūccamuḷḷa kuḻantiyāl āciriyarukkut tolli yoṉṟum nēriṭāviṭiṉum, ak kuḻanti pātukāppiṉmi yālum tēvik kuṟi vālum colloṇāt tuṉpatti aṉupavikkiṉṟatu.  atu pakaṟkaṉavu kāṇum; piṟa kuḻantikaḷuṭaṉ kalantu paḻakātu; taṉṉuṭiya kaṉavulakat tilēyē vāḻa virumpum.  camūka virōtamāṉa intap paṇpi muḷi yilēyē kiḷḷā viṭṭāl, atu kicarirīṉiyā48 eṉṟa nōykkuk kāraṇamāki viṭum.  kūccamuḷḷa ellāk kuḻantikaṭkum uḷa nōykaḷ uṇṭākum eṉṟu colluvataṟkilli.  eṉiṉum, ikkuḻantikaḷ vaḷarntavar kaḷāṉavuṭaṉ camūkap poruttak kuṟipāṭukaḷi aṭivārkaḷātaliṉ, kūccatti iḷamiyilēyē kaḷiya muṉiyavēṇṭum.
tuṇi ceyyum muṟi :  kūccamuḷḷa ciṟuvarkaḷi avarkaḷ āyattamāka illāta camūkac ceyalkaḷil īṭupaṭum paṭi kaṭṭāyappaṭuttak kūṭātu.  aṅṅaṉam ceytāl, acciṟuvarkaḷiṭamuḷḷa uḷac cikkali iṉṉum atikappaṭuttak kūṭum.  tiṟamiyuḷḷa āciriyar paṭippaṭiyāṉa ēṟpāṭukaḷi amittu acciṟuvarkaḷ tāmākavē paḷḷi vēlikaḷilum camūkac ceyalkaḷilum īṭupaṭumpaṭic ceyvār.  kūccamuḷḷa ciṟuvaṉ ētāvatu oru ceyalil īṭupaṭṭuviṭṭāl atu oru nalla toṭakkamākum.  tāṉum piṟaruṭiya makiḻccikku oru kāraṇam eṉṟum, tāṉum oru kuḻuviṉ mukkiya uṟuppiṉaṉ eṉṟum ciṟuvaṉ tāṉākavē uṇarum āṟṟal peṟṟu viṭṭāl, avaṉ pakaṟkaṉavu kāṇamāṭṭāṉ; otuṅkip pōkavum ceyvāṉ.  icciṟuvaṉiṭam kāṇappeṟum taṉittiṟami yoṉṟiṉik kuḻuc ceyalukkup payaṉpaṭac ceytāl, acciṟuvaṉukkup piṟaruṭiya pārāṭṭutal kiṭikkum; inta veṟṟiyiṉāl avaṉ nāḷaṭivil paṭippaṭiyākak kūccamum nīṅki camūkac ceyalkaḷukku naṉku porutta muṟuvāṉ.
veḷittōṟṟam49 allatu mēlvēṭam
oru nāḷ putitāka vanta kaṇita āciriyar tiru. pa-eṭṭām vakuppiṟkuk kaṇitap pāṭam naṭatta nēriṭṭatu.  aṉṟutāṉ avar mutaṉ mutalāka av vakuppiṟkuḷ nuḻintār.  mutalil varukippativu ceyya niṉittu ovvoru māṇākkaṉiyum peyarkoṇṭu aḻittār.  ellām cariyāka naṭipeṟṟatu.  ‘turirāj! eṉṟu kūppiṭṭatum vantatu viṉi.  vaḷarnta piyaṉ oruvaṉ eḻuntu puyaṅkaḷi muṉṉum piṉṉumāka acittuk koṇṭum, muṉ kiyiluḷḷa jippā pakutiyic curuṭṭikkoṇṭum kātu tuḷikkumpaṭi vantuḷḷēṉ aiyā, nāṉ cariyāka irukkiṟēṉ pāruṅkaḷ-turirāj vakuppil cariyākattāṉ irukkiṉṟāṉ eṉṟu kattiṉāṉ.  vakuppi luḷḷavarkaḷ aṉivarum nakittaṉar; taṟkālikamāṉa oruvita oḻuṅkiṉmi vakuppil nilaviyatu.  aṭutta nāḷum itē piracciṉitāṉ.
āciriyar tiru. pa - oṉṟum collavilli.  turirarājuviṉ naṭattiyip paṟṟi etuvum pēcavilli.  vakuppuvēḷiyiṉ iṟutiyil avar, māliyil paḷḷiviṭṭatum turirājit taṉ aṟiyil taṉiyākac cantikkumāṟu kūṟiṉār.  ovvoruvarum aṅṅaṉamē taṉṉit taṉimiyil kāṇa vēṇṭum eṉṟum aṟivittār.  turirāj cantippatāka oppuk koṇṭāṉ; coṉṉapaṭiyē ceytāṉ.
mutaṉ mutalil turirāj āciriyarit taṉimiyil cantitta poḻutu avaṉ coṉṉatu:  nāṉ caṟṟuk kaṭumi yāṉavaṉ eṉpatit tāṅkaḷ aṟivīrkaḷ eṉa niṉikkiṟēṉ.  ceṉṟa yāṇṭu nāṉ payiṉṟa paḷḷiyil āciriyar oruvarip paḷḷiyiliruntē viraṭṭi viṭṭēṉ eṉpatu.  āciriyar tiru. pa-ataṟku maṟumoḻi oṉṟum kūṟavilli.  turirāj pēcik koṇṭē pōṉāṉ.  avaṉ coṉṉāṉ:  eṉakkut taṭitta kural irukkiṉṟatu eṉpati nīṅkaḷ aṟivīrkaḷ eṉa niṉik kiṉṟēṉ.”  āciriyar tiru pa-taṉakkut taṭitta kuralip paṟṟik kavaliyilliyeṉṟum, tāṉ takarappeṭṭit toḻiṟ cāliyil vēli ceytu paḻakkam iruppatāl, avvoli taṉakkup paḻakkappaṭṭatu tāṉ eṉṟum colli vāḷā iruntār.  piṟaku turirāj coṉṉāṉ: nāṉ virumpiṉāl metuvākavum pēcamuṭiyum eṉṟu.  itaṟku āciriyar tiru pa-nēraṭiyāṉa patil collavilli.  oru māṇākkaṉ evvaḷavu urattup pēciṉālum atippaṟṟit tāṉ kavali koḷva tilli eṉṟu maṭṭilum patil kūṟiṉār.  turirāju piṟariṭam naṉku paḻakavēṇṭumeṉṟum, avaṉuṭiya etirkālattip paṟṟiya tiṭṭattil tāṉ tuṇi ceyya virumpuvatākavum, avaṉuṭiya piracciṉikaḷukkut tīrvu kāṇpatil tāṉ tuṇiceyvatākavum avar kūṟi, itaṟkākattāṉ taṉimiyil avaṉiṭam pēca virumpiṉatākavum urittār.  mēlum, avar avaṉiṭam nāḷtōṟum vakuppil peyarkaḷi aḻippatāl yātoru payaṉum illi eṉṟum, ataṟkup patilāka `irukkippaṭam50 eṉṟu āyattamceytu ataṉpaṭi māṇākkariṉ varukiyic caripārttāl mēl eṉṟu tāṉ karutuvatākak kūṟiṉār.  turirājum itu takunta tiṭṭam eṉṟu oppuk koṇṭāṉ.  piṟaku āciriyar tiru pa-at tiṭṭatti āyattam ceyyum paṭiyum, varukiṟa vārattil varukip pativu ceyyum poṟuppi avaṉi mēṟkoḷḷum paṭiyum kēṭṭukkoṇṭār; avaṉum aṅṅaṉam ceyvatāka oppukkoṇṭāṉ.
aṭutta nāḷ varukip pativu vēliyit turirāj mēṟkoṇṭāṉ.  avaṉuṭiya naṭattiyil viyattaku māṟṟam kāṇappeṟṟatu.  atiliruntu turirāj urattup pēcuvatiyē niṟuttik koṇṭāṉ; ottuḻikkum māṇākkaṉāka māṟi viṭṭāṉ.
itu māṇākkaṉiṉ veḷittōṟṟattiṟkum ataṉi āciriyar camāḷippataṟkum ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭākum.  immātiri naṭattikkuk kāraṇam ammāṇākkaṉiṭam paṭintirunta tāḻvuc cikkalum,51 tāṉ otukkap peṟṟatākak karututalumē.  āciriyar turirājuviṉiṭam paṭintirunta inta iraṇṭu maṉappōkkukaḷiyum nīkkiviṭṭatāl turirāju iyalpāṉa māṇākkaṉākiviṭṭāṉ.
tākkun taṉmi52
kuḻantikaḷukkum avarkaḷatu nōkkaṅkaḷukkum iṭiyil piṟarāl, taṉi maṉitaṉāl allatu camūkattāl tiṇikkappeṟṟa taṭikaḷukku ēṟpa mēṟkoḷḷappeṟum piṭivātamāṉa tulaṅkalkaḷē tākkuttaṉmiyākum eṉṟu marpiyum avaratu tōḻarkaḷum variyaṟi ceytuḷḷaṉar.  itiliruntu tākkun taṉmi oru piṟavip paṇpu aṉṟeṉṟum, aḵtu oru nerukkaṭiyic camāḷikka eḻum paṇpu eṉṟum peṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.  oru kuḻantiyiṉ nōkkattiṟkut taṭi yēṟpaṭṭālum, allatu ak kuḻanti avamāṉap paṭuttappeṟṟālum, allatu ataṟkut tōlviyēṟpaṭṭālum akkuḻanti yiṭam tākkun taṉmi veḷippaṭalām.
tākkuntaṉmiyil iruvakiyuṇṭu.  oṉṟu, kuḻantikaḷ tammuṭiya taṉṉampikkiyāl veḷikkāṭṭuvatu; iraṇṭu, pātukāppiṉmi uṇarcciyāl veḷippaṭuvatu.
iraṇṭāvatu vakiyil, kuḻantikaḷ tammi ulakam vēṇṭām eṉṟu colvatāka uṇarvatāl ulakiṉit tākka niṉikkiṉṟaṉa; tammuṭiya taṉ-matippi mīṇṭum peṟavēṇṭumeṉṟu taṉṉiviṭa iḷiyōrmītum allatu, enta nikaḻcciyiṉmītum ātikkam celuttu kiṉṟaṉa; allatu mikavum āḻnta maṉamuṟivu53 avikaḷiṭamiruppatāl, aḵtu avarkaḷiyē mīṟi veṭittut tākkun taṉmiyākat tulaṅku kiṉṟatu.
mutalvakit tākkuntaṉmiyi veḷikkāṭṭum kuḻantikaḷiṭam mutirnta naṭattik kōlaṅkaḷ tulaṅkum eṉpati etirpārkkalām.  kāraṇam, avarkaḷ nallatu etu, keṭṭatu etu eṉpatip pirittaṟintu ceyalāṟṟukiṉṟaṉar.  pātukāppiṉmiyāl tākkuntaṉmiyi mēṟkoḷḷum kuḻantikaḷ kaṇmūṭittaṉamāṉa uṭtēvikaḷiṉ kāraṇamāka viṉiyāṟṟukiṉṟaṉa; ātalāl, avi nallatu etu, keṭṭatu etu eṉṟu terintukoḷvatiyē vaḷarttukkoḷvatilli.
perumpālāṉa tākkuntūṇṭalkaḷ maṉamuṟiviṉ kāraṇamāka eḻupavi.  tammuṭiya vakuppu māṇākkaṉ oruvaṉāl tontaravu tarappeṟum māṇākkaṉ, tontaravu tantavaṉit tākkukiṉṟāṉ.  camūkat tāḻvuḷḷa vakuppiṉaric cārnta kuḻantikaḷiṭamtāṉ perumpālum innēraṭit tākkutalkaḷ nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa.  naṭuttara vakuppiṉar, uyarnili vakuppiṉaric cārntavarkaḷiṭam ippaṇpunēraṭiyāka veḷip paṭuvatilli; nēral muṟikaḷil atu veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu.  oruvarāl avamāṉappaṭuttap peṟṟavar avamāṉappaṭuttiya varit tākka niṉippattilli.  tāṉ virumpāta avarip paṟṟi ētāvatu kuttalāṉa pēcciṉip pēcit taṉ uṇarcciyi veḷippaṭutti viṭuvar.  kapaṭamaṟṟavarkaḷ tām tākkum uṇarcciyuḷḷavarkaṭku ilakkāka54 amikiṉṟaṉar.  illā ḷuṭaṉ kalām viḷittuk koṇṭu kāliyil vakuppiṟku varum āciriyar taṉ vakuppup piḷḷikaḷiṭam tākkum uṇarcciyip pala vaṭivaṅkaḷil kāṭṭukiṉṟār.  āciriyarāl vakuppaṟiyil taṭippaṭuttap peṟum māṇākkaṉ iṭi vēḷiyil veḷi varuṅkāl viḷiyāṭṭu mitāṉattiluḷḷa oru kuḻantiyic cēṟṟil taḷḷukiṉṟāṉ.  maṉamuṟivuṭaṉirupparvakaḷ uyiraṟṟa poruḷkaḷit tākkit tammuṭiya uṇarcciyi veḷip paṭuttukiṉṟaṉar.  taṭṭumuṭṭukaḷi uṭippōr, nāṟkāliyi utippōr, katavukaḷip paṭār eṉṟu kiyāḷupavar ākiyōrkaḷi nām kāṇāmal illi.  maṉapporuttam illāta poḻutu cila camayam vīṭṭiluḷḷa peṇkaḷ cāmāṉkaḷi loṭ eṉṟa ōciyuṭaṉ vittu veḷip paṭuttuvati nām kāṇattāṉ ceykiṉṟōm.
tākkuntaṉmit tūṇṭalkaḷ maṉita kulattiṟkuk kavaliyiyum pōrāṭṭattiyum koṭukkakkūṭiyaṉavāka uḷḷaṉa.  ivaṟṟi eppaṭic camāḷippatu eṉpatu evarum aṟiyāta putirāka irukkiṉṟatu.  ivaṟṟāl evarum iṉpam aṭivatu milli; iviyiṉṟi evarum uyir vāḻavum muṭiyātu.  āṉāl, tākkuntaṉmiyit takka muṟiyil maṭimāṟṟam ceyvatāl (e-ṭu.  pōṭṭip pantayam, nāṭakam pōṉṟavi) niṟinta camūka naṉmiyip peṟalām.
ottuḻippum pōṭṭiyum
iṉṟiya nākarikam taṉittaṉi makkaḷ pōṭṭiyiṭṭu evvāṟēṉum uyarntu varuvataṉiyē pārāṭṭi varukiṉṟatu.  paḷḷiyilum ippōṭṭi niliyē kuṭikoṇṭuḷḷatu.  innili māṟavēṇṭumeṉpatē aṟiñaruṭiya viruppam.  aintu yāṇṭiliruntu pattuyāṇṭu vayatuḷḷa kuḻavikaḷi iru pirivākap pirittu, oru pirivip paḻiyapaṭi aṭakki āḷum muṟivaḻiyē payiṟṟiyum maṟṟoru piriviṉi urimiyum poṟuppum peṟṟu viḷaṅkum kuṭiyaracu muṟivaḻiyē payiṟṟiyum cila aṟiñar āyntaṉar.  kuṭiyaracuk kalvi muṟiyiṉ payaṉākak kuḻavikaḷiṭiyē maṉattiṭpam, oḻukkamuṟi mutaliyaṉa ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉavām; kūṭṭuṟavum tōḻamiyum niṟintu oḷirnta ṉavām; pakiyum pōrāṭṭamum oru ciṟitum iṭampeṟa villiyām.  aṭakkiyāḷum kalvi muṟiyilō tākkalum pōrāṭṭamumē eṇ maṭaṅku mikku viḷaṅkiṉavām; oru pāvamum aṟiyār mēlum, intap pōrāṭṭam pāyntatām.  pōrāṭṭam illiyāṉāl ulakil oru cuviyum tōṉṟa iṭamiṉṟi inta muṟiyiṟ paḻakiya kuḻavikaḷ iṭar uṟukiṉ ṟaṉavām.
tērtal muṟiyē pōṭṭi muṟiyākum.  kalvi payiṟṟu vatil pōṭṭi muṟiyik kiyāḷuvatāl mellak kaṟkum kuḻavikaḷ ūkkam iḻantu maṉam kuṉṟic ceyalaṟṟu niṟkiṉṟaṉa.  allatu, eppaṭiyēṉum oḻiyaṭṭum, eṉ ceyvatu? eṉa vāḷā kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.  virintu kaṟkum kuḻavikaḷ tamakku uriyatallāta perumiyip peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  kalvi muṟit tērvil tērcci peṟāta kuḻavi ippaṭittāṉ niṟkum eṉa uṟuti kūṟuvataṟkilli.  tōlvi kuḻaviyippaṟṟiya oru kīḻmi uṇarviṉiyum, nili iḻaviṉiyum kuḻaviyiṉ uḷḷattē viḷivittati viṭavum kūṭum. appoḻutu, kuḻavi taṉakkuḷ tāṉē ceṉṟu oḷittup pakaṟkaṉavu kaṇṭu kaḷikkum; neṟi piṟaḻnta naṭattiyilum īṭupaṭa nērum.  piṟpāṭāṉa kuḻavikaḷ eṉap paḷḷiyil ikaḻappeṟuvaṉa camutāyattil peruṅ kēṭṭiṉi viḷivikkum.  ulakil maṉappāṭam ceyvatilēyē perumi cikkikkiṭakkiṉṟatu eṉṟu yārtāṉ collat tuṇivar? eppaṭiyēṉum vakuppil tam niliyik kāttu varutal vēṇṭum eṉṟu muyaṉṟum, veṟṟipeṟāviṭil iḻipiṟappāy viṭum eṉṟum nampappukuntu piṟaku kuḻavikaḷip paṟṟiya kavali eṉpatē eḷḷaḷavum iṉṟi vāḻum pōrāṭ ṭattiyē kāṇkiṉṟōm.  ottuḻiyāmiyē ittakiya paḷḷiyil vaḷarakkūṭum.  oruvarukkoruvar pōṭṭi pōṭuvatāl aiyaṅkaḷum, poṟāmiyum ericcalumē viḷikiṉṟaṉa.
āṟām iyalil taṉṉōkka muṟiyippaṟṟik kaṟṟōmallavā? atil muṉṉēṟpāṭākat tiṭṭam pōṭṭuk kuḻavikaḷē kūṭṭuṟavāka āciriyarkaḷiṉ kaṇkāṇippil payiṉṟu varuvatik kaṇṭōm.  itil kuḻavikaḷ ceyyavō ārāyavō muyalappōvataṉi muṉṉatākavē aviyaṉittum oṉṟu cērttu variyaṟippaṭuttik koṇṭu uṟutiyuṭaṉ uḻikka muntukiṉṟaṉa.  ceyyappōvataṟku ēṟṟa tiṭaṭaṅkaḷi vari yaṟuppatilum avi aṉittum ottuḻikkiṉṟaṉa.  ataṉi muṭippataṟkeṉa eḻum poṟuppilum avi aṉittum paṅku koḷḷukiṉṟaṉa.  tōlviyō veṟṟiyō avi aṉit tiṟkumē ēṟpaṭum eṉpatil ak kuḻavikaḷiṭiyē aiyam oṉṟum eḻuvatilli.
itiliruntu kūṭṭuṟavi vaḷarkkum kalvi muṟiyē ciṟantatu eṉpatu kuṉṟiṉ mēliṭṭa viḷakkākum.  kuḻavi kaḷiṭiyē takkatoru nōkkattit tūṇṭi eḻupputal vēṇṭum.  ataṟku ēṟṟa oru vaḻiyiṉiyum kuḻavi kāṇumāṟu ceytal vēṇṭum.  ataṉip piṉṉum muyalavēṇṭiya ōr ūkka niliyiyum kiḷarntu ōṅkac ceytal vēṇṭum.  innilikaḷil ellām māṇākkarkaḷiṭiyēyum āciriyar māṇākkariṭiyēyum uḷḷak kiḷarcciyippaṟṟi nerukkaṭi oṉṟum nērātavāṟu kāttal vēṇṭum.  ivaṟṟiṟkellām takka paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷi eḻuppi vaki ceytu viṭuvatē āciriyak kaliyiṉ aruntiṟaṉākum.  ittakiya amiti nili ēṟpaṭāta poḻutu kuḻavikaḷiṭiyē kontaḷippiyē kāṇkiṉṟōm.  etirppum kalakamumē maṉa nili aḷavilō, aṉṟi veḷic ceyalilō tōṉṟi viṭukiṉṟaṉa.  akkaṟi ciṟitumē iṉṟik kalviyippaṟṟik kavali koḷḷāta niliyum kuḻaviyiṉ maṉattē eḻum.  taṉ-muyaṟci eṉpatu ciṟitum illāmaṟ pōkum.  uḷḷūṟa aṉṟip puṟat tōṟṟat taḷavil maṭṭum oḻuṅku muṟikku oppa oḻuki aṭaṅki niṟkiṉṟa niliyum viḷiyum.  pakaṟkaṉavu kaṇṭu kaṟpaṉiyulakiṟ pukuvatum nikaḻum.  ivi aṉittiyum ārāyntu takka vaḻiyil ūkkattiṉi viḷivittu maṉaniliyic camaniliyiṟ koṇṭu vantu camutāyattōṭu poruttamuṟṟu vāḻumāṟu kuḻaviyit tiruttuvatē āciriyariṉ kaṭamiyākum.  inta muyaṟciyil kuṭumpamum paḷḷiyum ottuḻittālaṉṟi veṟṟi kāṇpatu eḷitaṉṟu.  itaṟkēṟṟa kalvittiṭṭam vakuppatu kalvi nipuṇarkaḷiṉ kaṭami; ataṉi veṟṟiyuṭaṉ niṟivēṟṟuvatu āciriyariṉ poṟuppu.  aṭippaṭik kalvittiṭṭam naṉṉiliyil ceyaṟpaṭṭāl itil ōraḷavu veṟṟi kāṇalām.
iyal-11
11. oḻukka vaḷarcci
oḻukkam eṉpatip paṟṟi nām palapaṭiyākap pēcukiṉṟōm. nalloḻukkam eṉṟum tīyoḻukkam eṉṟum, uyar oḻukkam eṉṟum tāḻvoḻukkam eṉṟum oḻukkam palavitamākap pākupaṭutti urikkap peṟukiṉṟatu. oḻukkam eṉpataṟkup palavitamāka ilakkaṇam kūṟalām. kūṟuvōr nōkkattiṟ kēṟpa avarāl kūṟappeṟum ilakkaṇamum amiyum. uyir nūlōr oruvitamāka urippar; aṟanūlār piṟitoruvitamākap pakarvar. kalvi uḷaviyal neṟippaṭi iṉṉoru vitamāka iv vilakkaṇam amiyum; iṅkuc camūka iyal paṟṟiya kūṟukaḷi itu aṭakkikkoṇṭu ilaṅkum. tiruvaḷḷuvar oḻukkamuṭimi eṉṟa atikārattil tarum cila kuṟaḷkaḷ īṇṭu ōrntu uṇartaṟpālaṉa.
kalvi uḷaviyal kūṟum oḻukkam yātu? taṉiyāḷ koṇṭirukkum ellā maṉap pōkkukaḷiṉ oṭṭumottamē oḻukkam eṉpatu. mutalāvatāka, atu kuṭivaḻiyi, aḵtāvatu piṟaviyilēyē iyalpāka aminta pōkkukaḷi-iyal pūkkaṅkaḷi-aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭatu. nāḷaṭivil ippōkku taṉiyāḷ camūkac cūḻniliyil peṟum paṭṭaṟiviṉ mūlam tiruttiyamikkap peṟṟup paḻakkaṅkaḷ avaṟṟiṉ iṭattip peṟukiṉṟaṉa. oḻukkam paḻakkaṅkaḷiṉ tiraṭciyē eṉṟu nām aṭikkaṭi kūṟukiṉṟō maṉṟō? āṉāl, oḻukkam paḻakkaṅkaḷukkum mēṟpaṭṭatu. kāraṇam, paḻakkaṅkaḷ poṟiyiyal taṉmiyuṭiyavi; oru kuṟippiṭṭa nilimi kaḷukkēṟṟavāṟu tāmāka iyaṅkupavi; āṉāl vāḻkki iṅṅaṉam tāṉāka iyaṅkuvataṉṟu; poṟiyiyal taṉmiyu ṭiyatumaṉṟu. paḻakkaṅkaḷukkup piṉṉaṇiyāka niṉṟu iyaṅkak kūṭiya ceyalāṇmi1 oṉṟirukka vēṇṭum; ivvāṟṟaltāṉ avaṟṟik kaṭṭuppaṭutti avi cellum vaḻiyiliruntu mīṭṭum putiya nilimikaḷic camāḷikkac ceykiṉṟatu. paṟṟukkaḷum maṉanilikaḷum oru paṟṟiṉ kīḻ aminta avaṟṟiṉ oḻuṅkāṉa amippumē intac ceyalāṇmiyil aṭaṅkiya kūṟukaḷākum. eṉavē, oḻukkam eṉpatu iyalpākavuḷḷa maṉanili, mīppaṇpu ākiyavaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭiyil aminta payiṉṟa pōkkukaḷ2 ākum; nammuṭiya paṟṟukkaḷum paḻakkaṅkaḷum ataṉuḷ aṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, aṟitiṟaṉ vaḻi kāṭ ṭiyāka amiya, iyalpāṉa maṉa nilikaḷ putikac cūḻnili yuṭaṉum, camūkac cūḻniliyuṭaṉum iṭiviṉi iyaṟṟuvatāl ēṟpaṭum viḷivē oḻukkam eṉpatu. paṟṟukkaḷē3 oḻukkattiṉ aṭippaṭikaḷāka amikiṉṟaṉa.
payiṟṟum āciriyar pāṭattiṭṭam, pāṭavēḷip paṭṭi, tērvukaḷ, nūlakaṅkaḷ, poruṭkāṭci cāli mutaliyavaṟṟik kavaṉikka vēṇṭum eṉṟālum, navīṉa kalvi muṟiyil mika mukkiyamākak kavaṉikka vēṇṭiyatu kuḻantikaḷiṉ oḻukka mēyākum. kalvi eṉpatu māṇākkarkaḷiṉ muḻu maṉitat taṉmiyi-āḷumiyi-malarac ceyvatu aṉṟō? payiṟṟaliṉ nōkkamum aḵtākat tāṉē irukkavēṇṭum? māṇākkar kaḷiṭam iyalpākat tōṉṟum maṉappōkkukaḷi māṉiṭa camūkattiṟkēṟṟavāṟu vaḷarttalē payiṟṟalākum. payiṟṟal naṉmuṟiyil amintāl oḻukkamum naṉmuṟiyil amiyum.
paṟṟukkaḷ
iṉi, oḻukkattiṉ aṭippaṭiyākavuḷḷa paṟṟukkaḷic ciṟitu ārāyvōm. 
vāḻkki aṉupavattil uḷḷak kiḷarccikaḷ pala poruḷkaḷic cuṟṟi amikiṉṟaṉa. piṉṉar apporuḷō allatu ataṉip paṟṟiya eṇṇamō eḻuntāl ivvuḷḷak kiḷarcciyiṉ cērkki muḻumiyum tūṇṭappeṟukiṉṟatu. uḷḷak kiḷarcciyiṉ cērkkiyiyē paṟṟu eṉṟu nām vaḻaṅkukiṉṟōm. paṟṟukkaḷ karuttu, maṉitar, paḷḷi pōṉṟa niliyaṅkaḷ, poruḷ-ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiṉic cuṟṟi amiyum. mika iḷamik kālattilēyē ciṟuvaṉ taṉ aṉṉiyippaṟṟip pala uḷḷak kiḷarccikaḷiyuṭiyavaṉāka irukkiṉṟāṉ. aṉṉi uṇavūṭṭum poḻutum, cīrāṭṭip pārāṭṭum poḻutum makiḻkiṉṟāṉ; avaḷ kaṭintukoḷḷum poḻutum oṟukkum poḻutum ciṉam koḷkiṉṟāṉ; accuṟuttum poḻutu veṟuppuk koḷkiṉṟāṉ. eṉavē, avaṉuṭiya aṉṉi pala nilikaḷukkēṟpa uvakiyūṭṭum poruḷākavum, ciṉamūṭṭum poruḷākavum, veṟuppūṭṭum poruḷākavum amikiṉṟāḷ. ciṟuvaṉuṭiya paṭṭaṟivukaḷiṉ4 payaṉāka avaṉuṭiya aṉṉiyic cuṟṟip pala uḷḷak kiḷarccikaḷ amikiṉṟaṉa. inta uḷḷak kiḷarccikaḷiṉ tokutiyē-piṇṭamē-paṟṟu eṉpatu. cila eḻuccikaḷiyum viḻivukaḷiyum kuṟippiṭṭa poruḷkaḷuṭaṉ iṇittut tuykkum taṉippaṭṭa pōkkē paṟṟu eṉpatu  mākṭōkaliṉ kūṟṟākum.
ciṟuvaṉ vaḷara vaḷara, avaṉuṭiya vāḻkkiyil perum pakuti paṟṟukkaḷāl niṟikiṉṟaṉa; paḷḷiyi naṭuvākak koṇṭu pala paṟṟukkaḷ vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa. taṉippaṭṭa āciriyar mītu paṟṟu uṇṭākiṉṟatu. namatu nāṭṭip poruttamaṭṭilum kuṭumpattiṉ mītu paṟṟu uṇṭākiṉṟatu. ataṉ piṟaku nāṭṭi virumpu kiṉṟōm. ataṉ payaṉāka nāṭṭuk kuḻittu, aḻiyāp pukaḻpeṟṟa, taṉṉalamaṟṟa vīrarkaḷ aṟiñarkaḷ camaya tūtarkaḷ, camūkac cīrtiruttavātikaḷ, nāṭṭukkoṭi, nāṭṭu vāḻttup pāṭal pōṉṟavaṟṟip pōṟṟukiṉṟōm. pāratiyāriṉ centamiḻ nāṭeṉum pōtiṉilē eṉṟa pāṭali īṇṭu niṉivu kūrka. inta vitamāṉa uḷḷak kiḷarccikaḷiṉ cērkki nāṭṭuppaṟṟik kuṟikkiṉṟatu. intap paṟṟukkaḷippōlavē, māṇākkarkaḷukkup pāṭaṅkaḷilum paṟṟu amivatuṇṭu. paṟṟup poruḷik katiravaṉākavum, uḷḷak kiḷarccikaḷik kōḷkaḷākavum uruvakappaṭutti urikkalām. kōḷkaḷ katiravaṉic cuṟṟi varuvaṉa pōlavē, uḷḷak kiḷarccikaḷ paṟṟup poruḷic cuṟṟukiṉṟaṉa.
kāṭcip poruḷkaḷic cuṟṟiyum maṉitarkaḷic cuṟṟiyum paṟṟukkaḷ amivaṉa pōlavē, karuttup poruḷkaḷiyum kuṟikkōṭ poruḷkaḷiyum cuṟṟip paṟṟukkaḷ paṭikiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟi aṟap paṟṟukkaḷ5 eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar. aṉpu, nīti, nērmi, uṇmi pōṉṟa karuttup poruḷkaḷic cuṟṟip paṟṟukkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭu māṇākkarkaḷiṉ nalloḻukkam vaḷarkiṉṟatu. aṟap paṟṟukkaḷ yāvum camūka vāḻkkiyiṉ viḷivāl ēṟpaṭupavi. cila camūkattiṟkum, cila camayaṅkaḷukkum, cila caṅkaṅkaḷukkum pala aṟap paṟṟukkaḷ talimuṟi talimuṟiyāka vantu koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. rā6 eṉṟa uḷaviyal aṟiñar aṟappaṟṟukkaḷ kāṭcic ciṟappu, kāṭcip potu, karuttu eṉṟa oḻuṅkil vaḷarvatākak kūṟukiṉṟār. oru ciṟuvaṉ tāṉ vāḻum cūḻniliyil uḷḷa kuṟippiṭṭa oruvarmītu aṉpu celuttu kiṉṟāṉ; iṅku ac ciṟuvaṉiṉ aṉpu eṉṉum paṟṟu kāṭcic ciṟappup paṟṟāka amikiṉṟatu. piṟaku ivarip pōṉṟa palariṭamum aṉpu celuttukiṉṟāṉ; ippoḻutu ap paṟṟu kāṭcip potup paṟṟāka ami kiṉṟatu. iṟutiyāka, ac ciṟuvaṉ ammāntar aṉivarum piṉpaṟṟum paṇpiṉmītu aṉpu celuttukiṉṟāṉ; ippoḻutu appaṟṟu karuttup poruḷ paṟṟāka amintu viṭukiṉṟatu. acciṟuvaṉ tuṇivāṉa oruvari virumpalām; piṟaku tuṇivāṉavarkaḷ aṉivariyum virumpuvāṉ; iṟutiyākat tuṇivu eṉpatiyē virumpum nili avaṉiṭam amintu viṭukiṉṟatu.
aṟap paṟṟukkaḷ eṅṅaṉam amikiṉṟaṉa eṉpati āciriyarkaḷ naṉku uṇarntu ataṟkēṟpak kuḻanti vāḻum cūḻniliyi naṟpaṇpukaḷiṉ eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷāl nirappa vēṇṭum. kuḻanti iḷaṅkumarap paruvatti eytiyatum ilakkiyattilum varalāṟṟilum kāṇum kuṟikkōḷ māntar kaḷi avaṉukku ilakkāka vikkalām. irāmaṉ, parataṉ, ilakkuvaṉ pōṉṟa kāviya māntarkaḷiyum, periya purāṇattil kāṇum pala nāyaṉmārkaḷiyum iṉṉum ivarkaḷ pōṉṟa palariyum kuṟikkōḷ māntarkaḷākak karutalām. puttaruṭiya vāḻkki ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭāṉa vāḻkkiyākum. nammuṭiya nāṭṭut tanti kāntiyaṭikaḷiṉ vāḻkkiyum ittakiyatē.
ittakiya aṟap paṟṟukkaḷip peṟṟu, avaṟṟi naṉku cintittu, avaṟṟit taṟpaṟṟiṉuḷ7 aṭakki yamippatē aṟattuṟip paṇpāṭeṉpatu. naṉmi, uṇmi, tūymi ākiyavaṟṟi nām virumpupavarkaḷāka nammi nām karutiṉāl, ippaṇpukaḷ nammuṭiya aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkiyil tali kāṭṭum. ivaṟṟi ārāyuṅkāl aṭi nilikkum avā nilikkum uḷḷa vēṟṟumiyi niṉivukūrtal iṉṟiyamiyātatu. uṇmiyil, eṉṉiṭamuḷḷa ellāp paṟṟukkaḷum eṉṉuṭiya taṟpaṟṟic cuṟṟilum amikiṉṟaṉa. eṉṉiṭam uṇmiyil uṟutiyāka illāta, āṉāl nāṉ avāvi niṟkum, paṟṟukkaḷ yāvi? ittakiya paṟṟukkaḷtām kuṟikkōḷkaḷ8; ivaṟṟit taḻuvik koṇṭu niṟkum tāṉ eṉpati nām karutalām. nām evvaḷavuk kevvaḷavu intat tāṉ eṉpati virumpu kiṉṟōmō avvaḷavukkavvaḷavu ataṉi aṭital kūṭum. atit teḷivākak karuti ataṉi aṭiya muyaluvōmāyiṉ, nām mika uyarnta oḻukkanilikku uyarntu koṇṭirukkiṉṟōm eṉṟa nili ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu.
ivviṭattil oṉṟu niṉivu kūrtaṟpālatu. pala paṟṟukkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭu oḻuṅku peṟuṅkāl, oru paṟṟu ciṟantatākavum ēṉiyavi tāḻntavikaḷākavum amiyum. icciṟanta paṟṟi mutaṉmip paṟṟu9 eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar. ittalimippaṟṟē oruvaruṭiya vāḻkkiyiṉ mukkiya nōkkattik kuṟikkum. ippaṟṟutāṉ ēṉiya paṟṟukkaḷi āṭkoḷḷukiṉṟatu. ēṉiya paṟṟukkaḷ taṉittaṉiyāka iyaṅkuvativiṭa avi ippaṟṟiṉkīḻ iyaṅkum poḻututāṉ ōr uyiriyiṉ naṭattiyil orumippāṭu amikiṉṟatu. mēlum, piṟa poruḷkaḷic cuṟṟip paṟṟukkaḷ uṇṭāvatupōl nammik kuṟittum paṟṟu ēṟpaṭum. itit taṉ-matippuppaṟṟu10 eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvar uḷaviyalār. itit tāṉ ulakavaḻakkil taṉ māṉam11 eṉṟu collukiṉṟōm. itil nammic cuṟṟi uḷḷak kiḷarccikaḷ amikiṉṟaṉa. maṉitaṉ taṉṉuṭiya taṉmiyit taṉip poruḷākavum matippuṭiyatākavum karutukiṉṟāṉ; pala paṭṭaṟivukaḷip peṟukiṉṟāṉ; taṉ-matippi vaḷarkka muyalukiṉṟāṉ. itaṉ mūlamākavē uḷḷatteḻum muraṇpāṭukaḷit tīrttal iyalum; kāntiyaṭikaḷ pōṉṟa periyārkaḷiṭam taṉ-matippuppaṟṟu ciṟantu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu; avarkaḷ taṉmiyil ōr orumippāṭṭiyum kāṇamuṭikiṉṟatu.
paṟṟukkaḷiṉ vaki : paṟṟukkaḷ iruvakippaṭum. oṉṟu, uṭaṉpāṭṭuppaṟṟu; maṟṟoṉṟu, etirmaṟippaṟṟu. aḵtāvatu, nammiṭam viruppappaṟṟum12 ēṟpaṭalām; veṟuppup paṟṟum13 uṇṭākalām. cila eṭuttuk kāṭṭukkaḷ itaṉit teḷivākkum. nāṭṭuppaṟṟu eṉṟa iyalpūkkam piṟa nāṭukaḷi veṟuppataṉālum amiyalām; puraṭci eṉṟa paṟṟu kuṟikkōḷ nāṭṭik kāṇavēṇṭum eṉṟa avāviṉālum amiyalām; allatu aṉṟiya araciṉmītu koṇṭuḷḷa veṟuppiṉālum uṇṭākalām. uṇmi eṉṟa paṟṟu uṇmi eṉṟa paṇpiṉ mītu uḷḷa ārvattiṉālum ēṟpaṭalām; allatu poymiyiṉ mītuḷḷa veṟuppiṉālum uṇṭākalām. māṇākkarkaḷiṭam uṭaṉpāṭṭup paṟṟukkaḷ amivatē virumpattakkatu. ciṟanta āḷumi vaḷarccikku ivikaḷē aṭippaṭi yāṉavi. etirmaṟip paṟṟukkaḷ neṟikōṇiya vāḻvil koṇṭu celuttiviṭum.
paṟṟukkaḷ uṇṭākum muṟi : paṟṟukkaḷ eṅṅaṉam uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa eṉpati ārāyvōm. oru kuḻantiyiṉ vāḻkki, toṭakkattil iyalpūkkattil mītūrntu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. ivi ceyaṟpaṭuvatil yātoru oḻuṅkum illi. kuḻantiyiṉ cūḻnili ataṟkup putiya nilimikaḷit tantu koṇṭē irukkiṉṟatu. kuḻantiyiṉ iyalpūkkaṅkaḷ avvappoḻutu eḻum viḻivukaḷukkum tēvikaḷukkum ēṟṟavāṟē ceyaṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nāḷaṭivil taṉittaṉiyāka, tōṉṟiyapaṭiyellām ceyaṟpaṭṭa iyalpūkkaṅkaḷ oru kaṭṭuppāṭṭiṉuḷ aṭaṅki oru kuṟippiṭṭa poruḷic cuṟṟi iyaṅkat toṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. paṭṭaṟivu vaḷara vaḷara, kāṭcip paṟṟu, karuttuppaṟṟu, aṟappaṟṟu, mutaṉmippaṟṟu, taṉ-matippup paṟṟu pōṉṟavikaḷākat tulakkamuṟukiṉṟaṉa.
oru kuṟippiṭṭa poruḷic cuṟṟi orupaṟṟu vaḷarat toṭaṅkiṉāl, apporuḷippaṟṟi iyaṅkum attaṉiyāḷiṉ iyalpūkkaṅkaḷ appaṟṟiṉāl kaṭṭuppaṭuttap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. apporuḷuṭaṉ icintupōkum iyalpūkkaṅkaḷ valup peṟukiṉṟaṉa; apporuḷuṭaṉ muraṇpaṭṭu niṟkum iyal pūkkaṅkaḷ kaṭṭuppaṭuttap peṟṟu maṭṭuppaṭuttap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. toṭakkattil ciṟuvaṉ aṉṉiyiṭam aḷavaṟṟa aṉpu kāṭṭukiṉṟāṉ; avaṉ uṇavūṭṭum poḻutum cīrāṭṭum poḻutum iṉpuṟukiṉṟāṉ. avaḷit taḻuvukiṉṟāṉ; avaḷuṭaṉ koñcukiṉṟāṉ; viḷiyāṭukiṉṟāṉ; ivvāṟu pala iṉpuṟuttum ceyalkaḷip purikiṉṟāṉ. avaḷ avaṉik kaṭintu koṇṭāl, avaḷuṭaṉ ciṉaṅkoḷḷukiṉṟāṉ; avaḷuṭaṉ pēca maṟukkiṉṟāṉ; allatu avaḷit tiṭṭu kiṉṟāṉ. cila camayam ciṉam mītūrntu niṟkum poḻutu avaḷuṭiya āṭiyik kaṭittukkiḻikkiṉṟāṉ. ittakiya ceyalkaḷ tiṭīreṉat tōṉṟupavi; toṭarpaṟṟavi. āṉāl, caṟṟu vaḷarnta piṟaku avaṉuṭiya naṭattiyum māṟukiṉṟatu. nāḷaṭivil taṉ pēcciṉālum, naṭatti yiṉālum avaḷ maṉattip puṇpaṭutta virumpuvatilli; taṉtāyiṉ nalattip poruṭpaṭuttat toṭaṅkukiṉṟāṉ. ippoḻutu avaḷuṭiya kuṟikaḷ yāvum niṟikaḷākap pulappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tāṉ ceyyum ovvoru ceyalum avaḷi evvāṟu pātikkum eṉṟu matippiṭukiṉṟāṉ. avaṉ taṉ aṉṉiyiṭam koṇṭuḷḷa aṉpup paṟṟu avaṉuṭiya vāḻvilum naṭattiyilum ōr orumippāṭu, oḻuṅku, nilipput taṉmi, toṭarcci mutaliyavaṟṟi uṇṭākkiviṭukiṉṟatu.
taṉippaṭṭa oru paṟṟu vaḷarntu iyalpūkka vāḻkkiyi oḻuṅkum icivum kūṭiyatākac ceyvatip pōlavē, paṟṟukkaḷ yāvum ōr oḻuṅkil amintu oṉṟōṭoṉṟu iṇinta amippāka vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa. tāḻnta paṟṟukkaḷ uyarnta paṟṟukkaḷākavum, uyarnta paṟṟukkaḷ mutaṉmip paṟṟākavum vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa. nāṭṭuppaṟṟu evvāṟu vaḷarkiṉṟatu eṉpati eṭuttukkāṭṭākak koṇṭu itaṉiviḷakkuvōm. ciṟuvaṉ mutalil peṟṟōriṭam paṟṟu koḷkiṉṟāṉ; ippaṟṟu nāḷaṭivil viḷiyāṭṭut tōḻarkaḷ, piṟa kuṭumpaṅkaḷ, āciriyarkaḷ, paḷḷikaḷ, māvaṭṭam pōṉṟavaṟṟiṉ pāl muṟiyē virintucelkiṉṟatu. kuṭumpap paṟṟu mutaliyavi muṟiyē ovvoṉṟukkum tāḻntaviyāka amintu nāṭṭup paṟṟukku mutaṉmi koṭuttaṉa. nāṭṭuppaṟṟum tāḻnta paṟṟāka amintu māṉiṭa iṉappaṟṟi mutaṉmiyuṭiyatāka oppuk koḷḷalām. māṉiṭa iṉattiṉ nalatti nammuṭiya vāḻkkiyiṉ payaṉākak koṇṭāl, nammuṭiya taṉ-matippup paṟṟut taliyōṅki ēṉiya paṟṟukkaḷ yāvaṟṟiyum aṭakkiyāḷak kūṭiyatāka amiyum. itaṉ vaḷarcciyē namatu oḻukkattiṉ vaḷarcci eṉṟu kūṟalām. eṉavē, oḻukka vaḷarcciyil uḷḷak kiḷarcci nili, kāṭcip poruḷ paṟṟunili, aṟappaṟṟu nili, taṉ-matippup paṟṟu nili eṉṟa nāṉku paṭikaḷik kāṇkiṉṟōm.
paṟṟukkaḷum naṭattiyum : paṟṟukkaḷē naṭattiyi aṟutiyiṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi naṭattiyiṉ ovvoru pākattilum piratipalikkiṉṟaṉa. kāliyil ceytittāḷkaḷ paṭippataṟku varukiṉṟaṉar. palar avaṟṟip paṭikkiṉṟaṉar. oruvar uḷnāṭṭu araciyal ceytikaḷiyum maṟṟoruvar viḷiyāṭṭup pakuti kaḷiyum, piṟitoruvar viḷamparap pakutikaḷiyum, iṉṉoruvar veḷināṭṭu araciyal ceytikaḷiyum ūkkamākap paṭippatik kāṇalām. itaṉāl avaravaruṭiya vāḻkkip paṟṟukaḷ iṉṉavi eṉpati aṟiyalām.
eṉavē, oruvaruṭiya paṟṟukkaḷi aṟintāl, avaruṭiya naṭatti eppaṭiyirukkum eṉpati muṉṉarē urittu viṭalām. ākavē, nām aṭikkaṭi paḻakum maṉitarkaḷiṉ paṟṟukkaḷi aṟiyavēṇṭiyatu iṉṟiyami yātatākiṉṟatu; avaruṭiya naṭattiyē uṟṟu nōkkutal mūlantāṉ ivaṟṟi aṟiyalām.
paṟṟukkaḷ naṭattiyi ōraḷavu orumippāṭaṭiyac ceyyumāyiṉum, uḷḷak kiḷarccikaḷip pōlavē iviyum naṭattiyip pala tikkukaḷil koṇṭu celuttum. ip paṟṟukkaḷi aṭakkiyāḷa oru talimippaṟṟu iṉṟiya miyātatu. ivaṟṟik kīḻē kāṇpōm.
ciṟuvarkaḷiṉ naṭattiyil paṟṟukkaḷ eṅṅaṉam paṅku koḷḷukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatic caṟṟu ārāyvōm. ciṟuvaṉ oruvaṉ tantiyiṉ aṟivik kaṇṭu viyakkiṉṟāṉ. piṟaku ācāṉatu aṟivu avaṉukku viyappiṉi viḷivikkiṉṟatu. aṟivuḷḷa ivarkaḷiṭam avaṉukku aṉpu uṇṭākiṉṟatu. piṟaku talimiyāciriyariṉ aṟivu, paḷḷiyilpalanikaḻccikaḷil paṅku koḷḷum periyārkaḷiṉ aṟivu avaṉ uḷḷattik kavarkiṉṟaṉa. ivarkaḷiṭam paṟṟu ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. nāḷaṭivil ippaṟṟu aṟivu paṟṟiya paṟṟāka māṟukiṉṟatu. aṟivip peṟuvatiyē mutaṉmip paṟṟākavum avaṉiṭam amiyalām; aḵtē avaṉuṭiya taṉ-matippup paṟṟākavum vaṭivu peṟalām. aṟivuṭiyār ellām uṭiyār eṉṟa vaḷḷuvap perun takiyiṉ vākku itaṉi uṟutippaṭuttavum ceyyalām. ciṟanta aṟiviṉip peṟuvatiyē avaṉ vāḻkkip payaṉākap-kuṟikkōḷākak-karutip piṟavaṟṟit tāḻntaviyāka eṇṇalām. aṟivip paṟṟiya paṟṟu ivvāṟu tōṉṟum; aṟivut toṭarpuḷḷa ceytikaḷil viyappu aṭivāṉ; taṉ aṟivi veḷiyiṭuṅkāl taṉṉeṭuppum, taṉṉiṉ mikka aṟivuṭiyārik kāṇuṅkāl taṉṉoṭukkamum avaṉiṭam kāṇappeṟum. ciṟanta nūlkaḷip paṭippatilum, avaṟṟit tiraṭṭuvatilum avaṉiṭam ārvam talikāṭṭum. iṅṅaṉam avaṉuṭiya vaṉmiyāṉa uḷḷak kiḷarccikaḷ yāvum aṟiviyoṭṭiyē kāṇappeṟukiṉṟaṉa; orumippāṭum aṭikiṉṟaṉa. ēṉiya paṟṟukkaḷiviṭa aṟivuppaṟṟē ivaṉiṭam mītūrntu niṟkum; ituvē vāḻkkiyil mukkiyamāṉatākavum tōṉṟum.
iṉṉoruvaṉ ariccantiraṉippōl cattiyattiyē kuṟikkōḷ poruḷākak koḷḷalām. piṟitoruvaṉukkuk kāviyat talivarkaḷ kuṟikkōḷ poruḷkaḷāka amiyalām. tiruttoṇṭar purāṇattil varum aṭiyārkaḷiṉ vāḻkki cilarukku ilaṭciyamāka amiyalām. eṉavē, vāḻkkiyiṉ kuṟikkōḷ oruvaruṭiya naṭattiyit tīrmāṉikkiṉṟatu. ataṉ poruṭṭu etiyum tuṟappataṟku āyattamāka iruppār. kāntiyaṭikaḷiṉ vāḻkki itaṟku ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭāka amiyum.
taṉ-matippup paṟṟu : taṉ-matippup paṟṟu eṉpati mēlē kuṟippiṭṭōm aṉṟō? aḵtu eṅṅaṉam vaḷarcci peṟukiṉṟatu eṉpatic ciṟitu ārāyvōm. ciṟuvaṉ iḷamiyilēyē taṉṉic cūḻniliyiliruntu pirittu aṟikiṉṟāṉ. veḷiyulakuṭaṉ toṭarpupaṭuttik kūṟap peṟāta avaṉ aṉupavak kūṟukaḷ yāvum tiṟaṉ eṉṟa uṭkaruvāka (rēuṭanaraḷa) ami kiṉṟatu. itil moḻi avaṉukkut tuṇiyāka niṟkiṉṟatu; avaṉuṭiya ciṟappup peyar(iṭukuṟippeyar) avaṉukku oru kippiṭiyāka amiya, ataṉik koṇṭu piṟavaṟṟit taṉṉiliruntu pirittaṟikiṉṟāṉ. tāṉ uyiri eṉṟum, poruḷkaḷ uyirili eṉṟum, tāṉ etic ceyya iyalum, etic ceyya iyalātu eṉpatiyum pākupaṭutti uṇarkiṉṟāṉ. nāḷaṭivil taṉṉuṭiya naṭattiyiyē tiṟaṉāyum āṟṟaliyum peṟukiṉṟāṉ. taṉ tōḻarkaḷ taṉṉip pukaḻvatiyum ikaḻvatiyum kāṇkiṉṟāṉ; iccamūka aṟiviṉāl taṉṉi iraṇṭu nilikaḷil aṟikiṉṟāṉ. tāṉ cintaṉiyiṉ ilakkākavum, cintaṉiyāḷaṉākavum iruppati uṇarkiṉṟāṉ. paṭippaṭiyākat taṉṉuṇarcci14 vaḷarat toṭaṅku kiṉṟatu. itiliruntu tāṉ taṉmatippup paṟṟu uṇṭākiṉṟatu. itic cuṟṟit taṉṉeṭuppu, taṉṉoṭukkam, accam, ciṉampōṉṟa uḷḷak kiḷarccikaḷ amikiṉṟaṉa. ippoḻutu taṉṉi oru cila viruppu veṟuppukkaḷ, eṇṇaṅkaḷ, karuttukkaḷ, āṟṟalkaḷ, tiṟaṉkaḷ, cuvikaḷ, vāḻkkik kōlaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ nilikkaḷaṉākak karutukiṉṟāṉ. taṉṉip paṟṟiya tiṭṭamāṉa karuttu avaṉiṭam amikiṉṟatu; tāṉ `ippaṭippaṭṭa oḻukka muṭiyavaṉeṉṟum karutukiṉṟāṉ. taṉṉuṭiya tōḻarkaḷ taṉṉi naṭattum muṟi taṉ niliyi aṟivataṟkup payaṉ paṭuvatōṭaṉṟi, tāṉ eppaṭi irukka vēṇṭum eṉpati aṟiyavum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟatu. itu kalvittuṟikku oruciṟanta vaḻikāṭṭiyāka amikiṉṟatu. ciṟuvarkaḷ taṅkaḷippaṟṟic ciṟanta eṇṇaṅkaḷik koḷḷum paṭi peṟṟōrkaḷum āciriyarkaḷum vaḻivaki ceyyavēṇṭum. iḵtu avarkaḷiṉ mutaṟkaṭami yēyaṉṟi mutalāya kaṭamiyu mākum. tammip piṟar nērmiyuṭiyavarkaḷ, uṇmi yuḷḷavarkaḷ, nampikki vikkat takkavarkaḷ, uḻippāḷikaḷ eṉṟu karutiṉāl avarkaḷum avvaruṅ kuṇaṅkaḷukkup pāttirarkaḷāvar; tammi appaṇpuṭivarkaḷākavē karuti aṅṅaṉamē naṭantu koḷvar. avarkaḷiṉ ceyalkaḷil ētēṉum kuṟi iruppatic cuṭṭiyurittāl avarkaḷ nāṇamuṟṟu ataṉi nīkka muyaluvar. aṅṅaṉamiṉṟi piṟar tammit tīyavarkaḷ, payaṉaṟṟavarkaḷ, nampikki yaṟṟavarkaḷ, cōmparkaḷ eṉak karutiṉāl, avarkaḷum tam koṭumiyi uṟuti ceytukoḷvar; attakiya ceyalkaḷil cerukkum koḷvar. eṉavē, cariyāṉa matippic ciṟuvarkaḷiṭam amiyac ceyvatu kalviyāḷarkaḷiṉ kaṭamiyākum. “nī aṅṅaṉam ceyvāy eṉṟu nāṉ karutavilli”, “nī puriyum ceyalā atu?”, orukaṇita āciriyariṉ makaṉā inta matippeṇ peṟuvatu? eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa tūṇṭurikaḷ ciṟuvarkaḷiṉ taṉmatippic ciṟanta muṟiyil vaḷarkkat tuṇi ceyyum; ittakiya pukaḻurikaḷ avarkaḷiṉ ceyalkaṭku orumippāṭu tarum; uyarnta camūkattil ciṟanta uṟuppiṉarkaḷākac ceyyum. avi avarkaḷuṭiya taṉ matippu, taṉmāṉam, oḻukkam ākiyavaṟṟi vaḷarkkum. mutalil avarkaḷ kuṟik kōḷiyoṭṭi vāḻa muyaluvar; mutalil `kuṟikkōḷ-tāṉ (ṉinayaṭa-ḷanaṭaka) eṉpataṟku matipput tantu piṟaku atu piṟappikkum āṇikaḷukkum kīḻppaṭintu naṭappar. iṅṅaṉam taṉ matippup paṟṟu vaḷarcciyuṟṟu vāḻkkiyil orumip pāṭum nilip puttaṉmiyum amiyak kāraṇamākiṉṟatu. ittakiya taṉ-matippi vaḷarkkum poruṭṭē taṟkālattil iḷaṅkuṟṟa vāḷikaḷ15 ciṟiccālikku aṉuppappeṟāmal tiruttac cālikku aṉuppap peṟukiṉṟaṉar.
oruvaruṭiya viruppu veṟuppukkaḷ niṟivēṟiṉāl kaḷippu16 uṇṭākiṉṟatu; avi niṟivēṟāviṭiṉ aruvaruppu17k koḷḷac ceykiṉṟatu. cila camayam ivvaruvaruppu vāḻk kiyilēyē veṟupput taṭṭum aḷavukkukkūṭa vaḷarntu viṭukiṉṟatu. mutalil taṉ-matippup paṟṟu kuṭumpattiyoṭṭi amikiṉṟatu; nammuṭiya viruppu muḻuvatum inta vaṭṭat tilēyē cuḻaluvatāl, aṅku nām kaḷippaṭikiṉṟōm; allatu ūkkamiḻantu niṟkiṉṟōm. nāḷaṭivil ivvaṭṭam virivaṭikiṉṟatu; taṉṉiyottavarkaḷiṭiyē paḷḷic ciṟuvaṉ taṉṉeṭuppum taṉṉoṭukkamum18 koḷkiṉṟāṉ. iṅṅaṉam vāḻkki muḻuvatum oppāriṉ19 ēṟpiyē (matippu) avāvi niṟkiṉṟōm.
avamatippum āṉṟa matippum iraṇṭu
mikimakka ḷāṉmatikkaṟ pāla20 
eṉṟa nālaṭiyāri nōkkuka. iṅṅaṉamē, oru nūlāciriyaṉum taraṅkuṟintavarkaḷiṉ pukaḻurikaḷiyō ikaḻurikaḷiyō poruṭpaṭuttu villi.
paṭippaṭiyāka nammuṭiya naṭattiyiṉ ēṟpiṟkō, aṉṟi ēṟpiṉmikkō nām intak kuṟikkōḷ-taṉiyē nōkku kiṉṟōm. muṉṉark kuṟippiṭṭa aṟappaṟṟukkaḷ vaḷarcci yuṟṟavuṭaṉ, taṉiyāḷ kuṟikkōḷ camūkattiṉ uṟuppiṉaṉāki viṭukiṉṟāṉ. nammuṭiya tāṉ uṟuppāka ilaṅkum antac camūkattiṟkē nām atika matippiṉit tarukiṉṟōm. innili aṭintatum, nām taṉ-vaḷarcciyiṉ21 elli kaḷiyē neruṅki viṭukiṉṟōm. ippoḻutu ellā iyalpūkkaṅkaḷum kaṭṭuppāṭṭiṉuḷ aṭaṅki kuṟikkōḷ-tāṉāl neṟippaṭuttap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. itittāṉ nām taṉ-aṭakkam22 allatu taṉ kaṭṭuppāṭu eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟōm. ittakiya taṉ-aṭakkam nīṇṭa nāḷ payiṟcikkup piṟakē eytak kūṭiyatu; intap payiṟciyiṉ poḻutu kuṟikkōḷ-tāṉ vaḷarcci peṟṟut tulakkamuṟukiṉṟatu. perumpālōriṭam innili avāvap peṟukiṉṟatu; āṉāl, ati eytutal aritākavēyuḷḷatu.
oḻukkamum maṉuṟutiyum23
iyalpūkkac ceyal mukkūṟukaḷuṭaṉ viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. poruḷkaḷi aṟikiṉṟōm; itu aṟivu nili24 poruḷkaḷi aṟiyumpoḻutu nām vēṇṭiya poruḷāyiṉ kaḷikkiṉṟōm; vēṇṭāp poruḷāyiṉ veṟukkiṉṟōm. ivvāṟu viruppum veṟuppum kalantatu uṇarccinili.25 nām vēṇṭum poruḷāyiṉ ataṉi aṭiya nam maṉam pati patikkiṉṟatu; tuṭituṭikkiṉṟatu. innili iyaṟṟinili26 eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟum. ivi taṉittaṉiyē iyaṅkā; avi āḷumiyiṉ maṉa vaṉmikaḷum alla. oru maṉitaṉ eṇṇumpoḻutu, uṇarum poḻutu allatu ceyalāṟṟumpoḻututāṉ āḷumi27 muḻutum ceyaṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. eṉavē, maṉuṟuti eṉpatu maṉavaṉmi28 aṉṟu eṉpatākiṉṟatu. atu āḷumiyiṉ oru kuṟippiṭṭa ceyalik kuṟikkiṉṟatu. oru maṉitaṉ aṉupavaṅ kaḷiyum uḷḷuṇarviyum29 taḻuvi ataṉpaṭi naṭattalē maṉa uṟutiyākum. maṉa uṟutiyittāṉ tirukkuṟaḷ maṉattiṇmi eṉakkuṟik kiṉṟatu. oruvaruṭiya tāṉ muḻuvatum tīrmāṉam ceytu ataṉpaṭi ceyalāṟṟukiṉṟatu. eṉavē, maṉa uṟuti eṉpatu āḷumiyiṉ ceyaṟpaṭu niliyākum. mākkēl eṉṟa uḷaviyal aṟiñar ceyaṟpaṭum oḻukkamē maṉa uṟuti eṉṟu kūṟuvar.
paṟṟukkaḷ eṅṅaṉam uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu mēlē kaṇṭōmallavā? paṟṟukkaḷil nallaṉavum uṇṭu; tīyaṉavum uṇṭu. ippaṟṟukkaḷ mika vaṉmiyāka iruppiṉ taṉiyāḷiṉ vāḻkkiyil nirantaramāṉa iyalpukaḷākap paṭintu viṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila camayam paṟṟukkaḷiṭiyē pōrāṭṭam30 nikaḻtal uṇṭu. cila eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷ itaṉit teḷivākkum. nāḷi naṭipeṟa irukkum tērvukkup paṭippatā, allatu iṉṟiravu naṭipeṟavirukkum arumiyāṉa paṭakkāṭcikkuppōvatā? eṉṟu māṇākkaṉ eṇṇukiṉṟāṉ. iṉṉoruvaṉ āciriyar vēlikkup payiṟci peṟukiṉṟāṉ; payiṟci iṉṉum oṉṟiraṇṭu tiṅkaḷil muṭintuviṭum. appoḻutu eḻuttar vēli31 niyamaṉa āṇi oṉṟu avaṉi vantaṭikiṉṟatu. payiṟciyi muṭippatā, aṉṟi eḻuttar pataviyi ēṟṟukkoḷvatā? eṉṟu kalaṅki niṟkiṉṟāṉ. iṉṉoru iḷiñaṉ, kātalūkkatti niṟivēṟṟa illattiliruppatā, allatu camūkat toṇṭu puriya veḷic celvatā? eṉṟu cintaṉi ceykiṉṟāṉ. iraṇṭilumuḷḷa naṉmi tīmikaḷi ārāyntu ētāvatoṉṟit tērnteṭukka vēṇṭum. iḵtu avaṉuṭiya paṟṟukkaḷip poṟuttatākum. ittakiya iṭarppāṭāṉa nilikaḷil tāṉ mēlē kuṟippiṭṭa taṉ matippuppaṟṟu tuṇiyāka niṟkiṉṟatu. aḵtu avaṉukku vēṇṭiya āṟṟalit tantu piṟavaḻikaḷip puṟakkaṇittu aṭakkiyāḷac ceyyum. aḵtāvatu, tāḻnta uḷ-tuṭippukkaḷiyum iyalpūkkaṅkaḷiyum kuṟikkōḷāka niṟkum taṉ matippuppaṟṟiṉ āṟṟalāl vellutal ākum.
maṉa uṟutiyiṉ uram iyalpūkkaṅkaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyil iyaṅkum uḷtuṭippukkaḷiṉ urattip poṟuttatu. maṉa uṟutiyiṉ taram avaṉ koṇṭa kuṟikkōḷiṉ tarattiyum, pōrāṭum kuṟikkōḷka ḷiṉmiyi yum poṟuttatu. kuṟikkōḷ ciṟappāka amiyiṉ maṉa uṟutiyum ciṟantatāka amiyum; oḻukkamum uyarntatāka amiyum. eṉavē, māṇākkarkaḷiṭam cariyāṉa maṉuṟuti amiya āciriyarkaḷum peṟṟōrkaḷum takka kavaṉam celuttit takunta niliyil payiṟciyum aḷittal vēṇṭum.
māṇākkarkaḷiṭam maṉa uṟutiyi vaḷarkka vēṇṭumāyiṉ, paḷḷi kaḷil avarkaḷ tāmākac ceyalāṟṟavum poṟup pēṟkavum vāyppukkaḷi nalkutal vēṇṭum. taṉṉāṭci muṟi, taṉivēli, kuṭimip payiṟci pōṉṟa tuṟikaḷil āciriyar mēṟkoḷḷum muyaṟcikaḷ itaṟkup peritum payaṉpaṭum. aṉupavamiṉmiyāl māṇākkarkaḷ tavaṟu iḻittal kūṭum eṉa eṇṇi avarkaḷ ceyyavēṇṭiya muṭivukaḷi nāmē avarkaḷukkāka iyaṟṟuvatil avarkaḷukku nalampayakkātu eṉpati nām uṇarntu varukiṉṟōm. uramāṉa uṟutiyuḷḷa peṟṟōriṉ kuḻantikaḷil palar uramaṟṟa maṉa uṟuti yuḷḷavarkaḷāka iruppataṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṉa? peṟṟōr tam piḷḷikaḷukku maṉuṟutiyil payiṟciyaḷikkāmiyē. ciṟuvayati liruntē tavaṟiḻikkātu taṭukkappeṟṟa oruvaṉ vaḷarntavaṉāṉa piṟaku paranta ivvulakil taṉ aluvalkaḷit tiṟamiyuṭaṉ mēṟkoḷvāṉ eṉṟu karutuvatu payaṉaṟṟa ceyalē. ceyal tavaṟākap pōkumeṉṟu añci cariyāṉatiyum iyaṟṟat tuṇivu illātavari uṇṭākkum kalvi muṟiyāl yātu payaṉ? itu kalvi taṉ kaṭamiyil tavaṟiyatāka muṭiyumallavā? cila camayam payiṟci yaḷikkum aḷavu aḷavu mīṟavum kūṭum. aḷavukkumīṟiṉāl amirtamum nañcu aṉṟō? aḷavukku mīṟiṉāl, piṭivātamum, muraṭṭut taṉamumē viḷivukaḷāka vantu cērum. āyiṉum, neṟikkalviyil32 tuṇitalukkup payiṟci taruvatiyum cērkkavēṇṭum. oḻukkap payiṟciyil aṟivu nilik kūṟukkum uṇarcci nilik kūṟukkum takka kavaṉam celuttap peṟṟāl, tuṇitaliṉ payiṟci aṟivuṭi miyākavum irukkum; naṉmiyāṉatākavum amiyum.
kuṟikkōḷkaḷum vāḻkkit tattuva vaḷarcciyum
muḻut taṉmi peṟṟa oḻukkattil cila kuṟikkōḷkaḷ niccayam ātikkam celuttukiṉṟaṉa eṉpati nām uṟutiyākak kūṟalām.
	oḻukkam viḻuppam taralāṉ oḻukkam 
	uyiriṉum ōmpap paṭum. 33
eṉṟa kuṟaḷ kāṭṭum oḻukkam ittakiyatē. camūkam pārāṭṭum allatu paḻittuk kūṟum naṭattiyiviṭak kuṟikkōḷkaḷ neṟippaṭuttum naṭattiyē mika uyarntatu. iṅṅaṉam tammiṭa muḷḷa uḷḷoḷi yāl iyaṅkuvōr oru cilarē; camūkat tākkutalkaḷi avarkaḷ ciṟitum poruṭpaṭuttamāṭṭārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ irukkum iṭam teriyāmal maṟintu kiṭappar. ittakiya kuṟikkōḷkaḷ kuḻantiyiṉ vāḻkkiyil mika metuvāka ēṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa; avi perumpālum kuḻanti atikamāka matikkum allatu virumpum makkaḷiṭamiruntē - (e-ṭu) peṟṟōrkaḷ, āciriyarkaḷ, naṇparkaḷ - karuttēṟṟattiṉ34 mūlam amikiṉṟaṉa. aṟappaṟṟukkaḷ eṅṅaṉam vaḷarukiṉṟaṉa eṉpati mēlē urittōm. ati iṉṉum caṟṟu viḷakku vōm. mākkēl eṉṟa uḷaviyal aṟiñar mukkiyamākat toṟṟunōy pōṉṟa ottuṇarcciyālum, ciṟanta periyōr kaḷiṉ karuttēṟṟattālum avi uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār. ciṟuvarkaḷiṉ vīra-vaḻipāṭṭālum35 ivi ēṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. perumpālum vāḻvil inta uyarnta camūka vaḷarcciyip peṟuvataṟku vāyppukkaḷ amivatilli. ciṟanta aruṅ kuṇaṅkaḷik koṇṭa uyarnta talivarkaḷi aṭikkaṭi cantittu avarkaḷuṭaṉ paḻakum vāyppukkaḷum ēṟpaṭu vatilli. aṉṟiyum, avarkaḷ paḻakum talivarkaḷiṭamum pala kuṟikaḷ kuṉṟiṉmēl viḷakkupōl veḷippaṭiyākat terikiṉṟaṉa. cilar nūlkaḷiṉ mūlam uyirppiyum uṟcākat tiyum peṟukiṉṟaṉar; āṉāl, nēr muṟiyil ciṟanta periyōrkaḷiṉ vāyilākap peṟum kuṟikkōḷkaḷē niṟinta palaṉkaḷi viḷivikkum. oru kuṟikkōḷ mītu nām koḷḷum ārvattiṟkēṟpa ataṉi aṭiya muṟpaṭuvōm. nām mēṟ koḷḷum kuṟikkōḷippaṟṟit teḷivāṉa karuttuṭaṉ pala iṭarppāṭukaḷiyum poruṭpaṭuttātu avaṟṟi etirttuk kuṟikkōḷi nōkki muṉṉēṟiṉāl, nal vāḻkkiyiṉ cikaratti eṭṭip piṭikkalām.
kuṟikkōḷ vaḷarcciyāl uṇṭākum vāḻkkit tattuvam nam maṉattiyum ceyaliyum neṟippaṭuttukiṉṟatu. vāḻkkit tattuvam eṉpatu ōr arumporuḷāka iruppiṉum, nām ellōrum aṟintō aṟiyāmalō ataṉi amittuk koṇṭuḷḷōm. nām mēṟ koṇṭuḷḷa kuṟikkōḷ kaḷukkēṟpa, vāḻkkiyil palavaṟṟit tērnteṭukkiṉṟōm. kuṇam nāṭik kuṟṟam nāṭi, avaṟṟuḷ miki nāṭi mikka koḷkiṉṟōm. kuṇamum kuṟṟamum avaravar koṇṭuḷḷa kuṟikkōḷkaḷip poṟuttavi. ciṟanta kalviyiṉ nōkkam eṉṉa eṉpati vaḷḷuvap peruntaki,
	kaṟṟataṉāl āya payaṉeṉkol? vālaṟivaṉ
	naṟṟāḷ toḻāar eṉiṉ.36
eṉṟu kūṟuvar. aṟam poruḷ iṉpam vīṭaṭital nūṟpayaṉē37 eṉa ilakkaṇa nūl kūṟum. iviyē namatu taṉ-matippup paṟṟāka amital vēṇṭum. inta uyarnta kuṟik kōḷkaḷiṉ vaḷarcci kuṭumpam, paḷḷi, camūkam eṉṟa cūḻ nilikaḷip poṟuttatu. 
oḻukkat tulakkattil paḷḷiyiṉ paṅku
kuḻantiyiṉ vaḷarcci kuṭumpattil toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu. eṉavē, kuṭumpattiṉ poṟuppu avvaḷarcciyil atikamākiṉṟatu. eṉṟālum, paḷḷikaṭkum ittuṟiyil ciṟanta poṟuppu irukkattāṉ ceykiṉṟatu. uyarnta kuṟikkōḷkaḷiyum, taṉ-matippup paṟṟiyum maṉattiṇmiyiyum vaḷarppatil paḷḷikkuc ciṟanta paṅku uṇṭu; ati atu eṭuttukkoḷḷattāṉ vēṇṭum. paḷḷi ataṟkeṉavē ēṟpaṭuttap peṟṟa oru niliyamākum. ippaṇiyit tiṟampaṭac ceyya vēṇṭu meṉpataṟkākavē taṉip payiṟcipeṟṟa āciriyarkaḷ aṅku niyamikkap peṟukiṉṟaṉar.
taṉ-matippup paṟṟu camūkam ēṟpaṭuttum paṇpu eṉṟu mēlē kaṇṭōm. vaḻivaḻiyāka vantu koṇṭirukkum paḷḷiyiṉ marapu,38 ataṉ kūṭṭu vāḻkki, paḷḷiyil ēṟpāṭu ceyyappeṟṟirukkum viḷiyāṭṭu vakikaḷ, payiṟṟummuṟi, paḷḷiyil paṇiyāṟṟum āciriyarkaḷiṉ celvākku mutaliyavi māṇākkarkaḷiṭam taṉippaṭṭa muṟiyilum kūṭivāḻ muṟiyilum payaṉpaṭum ciṟanta paṟṟukkaḷi vaḷarkkiṉṟaṉa; ivi takka muṟiyil neṟippaṭuttap peṟṟut taṉ matippup paṟṟāka amintāl ciṟanta muṟiyil oḻukkam amiyum.
payiṟṟum āciriyarkaḷum uṭaṉ payilum tōḻarkaḷum kuḻantiyiṭam taṉ-matippi uṇṭākkupavarkaḷ. avarkaḷ etirpārkkummuṟiyil tāṉ kuḻanti naṭantu koḷḷum. avaṉiṭam amiyum kuṟikkōḷkaḷ payiṟṟum āciriyar kaḷālum, payilum pāṭaṅkaḷālum, uṭaṉiruntu pāṭaṅkēṭkum ēṉiya tōḻarkaḷālumē ēṟpaṭum. āciriyariṉ pukaḻurikaḷ, kaṭuñ coṟkaḷ, ceyalkaḷ, uṇarccikaḷ, aṟivu pōṉṟa vikaḷum paricilkaḷ, taṇṭaṉikaḷ pōṉṟa ēṟpāṭukaḷum avaṉiṭam paṟṟukkaḷi uṇṭākkum. ivaṟṟic cariyāka uṇarāta āciriyarkaḷ māṇākkarkaḷukkuk kuṟumpukkāraṉ, oḻuṅkaṟṟavaṉ, maṭiyaṉ, pōkkiri eṉṟa paṭṭaṅkaḷi vaḻaṅku kiṉṟaṉar! itaṉāl palar ippaṭṭaṅkaḷi ēṟṟu avi uṟutiyākumāṟu naṭantu koḷkiṉṟaṉar! ippaṭip payaṉaṟṟup pōkum māṇākkar eṇṇiṟantōr. ivaṟṟi etirkkum oru cila tuṇivāṉa māṇākkarkaḷ āciriyarkaḷukkē paṭṭamaḷippu viḻā naṭatti viṭukiṉṟaṉar!
vakuppaṟikaḷilum, viḷiyāṭukaḷaṅkaḷilum, piṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum māṇākkarkaḷiṭam taṉṉampikkiyum taṉ-matippum vaḷara vāyppukkaḷ nalkutal vēṇṭum. maṉa uṟuti vaḷaravum, ciṟanta paḻakkaṅkaḷi vaḷarkkavum iṭam taravēṇṭum. ec ceyalkaḷ ceytālum avaṟṟiṉ nōkkaṅkaḷi māṇākkar aṟiyac ceytal vēṇṭum. nōkkaṅkaḷ aṭiya vēṇṭum eṉṟu māṇākkarkaḷ tāmāka muyaṉṟāltāṉ maṉattiṇmi vaḷarum. contak kavarccikaḷiṉṟēl cuṟucuṟuppum viṭā muyaṟciyum tōṉṟā. māṇākkarkaḷmuṉ vikkap peṟum nōkkaṅkaḷ virivil aṭiyac kūṭiyaṉavāka iruttal vēṇṭum. ennavēliyilum nirppantamum palavantamum kūṭā; etiyum viṭutali yuṇarcciyuṭaṉ ceytāl tāṉ maṉat tiṇmi vaḷarum. tērvukkumuṉ māṇākkarkaḷāl ceyyap peṟum vēli āṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil ceyyap peṟuvatilli eṉpatu nām aṟintatē. itiliruntu āciriyarkaḷ kaṟpikkum muṟikaḷilum piṟavaṟṟilum pala paṭippiṉikaḷi aṟintu koḷḷalām. pāṭaṅkaḷ campantamākap pala tiṭṭaṅkaḷi ēṟpaṭuttikkoṇṭu avaṟṟi niṟivēṟṟa vāyppukkaḷ taralām. iṉṟiya kuṭimip payiṟciyiluḷḷa pala naṟkūṟukaḷik koṇṭu ivaṟṟi eḷitil amikkalām. ciṟiya ceyalkaḷilum periya ceyalkaḷilum poṟuppu māṇākkarkaḷuṭiyatāka amintāl aṉittum cīrpaṭum.
varalāṟṟup pāṭaṅkaḷ, ilakkiyappāṭaṅkaḷ, aṟiviyala ṟiñarkaḷiṉ varalāṟukaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟiliruntu pala aṟiñarkaḷ, oḻukka cīlarkaḷ, toṇṭarkaḷ pōṉṟavarkaḷiṉ kuṟik kōḷkaḷik koṇṭu māṇākkarkaḷ tamakku vēṇṭiya kuṟikkōḷkaḷi ēṟpaṭuttikkoḷḷukiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē, ataṟkēṟṟa pakutikaḷ pāṭaṅkaḷil amiyumāṟu ceytal vēṇṭum. paḷḷi nūlakaṅkaḷilum māṇākkarkaḷiṉ pala nilikaḷukkēṟpa avarkaḷē paṭittaṟintu koḷḷakkūṭiya ittakiya varalāṟukaḷ aṭaṅkiya pala puttakaṅkaḷi vāṅki vittu avaṟṟi avarkaḷē virumpippaṭikkum vāyppuk kaḷiyum nalkutal vēṇṭum.
oḻukkak kalvi tarum nēraṅkaḷil uṇmiyiṉ iṉṟiya miyāmiyi uṇarttavum, poyyiṉ virumpattakāmiyi vaṟpuṟuttavum ciṟu coṟ poḻivukaḷ naṭattuvatāl payaṉilli. ivi naṉku amivataṟkup paḷḷi vāḻkkiyilēyē pala vāyppukkaḷi nalki avaṟṟiṉ mūlam payiṟci yaḷittal vēṇṭum. tuṇivu uṇṭāka vēṇṭumāyiṉ, tuṇivi veḷiyiṭa vāyppu vēṇṭum. periyōrkaḷiṉ eṭuttuk kāṭṭum iṉṟiyamiyātatu. aṟivuriyāl payaṉilli; ceytu kāṭṭalē vēṇṭappeṟuvatu.



	collutal yārkkum eḷiya; ariyavām
	colliya vaṇṇam ceyal.39
paḷḷiyiṉ aṉṟāṭa nikaḻccikaḷil ciṟuvarkaḷiṭam payaṉpaṭum pala paḻakkaṅkaḷi ēṟpaṭuttalām; vaḷarac ceyyalām. paṭṭaṟivu mikamika māṇākkarkaḷ tam maṉaccāṉṟiṟkup poruntum ceyalkaḷiyē ceyya viḻikiṉṟaṉar. taṅkaḷ naṭattiyiyum piṟar naṭattiyiyum matippiṭavum muyalkiṉṟaṉar.
viḷiyāṭukaḷattil nalloḻukka vaḷarccikkup pala vāyppukkaḷ taralām. ovvoruvaki viḷiyāṭṭiṟkum talimip poṟuppu māṇākkarkaḷukkē taralām. palvēṟu nikaḻccikaḷukkut tērtal muṟiyil māṇākkarkaḷit tērnteṭuttu avarkaḷiṭam pala poṟuppukkaḷi oppa ṭikkalām. paḷḷi viḷiyāṭṭup pōṭṭikaḷ naṭi peṟuṅkāl itaṟkup pala vāyppukkaḷ eḻukiṉṟaṉa.
paḷḷiyiṉ kuḻūuk kiḷarcci40 viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷālum, paḷḷiviḻākkaḷālum, ilakkiyak kaḻakaṅkaḷālum, palvēṟu pōṭṭikaḷālum, āciriyariṉ ceyalkaḷālum vaḷarkkap peṟukiṉṟatu. nāṭṭuppaṟṟu, carvatēcappaṟṟu, vāḻkki nōkkam, cakōtarattuvam, camatarumam, camūkat toṇṭu pōṉṟa paṇpukaḷ paḷḷiyiltāṉ vaḷarkkappeṟutal vēṇṭum. piṟkālattil māṇākkariṉ muḻumiyāṉa vāḻkkiyiṉ nōkkattip paḷḷi ōraḷavu niṟivēṟṟa vēṇṭum. mēṟ kūṟiyavi niṟivēṟāviṭil, paḷḷi etic cātittālum payaṉilli. oḻukkamē vāḻkkikku viḻuppam taruvatu; atuvē vāḻkkiyiṉ uyirnāṭi eṉpatip payiṟṟuvōr eṉṟum niṉivil vittup paṇiyāṟṟa vēṇṭum.
oḻukka vaḷarcciyil paḷḷiyiṉ muyaṟciyiyum vīṭṭiṉ muyaṟciyiyum iyipuṟuttal
paḷḷiyum vīṭum iraṇṭu ciṟanta kalvi niliyaṅkaḷ. oḻukkam mikacciṟiya paruvattilēyē amivatāl, atil vīṭu atikap paṅkiṉip peṟukiṉṟatu. māṇākkarkaḷiṉ vāḻkkiyil paḷḷiyil kaḻiyum nēramē atikam eṉpati evarum aṟivar. eṉavē, vīṭṭil naṭipeṟum nikaḻccikaḷ avaṉ paḷḷiyil peṟum kalviyip pātikkac ceykiṉṟaṉa. ākavē, paḷḷi iyaṟṟa virumpum viṉikaḷaṉittiyum tāṉākavē cātittu viṭa muṭiyātu. oḻukkam, āḷumi, kuṭimi ākiya paṇpukaḷ māṇākkarkaḷiṭam vaḷarppataṟkum, māṇākkarkaḷiṭam virumpattakka paḻakkaṅkaḷiyum maṉappāṉmikaḷiyum uṇṭākkuvataṟkum, peṟṟōriṉ uṭkarutti yaṟintu kuḻantikaḷiṉ vaḷarcciyil avarkaḷiṉ tuṇiyip peṟuvataṟkum paḷḷi taṉ vēliyi vīṭṭu vēliyuṭaṉ iyipu paṭutta vēṇṭum; iraṇṭum iṇintu neruṅkip piṇintu pōka vaḻivakikaḷi vakukka vēṇṭum.
peṟṟōriṉ uṭkaruttum māṇākkarkaḷiṉ ceyalip purintu koḷḷa virumpum āciriyarukkup peritum payaṉpaṭum. kuḻantiyiṉ uṭalnili, kuḻanti vāḻum illattiṉ cūḻ nili, aṅkuḷḷa kavarccikaḷ, cuṟṟuppuṟac cūḻnilikaḷ, vīṭṭil avaṉuṭiya naṭatti mutaliyavaṟṟi āciriyar naṉku aṟintu koṇṭāltāṉ āciriyarum tam toḻilit tiṟam paṭac ceyya muṭiyum. āciriyar aṭikkaṭi kuḻantikaḷiṉ illaṅkaḷip pārviyiṭutal, peṟṟōruṭaṉ uḷḷaṅ kalantu iṉṉuri yāṭutal pōṉṟavaṟṟālum, peṟṟōruṭaṉ iṇintu iyaṟṟum ceyalkaḷ mūlamum māṇākkarkaḷpaṟṟiya pala piracciṉikaḷit tīrttuk koḷḷavēṇṭum. peṟṟōrkaḷ tam contak kuḻantiyip paṟṟi atikamākat terintu koḷḷum aṟivuṭiyavarāka iruttal vēṇṭum. uṭal vaḷarcciyil tām kāṭṭum akkaṟiyip pōl uḷḷa vaḷarcciyilum tamakkup paṅku uṇṭu eṉpati ovvoru peṟṟōrum uṇartal vēṇṭum. uṇara muṭiyāta peṟ ṟōrkaḷuṭaṉ āciriyarkaḷ uriyāṭik kalantu pēci, avarkaḷ uṇarumpaṭic ceyyalām. 
māṇākkarkaḷiṉ vaḷarcciyil paḷḷiyum āciriyarum mēṟkoḷḷum ceyalkaḷ aṉittiyum peṟṟōrkaḷ naṉku aṟital vēṇṭum. peṟṟōr-āciriyar kūṭṭaṅkaḷ, taṉi uriyāṭalkaḷ, aṟikkikaḷ, vīṭukaḷukku aṉuppum kuṟippukkaḷ, paḻiya māṇākkark kaḻakak kūṭṭaṅkaḷ, tērcci aṟikkikaḷ mutaliyavi aṉittum innōkkatti niṟivēṟṟuvataṟku mēṟkoḷḷappeṟum vāyilkaḷ ākum. ivi ovvoṉṟiyum peṟṟōr purintu koḷḷa vēṇṭum. puriyāta peṟṟōr kaṭkup puriya vikka vēṇṭiyatu āciriyarkaḷiṉ kaṭami. kuḻantikaḷiṉ vaḷarcci ciṟanta muṟiyil pātukākkap peṟavēṇṭumeṉpataṟkākavē camūkam āciriyarkaṭkut taṉip payiṟci aḷittu varukiṉṟatu eṉpati āciriyarkaḷ uṇartal vēṇṭum.
nilitta payaṉip peṟavēṇṭumāyiṉ, paḷḷi toṭaṅkum nalviṉikaḷ aṉittum vīṭṭilum toṭarntu naṭipeṟa vacatikaḷ aḷittal vēṇṭum. uṭalnili, pala mutaliyavaṟṟip paṟṟiya uṭalnala vaḻikaḷuṭaṉ, nalla nūlkaḷ, paruva itaḻkaḷ, viḷiyāṭṭuc cāmāṉkaḷ mutaliyavi vīṭṭilum kiṭikka vēṇṭum. vīṭṭup poṟuppukkaḷ, kaṭamikaḷ, paṇattik kiyāḷutal, kāykaṟic canti, maḷikikkaṭi mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkuc ceṉṟu varutal pōṉṟa poṟuppukkaḷiyum cila camayam kuḻantikaṭku aḷittal naṟpayaṉ viḷivikkum. ivi paḷḷi vēlikkup peruntuṇiyākavum amiyum.
kuḻantikaḷik kālā kālattil paḷḷikku aṉuppuvatu, paḷḷiyil tarappeṟum vīṭṭu vēliyi (hṭiana-ṟaṭicama)c carivarac ceyyac colluvatu. paḷḷi oḻuṅku muṟikaḷi kavaṉikkac ceyvatu pōṉṟavaṟṟil peṟṟōrkaḷ āciriyarukkup peruntuṇiyāka iruttal vēṇṭum. kuḻanti vaḷaruṅkāl ataṉ oḻukkattip pātikkum nikaḻccikaḷ vīṭṭilum eḻalām, paḷḷiyilum eḻalām. vīṭṭil naṭipeṟupavi paḷḷi naṭattiyip pātikkum; paḷḷiyil naṭipeṟupavi vīṭṭu naṭattiyit tākkalām. peṟṟōr-āciriyar kūṭṭuṟavu, ittakiyavaṟṟip purintu koḷḷa vāyppukkaḷ nalkum. 



iyal-12
matippīṭum cōtaṉiyum
kuḻantikaḷ paḷḷikaḷilum paḷḷikku veḷiyilum kaṟṟavaṟṟi aḷantaṟiyap payaṉpaṭuttum cōtaṉikaḷik1 kalvit tuṟi āyvukaḷ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭalām. āciriyarkaḷ kaṟpittalāl kuḻantikaḷ aṟintu koṇṭavaṟṟiyum, vakuppaṟi, paḷḷi aṉupavaṅkaḷāl aṟintu koṇṭavaṟṟiyum, paḷḷikku veḷiyil peṟum aṉupavaṅkaḷāl aṟintu koṇṭavaṟṟiyum taṉittaṉiyākap pirittu aṟivatum, ovvoru vakiyilum aṟintukoṇṭatu evvaḷavu eṉpatik kaṇakkiṭṭu aṟivatum iyalāta kāriyam. eṉavē, ellāt tuṟikaḷilum kaṟṟavaṟṟi oru cērattāṉ aṟintukoḷḷa iyalum. māṇākkaṉ evaṟṟik kaṟṟirukkiṉṟāṉ, evvaḷavu naṉṟākak kaṟṟirukkiṉṟāṉ eṉṟu aṟutiyiṭuvatutāṉ matippiṭutal2 eṉpatu.
ciṟitu kālamākak kalvipaṟṟi mēṟkoḷḷappeṟṟu varum ārāyccikaḷil oṉṟu tērvukaḷip3 paṟṟiya ārāycciyākum. nīṇṭa kālamākap paḷḷikaḷil mēṟkoḷḷappeṟum tērvu kaḷiṉ nōkkaṅkaḷippaṟṟiyum, avi enta aḷavu muṟṟup peṟṟuḷḷaṉa eṉpatip paṟṟiyum pala nāṭukaḷil ārāyccikaḷ naṭipeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ payaṉākap pala putiya āyvukaḷum karuttukkaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.
tērvukaḷiṉ nōkkam: payiṟṟaliṉ payaṉi aḷantaṟiyap payaṉpaṭupavi tērvukaḷ. eṉavē, avi toṉṟutoṭṭu naṭipeṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa. vakuppu māṟṟam, paḷḷikkaṭṭaṇac caluki, toḻilakaṅkaḷukkum aluvalakaṅ kaḷukkum āṭkaḷip poṟukki eṭuttal ākiyavaṟṟiṟku avi mēṟkoḷḷap peṟuvati nām aṟivōm. tērvukaḷiṉṟi māṇākkarkaḷiṉ tērcciyi uttēcamāka aṟutiyiṭuvatu virumpattakkataṉṟu.  pala iṭaṅkaḷil māṇākkarkaḷ payilukiṉṟaṉar; pala paḷḷikaḷil payilukiṉṟaṉar.  pala āciriyarkaḷ kaṟpikkiṉṟaṉar. ākavē, potut tērvukaḷ iṉṟiyami yātavi.
tērvukaḷ māṇākkarkaḷiṉ tiṟaṉiyum kaṟṟavaṟṟiyum matippiṭuvataṟku ēṟpaṭṭa kaṭṭuppāṭṭuṭaṉ aminta uṟṟu nōkkal4 ākum.  orē kaṭṭuppāṭāṉa cūḻniliyil ellōriyum orē kālattil matippiṭuvataṟkut tērvukaḷ tuṇi ceykiṉṟaṉa.
4kaṭṭuppāṭṭuṭaṉ amintu uṟṟu nōkkal-uṭivēcaṭiṭaṭanaṉa ṭiḷenaceyaviṭin.
payilvōrukkum tērvukaḷ payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  avi māṇākkarkaḷi ūṉṟip paṭikkat tūṇṭit tiṭṭamāṉa aṟivip peṟac ceykiṉṟaṉa.  paḷḷi vāḻkkiyilum puṟa vāḻkkiyilum oruvita oḻuṅkiṉi ēṟpaṭutti virumpattakka vakiyil pōṭṭi  maṉappāṉmiyi uṇṭākkukiṉṟaṉa.  tērvukaḷil payaṉākap payilvōr tam aṟiviṉ kuṟipāṭukaḷi aṟiyavum, payiṟṟuvōr tam payiṟṟaliṉ kuṟiniṟikaḷi aṟiyavum muṭikiṉṟatu.  eṉavē, tērvukaḷ paṇṭutoṭṭu iṉṟuvari iṉṟiyamiyāta tolliyākavē iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa.
iṉṟu iruvita āyvukaḷ naṭimuṟiyil iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa.  oṉṟu, kaṭṭuri āyvukaḷ, maṟṟoṉṟu, putiya muṟi āyvukaḷ, vāymoḻi āyvukaḷum5 cila camayam mēṟkoḷḷappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.
kaṭṭuri āyvukaḷ6
kaṭṭuri āyvi nām naṉku aṟivōm.  itu pal lāṇṭukaḷāka naṭi muṟiyil iruntu varuvatu; mūṉṟu allatu iraṇṭari maṇikkāla aḷavil aintu allatu āṟu viṉākkaḷik kēṭṭu oru pāṭam muḻuvatilum māṇākkaṉukku irukka vēṇṭiya oṉṟu allatu iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟāṇṭu aṟivi aḷantaṟiya muyalkiṉṟatu inta āyvu.  putu muṟi āyvukaḷ celvākkup peṟṟa piṟaku itaṉ celvākku kuṟiyat toṭaṅkiyirukkiṉṟatu.  eṉiṉum, inta āyvi aṭiyōṭu nīkkavaḻiyē illi; kallūri vakuppukkaḷil itutāṉ iṉṟum āṭci celutti varukiṉṟatu.  pālārṭu7 pōṉṟa kalvi nipuṇarkaḷ ivvāyvu cariyāṉa aḷavuk karuviyalla eṉpatip pallāṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē meyppittuviṭṭaṉar.  itiluḷḷa kuṟikaḷiyum niṟikaḷiyum naṉku eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar.  avaṟṟuḷ oru cilavaṟṟi īṇṭu kūṟuvōm.
kuṟikaḷ : (1) inta āyvukaḷāl aḷantaṟiyakkūṭiya poruḷaṟivu kuṟivu.  ciṟiya kāla aḷavil āṇṭu muḻuvatum paṭittaṟinta pāṭa aṟivi aintāṟu viṉākkaḷāl tēṟiviṭa muṭiyum eṉṟu eṇṇuvatu poruntātavoṉṟu.  oru viṉāvit tappākap purintu koḷpavar allatu avviṭiyiṉ pakuti kaṟpikkappaṭṭapoḻutu paḷḷikku vārātiruntavar cariyāka viṭiyiṟukka muṭiyātu pōkak kūṭum.
(2) āyvāḷarkaḷ viṭiyēṭukaḷi orē potu nōkkamum orē vita nōkkamum koṇṭu tiruttukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟu kūṟuvataṟkilli.  viṭikaḷiṉ takutiyiyaṟintu ēṟṟa matip peṇkaḷik koṭuppatu āyvāḷarkaḷiṉ maṉaniliyiyē poṟuttirukkiṉṟatu.  mukkuṇavacattāl muṟi piṟaḻntu pōvatuṭaṉ nillātu, ciṉam, makiḻcci, mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkēṟpa matippeṇkaḷ kuṟintum mikuntum pōkakkūṭum.
(3) kaṭṭuṟi eṉpatu pala kūṟukaḷik koṇṭa tiṟaṉāl uṇṭākkap peṟṟatoru paṭippu. ovvoruvarum tattamak kēṟṟavāṟu aḷavukōlkaḷi māṟṟikkoṇṭu matippiṭuvar.
(4) ivvāyvāl peṟum matippeṇkaḷ nampat takuntaviyalla; māṟupāṭillātaviyumalla.  naṉku matippiṭa vēṇṭumāyiṉ atika kālam ākum.  poruḷaṟivu illi yāyiṉum coṟtiṟamiyāl atika matippeṇkaḷ peṟutal kūṭum.
niṟikaḷ : (1) āyvukaḷi āyattam ceyvatum eḷitu; kiyāḷuvatum eḷitu.  viṉākkaḷukku evvāṟu viṭiyiṟuppatu eṉpatu paṟṟit tērvāḷar māṇākkarkaṭku viḷakkat tēvi illi.
(2) paḷḷik kalvi ēṟpāṭṭiluḷḷa ellāp pāṭaṅkaṭkum immuṟi āyvukaḷi eḷitil kiyāḷalām.
(3) poruḷkaḷiṉ variyaṟivukaḷ kūṟuvatu, teḷivu paṭuttik kāṭṭuvatu, tokuppatu, vakuppatu, virippatu, curukkuvatu mutaliya tiṟaṉkaḷiyellām ivvaki āyvukaḷāltāṉ aḷantaṟiya iyalum.
putiya muṟi āyvukaḷ
putiya muṟi āyvukaḷ8 eṉpavi oru taṉiyāḷiṉ paṇpukaḷi oru eṇ matippāl viḷakkuvataṟkeṉṟu āyntu tiṭṭamiṭappeṟṟa nilimiyākum.  ivaṟṟil kiṭṭattaṭṭa muppattintukku mēṟpaṭṭa vakikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  eṉṟālum, ivaṟṟi aṟiñarkaḷ mīṭṭaṟital vaki (canuṭipanviiyīn valayīna), niṉivukūr vaki (canuyaṭaṭa valayīna) eṉa iru kūṟiṭṭu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar.
mīṭṭaṟital vakiyil, cari-tavaṟu āyvukaḷ, poṟukku āyvukaḷ, iṇikkum āyvukaḷ mutaliyavi aṭaṅkum; niṉivukūrvakiyil nirappu āyvukaḷ mutaliyavi aṭaṅkum.  āyvut tuṟiyil muṉṉēṟṟam aṭiya aṭiya, aṟiñarkaḷ putiyavakikaḷikkaṇṭu koṇṭē irukkiṉṟaṉar.  eṉavē, ivvakikaḷiṉ eṇṇikkiyum vaḷarntu koṇṭē yirukkiṉṟatu.  āṉāl, ēḻu allatu eṭṭu vaki āyvukaḷē naṭimuṟiyil payaṉpaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.
cari-tavaṟu āyvukaḷ9 : cila kuṟitta toṭar moḻikaḷik koṭuttu avaṟṟuḷ varum karuttukkaḷ cariyāṉaviyā, tavaṟāṉaviyā eṉpataṟkēṟpak kākkippuḷḷiyiyō cuḻiyiyō iṭumpaṭi kūṟutal.  ivaṟṟiyē viṉāvāka amittu ‘ām,’ ‘illi’, ‘teriyātu’, eṉṟa viṭikaḷil oṉṟi eḻutumāṟum kūṟalām.
se-ṭu]aṭiyiṟkāṇum coṟkaḷ cilavaṟṟil eḻuttup piḻikaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa cariyāṉavaṟṟiṟku  nērāka \ eṉṟum, piḻiyāṉavaṟṟiṟku  nērāka o eṉṟum kuṟikaḷi iṭuka.

2. nāṟāyaṇaṉ		6. araṅkēṟṟam
3. kōtumpi		7. vāḷippaḻam
4. kōttāṉ		8. viyāḷaṉ
eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavi.
palviṭiyiṟ poṟukku āyvukaḷ10: muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟi viṭa ivi caṟṟu arumiyum vaḷarcciyum uṭiyavi.  ivaṟṟuḷ oru poruḷpaṟṟiya pala toṭar moḻikaḷil etu cari eṉṟu māṇākkarkaḷ oṉṟit tērnteṭukka vēṇṭum.  ovvoru viṉāvukkum  mūṉṟiliruntu aintu varit toṭar moḻikaḷ koṭukkappeṟum,

nirappum āyvukaḷ11: kōṭiṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷil ēṟṟa coṟkaḷi amittut toṭarmoḻiyi muṭittukkāṭṭal.  ivvaki āyvukaḷ kīḻvakuppu māṇākkarkaṭku mikavum ēṟṟavi.  ēṟṟa colli eḻutuvataṟku muṉ oru muṟi toṭarmoḻiyip paṭittup piṉ colli
amittup piṉṉum oru muṟi toṭarmoḻiyip paṭittut toṭar moḻi cariyāṉa poruḷik koṭukkiṉṟatā eṉṟu pārkkumpaṭi aṟivuṟutta vēṇṭum.

2. kāṅkira makācapi 	        yāṇṭil ēṟpaṭṭatu.

iṇikkum āyvukaḷ12; iraṇṭu varicikaḷākat toṭarmoḻikaḷi uṇmiyum poruttamum muṟiyum piṟaḻa amittu avaṟṟip poruttumpaṭi ceytal iṇikkum āyvukaḷākum.  nikaḻccikaḷ, avi nikaḻnta yāṇṭukaḷ; pōrkaḷ, avaṟṟi naṭattiya cēṉit talivarkaḷ, pulavarkaḷ, avarkaḷ iyaṟṟiya nūlkaḷ; paḻamoḻikaḷ avaṟṟiṉ karuttukkaḷ; maraput toṭarkaḷ, avaṟṟiṉ poruḷkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟi ivvāṟu poruttac ceyyalām. kāraṇa kāriyaṅkaḷiyum varicippaṭuttalām.  kuṟitta poruḷaṟivit tēṟa itu ciṟanta muṟiyākum.
se-ṭu]  kīḻkkaṇṭavaṟṟil a piriviluḷḷavaṟṟi ā piriviluḷḷa vaṟṟuṭaṉ poruttik kāṭṭuka.  pacura vaḷiviṟkuḷ viṭiyiṉ eṇṇi maṭṭilum iṭṭāl pōtumāṉatu.
		a-pirivu			ā-pirivu
